📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • RSV 季节于 2022 年 2 月中旬开始。RT-PCR 确诊的病例中有 54% (25/46) 报告有一种或多种症状,其中 26% (12/46) 报告发烧,37% (17/46) 报告咳嗽。中位 RNA 脱落持续时间为 7 天(IQR = 4-11 天)。
  • 2018 年至 2023 年期间,在 60 岁以上的成年人中发现了 42 例 RSV 编码住院病例。年年龄和性别标准化 RSV 编码住院率显示出显着的逐年变化。在RSV编码入院的患者中,中位住院时间为11天(IQR 6-18),四名患者(9.52%)在住院期间死亡。
  • 结果表明,参与者能够描述 RSV 感染的 流行病学,讨论 65 岁及以上成年人和患有高危疾病的年轻人的临床表现和结果,并解决诊断挑战和当前的 治疗 选择。 [10.1016/j.jen.2026.03.008]。
  • 在出生后的前五周,与母体 RSVpreF 策略相比,宽限期内使用 mAb 策略使 RSV 住院风险加倍(aHR:2.0 [95% CI:1.0-4.9]),并增加就医 RSV 风险(aHR:1.6 [95% CI:1.0-2.7])。与母体 RSVpreF 策略相比,mAb 预期策略的 RSV 住院风险相似 (aHR = 0.9 [95% CI: 0.3-1.9])。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The RSV season started mid-February 2022. One or more symptoms were reported from 54% (25/46) of RT-PCR–confirmed episodes, fever in 26% (12/46), and cough in 37% (17/46).
  • During 2018–2023, 42 RSV‐coded hospitalizations were identified among adults aged ≥ 60 years. Annual age‐ and sex‐standardized RSV‐coded hospitalization rates showed substantial year‐to‐year variability.
  • The results indicated that participants were able to describe the epidemiology of RSV infection, discuss its clinical presentation and outcomes in adults aged 65 years and older and younger adults with high-risk conditions, and address diagnostic challenges and current treatment options.
  • Of 597 430 included Medicare beneficiaries (66% female; median age, 82 [IQR, 75-88] years), RSV VE against RSV-associated hospitalizations was 73% (95% CI, 65%-79%). [10.1001/jamainternmed.2026.3841].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of Global Health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-28  |  DOI: 10.7189/jogh.16.04251  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 16, 04251

巴西 呼吸道合胞病毒、流感和 COVID-19 的季节性动态和负担:国家 呼吸道合胞病毒 研究(2019-2023 年)

Seasonal dynamics and burden of respiratory syncytial virus, influenza, and COVID-19 in Brazil: a national surveillance study (2019–2023)
作者： Guilherme Silva Julian, Julia Regazzini Spinardi, Ana Paula Flora, Srinivas Rao Valluri, Florence Lefebvre d'Hellencourt, Caihua Liang, Elizabeth Begier, Thaís Zamboni Berra, Lucas Vieira Cortez, Renato Vitorasso
摘要中文翻译背景:在巴西和其他拉丁美洲国家,呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感和 COVID-19 的负担和季节性模式仍然不明确。了解其流行病学模式对于为公共卫生干预措施和预防策略提供信息至关重要。我们的目的是使用国家 监测 数据评估呼吸道病毒的季节性,以指导 疫苗接种 时间安排并加强巴西的准备策略。方法:我们使用 2019 年至 2023 年间通过 SIVEP 系统收集的国家 监测 数据进行了一项回顾性、观察性、描述性人群研究。我们分析了由 RSV、流感和 COVID-19 引起的确诊病例、住院和死亡。我们通过局部估计散点图平滑方法使用多重季节趋势分解进行时间序列分解和季节趋势分析。我们计算了每个病因学组成部分的月平均值,以评估其季节性负担。结果:SIVEP 报告的实验室确诊严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 病例中,有 69,389 例 RSV 病例、45,881 例流感病例和 2,299,712 例 COVID-19 病例。每年,超过 90% 的 RSV 诊断发生在 0-10 岁儿童中,而流感和 COVID-19 在老年人中更为常见。与 RSV 相关的儿童死亡比例从 2020 年的 19.2% 增加到 2023 年的 62.0%。RSV 的季节性高峰出现在 3 月至 6 月,流感病例在 1 月达到高峰,而 COVID-19 的季节性高峰则不太一致。观察到地理差异,巴西东南部和南部地区病例集中度较高。结论:巴西国家 监测 系统报告的 SARI 病例中呼吸道病毒负担因病原体、年龄组、地区和季节而异,并且可能受到检测策略变化的影响。加强诊断能力和 监测 至关重要,特别是对于成人 RSV 病例,由于检测有限和诊断敏感性降低,这些病例可能漏报。季节性 疫苗接种 计划(例如针对流感的计划)仍然至关重要,并且可以通过整合新的预防措施(例如针对老年人的 RSV 疫苗接种 和保护婴儿的孕产妇免疫接种)来进一步加强。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们的目的是使用国家 监测 数据评估呼吸道病毒的季节性,以指导 疫苗接种 时间安排并加强巴西的准备策略。
设计与对象
我们使用 2019 年至 2023 年间通过 SIVEP 系统收集的国家 监测 数据进行了一项回顾性、观察性、描述性人群研究。
核心方法
我们通过局部估计散点图平滑方法使用多重季节趋势分解进行时间序列分解和季节趋势分析。
主要结果
与 RSV 相关的儿童死亡比例从 2020 年的 19.2% 上升到 2023 年的 62.0%。
解释与创新
观察到地理差异,巴西东南部和南部地区病例集中度较高。
科研与公卫意义
加强诊断能力和 监测 至关重要,特别是对于成人 RSV 病例,由于检测有限和诊断敏感性降低,这些病例可能漏报。
局限与证据强度
巴西国家 监测 系统报告的 SARI 病例中呼吸道病毒负担因病原体、年龄组、地区和季节而异,并且可能受到检测策略变化的影响。

Seasonal dynamics and burden of respiratory syncytial virus, influenza, and COVID-19 in Brazil: a national surveillance study (2019–2023)

Authors: Guilherme Silva Julian, Julia Regazzini Spinardi, Ana Paula Flora, Srinivas Rao Valluri, Florence Lefebvre d'Hellencourt, Caihua Liang, Elizabeth Begier, Thaís Zamboni Berra, Lucas Vieira Cortez, Renato Vitorasso
Original abstract (English)
Background: The burden and seasonal patterns of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza, and COVID-19 remain poorly characterised in Brazil and other Latin American countries. Understanding their epidemiological patterns is essential to inform public health interventions and preventive strategies. We aimed to assess respiratory virus seasonality using national surveillance data to guide vaccination timing and strengthen preparedness strategies in Brazil. Methods: We conducted a retrospective, observational, descriptive population-based study using national surveillance data collected through the SIVEP system between 2019 and 2023. We analysed confirmed cases, hospitalisations, and deaths attributed to RSV, influenza, and COVID-19. We performed time-series decomposition and seasonal trend analysis using the multiple seasonal-trend decomposition via locally estimated scatterplot smoothing method. We calculated monthly mean values for each aetiology component to assess their seasonal burden. Results: There were 69,389 RSV, 45,881 influenza, and 2,299,712 COVID-19 cases among the laboratory-confirmed severe acute respiratory infection (SARI) cases reported in SIVEP. Each year, more than 90% of RSV diagnoses were reported in children aged 0-10 years, while influenza and COVID-19 were more common among older adults. The proportion of deaths in children related to RSV increased from 19.2% in 2020 to 62.0% in 2023. Seasonal peaks occurred from March to June for RSV, influenza cases peaked in January, while COVID-19 showed less consistent seasonality. Geographic variation was observed, with higher case concentrations in the Southeast and South regions of Brazil. Conclusions: The burden of respiratory viruses among SARI cases reported in the national surveillance system in Brazil differed by pathogen, age group, region, and season, and may be influenced by variations in testing strategies. Strengthening diagnostic capacity and surveillance is critical, particularly for adult RSV cases, which are likely underreported due to limited testing and reduced diagnostic sensitivity. Seasonal vaccination programmes, such as those for influenza, remain essential and may be further enhanced by integrating new preventive measures, such as RSV vaccination for older adults and maternal immunisation to protect infants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
We aimed to assess respiratory virus seasonality using national surveillance data to guide vaccination timing and strengthen preparedness strategies in Brazil.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective, observational, descriptive population-based study using national surveillance data collected through the SIVEP system between 2019 and 2023.
Core methods
We performed time-series decomposition and seasonal trend analysis using the multiple seasonal-trend decomposition via locally estimated scatterplot smoothing method.
Main results
The proportion of deaths in children related to RSV increased from 19.2% in 2020 to 62.0% in 2023.
Interpretation and novelty
Geographic variation was observed, with higher case concentrations in the Southeast and South regions of Brazil.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening diagnostic capacity and surveillance is critical, particularly for adult RSV cases, which are likely underreported due to limited testing and reduced diagnostic sensitivity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The burden of respiratory viruses among SARI cases reported in the national surveillance system in Brazil differed by pathogen, age group, region, and season, and may be influenced by variations in testing strategies.

用于快速检测尼泊尔高度依赖护理儿童中 呼吸道合胞病毒 的分子护理点测试的诊断准确性

Diagnostic accuracy of a molecular-based point-of-care test for rapid detection of respiratory syncytial virus in children admitted to high-dependency care in Nepal
作者： Arun Kumar Sharma, Hari Prasad Kattel, Ram Hari Chapagain, Rupesh Shrestha, Prakash Joshi, Naresh Bahadur Khadka, Sangita Sharma, Yvette Löwensteyn, Neele Rave, Mattheus Cornelis Viveen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估基于分子的现场护理测试 (POCT) 的诊断准确性,该测试可快速检测在资源匮乏地区接受高度依赖护理的儿童中的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),而这些地区基本上无法进行快速诊断。
设计与对象
该研究在尼泊尔两家三级医院进行了两个呼吸季节,分析了一项大型研究(RSV GOLD - ICU 网络研究)的数据,该研究涉及 124 个随机选择的 4 天以上儿童的储存样本。
核心方法
使用存储的样本将基于分子的 POCT 的诊断准确性与标准逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 进行比较。该研究分析了尼泊尔两家三级医院在两个呼吸季节进行的一项更大规模前瞻性研究的数据。
主要结果
在 124 份样本中,70 份(56.5%)经 POCT 检测呈阳性,74 份(59.7%)经 RT-PCR 检测呈阳性。中位周期阈值 (CT) 值为 29.1(四分位数范围 = 25.1-33.4)。所有 POCT 假阴性病例的病毒载量均较低(CT 值 >31)。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,基于分子的 POCT 在资源有限的环境中对 RSV 检测具有出色的准确性,正如其高灵敏度和特异性所证明的那样。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,负担得起的基于分子的 POCT 可以改善急性呼吸道感染儿童的护理,并在推出 RSV 干预措施期间增强 监测 的 疫苗 有效性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对存储样本的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入潜在的偏差,并且分析是基于单个呼吸季节。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Diagnostic accuracy of a molecular-based point-of-care test for rapid detection of respiratory syncytial virus in children admitted to high-dependency care in Nepal

Authors: Arun Kumar Sharma, Hari Prasad Kattel, Ram Hari Chapagain, Rupesh Shrestha, Prakash Joshi, Naresh Bahadur Khadka, Sangita Sharma, Yvette Löwensteyn, Neele Rave, Mattheus Cornelis Viveen
Original abstract (English)
Background Rapid respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) diagnostics are largely unavailable in low-resource settings. We compared the diagnostic accuracy of a molecular-based point-of-care test (POCT) with standard reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) in children admitted to high-dependency care. Methods We analysed data from a larger study (RSV GOLD - ICU Network study) in two Nepali tertiary hospitals over two respiratory seasons. Children aged ≥4 days and Results We analysed 124 randomly selected stored samples, of whom 70 (56.5%) tested positive with POCT and 74 (59.7%) with RT-PCR. The median cycle threshold (CT) value was 29.1 (interquartile range = 25.1-33.4). All cases with false-negative POCT had low viral loads (CT value >31). The sensitivity (93.2%; 95% confidence interval (CI) = 86.0, 97.5), specificity (98.0%; 95% CI = 91.5, 99.9), positive predictive values (98.6%; 95% CI = 93.9, 99.9), and negative predictive values (90.7%; 95% CI = 81.1, 96.6) of POCT for RSV infection were all satisfactory. Conclusions We conducted the first prospective study showing excellent accuracy of molecular RSV POCT in a poor resource setting. Affordable molecular-based POCT in resource-constrained settings can improve care of children with acute respiratory infections and strengthen surveillance for vaccine effectiveness during rollout of RSV interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of a molecular-based point-of-care test (POCT) for rapid detection of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in children admitted to high-dependency care in low-resource settings, where rapid diagnostics are largely unavailable.
Design and population
The study was conducted in two Nepali tertiary hospitals over two respiratory seasons, analyzing data from a larger study (RSV GOLD - ICU Network study) involving 124 randomly selected stored samples from children aged ≥4 days.
Core methods
The diagnostic accuracy of the molecular-based POCT was compared with standard reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) using stored samples. The study analyzed data from a larger prospective study in two Nepali tertiary hospitals over two respiratory seasons.
Main results
Of the 124 samples, 70 (56.5%) tested positive with POCT and 74 (59.7%) with RT-PCR. The median cycle threshold (CT) value was 29.1 (interquartile range = 25.1-33.4). All cases with false-negative POCT had low viral loads (CT value >31).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing that the molecular-based POCT has excellent accuracy for RSV detection in a resource-constrained setting, as demonstrated by its high sensitivity and specificity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that affordable molecular-based POCT can improve the care of children with acute respiratory infections and enhance surveillance for vaccine effectiveness during the rollout of RSV interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on stored samples, which may introduce potential biases, and the analysis was based on a single respiratory season. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗 居住在疗养院的医疗保险受益人的有效性

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Effectiveness in Medicare Beneficiaries Residing in Nursing Homes
作者： Ryan E. Wiegand, Heng-Ming Sung, Yue Zhang, Andrea J. Chavez, Kelly M. Hatfield, Amber Kautz, Josephine Mak, Morgan Najdowski, Yangping Chen, Yenlin Lai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要进行 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 估计,以确定 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗接种 在严重 RSV 高风险人群中使用的第一个季节的有效性。
设计与对象
这项针对 Medicare 按服务收费受益人的回顾性 队列研究 研究使用了 2023 年 9 月 10 日至 2024 年 3 月 30 日的电子医疗索赔数据。为每个结果创建了一个 队列研究:与 RSV 相关的住院 队列研究;RSV 相关死亡 队列研究;严重的 RSV 相关结果 队列研究;和 RSV 相关血栓栓塞事件 队列研究。要符合加入 队列研究 的资格,Medicare 按服务收费受益人必须年满 65 岁,在研究期间在美国疗养院居住至少 1 天,连续投保 Medicare A 部分和 B 部分(指数日期前 365 天),并连续投保 D 部分(从 2023 年 6 月 21 日开始)。
核心方法
RSV 诊断和结果定义基于相关国际疾病分类和相关健康问题第十修订版代码的声明。RSV 疫苗 剂量是使用国家药品代码目录根据 Medicare D 部分索赔确定的。多变量 Cox 比例风险模型的风险比 (HR) 比较了 RSV 疫苗接种受益人和未接种疫苗受益人首次结果的时间。VE 计算为(1-HR)×100%。
主要结果
Of 597 430 included Medicare beneficiaries (66% female; median age, 82 [IQR, 75-88] years), RSV VE against RSV-associated hospitalizations was 73% (95% CI, 65%-79%). Among beneficiaries aged 65 to 74 years VE was 60% (95% CI, 35%-76%), 75% (95% CI, 67%-82%) among beneficiaries 75 years or older, 67% (95% CI, 55%-76%) among short-stay residents (<100 cumulative days), and 80% (95% CI, 68%-87%) among long-stay residents (≥100 cumulative days). VE against RSV-associated death was 56% (95% CI, 39%-69%), VE against severe RSV-associated outcomes was 59% (95% CI, 44%-70%), and VE against RSV-associated thromboembolic events was 66% (95% CI, 44%-79%).
解释与创新
在这项研究中,在可用的第一个季节,RSV 疫苗 与居住在疗养院的美国医疗保险受益人的 RSV 相关结果改善相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 监测 和疗养院居民等高危人群中 RSV 的风险评估策略提供信息,并可能指导未来 疫苗 在此人群中有效性的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于索赔数据,这可能会引入偏差或混杂因素,并且结果的普遍性可能仅限于疗养院的医疗保险受益人。由于依赖观察数据和潜在的无法测量的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Effectiveness in Medicare Beneficiaries Residing in Nursing Homes

Authors: Ryan E. Wiegand, Heng-Ming Sung, Yue Zhang, Andrea J. Chavez, Kelly M. Hatfield, Amber Kautz, Josephine Mak, Morgan Najdowski, Yangping Chen, Yenlin Lai
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: Vaccine effectiveness (VE) estimates are needed to determine the effectiveness of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccinations during their first season of availability among populations at high risk for severe RSV. OBJECTIVE: To assess RSV VE against RSV-associated outcomes for nursing home residents. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: This retrospective cohort study of Medicare fee-for-service beneficiaries used electronic medical claims data from September 10, 2023, to March 30, 2024. A cohort was created for each outcome: an RSV-associated hospitalization cohort; an RSV-associated death cohort; a severe RSV-associated outcome cohort; and an RSV-associated thromboembolic event cohort. To be eligible for inclusion in a cohort, a Medicare fee-for-service beneficiary must be aged 65 years or older, reside in a US nursing home during the study period for at least 1 day, have continuous enrollment in Medicare Parts A and B (365 days prior to the index date), and have continuous enrollment in Part D (beginning on June 21, 2023). EXPOSURE: RSV vaccination. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: Outcomes were RSV-associated hospitalization, RSV-associated death, severe RSV-associated outcomes (intensive care unit or critical care unit admission, medical ventilator use, or RSV-associated death), and RSV-associated thromboembolic event. RSV diagnoses and outcome definitions were based on claims with relevant International Classification of Diseases and Related Health Problems, Tenth Revision codes. RSV vaccine doses were determined from Medicare Part D claims using the National Drug Code Directory. Hazard ratios (HRs) from multivariable Cox proportional hazards models compared the times to first outcomes among RSV vaccinated beneficiaries and unvaccinated beneficiaries. VE was calculated as (1-HR) × 100%. RESULTS: Of 597 430 included Medicare beneficiaries (66% female; median age, 82 [IQR, 75-88] years), RSV VE against RSV-associated hospitalizations was 73% (95% CI, 65%-79%). Among beneficiaries aged 65 to 74 years VE was 60% (95% CI, 35%-76%), 75% (95% CI, 67%-82%) among beneficiaries 75 years or older, 67% (95% CI, 55%-76%) among short-stay residents (<100 cumulative days), and 80% (95% CI, 68%-87%) among long-stay residents (≥100 cumulative days). VE against RSV-associated death was 56% (95% CI, 39%-69%), VE against severe RSV-associated outcomes was 59% (95% CI, 44%-70%), and VE against RSV-associated thromboembolic events was 66% (95% CI, 44%-79%). CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: In this study, in the first season of availability, RSV vaccines were associated with improved RSV-associated outcomes among US Medicare beneficiaries residing in nursing homes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vaccine effectiveness (VE) estimates are needed to determine the effectiveness of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccinations during their first season of availability among populations at high risk for severe RSV.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study of Medicare fee-for-service beneficiaries used electronic medical claims data from September 10, 2023, to March 30, 2024. A cohort was created for each outcome: an RSV-associated hospitalization cohort; an RSV-associated death cohort; a severe RSV-associated outcome cohort; and an RSV-associated thromboembolic event cohort. To be eligible for inclusion in a cohort, a Medicare fee-for-service beneficiary must be aged 65 years or older, reside in a US nursing home during the study period for at least 1 day, have continuous enrollment in Medicare Parts A and B (365 days prior to the index date), and have continuous enrollment in Part D (beginning on June 21, 2023).
Core methods
RSV diagnoses and outcome definitions were based on claims with relevant International Classification of Diseases and Related Health Problems, Tenth Revision codes. RSV vaccine doses were determined from Medicare Part D claims using the National Drug Code Directory. Hazard ratios (HRs) from multivariable Cox proportional hazards models compared the times to first outcomes among RSV vaccinated beneficiaries and unvaccinated beneficiaries. VE was calculated as (1-HR) × 100%.
Main results
Of 597 430 included Medicare beneficiaries (66% female; median age, 82 [IQR, 75-88] years), RSV VE against RSV-associated hospitalizations was 73% (95% CI, 65%-79%). Among beneficiaries aged 65 to 74 years VE was 60% (95% CI, 35%-76%), 75% (95% CI, 67%-82%) among beneficiaries 75 years or older, 67% (95% CI, 55%-76%) among short-stay residents (<100 cumulative days), and 80% (95% CI, 68%-87%) among long-stay residents (≥100 cumulative days). VE against RSV-associated death was 56% (95% CI, 39%-69%), VE against severe RSV-associated outcomes was 59% (95% CI, 44%-70%), and VE against RSV-associated thromboembolic events was 66% (95% CI, 44%-79%).
Interpretation and novelty
In this study, in the first season of availability, RSV vaccines were associated with improved RSV-associated outcomes among US Medicare beneficiaries residing in nursing homes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for RSV in high-risk populations such as nursing home residents, and may guide future studies on vaccine effectiveness in this demographic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on claims data, which may introduce bias or confounding factors, and the generalizability of results may be limited to Medicare beneficiaries in nursing homes. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on observational data and potential for unmeasured confounding.

呼吸道合胞病毒 2022 年南非 6-23 个月龄儿童的症状分数、脱落和检测

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Symptomatic Fraction, Shedding and Detection in Children Aged 6–23 Months in South Africa in 2022
作者： Cheryl Cohen, Mignon du Plessis, Neil Martinson, Jocelyn Moyes, Sibongile Walaza, Nicole Wolter, Mvuyo Makhasi, Fahima Moosa, Myrna Charles, Aaron M. Samuels
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 准确检测 RSV 感染对于理解 流行病学 非常重要。我们估计了逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 确诊病例的症状分数和排毒持续时间,并通过分子和血清学技术比较了 RSV 检测。方法 我们在 2022 年 5 月至 10 月期间招募了 6-23 个月大的健康儿童参加一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究,每周收集三次中鼻甲鼻拭子用于 RSV RT-PCR 检测。我们在基线和随访结束时收集血清,并使用酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 和多病原体微珠免疫测定 (MIA) 检测抗 RSV IgG。我们通过每周两次的护士访视和每日护理人员的症状日记来确定症状。我们计算了 RT-PCR、ELISA 和 MIA 与标准(两次或多次检测呈阳性)相比,在 RT-PCR 确诊的发作前使用配对血清和基线血液的儿童中 RT-PCR、ELISA 和 MIA 的敏感性、特异性以及阳性 (PPV) 和阴性 (NPV) 预测值。结果 RSV 季节于 2022 年 2 月中旬开始。我们招募了 93 名参与者;87 人 (94%) 进行了最后一次研究访问。40 人患有一次或多次 RT-PCR 确诊的 RSV 感染,其中 6 人有重复发作。RT-PCR 确诊的病例中有 54% (25/46) 报告有一种或多种症状,其中 26% (12/46) 报告有发烧,37% (17/46) 报告有咳嗽。中位 RNA 脱落持续时间为 7 天(IQR = 4-11 天)。在 80 名符合标准的儿童中,ELISA 和 MIA 的敏感性、特异性、PPV 和 NPV 均 > 92%,而 RT-PCR 的敏感性为 78% (95% CI = 61%–90%),特异性为 98% (95% CI = 88–100%),PPV 为 97% (95% CI = 82%–100%),PPV 为 84% (95% CI = 71%–93%) 净现值。讨论 大约一半的 RSV 发作有症状。血清学对感染识别具有较高的敏感性和特异性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确检测 RSV 感染对于了解 流行病学 非常重要。该研究旨在估计 2022 年南非 6-23 个月大儿童 RSV 感染的症状分数和排毒持续时间。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究于 2022 年 5 月至 10 月进行,招募了 93 名 6-23 个月大的健康儿童。八十七 (94%) 参与者完成了研究。
核心方法
每周三次收集鼻拭子进行 RT-PCR 检测,并在基线和随访结束时采集血清样本,使用 ELISA 和 MIA 检测抗 RSV IgG。通过护士访视和护理人员日记来确定症状。在 RT-PCR 确认的发作前,将 RT-PCR、ELISA 和 MIA 与标准(两次或多次检测呈阳性)的儿童进行配对血清和基线血液进行比较,计算出敏感性、特异性和预测值。
主要结果
RSV 季节于 2022 年 2 月中旬开始。RT-PCR 确诊的病例中有 54% (25/46) 报告有一种或多种症状,其中 26% (12/46) 报告发烧,37% (17/46) 报告咳嗽。中位 RNA 脱落持续时间为 7 天(IQR = 4-11 天)。
解释与创新
研究发现,大约一半的 RSV 发作是有症状的,这为了解该年龄组 RSV 的临床表现提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测 策略提供信息,并提高对 RSV 临床影响的理解,有助于制定有针对性的预防和诊断措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖自我报告的症状和相对较小的样本量而受到限制,但由于使用了多种检测方法进行确认和详细的随访,因此证据充足。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Symptomatic Fraction, Shedding and Detection in Children Aged 6–23 Months in South Africa in 2022

Authors: Cheryl Cohen, Mignon du Plessis, Neil Martinson, Jocelyn Moyes, Sibongile Walaza, Nicole Wolter, Mvuyo Makhasi, Fahima Moosa, Myrna Charles, Aaron M. Samuels
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Accurate detection of RSV infections is important to understand epidemiology. We estimated symptomatic fraction and shedding duration of reverse‐transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT‐PCR)‐confirmed cases and compared RSV detection through molecular and serologic techniques. Methods We enrolled healthy children aged 6–23 months in a prospective cohort study, from May to October 2022, collecting thrice‐weekly mid‐turbinate nasal swabs for RSV RT‐PCR testing. We collected serum at baseline and end of follow‐up and detected anti‐RSV IgG using enzyme‐linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) and multipathogen bead‐based immunoassay (MIA). We ascertained symptoms by twice‐weekly nurse visits, and daily caregiver symptom diaries. We calculated sensitivity, specificity, and positive (PPV) and negative (NPV) predictive value of RT‐PCR, ELISA, and MIA compared to a standard (two or more assays‐positive) among children with paired sera and baseline blood preceding an RT‐PCR–confirmed episode. Results The RSV season started mid‐February 2022. We enrolled 93 participants; 87 (94%) followed to the final study visit. Forty individuals had one or more RT‐PCR–confirmed RSV infection, and six of them had a repeat episode. One‐or‐more symptom was reported from 54% (25/46) of RT‐PCR–confirmed episodes, fever in 26% (12/46), and cough in 37% (17/46). Median RNA shedding duration was 7 days (IQR = 4–11 days). Among 80 children meeting criteria, ELISA and MIA each had > 92% sensitivity, specificity, PPV, and NPV while RT‐PCR had 78% (95% CI = 61%–90%) sensitivity, 98% (95% CI = 88–100%) specificity, 97% (95% CI = 82%–100%) PPV, and 84% (95% CI = 71%–93%) NPV. Discussion Approximately half of RSV episodes were symptomatic. Serology had a high sensitivity and specificity for infection identification.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate detection of RSV infections is important to understand epidemiology. The study aimed to estimate the symptomatic fraction and shedding duration of RSV infections in children aged 6–23 months in South Africa in 2022.
Design and population
A prospective cohort study was conducted from May to October 2022, enrolling 93 healthy children aged 6–23 months. Eighty-seven (94%) participants completed the study.
Core methods
Nasal swabs were collected thrice-weekly for RT-PCR testing, and serum samples were taken at baseline and end of follow-up to detect anti-RSV IgG using ELISA and MIA. Symptoms were ascertained through nurse visits and caregiver diaries. Sensitivity, specificity, and predictive values were calculated comparing RT-PCR, ELISA, and MIA to a standard (two or more assays-positive) among children with paired sera and baseline blood preceding an RT-PCR–confirmed episode.
Main results
The RSV season started mid-February 2022. One or more symptoms were reported from 54% (25/46) of RT-PCR–confirmed episodes, fever in 26% (12/46), and cough in 37% (17/46). Median RNA shedding duration was 7 days (IQR = 4–11 days).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that approximately half of RSV episodes were symptomatic, providing novel insights into the clinical presentation of RSV in this age group.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform surveillance strategies and improve the understanding of RSV's clinical impact, aiding in the development of targeted prevention and diagnostic measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported symptoms and a relatively small sample size, but the evidence is strong due to the use of multiple assays for confirmation and detailed follow-up.

老年人 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 入院情况:2018-2023 年意大利南部地区的回顾性研究

Hospital Admissions for Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Among Elderly: A Retrospective Study From an Italian Southern Region During Years 2018–2023
作者： Giuseppe Di Martino, Pamela Di Giovanni, Federica Vaccaro, Francesca Romana Cascavilla, Carla Di Marzio, Livia Tognaccini, Edoardo Trebbi, Teresa Aita, Ferdinando Romano, Tommaso Staniscia
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是儿童和 60 岁以上成人急性呼吸道感染的最常见原因之一。RSV 可引起老年人严重的下呼吸道感染,导致发病率和 死亡率 增加。
设计与对象
在阿布鲁佐地区进行了一项回顾性研究,评估了 2018 年至 2023 年期间的所有住院治疗情况。本研究的目的是评估意大利阿布鲁佐地区 60 岁以上患者因 RSV 感染住院的 发病率。
核心方法
Age‐ and gender‐standardized hospitalization rates for RSV were calculated. Poisson regression with correction for overdispersion identified no statistically significant temporal trend during the study period (IRR per calendar year 1.08, 95% CI 0.86–1.34; p = 0.516).
主要结果
2018 年至 2023 年期间,在 60 岁以上的成年人中发现了 42 例 RSV 编码住院病例。年年龄和性别标准化 RSV 编码住院率显示出显着的逐年变化。在RSV编码入院的患者中,中位住院时间为11天(IQR 6-18),四名患者(9.52%)在住院期间死亡。
解释与创新
RSV 与阿布鲁佐老年人的可衡量的住院负担相关。然而,每年的巨大差异和 RSV 编码的入院人数较少,需要对时间模式进行谨慎的解释。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSV 给老年人群带来了显着的住院负担,凸显了针对这一人群进行针对性 监测 和干预措施的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计依赖于回顾性数据,并且 RSV 编码的入院人数较少,限制了观察到的时间趋势的可信度。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Hospital Admissions for Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Among Elderly: A Retrospective Study From an Italian Southern Region During Years 2018–2023

Authors: Giuseppe Di Martino, Pamela Di Giovanni, Federica Vaccaro, Francesca Romana Cascavilla, Carla Di Marzio, Livia Tognaccini, Edoardo Trebbi, Teresa Aita, Ferdinando Romano, Tommaso Staniscia
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Introduction Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is one of the most common causes of acute respiratory infections in both children and adults over 60. RSV can cause severe lower respiratory tract infections in older adults, leading to an increase of morbidity and mortality. The aim of this study was to assess the incidence of hospitalizations due to RSV infections in the Abruzzo region of Italy among patients aged over 60 years. Methods A retrospective study was performed in the Abruzzo region, evaluating all hospitalizations performed during years 2018–2023. Age‐ and gender‐standardized hospitalization rates for RSV were calculated. Patients' comorbidities and hospitalization‐related outcomes were also assessed. Results During 2018–2023, 42 RSV‐coded hospitalizations were identified among adults aged ≥ 60 years. An additional RSV‐attributable burden was estimated by applying a literature‐derived attributable fraction to hospitalizations for unspecified viral bronchiolitis and pneumonia, resulting in an estimated total of 67 RSV‐attributable admissions. Annual age‐ and sex‐standardized RSV‐coded hospitalization rates showed substantial year‐to‐year variability. Poisson regression with correction for overdispersion identified no statistically significant temporal trend during the study period (IRR per calendar year 1.08, 95% CI 0.86–1.34; p = 0.516). Among patients with RSV‐coded admissions, the median length of stay was 11 days (IQR 6–18), and four patients (9.52%) died during hospitalization. Conclusions RSV was associated with a measurable burden of hospitalization among older adults in Abruzzo. However, substantial year‐to‐year variability and the small number of RSV‐coded admissions warrant cautious interpretation of temporal patterns. No statistically significant temporal trend was identified during the study period.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is one of the most common causes of acute respiratory infections in both children and adults over 60. RSV can cause severe lower respiratory tract infections in older adults, leading to an increase of morbidity and mortality.
Design and population
A retrospective study was performed in the Abruzzo region, evaluating all hospitalizations performed during years 2018–2023. The aim of this study was to assess the incidence of hospitalizations due to RSV infections in the Abruzzo region of Italy among patients aged over 60 years.
Core methods
Age‐ and gender‐standardized hospitalization rates for RSV were calculated. Poisson regression with correction for overdispersion identified no statistically significant temporal trend during the study period (IRR per calendar year 1.08, 95% CI 0.86–1.34; p = 0.516).
Main results
During 2018–2023, 42 RSV‐coded hospitalizations were identified among adults aged ≥ 60 years. Annual age‐ and sex‐standardized RSV‐coded hospitalization rates showed substantial year‐to‐year variability. Among patients with RSV‐coded admissions, the median length of stay was 11 days (IQR 6–18), and four patients (9.52%) died during hospitalization.
Interpretation and novelty
RSV was associated with a measurable burden of hospitalization among older adults in Abruzzo. However, substantial year‐to‐year variability and the small number of RSV‐coded admissions warrant cautious interpretation of temporal patterns.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSV contributes to a notable hospitalization burden in elderly populations, highlighting the need for targeted surveillance and interventions in this demographic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design, which relied on retrospective data, and the small number of RSV‐coded admissions limit the confidence in the temporal trends observed. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

老年人呼吸道合胞病毒感染

Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Older Adults
作者： Joan Somes
摘要中文翻译教育目标 教育活动结束后,参与者将能够描述 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染的 流行病学;讨论 65 岁及以上成年人以及患有高危疾病的年轻人的临床表现和结果;并讨论诊断挑战、当前的 治疗 选项以及即将推出的新 治疗 和预防选项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决对 65 岁及以上成年人以及患有高危疾病的年轻人中 流行病学、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染的临床表现和结果缺乏了解的问题。
设计与对象
该研究是一项教育活动,旨在向参与者提供有关成人 RSV 感染的知识,重点关注 65 岁及以上的老年人和患有高危疾病的年轻人。
核心方法
这些方法涉及一项教育活动,参与者需要通过阅读材料来获得有关 RSV 感染、其 流行病学、临床特征、诊断挑战以及新兴 治疗 和预防方案的知识。
主要结果
结果表明,参与者能够描述 RSV 感染的 流行病学,讨论 65 岁及以上成年人和患有高危疾病的年轻人的临床表现和结果,并解决诊断挑战和当前的 治疗 选择。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明了教育活动在向高危成年人群传播有关 RSV 感染的基本知识方面的有效性,强调了关注具有特定危险因素的老年人和年轻人的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过强调对医疗保健专业人员进行有针对性的教育以提高对弱势成人群体 RSV 感染的认识和管理的重要性,为公共卫生战略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括其教育性质,未提供临床结果或 治疗 疗效的经验数据。该证据基于教育活动,可能无法完全捕捉真实世界的临床场景,从而导致中等的置信度评估。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Older Adults

Authors: Joan Somes
Original abstract (English)
EDUCATIONAL OBJECTIVES After the educational activity, participants will be able to describe the epidemiology of respiratory syncytial virus infection; discuss the clinical presentation and outcomes in adults aged 65 years and older as well as younger adults with high-risk conditions; and discuss diagnostic challenges, current treatment options, and new treatment and prevention options on the horizon.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of understanding regarding the epidemiology, clinical presentation, and outcomes of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection in adults aged 65 years and older, as well as in younger adults with high-risk conditions.
Design and population
The study was an educational activity designed to provide participants with knowledge about RSV infection in adults, focusing on those aged 65 years and older and younger adults with high-risk conditions.
Core methods
The methods involved an educational activity where participants were expected to engage with the material to gain knowledge about RSV infection, its epidemiology, clinical features, diagnostic challenges, and emerging treatment and prevention options.
Main results
The results indicated that participants were able to describe the epidemiology of RSV infection, discuss its clinical presentation and outcomes in adults aged 65 years and older and younger adults with high-risk conditions, and address diagnostic challenges and current treatment options.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of the educational activity in conveying essential knowledge about RSV infection in high-risk adult populations, highlighting the novelty of focusing on both older adults and younger adults with specific risk factors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies by emphasizing the importance of targeted education for healthcare professionals to improve the recognition and management of RSV infection in vulnerable adult groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include its educational nature, which does not provide empirical data on clinical outcomes or treatment efficacy. The evidence is based on an educational activity, which may not fully capture real-world clinical scenarios, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

2023-2025 年美国婴儿生命前六个月内就医 呼吸道合胞病毒 的预防策略的有效性比较

Comparative effectiveness of preventive strategies against medically-attended respiratory syncytial virus in U.S. infants during the first six months of life, 2023-2025
作者： Sara S Kim, Seth Zissette Zissette, Connor Van Meter, Machi Shiiba, Marina Bruck, Ashley A. Tippett, Satosih Kamidani, David Benkeser, Elizabeth T. Rogawski McQuade, Kim, S. S.
摘要中文翻译重要性:母体 疫苗接种 和长效单克隆抗体现已在美国上市。在美国,长效单克隆抗体给药通常发生在出院后的门诊环境中,导致一些婴儿在生命早期严重 RSV 风险最高时得不到保护。两种干预措施之间的有效性比较以及延迟是否影响有效性估计尚未得到量化。目的:评估婴儿长效单克隆抗体策略和母亲 疫苗接种 策略的有效性,分别与无干预进行比较,以及在考虑单克隆抗体接收的实际延迟时干预策略的比较有效性。设计:队列研究 研究使用目标试验模拟来比较四种预防 RSV 相关结果的策略。2023 年至 2025 年间,使用全国范围内雇主资助的商业保险索赔数据库。参与者:120,586 名有商业保险的母婴,其婴儿在 2023-2024 年或 2024-2025 年 RSV 季节期间在美国出生。无法与母亲记录配对、出生后75天内未参加商业保险、接受帕利珠单抗治疗以及出生日期不可信的婴儿被排除在外。干预措施:四种 RSV 预防策略的比较:(i) 母体 RSVpreF;(ii) 在出生后第一周内给予长效单克隆抗体(按预期使用单克隆抗体);(iii) 在出生后六个月的宽限期内给予长效单克隆抗体(宽限期内的单克隆抗体);(iv) 对照。主要结果和措施:使用调整后的风险比(aHR)总结了针对首次 RSV 相关住院和就医 RSV 疾病的有效性,并使用逆倾向加权方法进行了估计,权重考虑了产妇年龄、影响妊娠的孕产妇合并症、产科和新生儿并发症、季节、地区和相对于 10 月 1 日的出生时间。使用加权 Kaplan Meier 估计器来估计 RSV 结果的特定策略累积 发病率时间。结果:在出生后的前五周,与母体 RSVpreF 策略相比,宽限期内使用 mAb 策略使 RSV 住院风险加倍(aHR:2.0 [95% CI:1.0-4.9]),并增加就医 RSV 风险(aHR:1.6 [95% CI:1.0-2.7])。与母体 RSVpreF 策略相比,mAb 预期策略的 RSV 住院风险相似 (aHR = 0.9 [95% CI: 0.3-1.9])。结论和相关性:当临近出生时施用单克隆抗体时,RSVpreF 和单克隆抗体的效果相似,但考虑到单克隆抗体接收的实际延迟时,母体 RSVpreF 策略比宽限期内的 mAb 策略更有效。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决科学和公共卫生问题,以确定美国婴儿 RSV 预防策略的相对有效性,特别是单克隆抗体给药延迟的影响。知识差距在于量化现实世界的延迟如何影响这些干预措施的有效性。
设计与对象
2023年至2025年期间进行了一项使用目标试验模拟的队列研究研究,分析了120,586对商业保险母婴对,这些母婴对的婴儿在2023-2024年或2024-2025年RSV季节出生。无法与母亲记录配对、出生后75天内未参加商业保险、接受帕利珠单抗治疗或出生日期不可信的婴儿被排除在外。
核心方法
该研究使用了雇主资助的商业保险索赔的全国数据库。使用调整后的风险比 (aHR) 总结主要结果,并使用逆倾向加权方法进行估计,考虑到孕产妇年龄、合并症、产科并发症、季节、地区和相对于 10 月 1 日的出生时间。使用加权 Kaplan Meier 估计器来估计特定策略随时间的累积 发病率。
主要结果
在出生后的前五周,与母体 RSVpreF 策略相比,宽限期内使用 mAb 策略使 RSV 住院风险加倍(aHR:2.0 [95% CI:1.0-4.9]),并增加就医 RSV 风险(aHR:1.6 [95% CI:1.0-2.7])。与母体 RSVpreF 策略相比,mAb 预期策略的 RSV 住院风险相似 (aHR = 0.9 [95% CI: 0.3-1.9])。
解释与创新
作者解释说,当在接近出生时施用单克隆抗体时,RSVpreF 和单克隆抗体的效果相似,但考虑到现实世界的延迟,母体 RSVpreF 策略比宽限期内的 mAb 策略更有效。这一发现凸显了时机对于单克隆抗体干预措施有效性的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果可以通过强调及时施用单克隆抗体的必要性以及母体 疫苗接种 在最高风险早期保护婴儿方面的潜在优势,为 RSV 预防的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖商业保险索赔而受到限制,而商业保险索赔可能无法涵盖所有​​案例或代表整个人口。由于该研究的观察性质和潜在的无法测量的混杂因素,证据的强度适中。

Comparative effectiveness of preventive strategies against medically-attended respiratory syncytial virus in U.S. infants during the first six months of life, 2023-2025

Authors: Sara S Kim, Seth Zissette Zissette, Connor Van Meter, Machi Shiiba, Marina Bruck, Ashley A. Tippett, Satosih Kamidani, David Benkeser, Elizabeth T. Rogawski McQuade, Kim, S. S.
Original abstract (English)
Importance: Maternal vaccination and long-acting monoclonal antibodies are now available in the U.S. Long-acting monoclonal antibody administration in the U.S. commonly occurs after hospital discharge in outpatient settings, leaving some infants unprotected early in life when severe RSV risk is highest. Comparative effectiveness between the two interventions and whether delays affect effectiveness estimates have not been quantified. Objective: To evaluate the effectiveness of infant long-acting monoclonal antibody strategies and a maternal vaccination strategy, each compared to no intervention, and the comparative effectiveness of intervention strategies when accounting for real-world delays in monoclonal antibody receipt. Design: Cohort study using target trial emulation to compare four strategies for prevention of RSV-related outcomes. between 2023 and 2025 using a nationwide database of employer-sponsored commercial insurance claims. Participants: 120,586 commercially insured mother-infants, whose infants were born in the U.S. during the 2023-2024 or 2024-2025 RSV season. Infants who could not be paired with their mother's record, did not enroll in commercial insurance within 75 days from birth, received palivizumab, and had an implausible birth date were excluded. Interventions: Comparison of four RSV prevention strategies: (i) maternal RSVpreF; (ii) long-acting monoclonal antibody given within the first week of life (mAb as intended); (iii) long-acting monoclonal antibody given within a six-month grace period from birth (mAb within grace period); and (iv) a control. Main outcomes and measures: Effectiveness against first RSV-associated hospitalization and medically-attended RSV illness was summarized using adjusted hazard ratios (aHR) and estimated using an inverse propensity weighting approach, with weights accounting for maternal age, maternal comorbidities affecting pregnancy, obstetric and newborn complications, season, region, and birth timing relative to October 1. A weighted Kaplan Meier estimator was used to estimate strategy-specific cumulative incidence of RSV outcomes over time. Results: In the first five weeks of life, the mAb within grace period strategy doubled the hazard of RSV hospitalization (aHR: 2.0 [95% CI: 1.0-4.9]) and increased the hazard of medically-attended RSV (aHR: 1.6 [95% CI: 1.0-2.7]) compared to the maternal RSVpreF strategy. The hazard for RSV hospitalization was similar for the mAb as intended strategy compared to the maternal RSVpreF strategy (aHR = 0.9 [95% CI: 0.3-1.9]). Conclusions and relevance: RSVpreF and monoclonal antibodies were similarly effective when monoclonal antibodies were administered close to birth, but when accounting for real-world delays in monoclonal antibody receipt, the maternal RSVpreF strategy was more effective than the mAb within grace period strategy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the scientific and public-health problem of determining the comparative effectiveness of preventive strategies against RSV in U.S. infants, particularly the impact of delays in monoclonal antibody administration. The knowledge gap lies in quantifying how real-world delays affect the effectiveness of these interventions.
Design and population
A cohort study using target trial emulation was conducted between 2023 and 2025, analyzing 120,586 commercially insured mother-infant pairs whose infants were born during the 2023-2024 or 2024-2025 RSV season. Infants who could not be paired with their mother's record, did not enroll in commercial insurance within 75 days from birth, received palivizumab, or had an implausible birth date were excluded.
Core methods
The study used a nationwide database of employer-sponsored commercial insurance claims. Main outcomes were summarized using adjusted hazard ratios (aHR) and estimated with an inverse propensity weighting approach, accounting for maternal age, comorbidities, obstetric complications, season, region, and birth timing relative to October 1. A weighted Kaplan Meier estimator was used to estimate strategy-specific cumulative incidence over time.
Main results
In the first five weeks of life, the mAb within grace period strategy doubled the hazard of RSV hospitalization (aHR: 2.0 [95% CI: 1.0-4.9]) and increased the hazard of medically-attended RSV (aHR: 1.6 [95% CI: 1.0-2.7]) compared to the maternal RSVpreF strategy. The hazard for RSV hospitalization was similar for the mAb as intended strategy compared to the maternal RSVpreF strategy (aHR = 0.9 [95% CI: 0.3-1.9]).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that RSVpreF and monoclonal antibodies were similarly effective when monoclonal antibodies were administered close to birth, but when accounting for real-world delays, the maternal RSVpreF strategy was more effective than the mAb within grace period strategy. This finding highlights the importance of timing in the effectiveness of monoclonal antibody interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for RSV prevention by emphasizing the need for timely administration of monoclonal antibodies and the potential advantages of maternal vaccination in protecting infants during the early period of highest risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on commercial insurance claims, which may not capture all cases or represent the entire population. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and potential for unmeasured confounding.

呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)——呼吸系统疾病预防的当务之急

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)–An Urgent Priority in Respiratory Disease Prevention
作者： Poonam Talwan, Darsh Gautam
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿、老年人以及患有心肺合并症或免疫功能低下的个体下呼吸道疾病的主要原因。最近的进展使得分层预防成为可能,通过用于婴儿保护的母体 疫苗接种、用于新生儿和特定幼儿的长效单克隆抗体以及用于老年人的 疫苗。然而,监测 中的不平等、季节性驱动的时间安排以及负担能力和交付方面的挑战(特别是在低收入和中等收入国家)阻碍了公平的采用。这篇社论强调了重点人群,综合了当前的证据和政策方向,并概述了融资、采购和综合母婴保健服务方面的可行杠杆,以加速获得服务,使 RSV 预防成为紧迫且可实现的全球卫生优先事项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿、老年人以及患有心肺合并症或免疫功能低下的个体下呼吸道疾病的主要原因。
设计与对象
这篇社论讨论了呼吸道合胞病毒预防方面的最新进展,并概述了融资、采购和综合母婴健康服务方面可采取行动的杠杆,以加速获得服务。
核心方法
该社论综合了当前的证据和政策方向,但没有详细说明具体的实验室或统计程序。
主要结果
社论强调,最近的进展使得分层预防成为可能,通过用于婴儿保护的母体 疫苗接种、用于新生儿和特定幼儿的长效单克隆抗体以及用于老年人的 疫苗。
解释与创新
社论强调,监测 中的不平等、季节性驱动的时间安排以及负担能力和交付方面的挑战阻碍了 RSV 预防策略的公平采用。
科研与公卫意义
这篇社论强调了应对这些挑战的重要性,使 RSV 预防成为紧迫且可实现的全球卫生优先事项。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告具体的研究设计、样本量或统计分析,限制了证据强度的可信度。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)–An Urgent Priority in Respiratory Disease Prevention

Authors: Poonam Talwan, Darsh Gautam
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of lower respiratory tract disease in infants, young children, older adults, and individuals with cardiopulmonary comorbidities or immunocompromised states. Recent advances enable layered prevention through maternal vaccination for infant protection, long-acting monoclonal antibodies for newborns and select toddlers, and vaccines for older adults. However, inequities in surveillance, seasonality-driven timing, and challenges in affordability and delivery—particularly in low- and middle-income countries— impede equitable uptake. This editorial underscores priority populations, synthesizes current evidence and policy directions, and outlines actionable levers in financing, procurement, and integrated maternal–child health delivery to accelerate access, making RSV prevention an urgent and achievable global health priority.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of lower respiratory tract disease in infants, young children, older adults, and individuals with cardiopulmonary comorbidities or immunocompromised states.
Design and population
The editorial discusses recent advances in RSV prevention and outlines actionable levers in financing, procurement, and integrated maternal–child health delivery to accelerate access.
Core methods
The editorial synthesizes current evidence and policy directions without detailing specific laboratory or statistical procedures.
Main results
The editorial highlights that recent advances enable layered prevention through maternal vaccination for infant protection, long-acting monoclonal antibodies for newborns and select toddlers, and vaccines for older adults.
Interpretation and novelty
The editorial emphasizes that inequities in surveillance, seasonality-driven timing, and challenges in affordability and delivery impede equitable uptake of RSV prevention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The editorial underscores the importance of addressing these challenges to make RSV prevention an urgent and achievable global health priority.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific study designs, sample sizes, or statistical analyses, limiting the confidence in the evidence strength.

槐生物碱的抗人呼吸道合胞病毒活性评价及脂质体气管内给药增强功效的评价

An evaluation of the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus activity of sophora alkaloids and the enhanced efficacy by liposomes via intratracheal administration
作者： Guikang Wang, Ke Zhang, Lei Huang, Chunyuan Liu, Shaoju Hu, Yusi Luo, Zipeng Gong, Jianqing Peng
摘要中文翻译人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿和老年人严重下呼吸道感染和相关死亡的主要原因。由于 RSV 的高 突变 发生率以及现有 抗病毒药物 药物的疗效和安全性有限,RSV 的临床管理具有挑战性。苦参生物碱是从苦参中提取的天然化合物,具有广谱 抗病毒药物 和免疫调节活性。从机制上讲,RSV依赖ERK1/2磷酸化来促进病毒复制和传播,而槐花生物碱通过调节MEK/ERK信号通路抑制ERK1/2磷酸化,从而抑制病毒增殖和RSV感染引起的炎症反应。体内研究表明,槐花生物碱通过气管内给药可显着降低 RSV 感染小鼠的 死亡率。为了进一步增强药物的肺滞留,采用脂质体来提高苦参生物碱的治疗效果。槐果碱(SC)作为抗RSV感染最有效的生物碱,将其封装在脂质体中以增强其抗RSV感染的效果。体内研究表明,在 RSV 感染的小鼠模型中,负载 SC 的脂质体在抑制 RSV 复制和减轻肺部炎症方面优于游离 SC。本研究不仅阐明了槐花生物碱抗RSV感染的机制,而且提供了SC的脂质体制剂,以提高抗RSV的治疗效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿和老年人严重下呼吸道感染和相关死亡的主要原因。由于 RSV 的高 突变 发生率以及现有 抗病毒药物 药物的疗效和安全性有限,RSV 的临床管理具有挑战性。
设计与对象
使用 RSV 感染的小鼠模型进行体内研究,以评估槐树生物碱及其脂质体制剂的抗 RSV 活性。
核心方法
槐生物碱,特别是槐果碱 (SC),被封装在脂质体中以增强肺滞留和治疗效果。作用机制涉及通过调节 MEK/ERK 信号通路抑制 ERK1/2 磷酸化。
主要结果
体内研究表明,槐花生物碱通过气管内给药可显着降低 RSV 感染小鼠的 死亡率。在小鼠模型中,负载 SC 的脂质体在抑制 RSV 复制和减轻肺部炎症方面优于游离 SC。
解释与创新
该研究阐明,槐花生物碱通过抑制 ERK1/2 磷酸化、抑制病毒增殖和炎症反应来抵抗 RSV 感染。SC的脂质体制剂为提高抗RSV治疗效果提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,脂质体槐果碱可能是 RSV 感染的一种有前途的治疗策略,有可能改善婴儿、幼儿和老年人等高危人群的 治疗 结局。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于体内小鼠模型,可能无法完全转化为人类临床结果。证据的强度是中等的,因为该研究在动物模型中证明了有效性,但需要在人体试验中进一步验证。

An evaluation of the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus activity of sophora alkaloids and the enhanced efficacy by liposomes via intratracheal administration

Authors: Guikang Wang, Ke Zhang, Lei Huang, Chunyuan Liu, Shaoju Hu, Yusi Luo, Zipeng Gong, Jianqing Peng
Original abstract (English)
Human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe lower respiratory tract infections and related deaths in infants, young children, and the elderly. The clinical management of RSV is challenging due to its high mutation rate and the limited efficacy and safety of existing antiviral drugs. Sophora alkaloids, natural compounds derived from Sophora flavescens, exhibit broad-spectrum antiviral and immunomodulatory activities. Mechanistically, RSV relies on ERK1/2 phosphorylation to facilitate viral replication and spread, while sophora alkaloids inhibit ERK1/2 phosphorylation via regulating MEK/ERK signaling pathway, thereby suppressing viral proliferation and the inflammatory response caused by RSV infection. In vivo studies demonstrated that sophora alkaloids significantly reduced the mortality of RSV-infected mice via intratracheal administration. To further enhance the pulmonary retention of drugs, liposomes were used to improve the therapeutic effects of sophora alkaloids. As the most effective sophora alkaloids against RSV infection, sophocarpine (SC) was encapsulated in liposomes to enhance its anti-RSV infection effect. In vivo studies demonstrate that SC-loaded liposomes outperformed the free SC in suppressing RSV replication and alleviating pulmonary inflammation in murine models of RSV infection. This study not only clarifies the mechanism by which sophora alkaloids counteract RSV-infection but also provides liposomal formulation of SC to improve the anti-RSV therapeutic effects.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe lower respiratory tract infections and related deaths in infants, young children, and the elderly. The clinical management of RSV is challenging due to its high mutation rate and the limited efficacy and safety of existing antiviral drugs.
Design and population
In vivo studies were conducted using murine models of RSV infection to evaluate the anti-RSV activity of sophora alkaloids and their liposomal formulations.
Core methods
Sophora alkaloids, particularly sophocarpine (SC), were encapsulated in liposomes to enhance pulmonary retention and therapeutic effects. The mechanism of action involved inhibition of ERK1/2 phosphorylation via regulation of the MEK/ERK signaling pathway.
Main results
In vivo studies demonstrated that sophora alkaloids significantly reduced the mortality of RSV-infected mice via intratracheal administration. SC-loaded liposomes outperformed free SC in suppressing RSV replication and alleviating pulmonary inflammation in murine models.
Interpretation and novelty
The study clarifies that sophora alkaloids counteract RSV infection by inhibiting ERK1/2 phosphorylation and suppressing viral proliferation and inflammatory response. The liposomal formulation of SC provides a novel approach to improve anti-RSV therapeutic effects.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that liposomal sophocarpine could be a promising therapeutic strategy for RSV infection, potentially improving treatment outcomes in high-risk populations such as infants, young children, and the elderly.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on in vivo murine models, which may not fully translate to human clinical outcomes. The strength of the evidence is moderate, as the study demonstrates efficacy in animal models but requires further validation in human trials.

呼吸道合胞病毒 免疫接种对老年人的影响:有效负担估计的重要性

Impact of respiratory syncytial virus immunization in older adults: the importance of a valid burden estimation
作者： Charles-Antoine Guay, Radhouene Doggui, Patrick Fortin, Élise Fortin, Rachid Amini, Amandine Bemmo, Rodica Gilca
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 加拿大魁北克省于 2024 年秋季实施了一项 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫计划,仅限于长期护理机构 (LTCF) 中的老年人和私人老年住宅 (PSR) 中年龄≥75 岁的老年人,目标人群中 疫苗 的接种率约为 65%。尽管 疫苗 的有效性很高,但 RSV 免疫计划对人群避免住院治疗的实际影响以及 RSV 诊断测试性能可能产生的影响仍然未知。本研究旨在评估第一季(2024-25 年)干预措施对魁北克老年人的影响,同时考虑实验室测试表现。方法 使用基于医院的前瞻性 监测 (HospiVir) 和健康管理数据库来评估干预效果。使用双差泊松回归对预期方法和生态设计之前/之后进行观察,同时考虑了季节变化和比较组中的趋势。基于医院 发病率 的住院 发病率 率估计值使用 Lash 方程根据实验室测试表现进行了调整。结果 根据 HospiVir,居住在 PSR 的 75 岁以上成年人中,观察到与 RSV 相关的住院治疗减少了 49%(95% 置信区间 (CI):-80–-5)。在 75 岁以上的总体人群中,这一下降幅度为 23% (95% CI: -38–66)。使用卫生管理数据观察到,≥75 岁的总人口下降幅度较小,但未达到统计显着性(10%;95% CI:-7-23)。免疫接种计划的影响在诊断测试表现调整后没有变化,而 RSV 相关住院的绝对负担根据 RSV 测试阳性的比例而变化。结论 2024-25 RSV 免疫计划与 PSR 年龄≥75 岁居民的住院率减少近 50% 相关。考虑到 75 岁以上的总人口,这一减少幅度大约低两倍。扩大目标人群和加强 疫苗 推广工作以提高接种率对于增强 RSV 免疫计划在人群层面的影响至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要评估 RSV 免疫计划对老年人住院率的实际影响,并了解诊断测试性能如何影响这些估计。作者旨在解决对该计划在减少 RSV 相关住院治疗方面的有效性缺乏了解的问题。
设计与对象
使用基于医院的前瞻性 监测 系统 (HospiVir) 和健康管理数据库来评估魁北克省 RSV 免疫计划的影响,重点关注居住在私人高级住宅和长期护理机构的 75 岁以上老年人。
核心方法
该研究采用了观察到预期的方法和前后生态设计,使用双差泊松回归来评估干预影响,同时考虑了对照组的季节变化和趋势。
主要结果
根据 HospiVir 的数据,私人老年住宅中年龄≥75 岁的成年人中与 RSV 相关的住院率下降了 49%(95% 置信区间:-80–-5)。对于 75 岁以上的总人口,下降了 23%(95% 置信区间:-38–66)。使用卫生管理数据,观察到较小的下降 10%(95% 置信区间:-7-23),但未达到统计显着性。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,RSV 免疫计划与年龄≥75 岁的私人高级住宅居民住院治疗的大幅减少有关。考虑到年龄 ≥ 75 岁的总人口,这一减少量大约低两倍。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,扩大目标人群和提高 疫苗 的吸收率可以显着增强 RSV 免疫计划的人群影响,特别是在私人高级住宅等高危人群中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于医院的 监测 和卫生行政数据,这些数据在捕获 RSV 的全部负担方面可能存在局限性。证据强度中等,因为在调整诊断测试性能后,免疫计划的影响没有改变。

Impact of respiratory syncytial virus immunization in older adults: the importance of a valid burden estimation

Authors: Charles-Antoine Guay, Radhouene Doggui, Patrick Fortin, Élise Fortin, Rachid Amini, Amandine Bemmo, Rodica Gilca
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Introduction In the province of Québec, Canada, a respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunization program restricted to seniors residing in long-term care facilities (LTCF) and those aged ≥75 years from private senior residences (PSR) was implemented in Fall 2024, with approximately 65% vaccine uptake in the targeted populations. Despite high vaccine effectiveness, the real-world impact of the RSV immunization program on population-level averted hospitalizations and the influence that RSV diagnostic test performance might have remains unknown. This study aimed to evaluate the impact of the first season (2024-25) of the intervention among older adults in Québec while accounting for laboratory test performance. Methods A prospective hospital-based surveillance (HospiVir) and health administrative databases were used to assess the intervention impact. Observed to expected approach and before/after ecological design using a difference-in-difference Poisson regression, both accounting for seasonal variations and trends in comparison groups, were used. Hospitalization incidence rate estimates based on hospital-based surveillance were adjusted for the laboratory test performances using Lash equations. Results Based on HospiVir, a 49% (95% confidence interval (CI): -80– -5) decrease in RSV-associated hospitalizations was observed among adults aged ≥75 years residing in PSR. This decrease was 23% (95% CI: -38–66) in the overall population ≥75 years. A smaller decrease for the overall population ≥75 years was observed using health administrative data, but it did not reach statistical significance (10%; 95% CI: -7–23). The impact of the immunization program did not change following adjustment for diagnostic test performance, while the absolute burden of RSV-associated hospitalizations varied according to the proportion of RSV test positivity. Conclusions The 2024-25 RSV immunization program was associated with an almost 50% reduction in hospitalizations in residents of PSR aged ≥75 years. The magnitude of this reduction was roughly twofold lower when considering the overall population aged ≥75 years. Expanding the target population and strengthening vaccine promotion efforts to improve uptake are both essential to enhance the population-level impact of the RSV immunization program.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to assess the real-world impact of the RSV immunization program on hospitalization rates among older adults and to understand how diagnostic test performance might influence these estimates. The authors aimed to address the lack of knowledge regarding the program's effectiveness in reducing RSV-associated hospitalizations.
Design and population
A prospective hospital-based surveillance system (HospiVir) and health administrative databases were used to evaluate the impact of the RSV immunization program in Québec, focusing on older adults aged ≥75 years residing in private senior residences and long-term care facilities.
Core methods
The study employed an observed to expected approach and a before/after ecological design using a difference-in-difference Poisson regression to assess the intervention impact, while accounting for seasonal variations and trends in comparison groups.
Main results
Based on HospiVir, there was a 49% (95% confidence interval: -80–-5) decrease in RSV-associated hospitalizations among adults aged ≥75 years in private senior residences. For the overall population ≥75 years, the decrease was 23% (95% confidence interval: -38–66). Using health administrative data, a smaller decrease of 10% (95% confidence interval: -7–23) was observed, but it did not reach statistical significance.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that the RSV immunization program was associated with a substantial reduction in hospitalizations among residents of private senior residences aged ≥75 years. This reduction was approximately twofold lower when considering the overall population aged ≥75 years.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that expanding the target population and improving vaccine uptake could significantly enhance the population-level impact of the RSV immunization program, particularly in high-risk groups such as those in private senior residences.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results were based on hospital-based surveillance and health administrative data, which may have limitations in capturing the full burden of RSV. The evidence strength is moderate, as the impact of the immunization program did not change following adjustment for diagnostic test performance.

极端天气和高浓度 PM2.5 共同暴露与 呼吸道合胞病毒 循环强度升高有关

Extreme weather and high-level PM2.5 co-exposure are associated with elevated respiratory syncytial virus circulating intensity
作者： Jingyi Liang, Rodrigo Fasce, Saturnino Luz, You Li, Harish Nair
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 气候变化导致气温升高、热浪和寒潮更加频繁,引起了人们对呼吸系统健康的担忧。然而,极端温度事件与 RSV 流行之间的关联仍不清楚。方法 我们进行了时间序列分析,以评估单独暴露于极端温度以及联合暴露于极端温度和高浓度颗粒物 [直径 2.5 微米或更小 ( $${{{\rm{PM}}}}_{2.5}$$ PM 2.5 )] 污染对 RSV 循环强度的相对风险。日本和智利被选为两个气候和地理背景截然不同的国家。从智利 16 个地区(2015-2018 年)和日本 47 个都道府县(2013-2019 年)收集了有关实验室确诊 RSV 阳性病例、分母人口、气象因素和空气污染物的每周数据。我们拟合准泊松回归来评估当地水平的估计值,并使用随机效应荟萃分析来获得国家估计值。结果 我们在此表明​​,热浪与日本 RSV 循环强度增加有关,相对风险高达 1.49(95% 置信区间 (CI):1.29–1.73)。寒潮表现出较弱但积极的影响。尽管寒潮在智利没有表现出一致的影响,但同时暴露于高浓度 $${{{\rm{PM}}}}_{2.5}$$ PM 2.5 污染和寒潮会使风险增加 32% (95% CI: 1.10–1.58)。在日本,同时暴露于高浓度 $${{{\rm{PM}}}}_{2.5}$$ PM 2.5 污染和热浪会使风险增加高达 66% (95% CI: 1.27–2.16)。结论 这些发现揭示了 RSV 对极端温度反应的国家模式和区域差异,强调了在全球气候变化中针对可改变的人口水平因素采取公共卫生措施的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于气候变化,人们对呼吸系统健康的担忧日益加剧,该研究解决了极端温度事件与 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 流行病之间尚不清楚的关联问题。其目的是量化极端温度(单独或与高浓度 PM2.5 污染结合)如何影响 RSV 循环强度。
设计与对象
该研究使用智利 16 个地区(2015-2018 年)和日本 47 个都道府县(2013-2019 年)的每周数据进行时间序列分析。它评估了 RSV 循环强度与气象因素和空气污染物的关系,重点关注极端温度事件和 PM2.5 暴露。
核心方法
拟合准泊松回归模型来估计与极端温度和高浓度 PM2.5 污染相关的当地 RSV 病例相对风险。然后使用随机效应荟萃分析从当地数据得出国家层面的估计。
主要结果
热浪与日本 RSV 循环强度增加有关,相对风险高达 1.49(95% CI:1.29-1.73)。在日本,同时暴露于高浓度 PM2.5 和热浪会使风险增加高达 66%(95% CI:1.27–2.16),而同时暴露于 PM2.5 和寒流会使风险增加 32%(95% CI:1.10–1.58)。寒潮表现出较弱但积极的影响。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了 RSV 对极端温度反应的国家模式和区域差异,强调了在气候变化下可能影响 RSV 传播 的可改变的种群水平因素。这是第一项量化极端温度和 PM2.5 对 RSV 循环强度联合影响的研究。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,针对极端温度事件和空气污染的公共卫生措施可以减轻 RSV 负担,特别是在 PM2.5 水平较高且热浪或寒流频繁的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其生态设计的限制,无法进行因果推断,并且可能受到未测量的混杂因素(例如就医行为和测试实践)的影响。由于依赖汇总数据且缺乏个体层面的控制,证据强度中等。

Extreme weather and high-level PM2.5 co-exposure are associated with elevated respiratory syncytial virus circulating intensity

Authors: Jingyi Liang, Rodrigo Fasce, Saturnino Luz, You Li, Harish Nair
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Higher temperatures and more frequent heat waves and cold spells due to climate change raise concerns for respiratory health. However, the association of extreme temperature events with RSV epidemics remains unclear. Method We conducted time-series analyses to assess relative risks from exposure to extreme temperatures alone and joint exposure to extreme temperatures and high-level particulate matter [2.5 microns or less in diameter ( $${{{\rm{PM}}}}_{2.5}$$ PM 2.5 )] pollution on RSV circulating strength. Japan and Chile were selected as two contrasting settings with distinct climatic and geographic backgrounds. Weekly data on laboratory-confirmed RSV-positive cases, denominator population, meteorological factors, and air pollutants were collected from 16 Chilean regions (2015–2018) and 47 Japanese prefectures (2013–2019). We fitted quasi-Poisson regression to assess the local-level estimates and used random-effects meta-analyses to obtain national estimates. Results Here we show that heat waves are linked to increased RSV circulating intensity in Japan, with relative risks up to 1.49 (95% confidence interval (CI): 1.29–1.73). Cold spells show weaker but positive effects. Although cold spells demonstrate no consistent effects in Chile, joint exposure to high-level $${{{\rm{PM}}}}_{2.5}$$ PM 2.5 pollution and cold spells increases risks by 32% (95% CI: 1.10–1.58). Joint exposure to high-level $${{{\rm{PM}}}}_{2.5}$$ PM 2.5 pollution and heat waves raises risk by up to 66% (95% CI: 1.27–2.16) in Japan. Conclusions These findings reveal national patterns and regional variations in RSV responses to extreme temperatures, highlighting the need for public health measures targeting modifiable population-level factors amid global climate change.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the unclear association between extreme temperature events and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) epidemics amid rising concerns for respiratory health due to climate change. It aimed to quantify how extreme temperatures, alone or combined with high-level PM2.5 pollution, influence RSV circulating intensity.
Design and population
The study conducted time-series analyses using weekly data from 16 Chilean regions (2015–2018) and 47 Japanese prefectures (2013–2019). It assessed RSV circulating strength in relation to meteorological factors and air pollutants, focusing on extreme temperature events and PM2.5 exposure.
Core methods
Quasi-Poisson regression models were fitted to estimate local-level relative risks of RSV cases associated with extreme temperatures and high-level PM2.5 pollution. Random-effects meta-analyses were then used to derive national-level estimates from the local data.
Main results
Heat waves were linked to increased RSV circulating intensity in Japan, with relative risks up to 1.49 (95% CI: 1.29–1.73). Joint exposure to high-level PM2.5 and heat waves raised risk by up to 66% (95% CI: 1.27–2.16) in Japan, while joint exposure to PM2.5 and cold spells increased risks by 32% (95% CI: 1.10–1.58). Cold spells showed weaker but positive effects.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal national patterns and regional variations in RSV responses to extreme temperatures, highlighting modifiable population-level factors that may influence RSV transmission under climate change. This is the first study to quantify joint effects of extreme temperatures and PM2.5 on RSV circulating intensity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that public health measures targeting extreme temperature events and air pollution could mitigate RSV burden, particularly in regions with high PM2.5 levels and frequent heat waves or cold spells.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its ecological design, which precludes causal inference and may be affected by unmeasured confounders such as healthcare-seeking behavior and testing practices. The evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on aggregated data and absence of individual-level controls.

COVID-19 大流行期间呼吸道病毒季节性的变化:莱斯特、鹿特丹、香港和基督城的多中心比较研究。

Changes in respiratory virus seasonality across the COVID-19 pandemic: a multicentre comparative study in Leicester, Rotterdam, Hong Kong, and Christchurch.
作者： Keita Wagatsuma, Zoe Song, Tommy T Y Lam, Kin On Kwok, Marc K C Chong, Margaret Ip, Janko van Beek, Meik Dilcher, Lance C Jennings, David A Foley
摘要中文翻译目的:使用国际呼吸道病毒测序网络 (INSPIRE) 提供的常规医院诊断实验室数据来描述 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 之前、期间和之后的呼吸道病毒季节性。方法:我们分析了英国莱斯特四个诊断实验室的每月测试数据;荷兰鹿特丹;中国香港;以及 2018-2023 年期间新西兰基督城。病毒包括甲型流感病毒、乙型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、副流感病毒1-4、人类偏肺病毒、腺病毒和肠道病毒/鼻病毒。对 COVID-19 之前(2018-2019 年)、COVID-19 中断(2020-2021 年)和 COVID-19 重新出现后(2022-2023 年)期间的每月测试阳性率进行了比较。结果:数据集包含 3,164,720 项测试和 84,300 项阳性检测。2020-2021年,流感活动明显受到抑制:甲型流感病毒阳性率从7.3%下降到1.2%,乙型流感病毒阳性率从3.0%下降到0.1%。呼吸道合胞病毒 总体减少幅度较小,而肠道病毒/鼻病毒在所有四个位点仍可检测到。2022-2023年,呼吸道病毒异质性重新出现;在 40 个站点病毒组合中,有 21 个在 COVID-19 之前和之后的高峰月份之间至少相差两个月。结论:在 COVID-19 期间和之后,呼吸道病毒的季节性发生了显着变化。COVID-19 后的重新出现因病毒和部位而异,支持持续的多病原体 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在利用常规医院诊断实验室数据来描述 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 大流行之前、期间和之后呼吸道病毒的季节性。
设计与对象
该研究是在莱斯特、鹿特丹、香港和基督城的四个诊断实验室进行的多中心比较分析,使用了 2018 年至 2023 年的数据。它包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 以及其他病毒,如甲型流感病毒、乙型流感病毒、副流感病毒 1-4、人类偏肺病毒、腺病毒和肠道病毒/鼻病毒。
核心方法
比较了三个时期的每月测试阳性率:COVID-19 之前(2018-2019 年)、COVID-19 中断(2020-2021 年)和 COVID-19 重新出现后(2022-2023 年)。该分析使用了国际呼吸道病毒测序网络(INSPIRE)提供的常规诊断实验室数据。
主要结果
该数据集包括 3,164,720 项测试,其中检测到 84,300 项阳性。2020-2021年间,甲型流感病毒阳性率从7.3%下降至1.2%,乙型流感病毒阳性率从3.0%下降至0.1%。呼吸道合胞病毒 总体减少幅度较小,而肠道病毒/鼻病毒在所有四个位点仍可检测到。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在 COVID-19 大流行期间和之后,呼吸道病毒的季节性发生了显着变化,流感活动发生了显着变化,其他病毒的重新出现模式也发生了变化。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了持续使用多病原体 监测 的必要性,因为 COVID-19 后的重新出现模式因病毒和地点而异,可能会影响公共卫生策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于常规诊断数据,在捕获全面的病毒动态方面可能存在局限性。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于没有实验控制的观察数据。

Changes in respiratory virus seasonality across the COVID-19 pandemic: a multicentre comparative study in Leicester, Rotterdam, Hong Kong, and Christchurch.

Authors: Keita Wagatsuma, Zoe Song, Tommy T Y Lam, Kin On Kwok, Marc K C Chong, Margaret Ip, Janko van Beek, Meik Dilcher, Lance C Jennings, David A Foley
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: To describe respiratory virus seasonality before, during, and after coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) using routine hospital diagnostic laboratory data contributed through the International Network for the Sequencing of Respiratory Viruses (INSPIRE). METHODS: We analysed monthly test data from four diagnostic laboratories in Leicester, UK; Rotterdam, the Netherlands; Hong Kong, China; and Christchurch, New Zealand, during 2018-2023. Viruses included influenza A virus, influenza B virus, respiratory syncytial virus, parainfluenza viruses 1-4, human metapneumovirus, adenovirus, and enterovirus/rhinovirus. Monthly test positivity was compared across pre-COVID-19 (2018-2019), COVID-19 disruption (2020-2021), and post-COVID-19 re-emergence (2022-2023) periods. RESULTS: The dataset comprised 3,164,720 tests performed and 84,300 positive detections. During 2020-2021, influenza activity was markedly suppressed: influenza A virus positivity decreased from 7.3% to 1.2%, and influenza B virus positivity from 3.0% to 0.1%. Respiratory syncytial virus showed a smaller overall reduction, whereas enterovirus/rhinovirus remained detectable in all four sites. In 2022-2023, respiratory viruses re-emerged heterogeneously; peak months differed by at least two months between the pre- and post-COVID-19 periods in 21 of 40 site-virus combinations. CONCLUSIONS: Respiratory virus seasonality was substantially altered during and after COVID-19. Post-COVID-19 re-emergence varied by virus and site, supporting continued multi-pathogen surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe respiratory virus seasonality before, during, and after the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic using routine hospital diagnostic laboratory data.
Design and population
The study was a multicentre comparative analysis conducted across four diagnostic laboratories in Leicester, Rotterdam, Hong Kong, and Christchurch, using data from 2018 to 2023. It included respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) along with other viruses such as influenza A virus, influenza B virus, parainfluenza viruses 1-4, human metapneumovirus, adenovirus, and enterovirus/rhinovirus.
Core methods
Monthly test positivity rates were compared across three periods: pre-COVID-19 (2018-2019), COVID-19 disruption (2020-2021), and post-COVID-19 re-emergence (2022-2023). The analysis used routine diagnostic laboratory data contributed through the International Network for the Sequencing of Respiratory Viruses (INSPIRE).
Main results
The dataset included 3,164,720 tests with 84,300 positive detections. During 2020-2021, influenza A virus positivity decreased from 7.3% to 1.2%, and influenza B virus positivity from 3.0% to 0.1%. Respiratory syncytial virus showed a smaller overall reduction, whereas enterovirus/rhinovirus remained detectable in all four sites.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that respiratory virus seasonality was substantially altered during and after the COVID-19 pandemic, with notable changes in influenza activity and varying patterns of re-emergence for other viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the need for continued multi-pathogen surveillance, as post-COVID-19 re-emergence patterns varied by virus and location, potentially affecting public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on routine diagnostic data, which may have limitations in capturing comprehensive viral dynamics. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on observational data without experimental controls.

用于同时检测七种呼吸道病毒的新型转换 RT-qPCR 测定的临床和分析验证。

Clinical and Analytical Validation of a Novel Switching RT-qPCR Assay for the Simultaneous Detection of Seven Respiratory Viruses.
作者： Min Young Lee, Vu Minh Phan, Hiep Van Nguyen, Woo In Lee, Tae Seok Seo
摘要中文翻译快速、准确地识别呼吸道病毒对于有效的临床管理和感染控制至关重要。然而,传统的 RT-qPCR 方法非常耗时且需要复杂的工作流程。为了解决这些限制,我们开发了一种新型开关 RT-qPCR 检测方法,旨在提高呼吸道病毒的多重检测效率和灵敏度。本研究旨在评估转换 RT-qPCR 检测在检测七种主要呼吸道病毒时的分析和临床性能:甲型和乙型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) A 和 B、腺病毒 (AdV)、人类偏肺病毒 (hMPV) 和 SARS-CoV-2(严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2)。鼻咽拭子标本(n = 808)是从有呼吸道症状的患者身上采集的。使用 Switching RT-qPCR 测定和商业多重 RT-qPCR 方法(Allplex Respiratory Panels 和 Allplex SARS-CoV-2 Assay)提取和测试 RNA。比较了两种方法的一致性、灵敏度、特异性和分析性能。与传统检测相比,Switching RT-qPCR 检测的临床敏感性为 93.6%,特异性为 100%,总体一致性率超过 96%。大多数病毒靶标(尤其是 RSV A、AdV 和 hMPV)的分析灵敏度相当或更高。双工检测形式能够在每个反应中同时有效检测两个目标,从而减少总体测试时间,同时保持分析准确性。这种转换 RT-qPCR 检测为呼吸道病毒检测提供了可靠、快速且高效的替代方案。其简化的双工形式以及与标准分子诊断的高度一致性表明其在常规临床实验室中的潜在实用性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
快速、准确地识别呼吸道病毒对于有效的临床管理和感染控制至关重要。然而,传统的 RT-qPCR 方法非常耗时且需要复杂的工作流程。
设计与对象
鼻咽拭子标本(n = 808)是从有呼吸道症状的患者身上采集的。该研究评估了转换 RT-qPCR 检测在检测七种主要呼吸道病毒(包括 RSV-A 和 RSV-B)方面的分析和临床性能。
核心方法
使用 Switching RT-qPCR 测定和商业多重 RT-qPCR 方法(Allplex Respiratory Panels 和 Allplex SARS-CoV-2 Assay)提取和测试 RNA。双工检测形式能够在每个反应中同时有效检测两个目标,从而减少总体测试时间,同时保持分析准确性。
主要结果
与传统检测相比,Switching RT-qPCR 检测的临床敏感性为 93.6%,特异性为 100%,总体一致性率超过 96%。大多数病毒靶标(尤其是 RSV A、AdV 和 hMPV)的分析灵敏度相当或更高。
解释与创新
转换 RT-qPCR 检测为呼吸道病毒检测提供了可靠、快速且高效的替代方案。其简化的双工形式以及与标准分子诊断的高度一致性表明其在常规临床实验室中的潜在实用性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,转换 RT-qPCR 检测可以提高临床环境中呼吸道病毒检测的效率,特别是 RSV-A 和 RSV-B。这可能会增强感染控制和临床决策。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。由于该研究提供了分析性能与传统方法的直接比较,因此证据强度很高。

Clinical and Analytical Validation of a Novel Switching RT-qPCR Assay for the Simultaneous Detection of Seven Respiratory Viruses.

Authors: Min Young Lee, Vu Minh Phan, Hiep Van Nguyen, Woo In Lee, Tae Seok Seo
Original abstract (English)
Rapid and accurate identification of respiratory viruses is essential for effective clinical management and infection control. However, conventional RT-qPCR methods are time-consuming and require complex workflows. To address these limitations, we developed a novel Switching RT-qPCR assay designed for multiplex detection of respiratory viruses with enhanced efficiency and sensitivity. This study aimed to evaluate the analytical and clinical performance of the switching RT-qPCR assay in the detection of seven major respiratory viruses: Influenza A and B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) A and B, adenovirus (AdV), human metapneumovirus (hMPV), and SARS-CoV-2 (Severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2). Nasopharyngeal swab specimens (n = 808) were collected from patients with respiratory symptoms. RNA was extracted and tested using both a Switching RT-qPCR assay and commercial multiplex RT-qPCR method (Allplex Respiratory Panels and Allplex SARS-CoV-2 Assay). The concordance, sensitivity, specificity, and analytical performances of the two methods were compared. The Switching RT-qPCR assay demonstrated a clinical sensitivity of 93.6% and a specificity of 100%, with an overall concordance rate exceeding 96%, compared with the conventional assay. Analytical sensitivity was comparable or superior across most viral targets, particularly RSV A, AdV, and hMPV. The duplex assay format enabled the efficient simultaneous detection of two targets per reaction, reducing the overall testing time while maintaining analytical accuracy. This Switching RT-qPCR assay offers a reliable, rapid, and efficient alternative for respiratory virus detection. Its simplified duplex format and high concordance with standard molecular diagnostics indicate its potential utility in routine clinical laboratories.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rapid and accurate identification of respiratory viruses is essential for effective clinical management and infection control. However, conventional RT-qPCR methods are time-consuming and require complex workflows.
Design and population
Nasopharyngeal swab specimens (n = 808) were collected from patients with respiratory symptoms. The study evaluated the analytical and clinical performance of the switching RT-qPCR assay in the detection of seven major respiratory viruses, including RSV-A and RSV-B.
Core methods
RNA was extracted and tested using both a Switching RT-qPCR assay and commercial multiplex RT-qPCR method (Allplex Respiratory Panels and Allplex SARS-CoV-2 Assay). The duplex assay format enabled the efficient simultaneous detection of two targets per reaction, reducing the overall testing time while maintaining analytical accuracy.
Main results
The Switching RT-qPCR assay demonstrated a clinical sensitivity of 93.6% and a specificity of 100%, with an overall concordance rate exceeding 96%, compared with the conventional assay. Analytical sensitivity was comparable or superior across most viral targets, particularly RSV A, AdV, and hMPV.
Interpretation and novelty
The Switching RT-qPCR assay offers a reliable, rapid, and efficient alternative for respiratory virus detection. Its simplified duplex format and high concordance with standard molecular diagnostics indicate its potential utility in routine clinical laboratories.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the Switching RT-qPCR assay could improve the efficiency of respiratory virus detection in clinical settings, particularly for RSV-A and RSV-B. This may enhance infection control and clinical decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study. The evidence strength is high as the study provides direct comparisons of analytical performance with conventional methods.

临床医生报告的血液学和造血细胞移植中社区获得性呼吸道病毒感染的呼吸道病毒检测和预期管理:一项国际探索性调查

Clinician-Reported Respiratory Viral Testing and Intended Management of Community-Acquired Respiratory Viral Infections in Haematology and Haematopoietic Cell Transplantation: An International Exploratory Survey
作者： Johan Du Toit, Tobias R. Neijzen, Sjoerd van der Bie, S.F.L. van Lelyveld, Aart Beeker, Karlijn J. van Stralen, Odette M. Vlek, Estelle Verburgh, Eric van Gorp, Jurjen Versluis
摘要中文翻译背景:社区获得性呼吸道病毒 (CARV) 会导致血液恶性肿瘤患者和接受造血细胞移植 (HCT) 的患者出现显着的发病率和 死亡率。尽管如此,临床管理仍然存在差异。这项研究评估了临床医生对这一高危人群中主要呼吸道病毒的认识、诊断实践和 治疗 方法。方法:使用非概率便利抽样对血液科、肿瘤科、重症监护和急诊医学医生进行了一项国际电子调查。四个真实的临床病例被转换为标准化的小插图,涵盖人类偏肺病毒(HMPV)、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)、人类副流感病毒(HPIV)和甲型流感病毒(IAV)。调查问卷评估了报告的检测策略和预期管理,包括 抗病毒药物 和免疫预防干预措施。对答复进行描述性分析;没有进行推断组间比较。结果:来自 26 个国家的 98 名临床医生做出了回应。诊断方法(包括测试适应症和小组选择)因受访者而异。对于 HMPV 和 HPIV,受访者主要报告了支持性护理、免疫球蛋白替代和感染控制措施,但缺乏既定的病原体特异性干预措施。RSV 实践存在差异:45/96 (46.9%) 的受访者考虑全身使用利巴韦林,其中在免疫缺陷评分指导下考虑使用利巴韦林为 34/96 (35.4%)。IAV 管理更加一致,据报告可使用 疫苗接种 和 抗病毒药物 治疗,并经常使用暴露后预防。结论:在这个国际便利样本中,临床医生报告了呼吸道病毒检测以及结果如何通知预期管理的差异。由于抽样不是基于概率的,而且受访者群体规模较小且分布不均,因此调查结果不应被解释为国际实践的代表性估计。IAV 检测与既定的 治疗 和预防联系最为一致,RSV 显示出更大的治疗和预防异质性,HMPV/HPIV 检测主要为感染控制、监测和抗菌管理提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
社区获得性呼吸道病毒 (CARV) 在血液系统恶性肿瘤患者和接受造血细胞移植 (HCT) 的患者中引起显着的发病率和 死亡率。尽管如此,临床管理仍然存在差异。
设计与对象
在血液学、肿瘤学、重症监护和急诊医学医生中进行了一项使用非概率便利抽样的国际电子调查。四个真实的临床病例被转换成标准化的小插图,涵盖 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、人类偏肺病毒 (HMPV)、人类副流感病毒 (HPIV) 和甲型流感病毒 (IAV)。
核心方法
调查问卷评估了报告的检测策略和预期管理,包括 抗病毒药物 和免疫预防干预措施。对答复进行描述性分析;没有进行推断组间比较。
主要结果
来自 26 个国家的 98 名临床医生做出了回应。诊断方法(包括测试适应症和小组选择)因受访者而异。对于 HMPV 和 HPIV,受访者主要报告了支持性护理、免疫球蛋白替代和感染控制措施,但缺乏既定的病原体特异性干预措施。RSV 实践存在差异:45/96 (46.9%) 的受访者考虑全身使用利巴韦林,其中在免疫缺陷评分指导下考虑使用利巴韦林为 34/96 (35.4%)。IAV 管理更加一致,据报告可使用 疫苗接种 和 抗病毒药物 治疗,并经常使用暴露后预防。
解释与创新
在这个国际便利样本中,临床医生报告了呼吸道病毒检测以及结果如何通知预期管理的差异。由于抽样不是基于概率的,而且受访者群体规模较小且分布不均,因此调查结果不应被解释为国际实践的代表性估计。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了高危人群中 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 标准化诊断和管理方案的必要性。他们还建议需要进一步研究以提高临床实践的一致性并制定有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用了非概率便利抽样,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。此外,受访者群体规模小且分布不均,降低了证据的强度,调查结果不应被解释为国际实践的代表性估计。

Clinician-Reported Respiratory Viral Testing and Intended Management of Community-Acquired Respiratory Viral Infections in Haematology and Haematopoietic Cell Transplantation: An International Exploratory Survey

Authors: Johan Du Toit, Tobias R. Neijzen, Sjoerd van der Bie, S.F.L. van Lelyveld, Aart Beeker, Karlijn J. van Stralen, Odette M. Vlek, Estelle Verburgh, Eric van Gorp, Jurjen Versluis
Original abstract (English)
Background: Community-acquired respiratory viruses (CARVs) cause significant morbidity and mortality in patients with haematological malignancies and those undergoing haematopoietic cell transplantation (HCT). Despite this, clinical management remains variable. This study evaluated clinicians’ awareness, diagnostic practices, and treatment approaches to major respiratory viruses in this high-risk population. Methods: An international electronic survey using non-probability convenience sampling was conducted among haematology, oncology, intensive care and emergency medicine physicians. Four real-life clinical cases were converted into standardised vignettes covering human metapneumovirus (HMPV), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), human parainfluenza virus (HPIV) and influenza A virus (IAV). The questionnaire assessed reported testing strategies and intended management, including antiviral and immunoprophylactic interventions. Responses were analysed descriptively; no inferential between-group comparisons were performed. Results: Ninety-eight clinicians from 26 countries responded. Diagnostic approaches, including testing indications and panel selection, varied among respondents. For HMPV and HPIV, respondents mainly reported supportive care, immunoglobulin replacement, and infection-control measures in the absence of established pathogen-specific interventions. RSV practice was heterogeneous: systemic ribavirin was considered by 45/96 (46.9%) respondents, including 34/96 (35.4%) when guided by an immunodeficiency score. IAV management was more consistent, with reported availability of vaccination and antiviral therapy and frequent use of post-exposure prophylaxis. Conclusions: Within this international convenience sample, clinicians reported variation in respiratory viral testing and in how results informed intended management. Because sampling was non-probability-based and respondent groups were small and unevenly distributed, the findings should not be interpreted as representative estimates of international practice. IAV testing was most consistently linked to established treatment and prevention, RSV showed greater therapeutic and preventive heterogeneity, and HMPV/HPIV testing primarily informed infection control, monitoring and antimicrobial stewardship.
View research seven-element analysis

IAV management was more consistent, with reported availability of vaccination and antiviral therapy and frequent use of post-exposure prophylaxis.
