Because sampling was non-probability-based and respondent groups were small and unevenly distributed, the findings should not be interpreted as representative estimates of international practice.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for standardised diagnostic and management protocols for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in high-risk populations. They also suggest that further research is required to improve consistency in clinical practices and to develop targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used non-probability convenience sampling, which may limit the generalisability of the findings. Additionally, the small and unevenly distributed respondent groups reduce the strength of the evidence, and the findings should not be interpreted as representative estimates of international practice.

分析 毛细支气管炎 引起的 毛细支气管炎 患儿血液学参数分析

Analiza hematoloških parametara u djece s bronhiolitisom uzrokovanim respiratornim sincicijskim virusom The analysis of haematological parameters in children with bronchiolitis caused by respiratory syncytial virus
作者： Ana Šomoljanski, Sanja Delin, Andrea Šimić Klarić
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在此情况下,请注意使用细支气管炎呼吸器和病毒科诊所的血液参数。
设计与对象
如果是,请在 2010 年的 2010 年 100 天内将其送往医院。
核心方法
患者信息及疾病经过数据均来自医疗记录。所有临床表现为毛细支气管炎且鼻咽拭子中分离出呼吸道合胞病毒的患者均进行了全血细胞计数及C反应蛋白检测。
主要结果
S obzirom na ovisnost o kisiku, utvrđene su statistički značajno niže vrijednosti eritrocita (p = 0,030) te značajno više ispitanika s vrijednostima trombocita iznad referentnog raspona (p = 0,030) u skupini koja je ovisna o kisiku, u odnosu na ispitanike koji nisu ovisni o kisiku.
解释与创新

科研与公卫意义
统计数据显示,由于病毒感染,您可以识别出细支气管炎病毒。
局限与证据强度

Analiza hematoloških parametara u djece s bronhiolitisom uzrokovanim respiratornim sincicijskim virusom The analysis of haematological parameters in children with bronchiolitis caused by respiratory syncytial virus

Authors: Ana Šomoljanski, Sanja Delin, Andrea Šimić Klarić
Original abstract (English)
Cilj: Cilj je ovog presječnog istraživanja ispitati povezanost hematoloških parametara u djece s bronhiolitisom uzrokovanim respiratornim sincicijskim virusom s kliničkim karakteristikama bolesti te analizirati njihovu moguću prediktivnu vrijednost. Ispitanici i metode: Istraživanje je presječno, provedeno na uzorku od 100 djece hospitalizirane na Odjelu pedijatrije Opće županijske bolnice u Požegi od 2010. godine zbog bronhiolitisa uzrokovanog respiratornim sincicijskim virusom. Podatci o pacijentima i tijeku bolesti prikupljeni su iz medicinske dokumentacije. Svim pacijentima s kliničkom slikom bronhiolitisa kojima je iz brisa nazofarinksa izoliran respiracijski sincicijski virus, učinjena je kompletna krvna slika i C-reaktivni protein. U statističkoj obradi korišteni su test 2, Mann-Whitneyev test, Spearmanov koeficijent korelacije (Rho) te binarna logistička regresija. Rezultati: S obzirom na ovisnost o kisiku, utvrđene su statistički značajno niže vrijednosti eritrocita (p = 0,030) te značajno više ispitanika s vrijednostima trombocita iznad referentnog raspona (p = 0,030) u skupini koja je ovisna o kisiku, u odnosu na ispitanike koji nisu ovisni o kisiku. C-reaktivni protein nije povezan s prisutnošću pneumonije i ovisnosti o kisiku. Što su više vrijednosti trombocita, a niže vrijednosti MPV-a, to je i dulje trajanje bolesti. Zaključak: Djeca koja tijekom hospitalizacije zbog akutnog bronhiolitisa uzrokovanog respiratornim sincicijskim virusom imaju niže vrijednosti eritrocita i više vrijednosti trombocita iz periferne krvi, imaju veći rizik za ovisnost o kisiku tijekom liječenja, a samim time i za teži tijek bolesti. C-reaktivni protein nije statistički značajno povezan s težinom ili trajanjem bolesti.

秘鲁严重呼吸道病毒感染预防:秘鲁婴儿严重 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染的 BASADAS EN LA METODOLOGÍA 等级预防建议:基于等级方法的建议

PREVENCIÓN DE LAS INFECCIONES SEVERAS POR VIRUS SINCITIAL RESPIRATORIO EN LACTANTES EN EL PERÚ: RECOMENDACIONES BASADAS EN LA METODOLOGÍA GRADE PREVENTION OF SEVERE RESPIRATORY SYNCYTIAL VIRUS INFECTIONS IN INFANTS IN PERU: RECOMMENDATIONS BASED ON THE GRADE METHODOLOGY
作者： Alfonso Francisco Pantoja Nieva, Héctor Nuñez Paucar, Fadia Karina Uribe Hidalgo, Ana F. Zúñiga Rivera, Javier Jugo Rebaza, Raúl Julián Llanos Lam, Jaime A. Zegarra Dueñas
摘要中文翻译简介:原发性呼吸系统病毒 (VSR) 是感染呼吸道感染的主要原因,代表着对疾病、住院和死亡的重要影响,特别是在秘鲁的下水道和中水道中的生命初期。目的:为秘鲁 VSR 的乳制品预防坟墓提供证据。方法:使用 GRADE 方法对临床实践进行详细说明。我们以 PICO 格式为临床预构建公式,并以生物医学数据为基础对证据系统进行修订。评估证据的证明要考虑其有效性、一致性、精确性和应用性。使用公式化建议的决策证据 (EtD)。结果:Se recomienda la vacunación materna entre las 32 y 36 semanas de gestación para reducir la enfermedadgrave y las Hospitalizaciones por VSR en milkantes(建议帮个忙,evidencia alta)。Enlacantes no protegidos por vacunación materna,se recomienda administrar 尼塞韦单抗 (recomendación fuerte avant,evidencia alta)。在准备工作和上级政策的实施过程中,尼塞韦单抗 会优先考虑策略,并推荐符合条件的子组和直接证据。结论:针对 VSR 的免疫预防策略、母体真空和抗抗单克隆抗体 (尼塞韦单抗) 的使用、降低秘鲁乳杆菌维持能力的潜力、以及实施需要考虑的计划公共健康的优先事项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
圣心呼吸系统病毒 (VSR) 是感染下呼吸道感染的主要原因,也是导致疾病、住院和死亡的重要因素,特别是在秘鲁的下水道和中道入口处。
设计与对象
我们以 PICO 格式为临床预构建公式,并以生物医学数据为基础对证据系统进行修订。使用公式化建议的决策证据 (EtD)。
核心方法
这是使用 GRADE 方法进行临床实践的详细说明。评估证据的证明要考虑其有效性、一致性、精确性和应用性。
主要结果
Se recomienda la vacunación materna entre las 32 y 36 semanas de gestación para reducir la enfermedadgrave y las Hospitalizaciones por VSR en milkantes(建议帮个忙,evidencia alta)。Enlacantes no protegidos por vacunación materna,se recomienda administrar 尼塞韦单抗 (recomendación fuerte avant,evidencia alta)。在准备工作和上级政策的实施过程中,尼塞韦单抗 会优先考虑策略,并推荐符合条件的子组和直接证据。
解释与创新
对抗 VSR 的免疫预防策略、母体真空和使用抗抗单克隆抗体 (尼塞韦单抗) 的基础,可降低秘鲁乳酸中的维持物质的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
我们建议您在母亲真空和使用 尼塞韦单抗 时了解有关公共健康的政治信息,以减少秘鲁乳制品中 VSR 的运输。
局限与证据强度
建议基于高质量证据。

PREVENCIÓN DE LAS INFECCIONES SEVERAS POR VIRUS SINCITIAL RESPIRATORIO EN LACTANTES EN EL PERÚ: RECOMENDACIONES BASADAS EN LA METODOLOGÍA GRADE PREVENTION OF SEVERE RESPIRATORY SYNCYTIAL VIRUS INFECTIONS IN INFANTS IN PERU: RECOMMENDATIONS BASED ON THE GRADE METHODOLOGY

Authors: Alfonso Francisco Pantoja Nieva, Héctor Nuñez Paucar, Fadia Karina Uribe Hidalgo, Ana F. Zúñiga Rivera, Javier Jugo Rebaza, Raúl Julián Llanos Lam, Jaime A. Zegarra Dueñas
Original abstract (English)
Introducción: El virus sincitial respiratorio (VSR) es la principal causa de infección respiratoria aguda baja en lactantes y representa una importante carga de morbilidad, hospitalización y mortalidad, especialmente durante los primeros meses de vida en países de ingresos bajos y medianos como el Perú. Objetivo: Desarrollar recomendaciones basadas en evidencia para prevenir la enfermedad grave por VSR en lactantes en el Perú. Métodos: Se elaboró una guía de práctica clínica utilizando la metodología GRADE. Se formularon cinco preguntas clínicas estructuradas en formato PICO y se realizó una revisión sistemática de la evidencia en bases de datos biomédicas. La certeza de la evidencia se evaluó considerando el riesgo de sesgo, la consistencia, la precisión y la aplicabilidad. Se utilizó un marco de la Evidencia a la Decisión (EtD) para formular recomendaciones. Resultados: Se recomienda la vacunación materna entre las 32 y 36 semanas de gestación para reducir la enfermedad grave y las hospitalizaciones por VSR en lactantes (recomendación fuerte a favor, evidencia alta). En lactantes no protegidos por vacunación materna, se recomienda administrar nirsevimab (recomendación fuerte a favor, evidencia alta). En prematuros y en poblaciones de alto riesgo, nirsevimab se considera la estrategia preferida, con recomendaciones fuertes o condicionales según el subgrupo y la disponibilidad de evidencia directa. Conclusión: Las estrategias de inmunoprofilaxis contra el VSR, basadas en la vacunación materna y el uso de un anticuerpo monoclonal (nirsevimab), tienen el potencial de reducir sustancialmente la carga de enfermedad en lactantes en el Perú, y su implementación programada debe considerarse una prioridad de salud pública.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
El virus sincitial respiratorio (VSR) es la principal causa de infección respiratoria aguda baja en lactantes y representa una importante carga de morbilidad, hospitalización y mortalidad, especialmente durante los primeros meses de vida en países de ingresos bajos y medianos como el Perú.

Se formularon cinco preguntas clínicas estructuradas en formato PICO y se realizó una revisión sistemática de la evidencia en bases de datos biomédicas. Se utilizó un marco de la Evidencia a la Decisión (EtD) para formular recomendaciones.
Core methods

Main results
Se recomienda la vacunación materna entre las 32 y 36 semanas de gestación para reducir la enfermedad grave y las hospitalizaciones por VSR en lactantes (recomendación fuerte a favor, evidencia alta). En lactantes no protegidos por vacunación materna, se recomienda administrar nirsevimab (recomendación fuerte a favor, evidencia alta). En prematuros y en poblaciones de alto riesgo, nirsevimab se considera la estrategia preferida, con recomendaciones fuertes o condicionales según el subgrupo y la disponibilidad de evidencia directa.

Las recomendaciones basadas en la vacunación materna y el uso de nirsevimab pueden informar políticas de salud pública para reducir la carga de enfermedad por VSR en lactantes en el Perú.
Limitations and evidence strength
Las recomendaciones se basan en evidencia alta.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

COVID-19 时代的 SARS-CoV-2、流感、RSV 和人类偏肺病毒的 流行病学:沙特阿拉伯三级中心为期八年的研究

Epidemiology of SARS-CoV-2, Influenza, RSV, and Human Metapneumovirus Across the COVID-19 Era: An Eight-Year Tertiary-Center Study in Saudi Arabia
作者： Lama Alzamil, Arwa Bagasi, Zeina S. Alkudmani, Hamad M. Almatrudi, Abdulrahman F. Alrezaihi, Abdulaziz M. Almuqrin
摘要中文翻译背景:由病毒引起的呼吸道感染(RTI)是一个公共卫生问题,导致全世界住院和发病。COVID-19 大流行通过公共卫生干预措施极大地改变了呼吸道病毒的检测模式。沙特阿拉伯有关这些变化和病毒季节性的纵向数据仍然有限。这项为期八年的研究评估了沙特阿拉伯一家主要三级护理中心在整个 COVID-19 时代主要呼吸道病毒的流行病学和季节性模式。方法:2017 年 10 月至 2025 年 12 月在利雅得哈立德国王大学医院进行了一项回顾性观察研究。从医院实验室信息系统中提取了 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感、乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和人类偏肺病毒 (HMPV) 的 PCR 确诊检测结果。使用描述性统计、Kruskal-Wallis 检验和多变量负二项式回归,对跨越 COVID-19 之前、封锁、限制和限制后四个时期的人口统计、共同检测、季节性和住院时间 (LOS) 进行了分析。结果:在 5708 例确诊的呼吸道病毒检测事件中,SARS-CoV-2 检测频率最高(52.5%),其次是甲型流感(19.1%)、RSV(16.4%)、乙型流感(8.4%)和 HMPV(3.6%)。在大流行之前,甲型流感占检测到的病例的最大比例(71.1%),检测集中在秋冬季节,而SARS-CoV-2占封锁和限制时期检测到的病例的大部分(97.4%和93.2%)。后限制时代变得更加多样化,RSV 检测到的比例很高(28.3%),显示出冬季活动突出。RSV 和 HMPV 主要影响 0-5 岁儿童,而 SARS-CoV-2 在老年人中更常见。在成年人中,年龄较大与 LOS 延长有关;与 SARS-CoV-2 相比,甲型流感和 HMPV 的停留时间较短。在儿童中,调整年龄和性别后,没有病毒与 LOS 独立相关;然而,年龄越大,住院时间越长。结论:在 COVID-19 时代,观察到的主要呼吸道病毒的检测模式发生了很大变化,观察到的阳性检测在限制后出现了显着的多样化。LOS 与成人和儿童的年龄以及成人的病毒类型相关,支持持续的 监测 来指导预防和临床策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由病毒引起的呼吸道感染 (RTI) 是一个公共卫生问题,导致全世界住院人数和发病率增加。本研究旨在评估整个 COVID-19 时代主要呼吸道病毒的流行病学和季节性模式。
设计与对象
2017 年 10 月至 2025 年 12 月在利雅得哈立德国王大学医院进行了一项回顾性观察研究。该研究包括来自医院实验室信息系统的 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感、乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和人类偏肺病毒 (HMPV) 的 PCR 确认检测。
核心方法
使用描述性统计、Kruskal-Wallis 检验和多变量负二项式回归,对跨越 COVID-19 之前、封锁、限制和限制后四个时期的人口统计、共同检测、季节性和住院时间 (LOS) 进行了分析。
主要结果
在 5708 例确诊的呼吸道病毒检测事件中,SARS-CoV-2 检测频率最高(52.5%),其次是甲型流感(19.1%)、RSV(16.4%)、乙型流感(8.4%)和 HMPV(3.6%)。在大流行之前,甲型流感占检测到的病例的最大比例(71.1%),检测集中在秋冬季节,而SARS-CoV-2占封锁和限制时期检测到的病例的大部分(97.4%和93.2%)。
解释与创新
在 COVID-19 时代,观察到的主要呼吸道病毒的检测模式发生了很大变化,观察到的阳性检测在限制后出现了显着的多样化。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持监测继续指导预防和临床策略,特别是在了解呼吸道病毒检测和住院模式的动态变化方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对医院数据的依赖而受到限制,这些数据可能无法代表更广泛的人群。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Epidemiology of SARS-CoV-2, Influenza, RSV, and Human Metapneumovirus Across the COVID-19 Era: An Eight-Year Tertiary-Center Study in Saudi Arabia

Authors: Lama Alzamil, Arwa Bagasi, Zeina S. Alkudmani, Hamad M. Almatrudi, Abdulrahman F. Alrezaihi, Abdulaziz M. Almuqrin
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory tract infections (RTIs) caused by viruses are a public health problem, contributing to hospital admissions and morbidity worldwide. The COVID-19 pandemic substantially altered the detection patterns of respiratory viruses through public health interventions. Longitudinal data on these changes and viral seasonality remain limited in Saudi Arabia. This eight-year study evaluated epidemiological and seasonal patterns of major respiratory viruses across the COVID-19 era at a major Saudi tertiary care center. Methods: A retrospective observational study was conducted at King Khalid University Hospital, Riyadh, from October 2017 to December 2025. PCR-confirmed detections for SARS-CoV-2, Influenza A, Influenza B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and human metapneumovirus (HMPV) were extracted from the hospital Laboratory Information System. Demographics, co-detection, seasonality, and length of stay (LOS) were analyzed across four eras spanning the pre-COVID-19, lockdown, restriction, and post-restriction periods using descriptive statistics, the Kruskal–Wallis test, and multivariable negative binomial regression. Results: Among 5708 confirmed respiratory viral detection episodes, SARS-CoV-2 was the most frequently detected (52.5%), followed by Influenza A (19.1%), RSV (16.4%), Influenza B (8.4%), and HMPV (3.6%). Before the pandemic, Influenza A accounted for the largest proportion of detected episodes (71.1%), with detections clustering particularly during autumn and winter seasons, whereas SARS-CoV-2 accounted for the majority of detected episodes during the lockdown and restriction eras (97.4% and 93.2%). The post-restriction era became more diverse, with RSV being detected in substantial proportions (28.3%), showing prominent winter activity. RSV and HMPV predominantly affected children aged 0–5 years, whereas SARS-CoV-2 was more frequently detected among older adults. Among adults, older age was associated with prolonged LOS; Influenza A and HMPV were associated with shorter stays compared to SARS-CoV-2. Among children, no virus was independently associated with LOS after adjustment for age and sex; however, older age was associated with longer hospital stays. Conclusion: The observed detection patterns of major respiratory viruses shifted substantially across the COVID-19 eras, with notable post-restriction diversification among observed positive detections. LOS was associated with age in both adults and children and with virus type among adults, supporting continued surveillance to guide preventive and clinical strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory tract infections (RTIs) caused by viruses are a public health problem, contributing to hospital admissions and morbidity worldwide. This study aimed to evaluate the epidemiological and seasonal patterns of major respiratory viruses across the COVID-19 era.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted at King Khalid University Hospital, Riyadh, from October 2017 to December 2025. The study included PCR-confirmed detections for SARS-CoV-2, Influenza A, Influenza B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and human metapneumovirus (HMPV) from the hospital Laboratory Information System.
Core methods
Demographics, co-detection, seasonality, and length of stay (LOS) were analyzed across four eras spanning the pre-COVID-19, lockdown, restriction, and post-restriction periods using descriptive statistics, the Kruskal–Wallis test, and multivariable negative binomial regression.
Main results
Among 5708 confirmed respiratory viral detection episodes, SARS-CoV-2 was the most frequently detected (52.5%), followed by Influenza A (19.1%), RSV (16.4%), Influenza B (8.4%), and HMPV (3.6%). Before the pandemic, Influenza A accounted for the largest proportion of detected episodes (71.1%), with detections clustering particularly during autumn and winter seasons, whereas SARS-CoV-2 accounted for the majority of detected episodes during the lockdown and restriction eras (97.4% and 93.2%).
Interpretation and novelty
The observed detection patterns of major respiratory viruses shifted substantially across the COVID-19 eras, with notable post-restriction diversification among observed positive detections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support continued surveillance to guide preventive and clinical strategies, particularly in understanding the changing dynamics of respiratory virus detection and hospitalization patterns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on hospital data, which may not represent the broader population. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

Morbidity and mortality of respiratory syncytial virus versus influenza virus infections in hospitalized older adults: an adjusted comparative analysis

Authors: Alba Cano Rodríguez, Jesús García-Martínez, Isabel Cano de Torres
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Pneumonia is diagnosed based on clinical symptoms and radiological features. It can be caused by a variety of pathogens including bacterial, mycobacterial, viral, fungal, and other atypical organisms. Most bacterial community-acquired pneumonia is due to Streptococcus pneumoniae, which is normally treated with penicillin and can be risk stratified using the CURB-65 score. Atypical organisms require macrolides. Hospital-acquired pneumonia involves Gram-negative or resistant organisms and treatment depends on local sensitivities. Viral pneumonias can include influenza and COVID-19, with antivirals used only in specific cases. Immunocompromised patients may develop invasive fungal infections such as Pneumocystis jiroveci pneumonia or invasive aspergillosis requiring targeted antifungals.

小儿癌症患者呼吸道病毒感染的临床特征和结果,单中心经验

Clinical Characteristics and Outcomes of Respiratory Viral Infections in Pediatric Cancer Patients, Single center experience
作者： Mona Mohammed, Yassin Taher, Naiven Mohamed, Nehad Abd Elfatah, Marwa Jamal, Omnya Ahmed, Amira Sameh, Mohamed Kamal, Youssef Madney
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 呼吸道病毒 (RV) 感染会导致患有癌症 治疗 的免疫功能低下儿科患者发病和 死亡率。低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 儿童肿瘤人群的 RV 感染负担仍然有限。我们的目的是检测引起儿科癌症患者呼吸道感染的病毒的 患病率,并指导经验性治疗和改善患者的治疗结果。方法 回顾性收集了 2023 年 9 月至 2025 年 3 月埃及 57357 儿童癌症医院有呼吸道症状的 治疗 癌症儿童患者的鼻咽拭子以及临床和人口统计数据。使用多重 PCR 对样本进行了 16 种呼吸道病毒检测。结果共收集784例患者1710份样本,中位年龄7岁,男女比例1.26:1。在 1037 份拭子(60.6%)中检测到病毒病原体。最常见的病毒是人鼻病毒 (HRV) (51.3%),其次是副流感病毒 (PIV) (9%)、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) (8%)、SARS-CoV-2 (6.4%) 和人偏肺病毒 (HmpV) (5.8%)。13% 的样本中观察到混合感染,通常涉及 RSV、PIV 和腺病毒。主要症状为发热(85.7%)、下呼吸道感染(LRTI)(59.4%)和咳嗽(57.7%)。HRV 与发烧 (p=0.01)、咳嗽 (p=0.02) 和入院 (p=0.006) 相关。PIV 感染与入院率增加 (p=0.04) 和正性肌力药物需求增加 (p=0.02) 相关,而 HmpV 与 LRTI 显着相关 (p=0.01)。病毒类型和 死亡率 之间没有统计学上的显着关联 (p=0.64)。当将患者特征与临床症状和结果关联起来时,发现了一些显着的关联。中性粒细胞计数较低(<500)与鼻漏(p<0.001)、需氧量(p=0.01)、入院(p=0.001)和入院重症监护病房(p=0.04)相关。女性性别与需氧量 (p=0.03) 和入住 ICU (p=0.02) 显着相关;患有血液系统恶性肿瘤的患者更有可能需要氧气支持(p=0.01)。结论 RV 感染在有呼吸道症状的儿童癌症患者中很常见,以 HRV 为主,且与关键临床表现和入院情况相关。宿主 因素(例如女性、血液肿瘤和严重中性粒细胞减少症)也与疾病严重程度增加和支持性护理需求增加相关。然而,死亡率 在病毒类型之间没有显着差异,这表明虽然 RV 感染对发病率有很大影响,但免疫功能低下儿童的 死亡率 可能取决于多因素 宿主 和疾病相关因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒(RV)感染可导致免疫功能低下的儿科癌症患者罹患疾病并发生死亡率。低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)儿科肿瘤人群中RV感染的负担仍有限。
设计与对象
我们回顾性收集了2023年9月至2025年3月埃及第57357儿童癌症医院有呼吸道症状的癌症治疗儿童患者的鼻咽拭子以及临床和人口统计数据。共收集了784名患者的1710份样本,中位年龄为7岁,男女比例为1.26:1。
核心方法
使用多重 PCR 对样本进行了 16 种呼吸道病毒检测。当将患者特征与临床症状和结果关联起来时,发现了一些显着的关联。
主要结果
在 1037 份拭子(60.6%)中检测到病毒病原体。最常见的病毒是人鼻病毒 (HRV) (51.3%),其次是副流感病毒 (PIV) (9%)、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) (8%)、SARS-CoV-2 (6.4%) 和人偏肺病毒 (HmpV) (5.8%)。13% 的样本中观察到混合感染,通常涉及 RSV、PIV 和腺病毒。
解释与创新
RSV 感染与入院率增加 (p=0.04) 和正性肌力药物需求增加 (p=0.02) 相关,而 HmpV 与 LRTI 显着相关 (p=0.01)。宿主 因素(例如女性、血液肿瘤和严重中性粒细胞减少症)也与疾病严重程度增加和支持性护理需求增加相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSV 感染对儿童癌症患者的发病率有很大影响,强调了免疫功能低下人群需要有针对性的 监测 和管理策略。
局限与证据强度
病毒类型和 死亡率 之间没有统计学上的显着关联 (p=0.64)。所提供的证据并未报告研究结果的普遍性或研究设计中的潜在偏差。

Clinical Characteristics and Outcomes of Respiratory Viral Infections in Pediatric Cancer Patients, Single center experience

Authors: Mona Mohammed, Yassin Taher, Naiven Mohamed, Nehad Abd Elfatah, Marwa Jamal, Omnya Ahmed, Amira Sameh, Mohamed Kamal, Youssef Madney
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Respiratory viral (RV) infections cause morbidity and mortality in immunocompromised pediatric patients undergoing cancer treatment. The burden of RV infections in pediatric oncology populations from low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) remain limited. We aimed to detect the prevalence of viruses causing respiratory infections among pediatric patients with cancer and guide empiric therapy and improve patient outcomes. Methods Retrospectively we collected nasopharyngeal swabs and clinical and demographic data from pediatric patients under cancer treatment with respiratory symptoms at Children’s Cancer Hospital Egypt-57357 from September 2023 until March 2025. Samples were tested using multiplex PCR for 16 respiratory viruses. Results A total of 1710 samples were collected from 784 patients with a median age of 7 years and a male-to-female ratio of 1.26:1. Viral pathogens were detected in 1037 swabs (60.6%). The most frequently identified virus was human rhinovirus (HRV) (51.3%), followed by parainfluenza virus (PIV) (9%), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) (8%), SARS-CoV-2 (6.4%), and human metapneumovirus (HmpV) (5.8%). Co-infections were observed in 13% of samples, commonly involving RSV, PIV, and adenovirus. The main symptoms were fever (85.7%), lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) (59.4%), and cough (57.7%). HRV were associated with fever (p=0.01), cough (p=0.02), and hospital admission (p=0.006). PIV infection were associated with increased hospital admission (p=0.04) and need for inotropes (p=0.02), while HmpV was significantly related to LRTI (p=0.01). There was no statistically significant association between virus type and mortality (p=0.64). When correlating patient characteristics with clinical symptoms and outcomes, several significant associations were found. Lower neutrophil count (<500) correlated with rhinorrhea (p<0.001), oxygen need (p=0.01), hospital admission (p=0.001) and intensive care unit (ICU) admission (p=0.04). Female sex was significantly associated with oxygen requirement (p=0.03) and ICU admission (p=0.02); and patients with hematologic malignancies were more likely to require oxygen support (p=0.01). Conclusion RV infections are common among pediatric cancer patients presenting with respiratory symptoms, with predominance of HRV and linked to key clinical manifestations and hospital admission. Host factors such as female sex, hematology tumors, and profound neutropenia were also associated with increased illness severity and need for supportive care. However, mortality did not significantly differ among virus types, suggesting that while RV infections contribute substantially to morbidity, mortality in immunocompromised children likely depends on multifactorial host and disease-related factors.

Question and background
Respiratory viral (RV) infections cause morbidity and mortality in immunocompromised pediatric patients undergoing cancer treatment. The burden of RV infections in pediatric oncology populations from low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) remain limited.
Design and population
Retrospectively we collected nasopharyngeal swabs and clinical and demographic data from pediatric patients under cancer treatment with respiratory symptoms at Children’s Cancer Hospital Egypt-57357 from September 2023 until March 2025. A total of 1710 samples were collected from 784 patients with a median age of 7 years and a male-to-female ratio of 1.26:1.
Core methods
Samples were tested using multiplex PCR for 16 respiratory viruses. When correlating patient characteristics with clinical symptoms and outcomes, several significant associations were found.
Main results
Viral pathogens were detected in 1037 swabs (60.6%). The most frequently identified virus was human rhinovirus (HRV) (51.3%), followed by parainfluenza virus (PIV) (9%), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) (8%), SARS-CoV-2 (6.4%), and human metapneumovirus (HmpV) (5.8%). Co-infections were observed in 13% of samples, commonly involving RSV, PIV, and adenovirus.
Interpretation and novelty
RSV infection were associated with increased hospital admission (p=0.04) and need for inotropes (p=0.02), while HmpV was significantly related to LRTI (p=0.01). Host factors such as female sex, hematology tumors, and profound neutropenia were also associated with increased illness severity and need for supportive care.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSV infections contribute substantially to morbidity in pediatric cancer patients, highlighting the need for targeted surveillance and management strategies in immunocompromised populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
There was no statistically significant association between virus type and mortality (p=0.64). The supplied evidence does not report on the generalisability of the findings or potential biases in the study design.

呼吸道合胞病毒前融合F蛋白疫苗疫苗在两个RSV流行季中的保护持久性

Durability of RSV Prefusion F Vaccine Protection across Two RSV Seasons
作者： Mats C Højbjerg Lassen, Niklas Dyrby Johansen, Sine H. Christensen, Abby E. Rudolph, Kristoffer G. Skaarup, Daniel Modin, Brian L. Claggett, Carsten S. Larsen, Lykke Larsen, Lothar Wiese
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是老年人住院和死亡的重要原因。缺乏有关 RSV 单个季节之后的保护持久性的数据。
设计与对象
在一项采用个体随机化的务实、开放标签试验中,60 岁或以上的参与者以 1:1 的比例分配,在 2024-2025 年冬季接受 RSVpreF 疫苗 或不接受 疫苗。对参与者进行了整个 2025-2026 年冬季的跟踪,以评估单次 疫苗 剂量后对连续两个 RSV 季节的保护作用。
核心方法
主要终点是 RSV 相关呼吸道疾病 (RTD) 住院治疗。次要终点包括RSV相关和全因下呼吸道疾病住院、全因呼吸系统住院、全因心肺住院和全因死亡率。
主要结果
在131,379名参与随机分配的受试者中,130,991名在第二季度被纳入意向性治疗人群。在第二呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)流行季,RSVpreF组的RSV相关下呼吸道疾病(RTD)住院率为每1000人年0.11例,对照组为每1000人年0.25例,对应的疫苗有效率为56.2%(95%置信区间[CI]:-12.4%至84.8%),而第一季度为83.3%(95% CI:42.9%至96.9%)。
解释与创新
与没有 疫苗 的情况相比,RSVpreF 疫苗 与第二个 RSV 季节期间 RSV 相关呼吸道疾病的 发病率 较低相关。与第一季相比,第二季的 疫苗 效率估计似乎较低。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSVpreF 疫苗 在第二个季节为老年人提供了一些针对 RSV 相关住院的保护,尽管其有效性可能会随着时间的推移而减弱。这表明需要进一步研究 疫苗 的耐久性和潜在的助推器策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项务实的、开放标签的试验,具有个体随机化,这可能会引入偏差。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Durability of RSV Prefusion F Vaccine Protection across Two RSV Seasons

Authors: Mats C Højbjerg Lassen, Niklas Dyrby Johansen, Sine H. Christensen, Abby E. Rudolph, Kristoffer G. Skaarup, Daniel Modin, Brian L. Claggett, Carsten S. Larsen, Lykke Larsen, Lothar Wiese
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is an important cause of hospitalization and death among older adults. Although first-season vaccine effectiveness against RSV-related hospitalization has been demonstrated for the bivalent RSV prefusion F protein-based vaccine (RSVpreF), data regarding the durability of protection beyond a single RSV season are lacking. METHODS: In a pragmatic, open-label trial with individual randomization, participants 60 years of age or older were assigned in a 1:1 ratio to receive RSVpreF vaccine or no vaccine during the 2024-2025 winter season. Participants were followed through the 2025-2026 winter season to evaluate protection across two consecutive RSV seasons after a single vaccine dose. The primary endpoint was RSV-related respiratory tract disease (RTD) hospitalization. Secondary endpoints included RSV-related and all-cause lower respiratory tract disease hospitalization, all-cause respiratory hospitalization, all-cause cardiorespiratory hospitalization, and all-cause mortality. RESULTS: Of 131,379 participants who underwent random assignment, 130,991 were included in the intention-to-treat population during the second season. During the second RSV season, the incidence rate of RSV-related RTD hospitalization was 0.11 events per 1000 person-years in the RSVpreF group and 0.25 events per 1000 person-years in the control group, corresponding to a vaccine effectiveness of 56.2% (95% confidence interval [CI], -12.4% to 84.8%), as compared with 83.3% (95% CI, 42.9%-96.9%) during the first season. Across both seasons, the corresponding incidence rates were 0.11 and 0.37 events per 1000 person-years in the RSVpreF and control groups, respectively, with a vaccine effectiveness of 70.6% (95% CI, 39.1%-87.0%). There was no apparent difference in all-cause respiratory and cardiorespiratory hospitalization outcomes in the second season among those randomly assigned to vaccine or no vaccine in the first season. CONCLUSIONS: Among adults 60 years of age or older, the RSVpreF vaccine was associated with a lower incidence of RSV-related respiratory tract disease during a second RSV season as compared to no vaccine. Vaccine effectiveness estimates appeared to be lower in the second season compared to the first. (Funded by Pfizer; ClinicalTrials.gov number, NCT06684743.).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is an important cause of hospitalization and death among older adults. Data regarding the durability of protection beyond a single RSV season are lacking.
Design and population
In a pragmatic, open-label trial with individual randomization, participants 60 years of age or older were assigned in a 1:1 ratio to receive RSVpreF vaccine or no vaccine during the 2024-2025 winter season. Participants were followed through the 2025-2026 winter season to evaluate protection across two consecutive RSV seasons after a single vaccine dose.
Core methods
The primary endpoint was RSV-related respiratory tract disease (RTD) hospitalization. Secondary endpoints included RSV-related and all-cause lower respiratory tract disease hospitalization, all-cause respiratory hospitalization, all-cause cardiorespiratory hospitalization, and all-cause mortality.
Main results
Of 131,379 participants who underwent random assignment, 130,991 were included in the intention-to-treat population during the second season. During the second RSV season, the incidence rate of RSV-related RTD hospitalization was 0.11 events per 1000 person-years in the RSVpreF group and 0.25 events per 1000 person-years in the control group, corresponding to a vaccine effectiveness of 56.2% (95% confidence interval [CI], -12.4% to 84.8%), as compared with 83.3% (95% CI, 42.9%-96.9%) during the first season.
Interpretation and novelty
The RSVpreF vaccine was associated with a lower incidence of RSV-related respiratory tract disease during a second RSV season as compared to no vaccine. Vaccine effectiveness estimates appeared to be lower in the second season compared to the first.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the RSVpreF vaccine provides some protection against RSV-related hospitalization in older adults during a second season, though the effectiveness may diminish over time. This informs the need for further studies on vaccine durability and potential booster strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a pragmatic, open-label trial with individual randomization, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the potential for confounding factors.

空腔减少的预融合 RSV F 二价 疫苗 引发持久的保护性免疫力

A cavity-reduced prefusion RSV F bivalent vaccine elicits durable and protective immunity
作者： Li-Jie Liu, Meng-Rong Yan, Ruo-Xu Liang, Qing-Xin Wu, Changbin Qu, Hui-Qi Wu, Zhi Liao, Hui-Yu Chen, Jing-Jing Zou, Ze-Yun Sun
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是严重呼吸道疾病的主要原因,F 糖蛋白预融合 (preF) 构象的稳定对于 疫苗 的发展至关重要。在这里,我们报告了一种结构引导的工程策略,该策略通过减少三聚体 F 蛋白内的疏水空腔来增强 preF 稳定性。对亚稳定性相关位点进行靶向修饰生成了 RVF-88,这是一种不含二硫键的稳定 preF 免疫原,可保留关键中和表位,包括抗原位点 Ø,同时表现出改善的结构完整性和长期储存稳定性。RVF-88 采用无佐剂二价 疫苗 配方,在小鼠体内引发有效的中和抗体反应和持久的免疫保护,可持续长达 5 个月。疫苗接种 还保护小鼠和棉鼠免受 RSV 攻击。结构分析证实了预期的空腔减少设计,揭示了顶端疏水空腔体积和表面积的减少,同时保持了预融合结构。总之,这些发现确立了疏水腔减少作为稳定预灌 RSV F 的合理策略,并为进一步的临床开发提供了具有改进的稳定性和免疫原性的有前途的下一代 疫苗 候选药物。

在这里,我们报告了一种结构引导的工程策略,该策略通过减少三聚体 F 蛋白内的疏水空腔来增强 preF 稳定性。对亚稳定性相关位点进行靶向修饰生成了 RVF-88,这是一种不含二硫键的稳定 preF 免疫原,可保留关键中和表位,包括抗原位点 Ø,同时表现出改善的结构完整性和长期储存稳定性。

主要结果
RVF-88 采用无佐剂二价 疫苗 配方,在小鼠体内引发有效的中和抗体反应和持久的免疫保护,可持续长达 5 个月。

所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

A cavity-reduced prefusion RSV F bivalent vaccine elicits durable and protective immunity

Authors: Li-Jie Liu, Meng-Rong Yan, Ruo-Xu Liang, Qing-Xin Wu, Changbin Qu, Hui-Qi Wu, Zhi Liao, Hui-Yu Chen, Jing-Jing Zou, Ze-Yun Sun
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a major cause of severe respiratory disease, and stabilization of the prefusion (preF) conformation of the F glycoprotein is central for vaccine development. Here, we report a structure-guided engineering strategy that enhances preF stability by reducing the hydrophobic cavity within the trimeric F protein. Targeted modifications at metastability-associated sites generated RVF-88, a disulfide-free stabilized preF immunogen that preserves key neutralizing epitopes, including antigenic site Ø, while exhibiting improved structural integrity and long-term storage stability. Formulated as an unadjuvanted bivalent vaccine, RVF-88 elicited potent neutralizing antibody responses and durable immune protection lasting up to 5 months in mice. Vaccination also protected both mice and cotton rats against RSV challenge. Structural analyses confirmed the intended cavity-reduction design, revealing a reduced apical hydrophobic cavity volume and surface area while maintaining the prefusion architecture. Together, these findings establish hydrophobic cavity reduction as a rational strategy for stabilizing prefusion RSV F and provide a promising next-generation vaccine candidate with improved stability and immunogenicity for further clinical development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a major cause of severe respiratory disease, and stabilization of the prefusion (preF) conformation of the F glycoprotein is central for vaccine development.
Design and population
Here, we report a structure-guided engineering strategy that enhances preF stability by reducing the hydrophobic cavity within the trimeric F protein. Targeted modifications at metastability-associated sites generated RVF-88, a disulfide-free stabilized preF immunogen that preserves key neutralizing epitopes, including antigenic site Ø, while exhibiting improved structural integrity and long-term storage stability.
Core methods
Structural analyses confirmed the intended cavity-reduction design, revealing a reduced apical hydrophobic cavity volume and surface area while maintaining the prefusion architecture.
Main results
Formulated as an unadjuvanted bivalent vaccine, RVF-88 elicited potent neutralizing antibody responses and durable immune protection lasting up to 5 months in mice.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

提高新生儿 RSV 免疫接种质量的举措

Quality Improvement Initiative to Increase Newborn RSV Immunization
作者： Shayda W. Daneshvari, Sheri L. Nemerofsky
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿呼吸道疾病的主要原因。本研究旨在满足改进 RSV 免疫实践的需求。
设计与对象
这项单中心质量改进计划是在 2023-2024 RSV 季节期间在 Montefiore 医疗中心韦克菲尔德分部进行的。该研究以新生儿为重点,涉及多学科团队,在孕前、产前和产后阶段实施有针对性的干预措施。
核心方法
使用孕产妇记录和新生儿出院数据跟踪免疫率。使用快速响应代码分析来衡量对教育材料的参与度。电子病历中所需的文件确保所有新生儿在出院前都接受了免疫评估。
主要结果
The average newborn RSV immunization rate increased to 75.9% during the 2024–2025 season. Maternal RSVpreF uptake significantly increased from 10.9 to 33.5% within the first month. The nirsevimab administration remained stable at 57.1% in the second season. Quick-response code flyers had moderate engagement, with higher click-through rates for Spanish-language materials.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为质量改进计划超出其目标的证据,即通过提供者教育、文件标准化和患者外展改善新生儿 RSV 保护。这些干预措施简化了工作流程并推进了公平的免疫实践。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为通过有针对性的干预措施、提供者教育和文件标准化来提高新生儿 RSV 免疫率的策略提供参考。在 疫苗 指南不断变化和犹豫不决的情况下,持续改进和提供者意识对于维持高免疫率至关重要。
局限与证据强度
这种单中心举措可能会限制普遍性。每周监测可以实现实时跟踪和调整,但该研究没有报告长期结果或更广泛的实施挑战。证据强度中等。

Quality Improvement Initiative to Increase Newborn RSV Immunization

Authors: Shayda W. Daneshvari, Sheri L. Nemerofsky
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of respiratory illness in infants. In 2023, the Food and Drug Administration approved 2 methods of RSV prevention: birthing parent bivalent RSVpreF vaccine and infant nirsevimab monoclonal antibody. During the 2023–2024 RSV season, 57.8% of newborns at Montefiore Medical Center Wakefield Division were immunized. This single-center quality improvement initiative aimed to increase RSV immunization rates by 10% in the following season. Methods: Multidisciplinary teams implemented targeted interventions across preconception, prenatal, and postnatal periods. Strategies included posting RSV educational flyers, conducting staff education meetings, and incorporating mandatory RSV-related phrases into newborn discharge summaries. Weekly monitoring enabled real-time tracking and adjustments. Immunization rates were tracked using maternal records and newborn discharge data; engagement with educational materials was measured using quick-response code analytics. Results: The average newborn RSV immunization rate increased to 75.9% during the 2024–2025 season. Maternal RSVpreF uptake significantly increased from 10.9 to 33.5% within the first month. The nirsevimab administration remained stable at 57.1% in the second season. Required documentation in the electronic medical record ensured that all newborns were assessed for immunization before discharge. Quick-response code flyers had moderate engagement, with higher click-through rates for Spanish-language materials. Conclusions: This quality improvement initiative exceeded its target, improving newborn RSV protection through provider education, documentation standardization, and patient outreach. These interventions streamlined workflow and advanced equitable immunization practices. Continued refinements and provider awareness will be essential to sustain high immunization rates amid evolving vaccine guidance and hesitancy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of respiratory illness in infants. This study aimed to address the need for improved RSV immunization practices.
Design and population
This single-center quality improvement initiative was conducted at Montefiore Medical Center Wakefield Division during the 2023–2024 RSV season. The study focused on newborns and involved multidisciplinary teams implementing targeted interventions across preconception, prenatal, and postnatal periods.
Core methods
Immunization rates were tracked using maternal records and newborn discharge data. Engagement with educational materials was measured using quick-response code analytics. Required documentation in the electronic medical record ensured that all newborns were assessed for immunization before discharge.
Main results
The average newborn RSV immunization rate increased to 75.9% during the 2024–2025 season. Maternal RSVpreF uptake significantly increased from 10.9 to 33.5% within the first month. The nirsevimab administration remained stable at 57.1% in the second season. Quick-response code flyers had moderate engagement, with higher click-through rates for Spanish-language materials.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that the quality improvement initiative exceeded its target, improving newborn RSV protection through provider education, documentation standardization, and patient outreach. These interventions streamlined workflow and advanced equitable immunization practices.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies for increasing RSV immunization rates in newborns through targeted interventions, provider education, and documentation standardization. Continued refinements and provider awareness will be essential to sustain high immunization rates amid evolving vaccine guidance and hesitancy.
Limitations and evidence strength
This single-center initiative may limit generalizability. Weekly monitoring enabled real-time tracking and adjustments, but the study did not report on long-term outcomes or broader implementation challenges. The evidence strength is moderate.

2023 年至 2025 年新 疫苗 监测 网络中 20 个月以下儿童接受 尼塞韦单抗 相关的社会人口统计学和临床​​因素。

Sociodemographic and Clinical Factors Associated With Nirsevimab Receipt Among Children Aged Younger Than 20 Months in the New Vaccine Surveillance Network in 2023 to 2025.
作者： Zheyi Teoh, Ayzsa Tannis, Erin Sullivan, Julie A. Boom, Natasha B. Halasa, Marian G. Michaels, Daniel C. Payne, Leila C. Sahni, Jennifer E Schuster, Rangaraj Selvarangan
摘要中文翻译目的:我们的目的是评估社会人口统计学和临床​​因素与在多个季节和出生后住院治疗的急性呼吸道疾病 (ARI) 儿童中接受 尼塞韦单抗 的关系。患者和方法:我们纳入了在 2023 年至 2024 年和 2024 年至 2025 年 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 季节(10 月 1 日至 3 月 31 日)期间加入新 疫苗 监测 网络的符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格的儿童,年龄小于 8 个月和 8 至 19 个月。使用混合效应逻辑回归模型来评估社会人口统计学和临床​​因素与 8 个月以下入组儿童接受 尼塞韦单抗 的可能性之间的关联。结果:在 2023 年至 2024 年 RSV 季节登记的 2151 名符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格的 ARI 儿童和 2024 年至 2025 年 RSV 季节登记的 1819 名儿童中,96% 的年龄小于 8 个月且处于第一个 RSV 季节。在 2023 年至 2024 年和 2024 年至 2025 年季节,分别有 14% 和 36% 的 8 个月以下符合条件的儿童获得了 尼塞韦单抗。在 8 个月以下的婴儿中,在 RSV 季节出生的婴儿和有基础疾病的婴儿更有可能在两个 RSV 季节接受 尼塞韦单抗;婴儿女性性别和母亲教育也与 2023 年至 2024 年期间的 尼塞韦单抗 接收相关,但与 2024 年至 2025 年无关。结论:本次分析中,大多数符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格且就医的 ARI 儿童在 尼塞韦单抗 可用性的前 2 个季节期间没有接受 尼塞韦单抗。RSV 季节之前出生等因素与 尼塞韦单抗 接收的可能性降低相关,强调需要在出生住院后采取量身定制的干预措施,以增加该组儿童对 尼塞韦单抗 的吸收。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估多个季节和出生后住院治疗的急性呼吸道疾病 (ARI) 儿童中社会人口统计学和临床​​因素与 尼塞韦单抗 收据的关联。
设计与对象
该研究包括在 2023 年至 2024 年和 2024 年至 2025 年 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 季节期间加入新 疫苗 监测 网络的符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格的儿童,年龄小于 8 个月和 8 至 19 个月,患有 ARI。
核心方法
使用混合效应逻辑回归模型来评估社会人口统计学和临床​​因素与 8 个月以下入组儿童接受 尼塞韦单抗 的可能性之间的关联。
主要结果
在2023至2024呼吸道合胞病毒流行季入组的2151名符合尼塞韦单抗条件的儿童中,以及在2024至2025呼吸道合胞病毒流行季入组的1819名儿童中,96%年龄小于8个月且处于首个呼吸道合胞病毒流行季。在年龄小于8个月的符合条件儿童中,于2023至2024流行季和2024至2025流行季分别有14%和36%接种了尼塞韦单抗。
解释与创新
在 8 个月以下的婴儿中,在 RSV 季节出生的婴儿和有基础疾病的婴儿更有可能在两个 RSV 季节接受 尼塞韦单抗;婴儿女性性别和母亲教育也与 2023 年至 2024 年期间的 尼塞韦单抗 收据相关,但与 2024 年至 2025 年无关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要采取有针对性的干预措施,以提高 RSV 相关并发症风险较高的儿童(尤其是在 RSV 季节之前出生的儿童)对 尼塞韦单抗 的吸收。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于新 疫苗 监测 网络的数据,该网络可能无法代表所有人群,并且分析基于观察数据而不是实验证据。

Sociodemographic and Clinical Factors Associated With Nirsevimab Receipt Among Children Aged Younger Than 20 Months in the New Vaccine Surveillance Network in 2023 to 2025.

Authors: Zheyi Teoh, Ayzsa Tannis, Erin Sullivan, Julie A. Boom, Natasha B. Halasa, Marian G. Michaels, Daniel C. Payne, Leila C. Sahni, Jennifer E Schuster, Rangaraj Selvarangan
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: We aim to evaluate the association of sociodemographic and clinical factors with nirsevimab receipt among children with medically attended acute respiratory illness (ARI) across multiple seasons and beyond birth hospitalization. PATIENTS AND METHODS: We included nirsevimab-eligible children aged younger than 8 months and 8 to 19 months with ARI enrolled in the New Vaccine Surveillance Network during the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) seasons (October 1 to March 31). A mixed-effects logistic regression model was used to evaluate the association between sociodemographic and clinical factors with the likelihood of nirsevimab receipt among enrolled children aged younger than 8 months. RESULTS: Of the 2151 nirsevimab-eligible children with ARI enrolled in the 2023 to 2024 RSV season and 1819 in the 2024 to 2025 RSV season, 96% were aged younger than 8 months and in their first RSV season. Fourteen percent and 36% of eligible children aged younger than 8 months received nirsevimab in the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 seasons, respectively. Among infants aged younger than 8 months, those born during the RSV season and those with an underlying medical condition were more likely to receive nirsevimab in both RSV seasons; infant female sex and maternal education were also associated with nirsevimab receipt during 2023 to 2024 but not 2024 to 2025. CONCLUSION: Most nirsevimab-eligible children with medically attended ARI in this analysis did not receive nirsevimab during the first 2 seasons of nirsevimab availability. Factors such as birth before the RSV season were associated with a decreased likelihood of nirsevimab receipt, emphasizing the need for tailored interventions following birth hospitalization to increase nirsevimab uptake for this group of children.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the association of sociodemographic and clinical factors with nirsevimab receipt among children with medically attended acute respiratory illness (ARI) across multiple seasons and beyond birth hospitalization.
Design and population
The study included nirsevimab-eligible children aged younger than 8 months and 8 to 19 months with ARI enrolled in the New Vaccine Surveillance Network during the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) seasons.
Core methods
A mixed-effects logistic regression model was used to evaluate the association between sociodemographic and clinical factors with the likelihood of nirsevimab receipt among enrolled children aged younger than 8 months.
Main results
Of the 2151 nirsevimab-eligible children with ARI enrolled in the 2023 to 2024 RSV season and 1819 in the 2024 to 2025 RSV season, 96% were aged younger than 8 months and in their first RSV season. Fourteen percent and 36% of eligible children aged younger than 8 months received nirsevimab in the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 seasons, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
Among infants aged younger than 8 months, those born during the RSV season and those with an underlying medical condition were more likely to receive nirsevimab in both RSV seasons; infant female sex and maternal education were also associated with nirsevimab receipt during 2023 to 2024 but not 2024 to 2025.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve nirsevimab uptake among children who may be at higher risk of RSV-related complications, particularly those born before the RSV season.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on data from the New Vaccine Surveillance Network, which may not be representative of all populations, and the analysis is based on observational data rather than experimental evidence.

英国新型呼吸道合胞病毒孕妇 疫苗接种 项目及多元城市人群接种差异:一项回顾性研究

The new UK RSV maternal vaccination programme and ethnic disparities in uptake in a diverse urban population: A Retrospective Study
作者： P Sandhu, B Sheridan, C Beresford, F Husain
摘要中文翻译摘要:RSV 是导致 6 个月以下婴儿 毛细支气管炎 和急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因。在英国,RSV 疫苗 自 2024 年 9 月起被引入国家卫生系统,建议从妊娠 28 周起进行产前接种。这项回顾性观察研究检查了英国两家医院的母亲对 RSV 疫苗 的摄取情况 目的:分析产前 RSV 疫苗 摄取的种族差异 方法:从 2024 年 9 月 15 日至 2025 年 4 月 29 日期间从电子病历以及因下呼吸道感染入院的婴儿中收集 疫苗 摄取数据(N = 5,757)。结果:2,931 名女性接受了产前 RSV 疫苗(51% 的接受率)。疫苗 的使用率因种族而异:最高的是英国白人 (62%)、孟加拉国人 (59%);巴基斯坦女性比例最低(30%)。在 67 名因 LRTI 入院的婴儿中,15 名 RSV 阳性(1 名接种疫苗的母亲),52 名 RSV 阴性(14 名接种疫苗的母亲)。讨论:疫苗 的总体采用率为 51%,表明在全国实施后第一个 RSV 季节期间有适度的早期接受。然而,不同种族群体的接受情况存在差异,凸显了随着该计划的建立,值得关注的新出现的不平等现象。本 队列研究 中的婴儿入院数据表明,尽管样本量较小,但母亲 队列研究 与 RSV 相关住院治疗之间存在潜在的保护性关联。结论:研究结果表明,接受程度适中,但存在显着的种族差异。解决这些差异对于最大限度地减少婴儿期呼吸道合胞病毒相关发病率的公共卫生影响至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨英国新型呼吸道合胞病毒孕妇 疫苗接种 项目在多元城市人群中的接种差异,因呼吸道合胞病毒是 6 个月以下婴儿毛细支气管炎和急性下呼吸道感染的主要病因。
设计与对象
这项回顾性观察研究在英国的两家医院进行,从 2024 年 9 月 15 日至 2025 年 4 月 29 日期间,从电子病历和因下呼吸道感染入院的婴儿中收集 疫苗 的吸收数据(N = 5,757)。
核心方法
数据从电子病历和婴儿入院记录中收集,重点关注母亲 RSV 疫苗 的吸收和婴儿 RSV 相关的住院情况。
主要结果
2,931 名妇女接受了产前 RSV 疫苗(51% 的接受率)。疫苗 的使用率因种族而异,其中英国白人 (62%) 和孟加拉国 (59%) 女性的使用率最高,巴基斯坦女性 (30%) 的使用率最低。
解释与创新
随着 RSV 疫苗接种 计划的建立,观察到的不同种族群体的吸收差异凸显了新出现的不平等现象,需要引起关注。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了解决 疫苗 吸收方面的种族差异的重要性,以最大限度地提高婴儿期 RSV 相关发病率的公共卫生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于相对较小的样本量,这可能会限制观察到的孕产妇 疫苗接种 与 RSV 相关住院治疗之间保护性关联的可信度。

The new UK RSV maternal vaccination programme and ethnic disparities in uptake in a diverse urban population: A Retrospective Study

Authors: P Sandhu, B Sheridan, C Beresford, F Husain
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction: RSV is the leading cause of bronchiolitis and acute lower respiratory tract infections in infants under 6 months of age. In the UK, the RSV vaccine was introduced in the National Health System from September 2024, recommended antenatally from 28 weeks of gestation. This retrospective observational study examines maternal uptake of the RSV vaccine across two hospital sites in the UK Aim: To analyse ethnic variations in antenatal RSV Vaccine uptake Method: Data was collected between the months of 15 September 2024 to 29 April 2025 on vaccine uptake from electronic patient records, as well as infants admitted with lower respiratory tract infections to the hospital (N = 5,757). Results: 2,931 women received the antenatal RSV vaccine (51% uptake). Vaccine uptake varied by ethnicity: highest amongst White British (62%), Bangladeshi (59%); lowest in Pakistani women (30%). Among 67 infants admitted with LRTI, 15 were RSV-positive (1 with a vaccinated mother) and 52 RSV-negative (14 with vaccinated mothers). Discussion: Overall vaccine uptake was 51%, indicating moderate early acceptance during the first RSV season following national implementation. However, variations in uptake was observed across ethnic groups, highlighting emerging inequalities that warrant attention as the programme becomes established. Infant admission data in this cohort suggest a potential protective association between maternal vaccination and RSV-related hospitalisation, although sample size was small. Conclusion: Findings demonstrates moderate uptake with significant ethnic variation. Addressing these disparities will be vital to maximise the public health impact against RSV-related morbidity in infancy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to examine ethnic disparities in the uptake of the new UK RSV maternal vaccination programme, as RSV is the leading cause of bronchiolitis and acute lower respiratory tract infections in infants under 6 months of age.
Design and population
This retrospective observational study was conducted across two hospital sites in the UK, collecting data from 15 September 2024 to 29 April 2025 on vaccine uptake from electronic patient records and infants admitted with lower respiratory tract infections (N = 5,757).
Core methods
Data was collected from electronic patient records and infant admission records, focusing on maternal RSV vaccine uptake and RSV-related hospitalisations in infants.
Main results
2,931 women received the antenatal RSV vaccine (51% uptake). Vaccine uptake varied by ethnicity, with the highest rates among White British (62%) and Bangladeshi (59%) women, and the lowest in Pakistani women (30%).
Interpretation and novelty
The observed variations in uptake across ethnic groups highlight emerging inequalities that require attention as the RSV vaccination programme becomes established.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the importance of addressing ethnic disparities in vaccine uptake to maximise the public health impact against RSV-related morbidity in infancy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are based on a relatively small sample size, which may limit the confidence in the observed protective association between maternal vaccination and RSV-related hospitalisation.

A549 细胞中 RSV 高效脱壳需要 Arp2/3 复合物依赖性肌动蛋白重塑

Arp2/3 complex-dependent actin remodeling is required for efficient RSV uncoating in A549 cells
作者： Enyu Xie, Maren Schubert, Jennifer Reeb, Benedikt Seligmann, Matthias Schaks, Mathias Müsken, Anika Steffen, Theresia E. B. Stradal, Sibylle Haid, Klemens Rottner
摘要中文翻译摘要 有效的人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 细胞进入需要病毒蛋白和 宿主 细胞因子在质膜-肌动蛋白皮质界面处的协调相互作用。分支肌动蛋白网络重塑了该界面,但它们对 RSV 感染早期阶段的确切贡献仍不清楚。在这里,我们通过生成 A549 细胞系来干扰 Arp2/3 复合物依赖性肌动蛋白丝分支,该细胞系被 CRISPR/Cas9 破坏以表达必需的 Arp2 亚基。根据感染后 24 小时内的量化结果,Arp2 的永久缺失减少了 RSV 长 GFP 报告病毒的感染。Arp2 敲除细胞的感染效率在以后的时间点持续存在,并且还导致合胞体形成减少。Arp2 重新表达可恢复 Arp2/3 复合物依赖性肌动蛋白重塑,并挽救 Arp2 KO 细胞中的 RSV 感染。值得注意的是,RSV 感染受损并不伴随病毒 宿主 细胞附着的明显变化。此外,光激活定位显微镜 (PALM) 研究揭示了稳定表达 mEos3.2 标记的胰岛素样生长因子 I 受体 (IGF1R) 的细胞中类似的受体扩散和聚集。尽管与 WT 细胞相比,Arp2/3 缺陷细胞显示出较少但较大的巨脂质体,但未观察到内化 RSV 基因组 水平的变化。相比之下,Arp2缺陷细胞的RSV-F介导的病毒样颗粒脱壳效率似乎受到抑制。因此,病毒 mRNA 表达和细胞 III 型干扰素反应降低。总之,这些数据表明 Arp2/3 复合物依赖性分支肌动蛋白网络有助于 RSV 脱壳的效率。重要性 人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是严重呼吸道疾病的主要原因。感染始于质膜-肌动蛋白皮质界面,但肌动蛋白在高效 RSV 进入中的作用仍不清楚。使用 CRISPR/Cas9 破坏 A549 细胞中必需的 Arp2/3 复合体亚基 Arp2,我们发现分支肌动蛋白网络是有效 RSV 感染所必需的。尽管肌动蛋白网络重塑,光激活定位显微镜显示 RSV 受体 IGF1R 的扩散和聚集没有变化。尽管 Arp2 缺陷细胞中的巨胞饮作用受到影响,但 RSV 的附着和内化并未受到影响。相比之下,基于β-内酰胺酶病毒样颗粒的测定显示RSV-F介导的脱壳缺陷,随后病毒基因表达减少和III型干扰素反应较弱。这些发现将 Arp2/3 复合物依赖性分支肌动蛋白网络定义为 RSV-F 介导的脱壳的 宿主 决定因素,并提供了一种无需设计 RSV 基因组 即可量化脱壳的实用方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 Arp2/3 复合物依赖性肌动蛋白重塑在 RSV 感染早期阶段的作用,因为肌动蛋白网络对 RSV 细胞进入的精确贡献仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究使用 CRISPR/Cas9 破坏 A549 细胞中的 Arp2 表达,创建 Arp2 敲除系。然后用 RSV 长 GFP 报告病毒感染这些细胞,以评估随时间推移的感染效率。
核心方法
研究人员使用 CRISPR/Cas9 生成了 Arp2 表达被破坏的 A549 细胞系,并采用基于 β-内酰胺酶病毒样颗粒的测定法来评估 RSV-F 介导的脱壳效率。
主要结果
Arp2 的永久缺失降低了感染后 24 小时内 RSV 的感染效率,并且这种效应在以后的时间点持续存在。此外,Arp2 缺陷细胞表现出 RSV-F 介导的脱壳效率受到抑制。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,Arp2/3 复合物依赖性分支肌动蛋白网络对于 RSV 有效脱壳至关重要,凸显了肌动蛋白重塑在病毒生命周期中的新作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 RSV 感染机制的见解,并提出了旨在破坏病毒脱壳的治疗干预的潜在目标。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 A549 细胞,并不涉及体内条件,但由于使用了靶向遗传破坏和直接测量脱壳效率,证据充足。

Arp2/3 complex-dependent actin remodeling is required for efficient RSV uncoating in A549 cells

Authors: Enyu Xie, Maren Schubert, Jennifer Reeb, Benedikt Seligmann, Matthias Schaks, Mathias Müsken, Anika Steffen, Theresia E. B. Stradal, Sibylle Haid, Klemens Rottner
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Productive human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) cell entry requires coordinated interactions between viral proteins and host-cell factors at the plasma membrane-actin cortex interface. Branched actin networks remodel this interface, but their precise contribution to the early stages of RSV infection remains unclear. Here, we interfered with Arp2/3 complex-dependent actin filament branching by generating A549 cell lines disrupted for expression of the essential Arp2 subunit by CRISPR/Cas9. Permanent loss of Arp2 reduced the infection of the RSV long GFP reporter virus, as quantified over the first 24 h post-infection. Compromised infection efficiency in Arp2 knockout cells persisted at later time points, and also resulted in reduced syncytia formation. Arp2 re-expression restored Arp2/3 complex-dependent actin remodeling and rescued RSV infection in Arp2 KO cells. Notably, impaired RSV infection was not accompanied by obvious changes in viral host cell attachment. Moreover, photoactivated localization microscopy (PALM) studies revealed comparable receptor diffusion and clustering in cells stably expressing mEos3.2-tagged insulin-like growth factor I receptor (IGF1R). Although Arp2/3-deficient cells displayed fewer, albeit larger, macropinosomes compared with WT cells, no changes were observed for internalized RSV genome levels. In contrast, Arp2-deficient cells appeared suppressed in RSV-F mediated virus-like particle uncoating efficiency. Consequently, viral mRNA expression and the cellular type III interferon response were reduced. Together, these data reveal that Arp2/3 complex-dependent, branched actin networks contribute to the efficiency of RSV uncoating. IMPORTANCE Human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe respiratory disease. The infection initiates at the plasma membrane-actin cortex interface, yet the role of actin in productive RSV entry has remained unclear. Using CRISPR/Cas9 disruption of the essential Arp2/3 complex subunit Arp2 in A549 cells, we show that branched actin networks are required for efficient RSV infection. Despite actin network remodeling, photoactivated localization microscopy showed unchanged diffusion and clustering of the RSV receptor IGF1R. Although macropinocytosis was affected in Arp2-deficient cells, RSV attachment and internalization were not influenced. In contrast, a β-lactamase virus-like-particle-based assay revealed a defect in RSV-F-mediated uncoating, followed by reduced viral gene expression and a weaker type III interferon response. These findings define Arp2/3 complex-dependent branched actin networks as a host determinant of RSV-F-mediated uncoating and provide a practical approach to quantify uncoating without engineering the RSV genome.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of Arp2/3 complex-dependent actin remodeling in the early stages of RSV infection, as the precise contribution of actin networks to RSV cell entry remains unclear.
Design and population
The study used CRISPR/Cas9 to disrupt Arp2 expression in A549 cells, creating Arp2 knockout lines. These cells were then infected with RSV long GFP reporter virus to assess infection efficiency over time.
Core methods
The researchers generated A549 cell lines with disrupted Arp2 expression using CRISPR/Cas9 and employed a β-lactamase virus-like-particle-based assay to evaluate RSV-F-mediated uncoating efficiency.
Main results
Permanent loss of Arp2 reduced RSV infection efficiency over the first 24 h post-infection, and this effect persisted at later time points. Additionally, Arp2-deficient cells showed suppressed RSV-F-mediated uncoating efficiency.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that Arp2/3 complex-dependent branched actin networks are essential for efficient RSV uncoating, highlighting a novel role for actin remodeling in the viral life cycle.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the mechanisms of RSV infection and suggest potential targets for therapeutic interventions aimed at disrupting viral uncoating.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to A549 cells and does not address in vivo conditions, but the evidence is strong due to the use of targeted genetic disruption and direct measurement of uncoating efficiency.

有下水道和无下水道卫生设施的城市中的废水和环境 监测:来自乌干达坎帕拉的证据

Wastewater and Environmental Surveillance in Cities with Sewered and Non-sewered Sanitation: Evidence from Kampala, Uganda
作者： Seju Kang, Annet Kagene, Gava Job Ssazi Pius, Jude Zziwa Byansi, Gloria Mirembe, Flavia Zabali Musisi, Charles B. Niwagaba, Karin Gallandat, Timothy R. Julian, Linda Strande
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:废水和环境 监测 (WES) 能够实现社区层面的传染病监测。大多数进展都集中在基于下水道的 监测,但全球近一半的人口依赖无下水道的卫生设施。在无下水道环境中,城市排水渠道已用于脊髓灰质炎病毒环境 监测,但其在空间限定集水区的多病原体 WES 中的潜力以及与基于下水道的 监测 的可比性仍有待探索。方法:在坎帕拉境内选择了10个划定微流域的排水沟采样点(0.95-3.83平方公里,12,885-44,146人)。在 2025 年 3 月和 9 月至 10 月的两次活动中,共收集了 255 个排水沟和 54 个废水 治疗 工厂 (WWTP) 进水样本。通过数字 PCR 对多目标样本进行了定量,包括肠道病毒(诺如病毒 GI 和 GII、轮状病毒)、呼吸道病毒(SARS-CoV-2、甲型和乙型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV))、非 O1/O139 霍乱弧菌 (V. cholerae) 和胡椒轻度斑驳病毒 (PMMoV) 作为粪便指标。研究结果:在所有排水渠道和污水处理厂进水中一致检测到肠道病毒、非 O1/O139 霍乱弧菌和 PMMoV。除非 O1/O139 V 外,排水中的浓度通常低于污水处理厂进水。当通过 PMMoV 标准化时,大多数排水渠道的浓度与污水处理厂进水相当,尽管可比性因目标和位置而异。不同采样地点的浓度和 PMMoV 标准化浓度均有所不同。运动之间的趋势因病原体目标而异,并且不能用任何单一的微流域特征来解释。甲型流感病毒是最常检测到的呼吸道病毒(排水道中为 8-23%;污水处理厂进水中为 3-10%),而 SARS-CoV-2、乙型流感和 RSV 则很少检测到。解释:大多数微流域的 PMMoV 标准化浓度与污水处理厂进水相当,空间异质性意味着疾病 患病率 的邻里水平差异。流域界定的排水 监测 有潜力在无下水道环境中提供空间解析的公共卫生信息,与基于下水道的废水监测相当。资金来源:Eawag 任意资金
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索城市排水沟在非污水收集环境中用于多病原体废水和环境监测的潜力,以填补其在效用方面相较于污水收集系统的认知缺口。
设计与对象
研究在乌干达坎帕拉市进行,共设置10个排水沟采样点,并在2025年3月及9-10月两个时段收集了54份废水治疗厂进水样本。
核心方法
采用数字PCR技术对多靶标面板进行定量分析,其中包括呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)等病原体,并以辣椒轻斑驳病毒(PMMoV)作为粪便指示剂对样本进行标准化处理。
主要结果
呼吸道合胞病毒 在排水沟和废水 治疗 工厂进水中很少检测到,不同采样地点的浓度各不相同。
解释与创新
RSV 的低检出率表明,与其他病原体相比,它在采样的非下水道环境中可能并不那么普遍,但该研究强调了排水 监测 提供空间解析公共卫生数据的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基于排水系统的 监测 可能是监测无下水道城市地区 RSV 的宝贵工具,可提供对社区层面疾病 患病率 的深入了解。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果受到RSV检出率低的限制,并且排水和废水监测的可比性因地点和病原体而异,表明需要进一步开展有针对性的研究。

Wastewater and Environmental Surveillance in Cities with Sewered and Non-sewered Sanitation: Evidence from Kampala, Uganda

Authors: Seju Kang, Annet Kagene, Gava Job Ssazi Pius, Jude Zziwa Byansi, Gloria Mirembe, Flavia Zabali Musisi, Charles B. Niwagaba, Karin Gallandat, Timothy R. Julian, Linda Strande
Original abstract (English)
Summary Background: Wastewater and environmental surveillance (WES) enables community-level monitoring of infectious diseases. Most progress has focused on sewer-based surveillance, yet nearly half of the global population relies on non-sewered sanitation. In non-sewered settings, urban drainage channels have been used for poliovirus environmental surveillance, but their potential for a multi-pathogen WES with spatially defined catchments, and comparability to sewer-based surveillance, remains under-explored. Methods: Ten drainage channel sampling points with delineated micro-catchments (0.95-3.83 km2 with 12,885-44,146 people) were selected within Kampala. A total of 255 drainage channel and 54 wastewater treatment plant (WWTP) influent samples were collected during two campaigns in March and September-October 2025. A multi-target panel was quantified by digital PCR, including enteric viruses (Norovirus GI and GII, Rotavirus), respiratory viruses (SARS-CoV-2, Influenza A and B viruses, Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)), non-O1/O139 Vibrio cholerae (V. cholerae), and Pepper Mild Mottle Virus (PMMoV) as a fecal indicator. Findings: Enteric viruses, non-O1/O139 V. cholerae, and PMMoV were consistently detected across all drainage channels and WWTP influents. Concentrations were generally lower in drainage than WWTP influents, except for non-O1/O139 V. When normalized by PMMoV, concentrations across most drainage channels were comparable to WWTP influents, although comparability varied by target and location. Both concentrations and PMMoV-normalized concentrations varied across sampling locations. Trends between campaigns varied by pathogen target and were not explained by any single micro-catchment characteristic. Influenza A virus was the most frequently detected respiratory virus (8-23% in drainage channels; 3-10% in WWTP influents), while SARS-CoV-2, Influenza B, and RSV were rarely detected. Interpretation: PMMoV-normalized concentrations across most micro-catchments were comparable to WWTP influents, with spatial heterogeneity implying neighborhood-level differences in disease prevalence. Catchment-delineated drainage surveillance has potential to offer spatially resolved public health information in non-sewered settings comparable to sewer-based wastewater monitoring. Funding: Eawag Discretionary Funding
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the potential of urban drainage channels for multi-pathogen wastewater and environmental surveillance in non-sewered settings, addressing the gap in understanding their utility compared to sewer-based systems.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Kampala, Uganda, with ten drainage channel sampling points and 54 wastewater treatment plant influent samples collected during two campaigns in March and September-October 2025.
Core methods
A multi-target panel was quantified using digital PCR, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) among other pathogens, and samples were normalized by Pepper Mild Mottle Virus (PMMoV) as a fecal indicator.
Main results
Respiratory syncytial virus was rarely detected in both drainage channels and wastewater treatment plant influents, with concentrations varying across sampling locations.
Interpretation and novelty
The low detection rates of RSV suggest that it may not be as prevalent in the sampled non-sewered settings compared to other pathogens, but the study highlights the potential of drainage surveillance to provide spatially resolved public health data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that drainage-based surveillance could be a valuable tool for monitoring RSV in non-sewered urban areas, offering insights into disease prevalence at the neighborhood level.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are limited by the low detection rates of RSV, and the comparability of drainage and wastewater surveillance varied by location and pathogen, indicating the need for further targeted research.

指环病毒介导的干扰素失调会增强病毒引起的肺损伤。

Anellovirus-mediated interferon dysregulation enhances virus-induced lung injury.
作者： Mudassir M Banday, Shahid Banday, Mizanur Rahman, Amelia O Harrison, Namrata Singh, Ryan M Moore, Mohd Moin Khan, Shruti Shankar, Yasufumi Goda, Antonio Coppolino
摘要中文翻译目的:表征肺移植后的肺病毒组,并测试指环病毒是否调节先天 免疫应答,从而影响对呼吸道病毒相关同种异体移植损伤的易感性。基本原理:肺病毒组 (LV) 及其与 宿主 免疫系统的相互作用导致肺移植后同种异体移植物损伤,目前尚不清楚。方法:对肺移植受者 (LTR) 的多中心 BAL/血清 队列研究 进行鸟枪法宏基因组学和 qPCR。将来自 Betatorquevirus clade (BTV) 和 2 组扭力腱病毒的病毒构建体转染到接触或未接触甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 或 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的 C57BL/6 小鼠的原代支气管上皮细胞 (PBEC) 或气道中。测量和主要结果:LTR 中的 LV 以指环病毒科病毒为主。慢性同种异体肺移植功能障碍(CLAD)LV 的特点是 BTV 富集。独立 队列研究 的验证证实了 CLAD BAL 液体、血清和肺组织中 BTV 的丰度。BTV ORF1 蛋白 (kV1) 通过阻止核内 STAT 易位来抑制 PBEC IFN-α 和 IFN-γ 反应。在 kV1 转染的 PBEC 中共培养 IAV 或 RSV 显着增强了 RSV 和 IAV 的复制并增加了细胞损伤。同样,kV1 的体内转染增加了与 IAV 相关的复制和肺损伤。结论:我们的工作阐明了一种新型病毒相关的 宿主 干扰素反应失调,这种失调会促进与呼吸道病毒感染相关的肺损伤,并在一定程度上解释了肺移植后 宿主 对病毒感染反应的差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查指环病毒在调节先天 免疫应答 中的作用及其对肺移植后呼吸道病毒相关同种异体移植损伤易感性的潜在影响。基本原理:肺病毒组 (LV) 及其与 宿主 免疫系统的相互作用导致肺移植后同种异体移植物损伤,目前尚不清楚。
设计与对象
对肺移植受者 (LTR) 的多中心 BAL/血清 队列研究 进行鸟枪法宏基因组学和 qPCR。独立 队列研究 的验证证实了 CLAD BAL 液体、血清和肺组织中 BTV 的丰度。
核心方法
将来自 Betatorquevirus clade (BTV) 和 2 组扭力腱病毒的病毒构建体转染到接触或未接触甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 或 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的 C57BL/6 小鼠的原代支气管上皮细胞 (PBEC) 或气道中。慢性同种异体肺移植功能障碍(CLAD)LV 的特点是 BTV 富集。
主要结果
在 kV1 转染的 PBEC 中共培养 IAV 或 RSV 显着增强了 RSV 和 IAV 的复制并增加了细胞损伤。同样,kV1 的体内转染增加了与 IAV 相关的复制和肺损伤。
解释与创新
我们的工作阐明了一种新型病毒相关的 宿主 干扰素反应失调,这种失调会促进与呼吸道病毒感染相关的肺损伤,并在一定程度上解释了肺移植后 宿主 对病毒感染反应的差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,指环病毒介导的干扰素失调可能是病毒引起的肺损伤 发病机制 的关键因素,为肺移植受者的靶向治疗干预提供了潜在的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,结果基于体外和体内模型,可能无法完全代表人类状况。由于对这些模型的依赖以及缺乏长期临床结果,证据强度中等。

Anellovirus-mediated interferon dysregulation enhances virus-induced lung injury.

Authors: Mudassir M Banday, Shahid Banday, Mizanur Rahman, Amelia O Harrison, Namrata Singh, Ryan M Moore, Mohd Moin Khan, Shruti Shankar, Yasufumi Goda, Antonio Coppolino
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: To characterize the lung virome after lung transplantation and test whether anelloviruses modulate innate immune responses that influence susceptibility to respiratory viral-associated allograft injury. RATIONALE: The lung virome (LV) and its interactions with the host immune system leading to allograft injury after lung -transplantation are not well characterized. METHODS: Shotgun metagenomics and qPCR was performed on a multicenter BAL/serum cohort from lung transplant recipients (LTRs). Viral constructs from a Betatorquevirus clade (BTV) and group 2 torque teno viruses were transfected in primary bronchial epithelial cells (PBECs) or airways of C57BL/6 mice with and without exposure to influenza A virus (IAV) or respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). MEASUREMENTS AND MAIN RESULTS: LV in LTRs was dominated by viruses from the family Anelloviridae. Chronic lung allograft dysfunction (CLAD) LV was characterized by the enrichment of BTV. Validation in an independent cohort confirmed BTV abundance in CLAD BAL fluid, serum, and lung tissues. BTV ORF1 protein (kV1) suppressed PBEC IFN-α and IFN-γ responses by preventing intranuclear STAT translocation. Co-culture of IAV or RSV in kV1-transfected PBECs significantly augmented replication of RSV and IAV and increased cellular injury. Likewise, in vivo transfection of kV1 increased replication and lung injury associated with IAV. CONCLUSIONS: Our work illuminates a novel virus-associated dysregulation of host interferon responses that promote lung injury associated with respiratory viral infections and, in part, explain differential host responses to viral infections after lung transplantation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of anelloviruses in modulating innate immune responses and their potential impact on susceptibility to respiratory viral-associated allograft injury after lung transplantation. RATIONALE: The lung virome (LV) and its interactions with the host immune system leading to allograft injury after lung transplantation are not well characterized.
Design and population
Shotgun metagenomics and qPCR were performed on a multicenter BAL/serum cohort from lung transplant recipients (LTRs). Validation in an independent cohort confirmed BTV abundance in CLAD BAL fluid, serum, and lung tissues.
Core methods
Viral constructs from a Betatorquevirus clade (BTV) and group 2 torque teno viruses were transfected in primary bronchial epithelial cells (PBECs) or airways of C57BL/6 mice with and without exposure to influenza A virus (IAV) or respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Chronic lung allograft dysfunction (CLAD) LV was characterized by the enrichment of BTV.
Main results
Co-culture of IAV or RSV in kV1-transfected PBECs significantly augmented replication of RSV and IAV and increased cellular injury. Likewise, in vivo transfection of kV1 increased replication and lung injury associated with IAV.
Interpretation and novelty
Our work illuminates a novel virus-associated dysregulation of host interferon responses that promote lung injury associated with respiratory viral infections and, in part, explain differential host responses to viral infections after lung transplantation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that anellovirus-mediated interferon dysregulation could be a key factor in the pathogenesis of virus-induced lung injury, offering potential insights for targeted therapeutic interventions in lung transplant recipients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent human conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on these models and the absence of long-term clinical outcomes.

了解加拿大安大略省 COVID-19 后 RSV 卷土重来:评估接触模式和母体免疫力的作用

Understanding RSV Resurgence Following COVID-19 in Ontario, Canada: Evaluating the Roles of Contact Patterns and Maternal Immunity
作者： Alyssa Parpia, James Wright, Ali Gharouni, Nisha Thampi, Tiffany Fitzpatrick
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是婴儿期住院的主要原因,其严重后果受到接触模式和被动免疫的影响。COVID-19 大流行期间的非药物干预措施 (NPI) 抑制了 RSV 循环,减少了母体免疫增强的机会,可能会改变对新生儿的保护。我们评估了纳入随时间变化的母体免疫力是否可以提高年龄结构的 传播 模型预测婴儿大流行后 RSV 住院模式的能力。方法:我们使用链接的管理数据库分析了 2017 年 7 月 2 日至 2024 年 6 月 25 日期间安大略省(加拿大)婴儿(<1 岁)中基于人群的 RSV 住院情况。使用拉丁超立方抽样法根据大流行前的数据校准了七个年龄段的确定性分区模型。我们将仅包含随时间变化的接触率的模型与另外包含随时间变化的母体免疫力的规范进行了比较。结果:这两个规范准确地再现了大流行前的季节性和大流行后的宏观复苏特征。与时变母体免疫规范相比,恒定母体免疫模型在捕获 2021/22 峰值方面显示出稍高的准确性。然而,两者都定性地捕捉到了 RSV 持续接近不存在的情况,并且观察到的峰值是在 95% 的可信区间内捕捉到的。虽然这两个模型准确地捕捉到了 2022/23 年发生的招生时间和压倒性的招生激增,但它们未能捕捉到 2023/24 年过早的高峰时间和规模。结论:纳入随时间变化的母体免疫力并没有提高大流行后模型的准确性。虽然孕产妇保护对于评估婴儿免疫接种至关重要,但人口水平的接触变化主要影响了大流行后 RSV 的季节性,这表明模型必须考虑 RSV 传播 动态的这些机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是婴儿期住院的主要原因,其严重后果受到接触模式和被动免疫的影响。COVID-19 大流行期间的非药物干预措施 (NPI) 抑制了 RSV 循环,减少了母体免疫增强的机会,可能会改变对新生儿的保护。
设计与对象
我们使用链接的管理数据库分析了 2017 年 7 月 2 日至 2024 年 6 月 25 日期间安大略省(加拿大)婴儿(<1 岁)中基于人群的 RSV 住院情况。我们将仅包含随时间变化的接触率的模型与另外包含随时间变化的母体免疫力的规范进行了比较。
核心方法
使用拉丁超立方抽样法根据大流行前的数据校准了七个年龄段的确定性分区模型。我们评估了纳入随时间变化的母体免疫力是否可以提高年龄结构的 传播 模型预测婴儿大流行后 RSV 住院模式的能力。
主要结果
这两个规范准确地再现了大流行前的季节性和大流行后的宏观复苏特征。与时变母体免疫规范相比,恒定母体免疫模型在捕获 2021/22 峰值方面显示出稍高的准确性。然而,两者都定性地捕捉到了 RSV 持续接近不存在的情况,并且观察到的峰值是在 95% 的可信区间内捕捉到的。虽然这两个模型准确地捕捉到了 2022/23 年发生的招生时间和压倒性的招生激增,但它们未能捕捉到 2023/24 年过早的高峰时间和规模。
解释与创新
纳入随时间变化的母体免疫力并没有提高大流行后模型的准确性。虽然孕产妇保护对于评估婴儿免疫接种至关重要,但人口水平的接触变化主要影响了大流行后 RSV 的季节性,这表明模型必须考虑 RSV 传播 动态的这些机制。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,人口层面的接触模式,而不是母体免疫力,是大流行后 RSV 季节性的主要驱动因素。这凸显了将接触动力学纳入预测和管理婴儿 RSV 暴发 模型的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,可能无法完全捕捉模型校准和验证的细微差别。证据强度中等,因为结果基于模型输出而不是直接的经验观察。

Understanding RSV Resurgence Following COVID-19 in Ontario, Canada: Evaluating the Roles of Contact Patterns and Maternal Immunity

Authors: Alyssa Parpia, James Wright, Ali Gharouni, Nisha Thampi, Tiffany Fitzpatrick
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a leading cause of hospitalization in infancy, with severe outcomes influenced by both contact patterns and passive immunity. Non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) during the COVID-19 pandemic suppressed RSV circulation and reduced opportunities for maternal immune boosting, potentially altering protection among newborns. We evaluated whether incorporating time-varying maternal immunity improves the ability of an age-structured transmission model to predict post-pandemic RSV hospitalization patterns in infants. Methods: We analyzed population-based RSV hospitalizations among Ontario (Canada) infants (<1 year) from July 2, 2017 to June 25, 2024, using linked administrative databases. A deterministic compartmental model across seven age classes was calibrated against pre-pandemic data using Latin Hypercube Sampling. We compared a model incorporating time-varying contact rates alone against a specification that additionally included time-varying maternal immunity. Results: Both specifications accurately reproduced pre-pandemic seasonality and macro-level post-pandemic resurgence features. The constant maternal immunity model showed slightly better accuracy in capturing the 2021/22 peak compared to the time-varying maternal immunity specification. However, both qualitatively captured the continued near-absence of RSV and the observed peak was captured within the 95% credible intervals. While both models precisely captured the timing and overwhelming surge of admissions that occurred in 2022/23, they failed to capture the premature peak timing and magnitude in 2023/24. Conclusions: Incorporating time-varying maternal immunity did not improve model accuracy post-pandemic. While maternal protection is essential for evaluating infant immunizations, population-level contact shifts primarily shaped post-pandemic RSV seasonality, indicating that models must account for these mechanisms of RSV transmission dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a leading cause of hospitalization in infancy, with severe outcomes influenced by both contact patterns and passive immunity. Non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) during the COVID-19 pandemic suppressed RSV circulation and reduced opportunities for maternal immune boosting, potentially altering protection among newborns.
Design and population
We analyzed population-based RSV hospitalizations among Ontario (Canada) infants (<1 year) from July 2, 2017 to June 25, 2024, using linked administrative databases. We compared a model incorporating time-varying contact rates alone against a specification that additionally included time-varying maternal immunity.
Core methods
A deterministic compartmental model across seven age classes was calibrated against pre-pandemic data using Latin Hypercube Sampling. We evaluated whether incorporating time-varying maternal immunity improves the ability of an age-structured transmission model to predict post-pandemic RSV hospitalization patterns in infants.
Main results
Both specifications accurately reproduced pre-pandemic seasonality and macro-level post-pandemic resurgence features. The constant maternal immunity model showed slightly better accuracy in capturing the 2021/22 peak compared to the time-varying maternal immunity specification. However, both qualitatively captured the continued near-absence of RSV and the observed peak was captured within the 95% credible intervals. While both models precisely captured the timing and overwhelming surge of admissions that occurred in 2022/23, they failed to capture the premature peak timing and magnitude in 2023/24.
Interpretation and novelty
Incorporating time-varying maternal immunity did not improve model accuracy post-pandemic. While maternal protection is essential for evaluating infant immunizations, population-level contact shifts primarily shaped post-pandemic RSV seasonality, indicating that models must account for these mechanisms of RSV transmission dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that population-level contact patterns, rather than maternal immunity, were the primary drivers of post-pandemic RSV seasonality. This highlights the importance of incorporating contact dynamics into models for predicting and managing RSV outbreaks in infants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, which may not fully capture the nuances of model calibration and validation. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are based on model outputs rather than direct empirical observations.
