预测下周:根据公开 监测 数据对州级呼吸道疾病升级的校准预测

Anticipating the Next Week: Calibrated Prediction of State-Level Respiratory Illness Escalation from Public Surveillance Data
作者： Tianyi Yu
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 呼吸道病毒 监测 团队需要早期、可靠且低负担的活动恶化信号。目前的仪表板很好地描述了近期的活动,但它们的设计不太直接地对已经处于中度流感样疾病 (ILI) 活动的辖区进行排名,并且可能在下一个报告周进入高活动度。方法 我们使用来自 CDC ILINEt 的美国公共门诊 ILI 监测(通过 Delphi Epidata FluView 访问)以及来自 CDC NREVSS 的区域 RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 核酸检测阳性结果,进行了一项回顾性州周预测研究。目标是从 t 周的中等状态特定 ILI 活动过渡到 t + 1 周的高活动。状态特定阈值、预处理、超参数调整、概率校准和警报阈值选择仅根据开发数据进行估计。通过滚动源交叉验证来选择候选模型,并在稍后按时间顺序保留时进行一次评估。保留的不确定性通过状态簇引导置信区间进行量化。结果 建模 队列研究 包含来自 51 个司法管辖区的 4,232 个中等州周。坚持期包含 943 个状态周和 127 个中度到高度的转换。所选校准逻辑回归模型的 AUROC 0.877、平均精度 0.529、F1 0.594、精度 0.589、召回率 0.598 和特异性 0.935。它发出了 129 个警报并识别了 127 个转换中的 76 个,而警报所有中等基线识别了所有转换,但需要 943 个警报。最有用的信号是接近州特定的高活动阈值、季节性时间、近期流感样病例历史、报告量以及滞后的 RSV/SARS-CoV-2 阳性率。结论 简单的校准 监测 模型减轻了警报负担,同时保留了大多数短期升级。该研究应被解释为呼吸系统 监测 分类研究的透明基准,而不是部署的公共卫生决策规则。它的贡献是一个评估框架,结合了时间交叉验证、独立时间测试、引导不确定性、校准、阈值分析和明确报告真正的外部验证仍然不可用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒 监测 团队需要早期、可靠且低负担的活动恶化信号。目前的仪表板很好地描述了近期的活动,但它们的设计不太直接地对已经处于中度流感样疾病 (ILI) 活动的辖区进行排名,并且可能在下一个报告周进入高活动度。
设计与对象
我们利用来自 CDC ILINEt 的美国公立门诊 ILI 监测(通过 Delphi Epidata FluView 访问)以及来自 CDC NREVSS 的区域 RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 核酸检测阳性结果,进行了一项回顾性州周预测研究。目标是从 t 周的中度州特定 ILI 活动过渡到 t + 1 周的高活动。
核心方法
仅根据开发数据估计特定于状态的阈值、预处理、超参数调整、概率校准和警报阈值选择。通过滚动源交叉验证来选择候选模型,并在稍后按时间顺序保留时进行一次评估。保留的不确定性通过状态簇引导置信区间进行量化。
主要结果
建模 队列研究 包含来自 51 个司法管辖区的 4,232 个中等州周。坚持期包含 943 个状态周和 127 个中度到高度的转换。所选校准逻辑回归模型的 AUROC 0.877、平均精度 0.529、F1 0.594、精度 0.589、召回率 0.598 和特异性 0.935。
解释与创新
简单的校准 监测 模型减轻了警报负担,同时保留了大多数短期升级。最有用的信号是接近州特定的高活动阈值、季节性时间、近期流感样病例历史、报告量以及滞后的 RSV/SARS-CoV-2 阳性率。
科研与公卫意义
该研究为呼吸道 监测 分类研究提供了透明的基准,可以为开发更有效的呼吸道病毒 监测 系统提供信息。它强调了校准模型在减轻警报负担、同时保持预测活动升级的准确性方面的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究应被解释为呼吸系统 监测 分类研究的透明基准,而不是部署的公共卫生决策规则。它的贡献是一个评估框架,结合了时间交叉验证、独立时间测试、引导不确定性、校准、阈值分析和明确报告真正的外部验证仍然不可用。

Anticipating the Next Week: Calibrated Prediction of State-Level Respiratory Illness Escalation from Public Surveillance Data

Authors: Tianyi Yu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Respiratory-virus surveillance teams need early, credible, and low-burden signals of worsening activity. Current dashboards describe recent activity well, but they are less directly designed to rank jurisdictions that are already at moderate influenza-like illness (ILI) activity and may cross into high activity in the next reporting week. Methods We conducted a retrospective state-week prediction study using public U.S. outpatient ILI surveillance from CDC ILINet, accessed through Delphi Epidata FluView, and regional RSV and SARS-CoV-2 nucleic-acid test positivity from CDC NREVSS. The target was transition from moderate state-specific ILI activity at week t to high activity at week t + 1. State-specific thresholds, preprocessing, hyperparameter tuning, probability calibration, and alert-threshold selection were estimated from development data only. Candidate models were selected by rolling-origin cross-validation and evaluated once on a later chronological holdout. Held-out uncertainty was quantified with state-cluster bootstrap confidence intervals. Results The modelling cohort contained 4,232 moderate state-weeks from 51 jurisdictions. The holdout period contained 943 state-weeks and 127 moderate-to-high transitions. The selected calibrated logistic regression model achieved AUROC 0.877, average precision 0.529, F1 0.594, precision 0.589, recall 0.598, and specificity 0.935. It issued 129 alerts and identified 76 of 127 transitions, whereas an alert-all-moderate baseline identified all transitions but required 943 alerts. The most useful signals were proximity to the state-specific high-activity threshold, seasonal timing, recent ILI history, reporting volume, and lagged RSV/SARS-CoV-2 positivity. Conclusions A simple calibrated surveillance model reduced alert burden while retaining most short-horizon escalations. The study should be interpreted as a transparent benchmark for respiratory-surveillance triage research, not as a deployed public health decision rule. Its contribution is an evaluation framework that combines temporal cross-validation, independent temporal testing, bootstrap uncertainty, calibration, threshold analysis, and explicit reporting that true external validation remains unavailable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory-virus surveillance teams need early, credible, and low-burden signals of worsening activity. Current dashboards describe recent activity well, but they are less directly designed to rank jurisdictions that are already at moderate influenza-like illness (ILI) activity and may cross into high activity in the next reporting week.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective state-week prediction study using public U.S. outpatient ILI surveillance from CDC ILINet, accessed through Delphi Epidata FluView, and regional RSV and SARS-CoV-2 nucleic-acid test positivity from CDC NREVSS. The target was transition from moderate state-specific ILI activity at week t to high activity at week t + 1.
Core methods
State-specific thresholds, preprocessing, hyperparameter tuning, probability calibration, and alert-threshold selection were estimated from development data only. Candidate models were selected by rolling-origin cross-validation and evaluated once on a later chronological holdout. Held-out uncertainty was quantified with state-cluster bootstrap confidence intervals.
Main results
The modelling cohort contained 4,232 moderate state-weeks from 51 jurisdictions. The holdout period contained 943 state-weeks and 127 moderate-to-high transitions. The selected calibrated logistic regression model achieved AUROC 0.877, average precision 0.529, F1 0.594, precision 0.589, recall 0.598, and specificity 0.935.
Interpretation and novelty
A simple calibrated surveillance model reduced alert burden while retaining most short-horizon escalations. The most useful signals were proximity to the state-specific high-activity threshold, seasonal timing, recent ILI history, reporting volume, and lagged RSV/SARS-CoV-2 positivity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides a transparent benchmark for respiratory-surveillance triage research, which can inform the development of more efficient surveillance systems for respiratory viruses. It highlights the importance of calibrated models in reducing alert burden while maintaining accuracy in predicting activity escalation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study should be interpreted as a transparent benchmark for respiratory-surveillance triage research, not as a deployed public health decision rule. Its contribution is an evaluation framework that combines temporal cross-validation, independent temporal testing, bootstrap uncertainty, calibration, threshold analysis, and explicit reporting that true external validation remains unavailable.

美国婴儿 RSV 预防策略的有效性:2024-2025 年。

Effectiveness of RSV Prevention Strategies in US Infants: 2024-2025.
作者： Amanda B Payne, Steph Battan-Wraith, Sarah E Reese, Cassandra A Hathaway, Sara Y Tartof, Karthik Natarajan, Stephanie A Irving, Shaun Grannis, Sean Chickery, Gabriela Vazquez-Benitez
摘要中文翻译目标:2023年,美国推荐长效单克隆抗体(尼塞韦单抗)和母体呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)疫苗接种这2种产品在婴儿第一个RSV季节预防严重RSV疾病。2023 年至 2024 年 RSV 季节期间使用率较低,限制了有效性估计。我们估计了 2024 年至 2025 年 RSV 季节期间,尼塞韦单抗 和母体 RSV 疫苗 对美国婴儿与 RSV 相关的急诊室 (ED) 接触和住院治疗的有效性,当时的摄入量较高。方法:我们使用来自 5 个医疗保健系统的电子健康记录数据,对 2024 年 10 月 1 日至 2025 年 3 月 31 日期间,在第一个 RSV 季节的婴儿中,对 ED 遭遇和因 RSV 样疾病住院的情况进行测试阴性分析。尼塞韦单抗 和母亲 RSV 疫苗 的有效性是通过比较 RSV 阳性和 RSV 阴性的情况来估计的,这些情况是在单独的逻辑回归模型中针对年龄、种族和民族、性别、日历日和地理区域进行调整后的免疫状态。结果:在 尼塞韦单抗 分析中纳入的 3531 例急诊就诊和 470 例住院治疗中,尼塞韦单抗 针对 RSV 相关急诊就诊和住院的有效性分别为 62%(95% CI:54-68)和 77%(95% CI:61-87)。在母体 RSV 疫苗 分析中纳入的 810 例 ED 就诊和 187 例住院治疗中,疫苗 针对 RSV 相关 ED 就诊和住院的有效性分别为 49% (95% CI: 26-65) 和 82% (95% CI: 54-93)。结论:尼塞韦单抗 和母体 RSV 疫苗接种 对于第一个 RSV 季节婴儿中与 RSV 相关的 ED 事件和住院治疗有效。随着吸收量的增加和其他产品的推荐,监测婴儿 RSV 免疫效果仍然很重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)预防策略的有效性,具体包括尼塞韦单抗(尼塞韦单抗)和孕妇呼吸道合胞病毒疫苗(maternal RSV 疫苗),以减少美国婴儿在首个RSV流行季期间因RSV相关急诊就诊和住院的发生率。背景部分强调,这两种产品于2023年被推荐使用,但在2023至2024流行季期间的采用率有限,影响了对其有效性的评估能力。
设计与对象
该研究使用 2024 年至 2025 年 RSV 季节期间来自 5 个医疗保健系统的电子健康记录数据进行,重点关注第一个 RSV 季节的婴儿。该人群包括 3531 例急诊科就诊病例和 470 例因 RSV 类疾病住院的病例,并对 尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇 RSV 疫苗 有效性进行了单独分析。
核心方法
通过对急诊科就诊和因 RSV 样疾病住院治疗的测试阴性分析,根据免疫状态比较 RSV 阳性和 RSV 阴性病例来评估有效性。使用逻辑回归模型,调整年龄、种族和民族、性别、日历日和地理区域。
主要结果
尼塞韦单抗 对 RSV 相关 ED 患者的有效率为 62% (95% CI: 54-68),对住院治疗的有效率为 77% (95% CI: 61-87)。母亲 RSV 疫苗 对抗 ED 的有效性为 49% (95% CI: 26-65),对抗住院治疗的有效性为 82% (95% CI: 54-93)。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为尼塞韦单抗和母体RSV 疫苗接种在预防第一个RSV季节婴儿中与RSV相关的ED遭遇和住院治疗方面的有效性的证据,与母体疫苗相比,尼塞韦单抗在预防ED遭遇方面表现出更高的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,随着吸收率的增加和推荐其他产品,监测婴儿 RSV 免疫效果的重要性,为公共卫生策略和临床决策提供关键数据。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于对电子健康记录数据的依赖,可能存在偏倚或缺失信息。然而,由于采用了检测阴性分析并针对关键人口统计学和时间因素进行了调整,证据强度较高,支持中等置信度评估。

Effectiveness of RSV Prevention Strategies in US Infants: 2024-2025.

Authors: Amanda B Payne, Steph Battan-Wraith, Sarah E Reese, Cassandra A Hathaway, Sara Y Tartof, Karthik Natarajan, Stephanie A Irving, Shaun Grannis, Sean Chickery, Gabriela Vazquez-Benitez
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: In 2023, 2 products, a long-acting monoclonal antibody (nirsevimab) and maternal respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination, were recommended in the United States to prevent severe RSV disease among infants in their first RSV season. Low uptake during the 2023 to 2024 RSV season limited effectiveness estimates. We estimated nirsevimab and maternal RSV vaccine effectiveness against RSV-associated emergency department (ED) encounters and hospitalization among US infants during the 2024 to 2025 RSV season, when uptake was higher. METHODS: We used electronic health record data from 5 health care systems in test-negative analyses of ED encounters and hospitalizations with RSV-like illness during October 1, 2024 to March 31, 2025, among infants in their first RSV season. Nirsevimab and maternal RSV vaccine effectiveness were estimated by comparing RSV-positive with RSV-negative encounters with respect to immunization status in separate logistic regression models adjusted for age, race and ethnicity, sex, calendar day, and geographic region. RESULTS: Among 3531 ED encounters and 470 hospitalizations included in the nirsevimab analyses, nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV-associated ED encounters and hospitalization was 62% (95% CI: 54-68) and 77% (95% CI: 61-87), respectively. Among 810 ED encounters and 187 hospitalizations included in the maternal RSV vaccine analyses, vaccine effectiveness against RSV-associated ED encounters and hospitalization was 49% (95% CI: 26-65) and 82% (95% CI: 54-93), respectively. CONCLUSION: Nirsevimab and maternal RSV vaccination were effective against RSV-associated ED encounters and hospitalization among infants in their first RSV season. As uptake increases and additional products are recommended, monitoring infant RSV immunization effectiveness remains important.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of RSV prevention strategies, specifically nirsevimab and maternal RSV vaccination, in reducing RSV-associated emergency department encounters and hospitalizations among US infants during their first RSV season. The background highlights that these two products were recommended in 2023 but had limited uptake during the 2023 to 2024 season, which affected the ability to estimate their effectiveness.
Design and population
The study was conducted using electronic health record data from 5 healthcare systems during the 2024 to 2025 RSV season, focusing on infants in their first RSV season. The population included 3531 emergency department encounters and 470 hospitalizations for RSV-like illness, with separate analyses for nirsevimab and maternal RSV vaccine effectiveness.
Core methods
Effectiveness was estimated through test-negative analyses of ED encounters and hospitalizations with RSV-like illness, comparing RSV-positive with RSV-negative cases based on immunization status. Logistic regression models were used, adjusting for age, race and ethnicity, sex, calendar day, and geographic region.
Main results
Nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV-associated ED encounters was 62% (95% CI: 54-68), and against hospitalization was 77% (95% CI: 61-87). Maternal RSV vaccine effectiveness against ED encounters was 49% (95% CI: 26-65), and against hospitalization was 82% (95% CI: 54-93).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these results as evidence of the effectiveness of both nirsevimab and maternal RSV vaccination in preventing RSV-associated ED encounters and hospitalizations among infants in their first RSV season, with nirsevimab showing higher effectiveness against ED encounters compared to the maternal vaccine.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the importance of monitoring infant RSV immunization effectiveness as uptake increases and additional products are recommended, providing critical data for public health strategies and clinical decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on electronic health record data, which may have biases or missing information. However, the evidence is strong given the use of test-negative analyses and adjustment for key demographic and temporal factors, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

PIWIL4 调节 RSV 感染的气道上皮细胞的基因表达和 piRNA 水平

PIWIL4 regulates gene expression and piRNA levels in RSV-infected airway epithelial cells
作者： Tiziana Corsello, Tianshuang Liu, Andrzej S. Kudlicki, Yuanyi Zhang, Nicholas Dillman, Sehar Fazal, Roberto P. Garofalo, Antonella Casola
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因,在幼儿、老年人和免疫功能低下的 宿主 中具有显着的发病率和 死亡率。尽管有临床负担,但对于婴儿尚无有效的 RSV 疫苗 或疗法,只有预防性 治疗。小非编码 RNA 已成为 宿主 与病原体相互作用的重要调节因子。PIWI 相互作用 RNA (piRNA) 是一类独特的小非编码 RNA,以维持性腺细胞中 基因组 的复杂性和完整性而闻名。然而,越来越多的证据表明它们在控制体细胞基因表达方面的作用。piRNA 的生物发生和功能与 P 元素诱导的 Wimpy 睾丸 (Piwi) 蛋白相关,其在呼吸道上皮细胞中响应感染的功能在很大程度上尚未被探索。在这里,我们描述了 Piwi 样蛋白 PIWIL4 在 RSV 感染背景下的表达和功能。我们发现 PIWIL4 在原代和永生化小气道上皮细胞中表达,并且在 RSV 感染或 Poly I:C 刺激(病毒感染的代表)后在 mRNA 和蛋白质水平上显着诱导。免疫荧光显微镜显示,PIWIL4 在未感染的细胞中主要位于细胞核,但在接触 RSV 后易位至细胞质。虽然 siRNA 介导的 PIWIL4 敲低并未显着影响 RSV 复制,但它导致多种细胞因子、趋化因子和生长因子的分泌减少,表明在调节 宿主 先天 免疫应答 中发挥作用。PIWIL4 沉默的 iSAE 细胞的转录组分析显示,在基础条件下和 RSV 感染后基因表达均发生显着变化。Ingenuity 对差异表达基因的通路分析强调了 PIWIL4 在干扰素信号传导、细胞因子产生、应激和代谢反应以及气道重塑通路调节中的作用。PIWIL4 的沉默还导致未感染和感染细胞中 piRNA 表达的整体改变。然而,差异表达piRNA的预测靶标与转录组学鉴定的差异表达基因的重叠有限,这表明PIWIL4在调节气道上皮细胞反应中的功能至少部分独立于piRNA。综上所述,我们的研究揭示了 PIWIL4 在体细胞中的重要作用,并将其定位为气道上皮先天免疫的关键调节因子。更好地了解 PIWIL4 在病原体暴露后影响 宿主 细胞反应的机制可能会确定针对 RSV 以及其他病毒性呼吸道感染的新治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因,在幼儿、老年人和免疫功能低下的 宿主 中具有显着的发病率和 死亡率。尽管有临床负担,但对于婴儿尚无有效的 RSV 疫苗 或疗法,只有预防性 治疗。
设计与对象
该研究表征了 Piwi 样蛋白 PIWIL4 在 RSV 感染情况下的表达和功能。它专注于原代和永生化的小气道上皮细胞,检查基线和感染状况。
核心方法
该研究使用 siRNA 介导的 PIWIL4 敲低来评估其对 RSV 复制和 宿主 免疫应答 的影响。进行转录组分析以评估 PIWIL4 沉默细胞中的基因表达变化,并使用免疫荧光显微镜观察 PIWIL4 定位。
主要结果
PIWIL4 在原代和永生化小气道上皮细胞中表达,并在 RSV 感染或 Poly I:C 刺激后在 mRNA 和蛋白质水平上显着诱导。免疫荧光显微镜显示,PIWIL4 在未感染的细胞中主要位于细胞核,但在接触 RSV 后易位至细胞质。转录组分析显示,在基础条件下和 RSV 感染后,基因表达均发生显着变化。
解释与创新
该研究强调了 PIWIL4 在调节 宿主 先天 免疫应答 和气道上皮细胞反应中的作用,至少部分独立于 piRNA。它将 PIWIL4 定位为气道上皮先天免疫的关键调节因子。
科研与公卫意义
了解 PIWIL4 在病原体暴露后影响 宿主 细胞反应的机制可能有助于确定 RSV 和其他病毒性呼吸道感染的新治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于使用 siRNA 介导的敲低的体外实验,这可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据是中等的,因为它来自一项直接临床结果有限的研究。

PIWIL4 regulates gene expression and piRNA levels in RSV-infected airway epithelial cells

Authors: Tiziana Corsello, Tianshuang Liu, Andrzej S. Kudlicki, Yuanyi Zhang, Nicholas Dillman, Sehar Fazal, Roberto P. Garofalo, Antonella Casola
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory tract infections with significant morbidity and mortality in young children, the elderly and immunocompromised hosts. Despite its clinical burden, no effective RSV vaccine or therapy exists for infants, only prophylactic treatment. Small non-coding RNAs have emerged as important regulators of host-pathogen interactions. PIWI-interacting RNAs (piRNAs) are a distinct class of small non-coding RNAs known for maintaining the genome complexity and integrity in gonadal cells. However, there is growing evidence of their role in controlling gene expression in somatic cells. The biogenesis and function of piRNAs is associated with P-element Induced Wimpy testis (Piwi) proteins, whose function in the respiratory epithelium in response to infections remains largely unexplored. Here, we characterize the expression and function of the Piwi-like protein PIWIL4 in the context of RSV infection. We found that PIWIL4 is expressed in both primary and immortalized small airway epithelial cells and is significantly induced at the mRNA and protein levels following RSV infection or poly I:C stimulation, a proxy of viral infection. Immunofluorescence microscopy revealed that PIWIL4 was primarily nuclear in uninfected cells but translocated to the cytoplasm upon RSV exposure. While siRNA-mediated knockdown of PIWIL4 did not significantly affect RSV replication, it led to decreased secretion of several cytokines, chemokines and growth factors, indicating a role in modulating host innate immune responses. Transcriptomic analysis of PIWIL4-silenced iSAE cells showed significant changes in gene expression both in basal conditions and upon RSV infection. Ingenuity Pathway analysis of differentially expressed genes underscored the role of PIWIL4 in modulation of interferon signaling, cytokine production, stress and metabolic responses, as well as airway remodeling pathways. Silencing of PIWIL4 also resulted in global alteration of piRNA expression both in uninfected and infected cells. However, the predicted targets of the differentially expressed piRNAs had limited overlap with the differentially expressed genes identified by transcriptomics, suggesting a function of PIWIL4 in regulating airway epithelial cell responses at least in part independent of piRNAs. Taken together, our study uncovers an important role for PIWIL4 in somatic cells and position it as a key regulator of airway epithelial innate immunity. A better understanding of the mechanisms by which PIWIL4 affects host cells responses following a pathogen exposure may identify novel therapeutic strategies for RSV, as well as other viral respiratory infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory tract infections with significant morbidity and mortality in young children, the elderly and immunocompromised hosts. Despite its clinical burden, no effective RSV vaccine or therapy exists for infants, only prophylactic treatment.
Design and population
The study characterized the expression and function of the Piwi-like protein PIWIL4 in the context of RSV infection. It focused on primary and immortalized small airway epithelial cells, examining both baseline and infected conditions.
Core methods
The study used siRNA-mediated knockdown of PIWIL4 to assess its impact on RSV replication and host immune responses. Transcriptomic analysis was performed to evaluate gene expression changes in PIWIL4-silenced cells, and immunofluorescence microscopy was used to observe PIWIL4 localization.
Main results
PIWIL4 is expressed in both primary and immortalized small airway epithelial cells and is significantly induced at the mRNA and protein levels following RSV infection or poly I:C stimulation. Immunofluorescence microscopy revealed that PIWIL4 was primarily nuclear in uninfected cells but translocated to the cytoplasm upon RSV exposure. Transcriptomic analysis showed significant changes in gene expression both in basal conditions and upon RSV infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the role of PIWIL4 in modulating host innate immune responses and airway epithelial cell responses, at least in part independent of piRNAs. It positions PIWIL4 as a key regulator of airway epithelial innate immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
Understanding the mechanisms by which PIWIL4 affects host cell responses following pathogen exposure may lead to the identification of novel therapeutic strategies for RSV and other viral respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in vitro experiments using siRNA-mediated knockdown, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate as it is derived from a single study with limited direct clinical outcomes.

Clesrovimab 预防 RSV 下呼吸道疾病:其在婴儿中的应用概况

Clesrovimab in prevention of RSV lower respiratory tract disease: a profile of its use in infants
作者： Michael B. Brown, Yahiya Y. Syed
摘要中文翻译Clesrovimab (ENFLONSIATM,clesrovimab-cfor) 是一种针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 融合蛋白的长效单克隆抗体,对于在第一个 RSV 季节期间出生或进入其第一个 RSV 季节的新生儿和婴儿来说,是 RSV 相关下呼吸道 (LRT) 疾病预防选择的有用补充。正如 2b/3 期 CLEVER 临床试验所证明的那样,无论体重如何,单次 105 mg 剂量的 clesrovimab 都可以针对 RSV 相关的医疗下呼吸道感染 (RSV-MALRI) 提供持久的保护,并减少在第一个 RSV 季节出生的新生儿和婴儿与 RSV 相关的住院治疗。3 期 SMART 试验的药代动力学数据支持 clesrovimab 对严重 RSV 疾病风险增加的婴儿和儿童的疗效。Clesrovimab 通常耐受性良好,反应原性低,并且与安慰剂 (CLEVER) 和帕利珠单抗 (SMART) 具有相似的不良事件特征。呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿住院和死亡的一个重要原因。Clesrovimab (ENFLONSIATM) 是一种新型长效单克隆抗体 (mAb),可结合 RSV 融合蛋白上高度保守的位点,最近在美国和欧盟获得批准,用于预防在第一个 RSV 季节期间出生的新生儿和婴儿与 RSV 相关的下呼吸道 (LRT) 疾病。临床试验表明,无论体重如何,单次 105 mg 剂量的 clesrovimab 都能为新生儿和在第一个 RSV 季节出生或进入其第一个 RSV 季节的婴儿提供针对 RSV 疾病的持久保护。3 期临床试验的数据支持 clesrovimab 对患有严重 RSV 疾病风险增加的 2 岁以下婴儿和儿童的疗效。Clesrovimab 耐受性良好,不良事件发生率低。作为第二种被批准用于预防 RSV 疾病的长效单克隆抗体,clesrovimab 代表了 治疗 选项的有用补充,适用于在第一个 RSV 季节出生的新生儿和婴儿。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估克雷索单抗(clesrovimab)作为预防新生儿和婴儿首个呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)季节期间RSV相关下呼吸道疾病的潜在预防性选择的有效性。
设计与对象
该研究聚焦于首个RSV季节期间出生或进入首个RSV季节的婴幼儿的临床试验,无论体重如何,均给予单次105 mg剂量的克雷索单抗。
核心方法
clesrovimab 的疗效通过 2b/3 期临床试验进行评估,3 期试验的药代动力学数据支持其预防 RSV 疾病的有效性。
主要结果
单次 105 毫克剂量的 clesrovimab 可针对 RSV 相关的医疗下呼吸道感染提供持久保护,并减少目标人群的住院率。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,clesrovimab 是一种新型长效单克隆抗体,可有效预防高危婴儿的 RSV 疾病,为 治疗 提供了新的选择。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,clesrovimab 可能是新生儿和婴儿 RSV 疾病预防策略的一个有价值的补充,特别是在他们的第一个 RSV 季节。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于临床试验,其普遍性可能存在局限性,但结果得到持久保护和住院治疗减少的支持,表明有中等信心。

Clesrovimab in prevention of RSV lower respiratory tract disease: a profile of its use in infants

Authors: Michael B. Brown, Yahiya Y. Syed
Original abstract (English)
Clesrovimab (ENFLONSIATM, clesrovimab-cfor), a long-acting monoclonal antibody that targets the respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) fusion protein, is a useful addition to prophylactic options for RSV-associated lower respiratory tract (LRT) disease in neonates and infants born during or entering their first RSV season. As demonstrated in the phase 2b/3 CLEVER clinical trial, a single 105 mg dose of clesrovimab, irrespective of body weight, provides durable protection against RSV-associated medically-attended lower respiratory infection (RSV-MALRI), and reduces RSV-associated hospitalizations in neonates and infants born during or entering their first RSV season. Pharmacokinetic data from the phase 3 SMART trial supports the efficacy of clesrovimab in infants and children at increased risk of severe RSV disease. Clesrovimab was generally well tolerated, with low reactogenicity, and had a similar adverse event profile to placebo (CLEVER) and palivizumab (SMART). Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a significant cause of infant hospitalizations and deaths. Clesrovimab (ENFLONSIATM), a novel long-acting monoclonal antibody (mAb) that binds a highly conserved site on the RSV fusion protein, was recently approved in the USA and the EU for the prevention of RSV-associated lower respiratory tract (LRT) disease in neonates and infants born during or entering their first RSV season. As demonstrated in clinical trials, clesrovimab, administered as a single 105 mg dose regardless of body weight, provides durable protection against RSV disease in neonates and infants born during or entering their first RSV season. Data from a phase 3 clinical trial supports the efficacy of clesrovimab in infants and children aged < 2 years at increased risk of severe RSV disease. Clesrovimab is well tolerated, with low rates of adverse events. As the second long-acting mAb approved for prevention of RSV disease, clesrovimab represents a useful addition to treatment options for neonates and infants born during or entering their first RSV season.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the potential of clesrovimab as a prophylactic option for RSV-associated lower respiratory tract disease in neonates and infants during their first RSV season.
Design and population
The study focused on clinical trials involving infants and children born during or entering their first RSV season, with a single 105 mg dose of clesrovimab administered regardless of body weight.
Core methods
The efficacy of clesrovimab was assessed through phase 2b/3 clinical trials, with pharmacokinetic data from phase 3 trials supporting its effectiveness in preventing RSV disease.
Main results
A single 105 mg dose of clesrovimab provided durable protection against RSV-associated medically-attended lower respiratory infection and reduced hospitalizations in the target population.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that clesrovimab is a novel long-acting monoclonal antibody that effectively prevents RSV disease in high-risk infants, offering a new treatment option.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results indicate that clesrovimab could be a valuable addition to the prevention strategies for RSV disease in neonates and infants, particularly during their first RSV season.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on clinical trials, which may have limitations in generalizability, but the results are supported by durable protection and reduced hospitalizations, indicating moderate confidence.

阿那白滞素治疗严重 RSV 相关自身炎症性脑病并伴有 ADEM 样延迟变化。

Anakinra in Severe RSV-Associated Autoinflammatory Encephalopathy With Delayed ADEM-Like Changes.
作者： Oi-Wa Chan, Yi-Ting Cheng, Yi-Hsuan Liu, I-Jun Chou, En-Pei Lee, Kuang-Lin Lin, Jainn-Jim Lin
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 相关脑病是儿童中一种罕见但具有潜在破坏性的并发症。越来越多的证据表明,感染引发的神经炎症而不是直接的病毒侵袭是部分患者神经损伤的基础。我们描述了 2 名先前健康、无血缘关系的儿童,他们在 RSV 感染后出现快速进展性脑病。最初的脑磁共振成像扫描结果正常,但后续成像结果显示多灶性白质病变,与急性播散性脑脊髓炎样模式一致。两名患者均接受了高剂量皮质类固醇、静脉注射免疫球蛋白和白细胞介素 (IL)-6 阻断治疗,但仍难以缓解。一名患者的血清 IL-1β 水平显着升高。治疗 与 IL-1 受体拮抗剂阿那白滞素联合使用,在两种情况下均与神经系统和放射学恢复暂时相关。这些观察结果表明,IL-1 驱动的自身炎症表型可能会导致特定患有 RSV 相关脑病的儿童的疾病严重程度,并强调在难治性病例中重复神经影像学检查和考虑靶向免疫调节的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)相关性脑病是儿童罕见但可能致命的并发症,其潜在机制尚未完全阐明。本研究旨在探讨感染触发的神经炎症是否会导致部分患者出现神经损伤。
设计与对象
本研究为一份病例报告,描述了两名此前健康且无血缘关系的儿童在感染RSV后出现快速进展性脑病。两名患者对包括高剂量皮质类固醇、静脉免疫球蛋白及白细胞介素(IL)-6阻断剂在内的标准治疗均无反应。
核心方法
作者记录了一名患者血清 IL-1β 水平显着升高,并给予 IL-1 受体拮抗剂阿那白滞素作为靶向免疫调节剂。治疗 后监测神经系统和放射学恢复情况。
主要结果
最初的脑磁共振成像扫描结果正常,但后续成像显示多灶性白质病变,与急性播散性脑脊髓炎样模式一致。在这两种情况下,治疗 联合阿那白滞素与神经系统和放射学恢复均暂时相关。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为,IL-1 驱动的自身炎症表型可能会导致特定患有 RSV 相关脑病的儿童的疾病严重程度。他们强调了重复神经影像学检查的重要性以及在难治性病例中考虑靶向免疫调节的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些观察结果表明,用阿那白滞素阻断 IL-1 可能是患有严重、难治性 RSV 相关脑病的儿童的一种治疗选择。研究结果强调需要警惕监测迟发性神经炎症并发症。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于两个 病例报告,这排除了概括性并且无法建立因果关系。由于缺乏对照、样本量小以及对时间关联的依赖,证据的强度较低。

Anakinra in Severe RSV-Associated Autoinflammatory Encephalopathy With Delayed ADEM-Like Changes.

Authors: Oi-Wa Chan, Yi-Ting Cheng, Yi-Hsuan Liu, I-Jun Chou, En-Pei Lee, Kuang-Lin Lin, Jainn-Jim Lin
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-associated encephalopathy is an uncommon but potentially devastating complication in children. Increasing evidence suggests that infection-triggered neuroinflammation rather than direct viral invasion underlies neurologic injury in a subset of patients. We describe 2 previously healthy, unrelated children who developed rapidly progressive encephalopathy after RSV infection. Initial brain magnetic resonance imaging scan results were normal, but follow-up imaging results showed multifocal white matter lesions consistent with an acute disseminated encephalomyelitis-like pattern. Both patients were given refractory to high-dose corticosteroids, intravenous immunoglobulin, and interleukin (IL)-6 blockade. Markedly elevated serum IL-1β levels were documented in one patient. Treatment with the IL-1 receptor antagonist, anakinra, was temporally associated with neurologic and radiologic recovery in both cases. These observations suggest that an IL-1-driven autoinflammatory phenotype may contribute to disease severity in selected children with RSV-associated encephalopathy and highlight the importance of repeat neuroimaging and consideration of targeted immunomodulation in refractory cases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-associated encephalopathy is an uncommon but potentially devastating complication in children, and the underlying mechanisms remain incompletely understood. The study was needed to explore whether infection-triggered neuroinflammation contributes to neurologic injury in a subset of patients.
Design and population
This is a case report describing two previously healthy, unrelated children who developed rapidly progressive encephalopathy after RSV infection. Both patients were refractory to standard therapies including high-dose corticosteroids, intravenous immunoglobulin, and interleukin (IL)-6 blockade.
Core methods
The authors documented markedly elevated serum IL-1β levels in one patient and administered the IL-1 receptor antagonist anakinra as targeted immunomodulation. Neurologic and radiologic recovery were monitored following treatment.
Main results
Initial brain magnetic resonance imaging scan results were normal, but follow-up imaging showed multifocal white matter lesions consistent with an acute disseminated encephalomyelitis-like pattern. Treatment with anakinra was temporally associated with neurologic and radiologic recovery in both cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as suggesting that an IL-1-driven autoinflammatory phenotype may contribute to disease severity in selected children with RSV-associated encephalopathy. They highlight the importance of repeat neuroimaging and consideration of targeted immunomodulation in refractory cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
These observations suggest that IL-1 blockade with anakinra may represent a therapeutic option for children with severe, refractory RSV-associated encephalopathy. The findings underscore the need for vigilance in monitoring for delayed neuroinflammatory complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to two case reports, which precludes generalization and does not establish causality. The strength of the evidence is low due to the absence of controls, small sample size, and reliance on temporal associations.

人类腺病毒联合检测对急性呼吸道感染儿童疾病严重程度的影响

Impact of Human Adenovirus Co-detection on Disease Severity in Children with Medically Attended Acute Respiratory Infections
作者： Adam Gailani, Tess Stopczynski, Olla Hamdan, Haya Hayek, Laura S Stewart, Marian G. Michaels, John V. Williams, Jennifer E. Schuster, Rangaraj Selvarangan, Leila C. Sahni
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述与 HAdV 共同检测的其他呼吸道病毒的谱和频率,并确定共同检测对 HAdV 疾病严重程度的影响。人类腺病毒 (HAdV) 经常被认为是儿童急性呼吸道感染 (ARIs) 的原因,并且经常与其他常见呼吸道病毒同时检测,但同时检测对疾病严重程度的影响尚不清楚。
设计与对象
Children admitted to the hospital or seen in the emergency department with fever and/or respiratory symptoms <14 days duration were enrolled at 7 sites across the US, from 12/01/2016 through 08/31/2023, as part of the CDC-sponsored New Vaccine Surveillance Network (NVSN). The study compared demographics, clinical characteristics, and disease severity (hospitalization and in-hospital oxygen use) among children with HAdV single detection, HAdV co-detection with other respiratory viral pathogens, and single detection of a non-HAdV virus.
核心方法
系统地获取呼吸道样本并检测 HAdV 和 ARI 的其他常见病毒病因。对HAdV单一检测、HAdV与其他呼吸道病毒病原体联合检测以及单一检测非HAdV病毒的儿童进行人口统计学、临床特征和疾病严重程度的比较。
主要结果
在 68,004 名接受呼吸道样本检测的儿童中,4,292 名(6.3%)HAdV 呈阳性;其中 1,779 例 (41.4%) 为 HAdV 单一检测。与 HAdV 单独检测相比,HAdV 与 HRV/EV、RSV 或 PIV 联合检测与住院和院内氧气使用的几率增加相关。然而,与 HAdV 单一检测相比,HRV/EV、RSV 和 PIV 单一检测的住院和院内吸氧的几率也有所增加。
解释与创新
与 HAdV 单独检测相比,HAdV 与 HRV/EV、RSV 或 PIV 联合检测与疾病严重程度增加相关。然而,与这些病原体的 HAdV 单一检测相比,HRV/EV、RSV 和 PIV 单一检测也与严重程度增加相关。
科研与公卫意义
当检测到多种病毒时,这些结果可以帮助指导临床医生对病毒检测的解释。HRV/EV、RSV 和 PIV 单独检测中出现的严重疾病几率的增加通常大于 HAdV 与 HRV/EV、RSV 或 PIV 联合检测中出现的严重疾病几率的增加。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。证据强度中等,因为该研究基于观察数据并且没有建立因果关系。

Impact of Human Adenovirus Co-detection on Disease Severity in Children with Medically Attended Acute Respiratory Infections

Authors: Adam Gailani, Tess Stopczynski, Olla Hamdan, Haya Hayek, Laura S Stewart, Marian G. Michaels, John V. Williams, Jennifer E. Schuster, Rangaraj Selvarangan, Leila C. Sahni
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human Adenovirus (HAdV) is frequently identified as a cause of acute respiratory infections (ARIs) in children and often co-detected with other common respiratory viruses. However, the effect of co-detection on disease severity is not well understood. This study aimed to describe the spectrum and frequency of other respiratory viruses co-detected with HAdV and define the impact of co-detection on HAdV disease severity. Methods Children admitted to the hospital or seen in the emergency department with fever and/or respiratory symptoms <14 days duration were enrolled at 7 sites across the US, from 12/01/2016 through 08/31/2023, as part of the CDC-sponsored New Vaccine Surveillance Network (NVSN). Respiratory specimens were systematically obtained and tested for HAdV and other common viral etiologies of ARI. Demographics, clinical characteristics, and disease severity (hospitalization and in-hospital oxygen use) were compared among children with HAdV single detection, HAdV co-detection with other respiratory viral pathogens, and single detection of a non-HAdV virus. Results Of 68,004 children enrolled with a respiratory specimen tested, 4,292 (6.3%) were positive for HAdV; 1,779 (41.4%) of these were HAdV single detections. Human rhinovirus/enterovirus (HRV/EV) (43.1%), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) (14.7%), and parainfluenza virus (PIV) (7.6%) were the most common viruses co-detected with HAdV and selected for further analysis. HAdV co-detection with these 3 viruses was associated with increased odds of hospitalization and in-hospital oxygen use when compared to HAdV single detection. However, the odds of hospitalization and in-hospital oxygen use with HRV/EV, RSV, and PIV single detection were also increased compared to HAdV single detection. Conclusion HAdV co-detection with HRV/EV, RSV, or PIV was associated with increased disease severity when compared to HAdV single detection. However, HRV/EV, RSV, and PIV single detections were also associated with increased severity in comparison to HAdV single detection with these pathogens. Additionally, the increased odds of severe disease seen in HRV/EV, RSV, and PIV single detections was often greater than the increase seen with HAdV co-detection with HRV/EV, RSV, or PIV. This could suggest that the overall severity of these ARIs is being driven primarily by the co-detected viruses, HRV/EV, RSV, and PIV. These results could help guide interpretation of viral testing by clinicians, when multiple viruses are detected. Further research involving asymptomatic and mild HAdV ARI cases, as well as longitudinal studies to determine timing of co-infections, are needed to fully understand the role of HAdV in pediatric ARI of mixed viral etiology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to describe the spectrum and frequency of other respiratory viruses co-detected with HAdV and define the impact of co-detection on HAdV disease severity. Human Adenovirus (HAdV) is frequently identified as a cause of acute respiratory infections (ARIs) in children and often co-detected with other common respiratory viruses, but the effect of co-detection on disease severity is not well understood.

HAdV co-detection with HRV/EV, RSV, or PIV was associated with increased odds of hospitalization and in-hospital oxygen use when compared to HAdV single detection. However, the odds of hospitalization and in-hospital oxygen use with HRV/EV, RSV, and PIV single detection were also increased compared to HAdV single detection.

HAdV co-detection with HRV/EV, RSV, or PIV was associated with increased disease severity when compared to HAdV single detection. However, HRV/EV, RSV, and PIV single detections were also associated with increased severity in comparison to HAdV single detection with these pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results could help guide interpretation of viral testing by clinicians, when multiple viruses are detected. The increased odds of severe disease seen in HRV/EV, RSV, and PIV single detections was often greater than the increase seen with HAdV co-detection with HRV/EV, RSV, or PIV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study is based on observational data and does not establish causality.

致编辑的信:“血液系统恶性肿瘤中的院内呼吸道病毒感染:进展为下呼吸道感染的危险因素”。

Letter to the Editor on: "Nosocomial Respiratory Virus Infections in Hematologic Malignancies: Risk Factors for Progression to Lower Respiratory Tract Infection".
作者： Xiang Ma, Qingqing Shan
摘要中文翻译本通讯回应了 Oh 及其同事关于血液系统恶性肿瘤中的院内呼吸道病毒感染以及进展为下呼吸道感染的危险因素的研究。我们提出了三个方法学问题:(1)用于评估 URTI 阶段利巴韦林治疗的里程碑分析排除了在里程碑时间点之前进展或死亡的患者,通过对撞机的调节引入了选择偏差;(2) 抗病毒药物 治疗分析的效力严重不足(仅 15 名 PIV 和 9 名 RSV 治疗患者),置信区间宽,无法得出明确的结论,并且存在大量 II 型错误风险;(3) 结合不同适应症(短程脉冲治疗与长期治疗)的糖皮质激素暴露可能掩盖对免疫功能和病毒进展的异质性影响。我们赞扬作者的风险分层框架,同时敦促谨慎解释 抗病毒药物 疗效数据,并建议未来进行具有足够样本量和精细皮质类固醇特征的前瞻性研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这封信批评了先前一项关于血液系统恶性肿瘤患者院内呼吸道病毒感染的研究,重点关注可能影响 抗病毒药物 疗效解释和进展为下呼吸道感染的危险因素的方法学局限性。
设计与对象
Oh 及其同事的最初研究评估了血液系统恶性肿瘤患者的院内呼吸道病毒感染情况,包括患有上呼吸道感染 (URTI) 的患者以及进展为下呼吸道感染的危险因素。
核心方法
作者强调了原始研究中的方法学问题,包括使用 URTI 阶段利巴韦林治疗的标志性分析、动力不足的 抗病毒药物 治疗分析以及异质糖皮质激素暴露分类。
主要结果
该信函报告称,抗病毒药物 治疗分析的效力严重不足,只有 15 名副流感病毒 (PIV) 和 9 名 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 治疗的患者,导致置信区间过宽,并且存在 II 型错误的巨大风险。
解释与创新
作者赞扬了原始研究的风险分层框架,但由于方法学的限制,敦促谨慎解释 抗病毒药物 疗效数据,建议未来进行具有足够样本量的前瞻性研究。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要改进研究设计,以准确评估 抗病毒药物 的疗效以及呼吸道病毒感染免疫功能低下患者病情进展的危险因素。
局限与证据强度
证据强度受到原始研究中方法学缺陷的限制,包括标志性分析的选择偏差、动力不足的 抗病毒药物 治疗分析以及异质性糖皮质激素暴露分类,需要进行低置信度评估。

Letter to the Editor on: "Nosocomial Respiratory Virus Infections in Hematologic Malignancies: Risk Factors for Progression to Lower Respiratory Tract Infection".

Authors: Xiang Ma, Qingqing Shan
Original abstract (English)
This Correspondence responds to Oh and colleagues' study on nosocomial respiratory virus infections in hematologic malignancies and risk factors for progression to lower respiratory tract infection. We raise three methodological concerns: (1) the landmark analysis used to evaluate URTI-stage ribavirin therapy excludes patients who progressed or died before landmark time points, introducing selection bias by conditioning on a collider; (2) antiviral therapy analyses are severely underpowered (only 15 PIV and 9 RSV treated patients), with wide confidence intervals precluding definitive conclusions and substantial Type II error risk; and (3) combining glucocorticoid exposures from different indications (short-course pulse vs. prolonged therapy) may mask heterogeneous effects on immune function and viral progression. We commend the authors' risk stratification framework while urging cautious interpretation of antiviral efficacy data and recommending future prospective studies with adequate sample sizes and refined corticosteroid characterization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The letter critiques a prior study on nosocomial respiratory virus infections in patients with hematologic malignancies, focusing on methodological limitations that may affect the interpretation of antiviral efficacy and risk factors for progression to lower respiratory tract infection.
Design and population
The original study by Oh and colleagues evaluated nosocomial respiratory virus infections in patients with hematologic malignancies, including those with upper respiratory tract infections (URTI) and risk factors for progression to lower respiratory tract infection.
Core methods
The authors highlight methodological concerns in the original study, including the use of a landmark analysis for URTI-stage ribavirin therapy, underpowered antiviral therapy analyses, and heterogeneous glucocorticoid exposure categorization.
Main results
The letter reports that antiviral therapy analyses were severely underpowered, with only 15 parainfluenza virus (PIV) and 9 respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) treated patients, leading to wide confidence intervals and substantial risk of Type II error.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors commend the risk stratification framework of the original study but urge cautious interpretation of antiviral efficacy data due to methodological limitations, recommending future prospective studies with adequate sample sizes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for improved study designs to accurately assess antiviral efficacy and risk factors for progression in immunocompromised patients with respiratory virus infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is limited by methodological flaws in the original study, including selection bias from landmark analysis, underpowered antiviral therapy analyses, and heterogeneous glucocorticoid exposure categorization, warranting a low confidence assessment.

婴儿 RSV 急性 毛细支气管炎 急性期肺音的变化。

Changes in Lung Sounds During the Acute Stage of RSV Acute Bronchiolitis in Infants.
作者： Takashi Matsushita, Chinami Kaiga, Kota Hirai, Fumio Niimura, Hiroyuki Furuya, Yoshiyuki Yamada, Atsushi Uchiyama, Hiroyuki Mochizuki
摘要中文翻译目的:通过肺音分析评估 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 诱导的急性 毛细支气管炎 呼吸功能随时间的变化。方法:我们回顾性检查了 54 名诊断为急性 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的儿童(中位年龄为 7 个月)肺音参数随时间的变化。我们使用传统方法评估吸气和呼气声音的功率,并使用新的自动分析软件程序分析吸气声音的频率特性。我们检查了住院期间和住院后这些肺音参数随时间的变化,并将这些数据与年龄匹配的健康儿童的数据进行了比较。结果:在 54 名婴儿的 138 个肺音样本中,吸气音和呼气音的力量在发病后 4 天内最强,并随着时间的推移而改善。相比之下,RSV 急性 毛细支气管炎 发病后 5 至 10 天观察到吸气音参数的最大变化。RSV急性毛细支气管炎患者的一些肺音参数即使在发病后14天也没有恢复正常。结论:这一发现被认为是婴儿 RSV 急性 毛细支气管炎 的一个特征,对于 毛细支气管炎 后的管理具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
通过肺音分析评估 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 诱导的急性 毛细支气管炎 中呼吸功能随时间的变化。
设计与对象
我们回顾性检查了 54 名诊断为急性 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的儿童(中位年龄为 7 个月)的肺音参数随时间的变化。
核心方法
我们检查了住院期间和住院后这些肺音参数随时间的变化,并将这些数据与年龄匹配的健康儿童的数据进行了比较。
主要结果
相比之下,RSV 急性 毛细支气管炎 发病后 5 至 10 天观察到吸气音参数的最大变化。
解释与创新
RSV急性毛细支气管炎患者的一些肺音参数即使在发病后14天也没有恢复正常。
科研与公卫意义
这一发现被认为是婴儿 RSV 急性 毛细支气管炎 的一个特征,对于 治疗 后的管理具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Changes in Lung Sounds During the Acute Stage of RSV Acute Bronchiolitis in Infants.

Authors: Takashi Matsushita, Chinami Kaiga, Kota Hirai, Fumio Niimura, Hiroyuki Furuya, Yoshiyuki Yamada, Atsushi Uchiyama, Hiroyuki Mochizuki
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Changes in the respiratory function over time in respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-induced acute bronchiolitis were evaluated by a lung sound analysis. METHODS: We retrospectively examined changes in lung sound parameters over time in 54 children (median age, 7 months) diagnosed with RSV acute bronchiolitis. We evaluated the power of inspiratory and expiratory sounds using a conventional method and analyzed the frequency characteristics of inspiratory sounds using new automated analysis software program. We examined changes in these lung sound parameters over time during and after hospitalization and compared these data with those of age-matched healthy children. RESULTS: In 138 lung sound samples from 54 infants, the power of inspiratory and expiratory sounds was greatest within 4 days after the onset and improved over time. In contrast, the greatest changes in inspiratory sound parameters were observed at 5 to 10 days after the onset of RSV acute bronchiolitis. Some lung sound parameters in patients with RSV acute bronchiolitis did not return to normal even by 14 days after the onset. CONCLUSIONS: This finding is considered a characteristic of RSV acute bronchiolitis in infants and is meaningful in terms of post-treatment management.

Question and background
Changes in the respiratory function over time in respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-induced acute bronchiolitis were evaluated by a lung sound analysis.
Design and population
We retrospectively examined changes in lung sound parameters over time in 54 children (median age, 7 months) diagnosed with RSV acute bronchiolitis.
Core methods
We examined changes in these lung sound parameters over time during and after hospitalization and compared these data with those of age-matched healthy children.
Main results
In contrast, the greatest changes in inspiratory sound parameters were observed at 5 to 10 days after the onset of RSV acute bronchiolitis.
Interpretation and novelty
Some lung sound parameters in patients with RSV acute bronchiolitis did not return to normal even by 14 days after the onset.
Scientific and public-health significance
This finding is considered a characteristic of RSV acute bronchiolitis in infants and is meaningful in terms of post-treatment management.
Limitations and evidence strength

RNU4ATAC 病病例中感染引发的神经功能恶化和免疫缺陷

Infection-triggered neurological deterioration and immunodeficiency in a case of RNU4ATAC-opathy
作者： Deniz Yaşar, Mustafa Kılıç, Abdulkerim Kolkıran, Ayşe Kaçar Bayram, O. Saritas Nakip, Berna Uçan, Hüsniye Yücel, Elif Soyak Aytekin
摘要中文翻译RNU4ATAC 病是一种罕见的常染色体隐性遗传疾病,其临床表现广泛,包括脑异常、癫痫发作、中风、免疫缺陷和心脏缺陷。其他表现包括眼科、皮肤科、肾脏、胃肠道、听觉和内分泌受累。在这里,我们报告了通过对一名患有小头畸形、生长障碍和免疫缺陷的婴儿进行全外显子组测序来鉴定致病性 RNU4ATAC 变异。我们描述了一名 11 个月大的婴儿,该婴儿从 4 个月大起就因生长障碍和发育迟缓而接受随访。在随访过程中,患者分别在 9 个月和 11 个月大时经历了由与 SARS-CoV-2 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染 (RSV) 相关的发热性疾病引发的神经功能恶化。免疫学评估显示免疫球蛋白水平正常,转换记忆和记忆 B 细胞显着减少,T 细胞活化受损,T 细胞亚群减少,表明联合免疫缺陷可能导致对病毒感染的易感性增加。全外显子组测序随后鉴定出 RNU4ATAC 基因中的复合杂合致病性变异,证实了 RNU4ATAC 疾病的 诊断。对于小头畸形、生长障碍、免疫缺陷和骨骼异常的病例,应考虑 RNU4ATAC 病。我们的病例表明,RNU4ATAC 病可能表现为感染相关脑病,并伴有 T 细胞和 B 细胞功能障碍。早期免疫学评估和及时开始预防性治疗可以在提高生存率方面发挥关键作用。遗传和免疫学评估对于准确的 诊断、早期干预和有效的管理至关重要。需要进一步的研究来阐明该综合征对免疫功能的影响并指导靶向治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查与 RNU4ATAC 病相关的临床表现和免疫功能障碍,特别是在病毒感染引发的神经功能恶化的情况下。背景强调了 RNU4ATAC 疾病的罕见性和复杂性,其中包括神经学和免疫学特征。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一名患有 RNU4ATAC 病的 11 个月大婴儿的 病例报告,该婴儿从 4 个月大起就因生长障碍和发育迟缓而接受随访。该患者出现了与 SARS-CoV-2 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染引起的发热性疾病相关的神经功能恶化。
核心方法
全外显子组测序用于鉴定婴儿 RNU4ATAC 基因的致病性变异,确认 RNU4ATAC 病的 诊断。进行免疫学评估以评估 B 细胞和 T 细胞功能,揭示联合免疫缺陷。
主要结果
该婴儿分别在 9 个月和 11 个月大时表现出由 SARS-CoV-2 和 RSV 感染引发的神经功能恶化。免疫学结果表明免疫球蛋白水平正常,转换记忆和记忆 B 细胞减少,T 细胞活化受损,T 细胞亚群减少。
解释与创新
作者解释说,RNU4ATAC 病可能表现为感染相关脑病并伴有 T 细胞和 B 细胞功能障碍。该病例增加了对该综合征的临床变异性和免疫影响的理解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,早期免疫学评估和预防性治疗对于控制 RNU4ATAC 疾病至关重要,有可能提高受影响婴儿的生存率。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个病例报告,可能无法推广。这些证据基于临床观察和基因检测,为解释该综合征的免疫和神经学特征提供了一定的可信度。

Infection-triggered neurological deterioration and immunodeficiency in a case of RNU4ATAC-opathy

Authors: Deniz Yaşar, Mustafa Kılıç, Abdulkerim Kolkıran, Ayşe Kaçar Bayram, O. Saritas Nakip, Berna Uçan, Hüsniye Yücel, Elif Soyak Aytekin
Original abstract (English)
RNU4ATAC-opathy is a rare autosomal recessive disorder characterized by a wide clinical spectrum, including brain anomalies, seizures, strokes, immunodeficiency, and cardiac defects. Other manifestations include ophthalmologic, dermatologic, renal, gastrointestinal, auditory, and endocrine involvement. Here, we report the identification of pathogenic RNU4ATAC variants through whole exome sequencing in an infant presenting with microcephaly, growth failure, and immunodeficiency. We describe an 11-month-old infant who had been followed since 4 months of age for growth failure and developmental delay. During follow-up, the patient experienced episodes of neurological deterioration triggered by febrile illnesses associated with SARS-CoV-2 and respiratory syncytial virus infections (RSV) at 9 and 11 months of age, respectively. Immunological evaluation showed normal immunoglobulin levels, a marked reduction in switched memory and memory B cells, impaired T-cell activation, and decreased T-cell subpopulations, suggesting combined immunodeficiency that may have contributed to increased susceptibility to viral infections. Whole exome sequencing subsequently identified compound heterozygous pathogenic variants in the RNU4ATAC gene, confirming the diagnosis of RNU4ATAC-opathy. RNU4ATAC-opathy should be considered in cases with microcephaly, growth failure, immunodeficiency, and skeletal abnormalities. Our case shows that RNU4ATAC-opathy may present as infection-related encephalopathy, together with combined T- and B-cell dysfunction. Early immunological assessment and timely initiation of prophylactic therapy could play a key role in improving survival. Genetic and immunological evaluations are essential for accurate diagnosis, early intervention, and effective management. Further research is needed to clarify the syndrome’s impact on immune function and guide targeted therapies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the clinical manifestations and immune dysfunction associated with RNU4ATAC-opathy, particularly in the context of neurological deterioration triggered by viral infections. The background highlights the rarity and complexity of RNU4ATAC-opathy, which includes neurological and immunological features.
Design and population
The study describes a single case report of an 11-month-old infant with RNU4ATAC-opathy, who was followed since 4 months of age for growth failure and developmental delay. The patient experienced neurological deterioration linked to febrile illnesses caused by SARS-CoV-2 and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infections.
Core methods
Whole exome sequencing was used to identify pathogenic variants in the RNU4ATAC gene in the infant, confirming the diagnosis of RNU4ATAC-opathy. Immunological evaluation was conducted to assess B-cell and T-cell function, revealing combined immunodeficiency.
Main results
The infant exhibited neurological deterioration episodes triggered by SARS-CoV-2 and RSV infections at 9 and 11 months of age, respectively. Immunological findings indicated normal immunoglobulin levels, reduced switched memory and memory B cells, impaired T-cell activation, and decreased T-cell subpopulations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that RNU4ATAC-opathy may present as infection-related encephalopathy alongside combined T- and B-cell dysfunction. This case adds to the understanding of the syndrome's clinical variability and immune implications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that early immunological assessment and prophylactic therapy could be critical in managing RNU4ATAC-opathy, potentially improving survival rates in affected infants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable. The evidence is based on clinical observations and genetic testing, providing moderate confidence in the interpretation of the syndrome's immune and neurological features.

母体呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)预融合F(RSVpreF)疫苗对婴儿保护作用:更新的系统综述和荟萃分析评估其有效性、效果和安全性

Maternal respiratory syncytial virus vaccination for infant protection: updated systematic review and meta-analysis of efficacy, effectiveness, and safety
作者： KM Saif-Ur-Rahman, Catherine King, Sean Whelan, Matthew Blair, Sean Donohue, Caoimhe Madden, Kavita Kothari, Isolde Sommer, Thomas Harder, Nicolas Dauby
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了人类 呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV;人型正肺病毒,包括 RSV-A 和 RSV-B)作为婴儿和幼儿严重疾病的原因,重点关注带有 RSV prefusion F (RSVpreF) 疫苗 的母亲 呼吸道合胞病毒,以保护婴儿免受 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染 (RSV-LRTI)、严重 RSV-LRTI 和RSV 相关住院治疗。
证据基础与方法
这是一项更新的系统综述,检索了 2000 年至 2025 年 6 月的 MEDLINE、Cochrane 图书馆/Central、Embase 和灰色文献。它包括随机对照试验 (RCT) 和非随机干预研究 (NRSI),以评估母亲 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 对婴儿保护的功效、有效性和安全性。
共识与结论
RCT 的荟萃分析高度确定地表明,母亲 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 显着降低了就医的 RSV-LRTI(疫苗 疗效 68%,RR 0.32;95% CI 0.13 至 0.77)、严重 RSV-LRTI(84%,RR 0.16;95% CI 0.07 至 0.36)和婴儿中 RSV 相关住院治疗(70%,RR 0.30;95% CI 0.15 至 0.61)。解释表明母亲 疫苗接种 减少了这些结果。
争议与缺口
证据差距包括试验中缺乏有创通气要求和因 RSV 疾病住院时间的报告数据。NRSI 表明对住院治疗有效(72%,RR 0.28;95% CI 0.17 至 0.46),但确定性非常低。授权的 疫苗 的长期有效性仍不清楚。
科研与实践启示
优先事项包括明确母体RSVpreF 疫苗接种的长期效果,并弥补证据缺口(如有创通气需求及住院时长)。应继续进行监测并监测罕见不良事件,以确保安全性。

Maternal respiratory syncytial virus vaccination for infant protection: updated systematic review and meta-analysis of efficacy, effectiveness, and safety

Authors: KM Saif-Ur-Rahman, Catherine King, Sean Whelan, Matthew Blair, Sean Donohue, Caoimhe Madden, Kavita Kothari, Isolde Sommer, Thomas Harder, Nicolas Dauby
Original abstract (English)
Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe illness in infants and young children. Methods We updated this systematic review to explore the efficacy, effectiveness and safety of authorised maternal RSV prefusion F (RSVpreF) vaccine for infant protection. We searched MEDLINE, Cochrane Library/Central, Embase and grey literature from 2000 to June 2025. We included randomised controlled trials (RCTs) and non-randomised studies of interventions (NRSIs). Results Meta-analyses of RCTs showed high certainty of evidence that maternal RSVpreF vaccine lowered medically attended RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections (RSV-LRTI) with vaccine efficacy of 68% (relative risk (RR) 0.32; 95% confidence interval (CI) 0.13 to 0.77, 2 trials; 7656 participants), severe RSV-LRTI with a vaccine efficacy of 84% (RR 0.16; 95% CI 0.07 to 0.36, 2 trials; 7656 participants) and RSV-related hospitalisations with a vaccine efficacy of 70% (RR 0.30; 95% CI 0.15 to 0.61, 1 trial; 7148 participants) in infants. Within the limited evidence base available, there may be little to no difference in RSV-related mortality, all-cause mortality, vaccine-related serious adverse events, and intensive care admission. Requirements for invasive ventilation and duration of hospitalisation due to RSV disease were not reported by any trials. NRSIs suggested effectiveness of 72% (RR 0.28; 95% CI: 0.17 to 0.46, 3 NRSIs; 1229 participants) against hospitalisation, but certainty was very low. Interpretation Maternal RSVpreF vaccination reduced medically attended and severe RSV-LRTI and RSV-related hospitalisation in infants. No safety signals were identified. Further research is required to clarify the long-term effectiveness of the authorised vaccine.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV; Orthopneumovirus hominis, including RSV-A and RSV-B) as a cause of severe illness in infants and young children, focusing on maternal vaccination with the RSV prefusion F (RSVpreF) vaccine to protect infants from RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections (RSV-LRTI), severe RSV-LRTI, and RSV-related hospitalizations.
Evidence base and method
This is an updated systematic review that searched MEDLINE, Cochrane Library/Central, Embase, and grey literature from 2000 to June 2025. It included randomized controlled trials (RCTs) and non-randomised studies of interventions (NRSIs) to assess efficacy, effectiveness, and safety of maternal RSVpreF vaccination for infant protection.
Consensus and conclusions
Meta-analyses of RCTs demonstrated high certainty that maternal RSVpreF vaccination significantly reduced medically attended RSV-LRTI (vaccine efficacy 68%, RR 0.32; 95% CI 0.13 to 0.77), severe RSV-LRTI (84%, RR 0.16; 95% CI 0.07 to 0.36), and RSV-related hospitalizations (70%, RR 0.30; 95% CI 0.15 to 0.61) in infants. Interpretation indicates maternal vaccination reduced these outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
Evidence gaps include the absence of reported data on requirements for invasive ventilation and duration of hospitalisation due to RSV disease in trials. NRSIs suggested effectiveness against hospitalisation (72%, RR 0.28; 95% CI 0.17 to 0.46) but with very low certainty. Long-term effectiveness of the authorised vaccine remains unclear.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include clarifying long-term effectiveness of maternal RSVpreF vaccination and addressing evidence gaps such as invasive ventilation requirements and hospitalisation duration. Surveillance and monitoring for rare adverse events should continue to ensure safety.

成人心血管结局和急性人间质肺病毒感染:系统文献综述。

Cardiovascular Outcomes and Acute Human Metapneumovirus Infection in Adults: A Systematic Literature Review.
作者： Paul Loubet, François Roubille, Odile Launay, Slim Fourati, Fabienne Peretz, Carolina Rojido, Hélène Bricout, Clémence Touron, Oliver Martyn
摘要中文翻译目的:急性呼吸道病毒感染,包括流感、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2,被广泛认为与心血管事件 (CVE) 相关。相比之下,人类偏肺病毒(HMPV)对心血管的影响仍然知之甚少。我们的目的是描述实验室确诊的 HMPV 感染成人中既存心血管疾病 (CVD) 的 发病率 和急性 CVE 的 发病率。方法:PRISMA 引导的 MEDLINE、Embase 和 Google Scholar 系统文献综述(2000 年 1 月/2025 年 9 月)。报告成人(≥18 岁)已有 CVD 和/或 CVE 的观察性研究和临床试验也包括在内。结果:十八项研究符合纳入标准,其中九项接受了严格评估。大多数涉及住院的老年人。据报道,10%-80% 的 HMPV 感染患者已有 CVD,其中慢性心力衰竭和冠状动脉疾病是最常见的病症。5%-25% 的住院患者发生急性 CVE,尤其是心力衰竭加重。潜在的 CVD 始终与 HMPV 疾病严重程度的增加有关,包括更高的 ICU 入住率和 死亡率 率。结论:急性 ​​HMPV 感染经常发生在已有 CVD 的成年人中,并且可能与老年住院人群的 CVE 相关。然而,证据仍然有限,并且主要是观察性的。这些发现强调需要提高对与 HMPV 感染相关的潜在心血管并发症的认识。试用注册:PROSPERO [CRD420251164583]。

急性呼吸道病毒感染,包括流感、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2,被广泛认为与心血管事件 (CVE) 相关。
证据基础与方法
PRISMA 指导的 MEDLINE、Embase 和 Google Scholar 系统文献综述(2000 年 1 月/2025 年 9 月)。
共识与结论
据报道,10%-80% 的 HMPV 感染患者已有 CVD,其中慢性心力衰竭和冠状动脉疾病是最常见的病症。5%-25% 的住院患者发生急性 CVE,尤其是心力衰竭加重。潜在的 CVD 始终与 HMPV 疾病严重程度的增加有关,包括更高的 ICU 入住率和 死亡率 率。
争议与缺口
然而,证据仍然有限,并且主要是观察性的。
科研与实践启示
这些发现强调需要提高对与 HMPV 感染相关的潜在心血管并发症的认识。

Cardiovascular Outcomes and Acute Human Metapneumovirus Infection in Adults: A Systematic Literature Review.

Authors: Paul Loubet, François Roubille, Odile Launay, Slim Fourati, Fabienne Peretz, Carolina Rojido, Hélène Bricout, Clémence Touron, Oliver Martyn
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Acute respiratory viral infections, including influenza, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2, are well-recognized as being associated with cardiovascular events (CVEs). In contrast, the cardiovascular impact of human metapneumovirus (HMPV) remains poorly characterized. We aimed to describe the prevalence of pre-existing cardiovascular diseases (CVDs) and the incidence of acute CVEs in adults with laboratory-confirmed HMPV infection. METHODS: PRISMA-guided systematic literature review of MEDLINE, Embase, and Google Scholar (Jan.-2000/Sept.-2025). Observational studies and clinical trials reporting pre-existing CVDs and/or CVEs in adults (≥18 years) were included. RESULTS: Eighteen studies met the inclusion criteria, of which nine underwent critical appraisal. Most involved hospitalized older adults. Pre-existing CVD was reported in 10%-80% of HMPV-infected patients, with chronic heart failure and coronary artery disease being the most common conditions. Acute CVEs, particularly heart failure exacerbations, occurred in 5%-25% of hospitalized patients. Underlying CVD was consistently linked to increased severity of HMPV illness, including higher rates of ICU admission and mortality. CONCLUSIONS: Acute HMPV infection frequently occurs in adults with pre-existing CVD and may be associated with CVEs in older hospitalized populations. However, evidence remains limited and largely observational. These findings highlight the need to increase awareness of the potential CV complications associated with HMPV infection. TRIAL REGISTRATION: PROSPERO [CRD420251164583].
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Acute respiratory viral infections, including influenza, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2, are well-recognized as being associated with cardiovascular events (CVEs).
Evidence base and method
PRISMA-guided systematic literature review of MEDLINE, Embase, and Google Scholar (Jan.-2000/Sept.-2025).
Consensus and conclusions
Pre-existing CVD was reported in 10%-80% of HMPV-infected patients, with chronic heart failure and coronary artery disease being the most common conditions. Acute CVEs, particularly heart failure exacerbations, occurred in 5%-25% of hospitalized patients. Underlying CVD was consistently linked to increased severity of HMPV illness, including higher rates of ICU admission and mortality.
Controversies and gaps
However, evidence remains limited and largely observational.
Research and practice implications
These findings highlight the need to increase awareness of the potential CV complications associated with HMPV infection.

患有 RSVpreF 的母亲 疫苗接种 和妊娠期高血压疾病的风险:系统评价和荟萃分析。

Maternal vaccination with RSVpreF and risk of hypertensive disorders of pregnancy: a systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Abdallah Alami, Meghan Lewis, Darine El-Chaâr, Mark C. Walker, Shi Wu Wen, Daniel Krewski, Darine El‐Chaâr
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是母亲 疫苗接种 与 RSVpreF 与妊娠期高血压疾病 (HDP) 风险之间的关联。目标病原体是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),特别是 RSV-A 和 RSV-B。核心问题是妊娠期间 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 是否会增加 HDP 的风险,特别是妊娠期高血压。
证据基础与方法
该综述是系统综述和荟萃分析。证据基础包括随机对照试验 (RCT) 和观察性研究。该检索在 MEDLINE、Embase、CENTRAL、Scopus、ClinicalTrials.gov 和 WHO ICTRP 上进行,从开始到 2026 年 1 月 26 日。使用随机效应模型汇总未调整的风险比 (RR),而使用逆方差方法单独汇总观察性研究的调整估计值。
共识与结论
Maternal RSVpreF vaccination was associated with a small but statistically significant increase in overall HDP risk (RR 1·08, 95% CI 1·02–1·13; p = 0·004; I 2 = 44%). This association was primarily driven by observational studies (RR 1·08, 95% CI 1·02–1·14; I 2 = 61%), with RCTs showing a directionally consistent but non-significant RR (1·12, 0·87–1·43; I 2 = 0%). The association was attributed to gestational hypertension, with no significant link to preeclampsia/eclampsia.
争议与缺口
关键的 3 期试验发现 HDP 的数值不平衡,但未达到统计显着性,并且上市后观察性研究报告的结果不一致。观察性研究中观察到的关联可能受到残留混杂因素的影响,凸显了方法学的局限性。妊娠期高血压风险增加的机制以及其他因素的潜在作用尚不清楚。
科研与实践启示
通过母亲 疫苗接种 预防婴儿 RSV 的益处仍然很大。这些发现支持监测在上市后继续监测RSVpreF 疫苗接种在怀孕期间的安全性,并为医疗保健提供者和孕妇之间的共同决策提供信息。

Maternal vaccination with RSVpreF and risk of hypertensive disorders of pregnancy: a systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Abdallah Alami, Meghan Lewis, Darine El-Chaâr, Mark C. Walker, Shi Wu Wen, Daniel Krewski, Darine El‐Chaâr
Original abstract (English)
Background A bivalent respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prefusion F protein–based vaccine (RSVpreF) was approved in the United States in August 2023 for use during pregnancy to prevent infant RSV-associated lower respiratory tract disease. The pivotal phase 3 trial identified a numerical imbalance in hypertensive disorders of pregnancy (HDP) that did not reach statistical significance; postmarketing observational studies have since reported inconsistent findings. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis to assess this association. Methods We searched MEDLINE, Embase, CENTRAL, Scopus, ClinicalTrials.gov , and WHO ICTRP from inception to Jan 26, 2026, for randomized controlled trials (RCTs) and observational studies comparing HDP outcomes in RSVpreF-vaccinated versus unvaccinated or placebo-receiving pregnant individuals. Unadjusted risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using a random-effects model; adjusted estimates from observational studies were pooled separately by inverse variance methods. This study is registered with PROSPERO (CRD420251026835). Results Nine studies were included (3 RCTs, 6 retrospective cohort studies; n = 148,267). RSVpreF vaccination was associated with a small but statistically significant increase in overall HDP risk (RR 1·08, 95% CI 1·02–1·13; p = 0·004; I 2 = 44%), driven by the observational studies group (1·08, 1·02–1·14; I 2 = 61%); RCTs showed a directionally consistent but non-significant RR (1·12, 0·87–1·43; I 2 = 0%). The association was attributable to gestational hypertension, with no significant association for preeclampsia/eclampsia. Conclusion Maternal RSVpreF vaccination was associated with a small increase in HDP attributable to gestational hypertension and driven primarily by observational studies, in which residual confounding remains possible. The benefits of infant RSV prevention remain substantial, and these findings support continued postmarketing surveillance and informed shared decision-making.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the association between maternal vaccination with RSVpreF and the risk of hypertensive disorders of pregnancy (HDP). The target pathogen is respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), specifically RSV-A and RSV-B. The central question is whether RSVpreF vaccination during pregnancy increases the risk of HDP, with a particular emphasis on gestational hypertension.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic review and meta-analysis. The evidence base includes randomized controlled trials (RCTs) and observational studies. The search was conducted across MEDLINE, Embase, CENTRAL, Scopus, ClinicalTrials.gov, and WHO ICTRP from inception to January 26, 2026. Unadjusted risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using a random-effects model, while adjusted estimates from observational studies were pooled separately using inverse variance methods.
Consensus and conclusions
Maternal RSVpreF vaccination was associated with a small but statistically significant increase in overall HDP risk (RR 1·08, 95% CI 1·02–1·13; p = 0·004; I 2 = 44%). This association was primarily driven by observational studies (RR 1·08, 95% CI 1·02–1·14; I 2 = 61%), with RCTs showing a directionally consistent but non-significant RR (1·12, 0·87–1·43; I 2 = 0%). The association was attributed to gestational hypertension, with no significant link to preeclampsia/eclampsia.
Controversies and gaps
The pivotal phase 3 trial found a numerical imbalance in HDP that did not reach statistical significance, and postmarketing observational studies reported inconsistent findings. The association observed in observational studies may be influenced by residual confounding, highlighting methodological limitations. There is a lack of clarity on the mechanisms underlying the increased risk of gestational hypertension and the potential role of other factors.
Research and practice implications
The benefits of infant RSV prevention through maternal vaccination remain substantial. These findings support continued postmarketing surveillance to monitor the safety profile of RSVpreF vaccination during pregnancy and informed shared decision-making between healthcare providers and pregnant individuals.

RSV 疫苗接种 对老年人的潜在下游益处评估

An Appraisal of the Potential Downstream Benefits of RSV Vaccination in Older Adults
作者： Piotr Rzymski
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 长期以来一直未被认识到是老年人中的一种重要病原体,尽管越来越多的证据表明其在该人群中具有很高的发病率和 死亡率 负担。虽然 RSV 疫苗接种 现已被认为是预防下呼吸道疾病的有效干预措施,但这篇叙述性综述探讨了其公共卫生价值是否可能超出这些主要临床终点。从数据库建立到 2026 年 5 月,对 Scopus 和 Web of Science 进行了有针对性的搜索,以确定与潜在下游 疫苗 效应相关的生物学、流行病学、观察、干预和建模证据。现有证据可能支持以下假设:心血管并发症和继发性细菌感染可能减少,年轻家庭接触者的 传播 可能减少,以及功能状态和独立性可能得到保留。然而,这些结果的证据强度差异很大。心血管保护尚未在随机试验中得到独立证明,细菌和抗菌药物的使用结果很少被预先设定,现实世界的家庭 传播 研究无法获得,功能轨迹可能会因基线虚弱和预先存在的残疾而发生强烈改变。因此,RSV 疫苗接种 在老年人中仍然是一种经过充分验证的预防急性呼吸道疾病的策略,而其提出的更广泛的作用应被视为生物学上合理但尚未完全证明的益处。未来的研究应纳入预先指定的心血管、细菌、传播、老年和功能结局,特别关注虚弱、抗菌药物的使用和长期随访。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
尽管越来越多的证据表明 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 在老年人群中具有显着的发病率和 死亡率 负担,但长期以来,人们一直没有充分认识到 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是老年人中的一种重要病原体。

从数据库建立到 2026 年 5 月,对 Scopus 和 Web of Science 进行了有针对性的搜索,以确定与潜在下游 疫苗 效应相关的生物学、流行病学、观察、干预和建模证据。
共识与结论
RSV 疫苗接种 在老年人中仍然是一种经过充分验证的预防急性呼吸道疾病的策略,而其提出的更广泛的作用应被视为生物学上合理但尚未完全证明的益处。
争议与缺口
心血管保护尚未在随机试验中得到独立证明,细菌和抗菌药物的使用结果很少被预先设定,现实世界的家庭 传播 研究无法获得,功能轨迹可能会因基线虚弱和预先存在的残疾而发生强烈改变。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应纳入预先指定的心血管、细菌、传播、老年和功能结局,特别关注虚弱、抗菌药物的使用和长期随访。

An Appraisal of the Potential Downstream Benefits of RSV Vaccination in Older Adults

Authors: Piotr Rzymski
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) has long been underrecognized as a significant pathogen in older adults, despite accumulating evidence of its substantial morbidity and mortality burden in this population. While RSV vaccination is now well established as an effective intervention for preventing lower respiratory tract disease, this narrative review examines whether its public health value may extend beyond these primary clinical endpoints. A targeted search of Scopus and Web of Science from database inception to May 2026 was conducted to identify biological, epidemiological, observational, interventional, and modelling evidence relevant to potential downstream vaccine effects. The available evidence potentially supports hypotheses concerning possible reductions in cardiovascular complications and secondary bacterial infections, possible decreases in transmission to younger household contacts, and potential preservation of functional status and independence. However, the strength of evidence varies substantially across these outcomes. Cardiovascular protection has not been independently demonstrated in randomized trials, bacterial and antimicrobial‐use outcomes have rarely been prespecified, real‐world household transmission studies are unavailable, and functional trajectories may be strongly modified by baseline frailty and pre‐existing disability. RSV vaccination in older adults therefore remains a well‐validated strategy for preventing acute respiratory disease, whereas its proposed broader effects should be regarded as biologically plausible but incompletely demonstrated benefits. Future studies should incorporate prespecified cardiovascular, bacterial, transmission, geriatric, and functional outcomes, with particular attention to frailty, antimicrobial use, and longer‐term follow‐up.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) has long been underrecognized as a significant pathogen in older adults, despite accumulating evidence of its substantial morbidity and mortality burden in this population.
Evidence base and method
A targeted search of Scopus and Web of Science from database inception to May 2026 was conducted to identify biological, epidemiological, observational, interventional, and modelling evidence relevant to potential downstream vaccine effects.
Consensus and conclusions
RSV vaccination in older adults remains a well‐validated strategy for preventing acute respiratory disease, whereas its proposed broader effects should be regarded as biologically plausible but incompletely demonstrated benefits.
Controversies and gaps
Cardiovascular protection has not been independently demonstrated in randomized trials, bacterial and antimicrobial‐use outcomes have rarely been prespecified, real‐world household transmission studies are unavailable, and functional trajectories may be strongly modified by baseline frailty and pre‐existing disability.
Research and practice implications
Future studies should incorporate prespecified cardiovascular, bacterial, transmission, geriatric, and functional outcomes, with particular attention to frailty, antimicrobial use, and longer‐term follow‐up.

日本基层医生在识别资格困难与自我报告预防性医疗实施中的差异

Eligibility-Identification Difficulty and Self-Reported Preventive Care Implementation Among Japanese Primary Care Physicians.
作者： Hironori Yamada, Kei Miyazaki, Makito Yaegashi, Kuniko Nakayama, Rei Suganaga, Nobuaki Kusaka, Wataru Hirohashi, Tomio Suzuki, 伸明 日下
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 初级保健是预防的重要场所,但在临床医生识别出符合预防性服务资格的患者之前,可能会出现实施差距。我们描述了日本初级保健医生自我报告的预防保健实施情况,并检查了较低的实施情况是否与资格识别困难同时发生。方法 我们于 2025 年 5 月至 6 月对日本初级保健协会的医生成员进行了一项全国性的网络横断面调查。受访者报告了 30 种预防性服务的推荐一致性、识别合格患者的困难以及感知到的障碍。服务水平实施和资格识别难度总结在双轴图中。结果 在 258 名受访者中,56.2% 的受访者表示接受了结构化的预防护理培训,53.9% 的受访者使用了门诊提醒。高血压、糖尿病、血脂异常筛查和流感 疫苗接种 的“基本达到”建议相对较高,但家庭暴力、衣原体/淋病、腹主动脉瘤、焦虑和抑郁、呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗接种 和叶酸咨询的筛查建议较低。家庭暴力筛查 (14.0%)、衣原体/淋病筛查 (13.6%)、丙型肝炎筛查 (10.5%) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗接种 (7.8%) 筛选合格患者的难度最高。常见的障碍是时间限制、知识或技能不足、患者兴趣低以及财务或报销问题。结论 不同服务的自我报告实施情况差异很大。一些低实施服务还涉及资格识别困难,表明存在上游实施问题。日本初级保健可能需要通过培训、提醒、提示、登记和基于团队的工作流程使资格可见的系统。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本次审查的重点是日本初级保健医生对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防性护理的实施情况。它研究了确定符合 RSV 相关预防服务资格的患者的困难以及临床医生自我报告的这些服务的实施情况。
证据基础与方法
该审查是 2025 年 5 月至 6 月在日本初级保健协会的医生成员中进行的一项全国性网络横断面调查。它收集了有关自我报告的预防性护理实施情况以及 RSV 相关服务的资格识别难度的数据。
共识与结论
各服务部门自我报告的 RSV 预防护理实施情况存在很大差异。具体而言,RSV 毛细支气管炎 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗接种 等服务的实施率较低,识别合格患者的难度较高。
争议与缺口
审查强调,一些实施程度较低的服务还难以识别符合条件的患者,这表明潜在的上游实施问题。然而,证据并未提供应对这些挑战的详细机制或解决方案。
科研与实践启示
审查表明,日本初级保健可能需要系统来使 RSV 预防服务的资格更加明显,例如培训、提醒、提示、登记和基于团队的工作流程。

Eligibility-Identification Difficulty and Self-Reported Preventive Care Implementation Among Japanese Primary Care Physicians.

Authors: Hironori Yamada, Kei Miyazaki, Makito Yaegashi, Kuniko Nakayama, Rei Suganaga, Nobuaki Kusaka, Wataru Hirohashi, Tomio Suzuki, 伸明 日下
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Primary care is an important setting for prevention, but implementation gaps may arise before clinicians can identify patients eligible for preventive services. We described self‐reported preventive care implementation among Japanese primary care physicians and examined whether lower implementation co‐occurred with eligibility‐identification difficulty. Methods We conducted a nationwide web‐based cross‐sectional survey of physician members of the Japan Primary Care Association from May to June 2025. Respondents reported recommendation consistency for 30 preventive services, difficulty identifying eligible patients, and perceived barriers. Service‐level implementation and eligibility‐identification difficulty were summarized in a two‐axis map. Results Among 258 respondents, 56.2% reported structured preventive care training and 53.9% used outpatient reminders. “Mostly achieved” recommendation was relatively high for hypertension, diabetes, and dyslipidaemia screening and influenza vaccination, but low for screening for domestic violence, chlamydia/gonorrhea, abdominal aortic aneurysm, anxiety, and depression, respiratory syncytial virus vaccination, and folic acid counseling. Difficulty identifying eligible patients was highest for domestic violence screening (14.0%), chlamydia/gonorrhea screening (13.6%), hepatitis C screening (10.5%), and respiratory syncytial virus vaccination (7.8%). Common barriers were time constraints, insufficient knowledge or skills, low patient interest, and financial or reimbursement issues. Conclusions Self‐reported implementation varied substantially across services. Some low‐implementation services also involved eligibility‐identification difficulty, suggesting an upstream implementation problem. Japanese primary care may need systems that make eligibility visible through training, reminders, prompts, registries, and team‐based workflows.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the implementation of preventive care for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) among Japanese primary care physicians. It examines the difficulty in identifying eligible patients for RSV-related preventive services and the self-reported implementation of these services by clinicians.
Evidence base and method
The review is a nationwide web-based cross-sectional survey conducted among physician members of the Japan Primary Care Association from May to June 2025. It collects data on self-reported preventive care implementation and eligibility-identification difficulty for RSV-related services.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that self-reported implementation of RSV preventive care varies substantially across services. Specifically, services such as RSV bronchiolitis and respiratory syncytial virus vaccination show lower implementation rates and higher difficulty in identifying eligible patients.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that some low-implementation services also involve difficulty in identifying eligible patients, suggesting potential upstream implementation problems. However, the evidence does not provide detailed mechanisms or solutions for these challenges.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that Japanese primary care may need systems to make eligibility for RSV preventive services more visible, such as training, reminders, prompts, registries, and team-based workflows.
