哮喘患者的气道微生物组

The airway microbiome in asthma.
作者： Hollian Richardson, Jennifer Pollock, Rory Chan, James D Chalmers
摘要中文翻译主题重要性:哮喘是一种异质性气道疾病,其特征是可变的气流受限、气道炎症和支气管高反应性。有证据表明气道微生物群在哮喘病理生理学中发挥重要作用。大多数微生物组研究已经调查了微生物组的细菌成分,需要进一步研究来调查气道病毒组和真菌组在哮喘中的作用。综述结果:气道微生物群失调与哮喘发展、疾病严重程度和炎症内型相关。然而,目前尚不清楚生态失调是导致炎症还是炎症的结果。嗜血杆菌和莫拉氏菌以及鼻病毒和 呼吸道合胞病毒 已被证明与儿童哮喘的发生有关。在已确诊的哮喘中,气道微生物群失调与严重程度和恶化风险相关。不同的炎症内型观察到不同的气道微生物组特征。患有嗜酸性粒细胞性炎症的哮喘患者与健康个体相似,具有较高的微生物多样性,而中性粒细胞性炎症与微生物多样性降低、细菌负荷较高和病原体主导的微生物组特征相关,表明生态失调在目前抗 T2 生物治疗难治性哮喘中发挥着重要作用。摘要:为了了解微生物组在哮喘中的作用,微生物组数据必须与蛋白质组学等其他“组学方法”相结合。最近的研究使用多组学方法来研究微生物组和 宿主 相互作用,并确定哮喘内型和潜在的治疗靶点。理论上,抗生素、益生菌、单克隆抗体和饮食可以用于针对哮喘气道和肠道微生物组进行治疗。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注气道微生物组在哮喘中的作用,特别是研究微生物失调如何影响哮喘的发展、严重程度和炎症内型。核心问题是气道微生物群失调是否会导致炎症,或者是炎症的结果。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对现有文献的叙述性综合,没有明确提及系统搜索方法、数据库或荟萃分析。它包括对微生物组和哮喘的各种研究的一般观察和解释。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,气道微生物群失调与哮喘发展、疾病严重程度和炎症内型有关。它强调,不同内型观察到不同的微生物组概况,嗜酸性粒细胞炎症显示出更高的微生物多样性,而中性粒细胞炎症则与多样性降低和病原体主导的微生物组相关。
争议与缺口
关于微生物群失调是哮喘炎症的原因还是结果尚不确定。该评论还指出,大多数研究都集中在细菌成分上,而对病毒组和真菌组的研究有限。此外,鼻病毒和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 等特定病毒在哮喘病理生理学中的作用仍未得到充分研究。
科研与实践启示
该评论建议将微生物组数据与其他“组学方法”相结合,以更好地了解哮喘机制。它还强调了针对微生物组的治疗干预措施的潜力,例如抗生素、益生菌、单克隆抗体和饮食调整,特别是在难治性哮喘病例中。

The airway microbiome in asthma.

Authors: Hollian Richardson, Jennifer Pollock, Rory Chan, James D Chalmers
Original abstract (English)
TOPIC IMPORTANCE: Asthma is a heterogenous airways disease characterized by variable airflow limitation, airway inflammation and bronchial hyperresponsiveness. There is evidence to suggest that the airway microbiome plays an important role in asthma pathophysiology. Most microbiome studies have investigated the bacterial constituents of the microbiome and further studies are needed to investigate the role of the airway virome and mycobiome in asthma. REVIEW FINDINGS: Airway microbiome dysbiosis is associated with asthma development, disease severity and inflammatory endotypes. However, it remains unclear whether dysbiosis drives inflammation or is a result of inflammation. Haemophilus and Moraxella alongside rhinovirus and respiratory syncytial virus have been shown to be associated with the development of asthma in children. In established asthma, airway microbiome dysbiosis is associated with severity and risk of exacerbation. Distinct airway microbiome profiles are observed for the different inflammatory endotypes. Asthmatic patients with eosinophilic inflammation have higher microbial diversity similar to healthy individuals while neutrophilic inflammation is associated with reduced microbial diversity, higher bacterial load and a pathogen-dominated microbiome profile suggesting an important role for dysbiosis in asthma that is currently refractory to anti-T2 biologic therapy. SUMMARY: To understand the role of the microbiome in asthma, microbiome data must be integrated with other 'omic approaches' such as proteomics. Recent studies have used a multi-omic approach to investigate the microbiome and host interaction as well as identifying asthma endotypes and potential therapeutic targets. Antibiotics, probiotics, monoclonal antibodies, and diet could theoretically be used to therapeutically target the airway and gut microbiome in asthma.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the role of the airway microbiome in asthma, particularly examining how microbial dysbiosis may influence asthma development, severity, and inflammatory endotypes. The central question is whether airway microbiome dysbiosis drives inflammation or is a consequence of it.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature, with no explicit mention of systematic search methods, databases, or meta-analysis. It includes general observations and interpretations from various studies on the microbiome and asthma.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that airway microbiome dysbiosis is associated with asthma development, disease severity, and inflammatory endotypes. It highlights that distinct microbiome profiles are observed for different endotypes, with eosinophilic inflammation showing higher microbial diversity and neutrophilic inflammation associated with reduced diversity and a pathogen-dominated microbiome.
Controversies and gaps
There is uncertainty regarding whether microbiome dysbiosis is a cause or a result of inflammation in asthma. The review also notes that most studies have focused on bacterial components, with limited investigation into the virome and mycobiome. Additionally, the role of specific viruses like rhinovirus and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in asthma pathophysiology remains underexplored.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests integrating microbiome data with other 'omic approaches' to better understand asthma mechanisms. It also highlights the potential for therapeutic interventions targeting the microbiome, such as antibiotics, probiotics, monoclonal antibodies, and dietary modifications, particularly in refractory asthma cases.

首个季节中,联邦合格健康中心孕期呼吸道合胞病毒RSVpreF疫苗尼塞韦单抗接种率与非英语母语的关联

Prenatal RSVpreF Vaccination Uptake Associated with Non-English Primary Language During Its First Season at a Federally Qualified Health Center
作者： Corinne Thornton, Samina Bhumbra
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 RSV 和 毛细支气管炎 是婴儿住院和死亡的主要原因。RSVpreF 疫苗 于 2023 年获得批准并发布,适用于孕妇,以预防婴儿在第一个 RSV 季节感染严重 RSV。这是自 COVID-19 疫苗 获得批准以来第一个推荐的产前 疫苗。然而,其他产前 疫苗接种 率仍然较低,近年来总体 疫苗 犹豫不决的情况有所增加。我们的横断面研究描述并比较了在联邦合格健康中心 (FQHC) 接受产前护理的妇女中与第一季收到 RSVpreF 疫苗 相关的社会人口特征和健康行为。方法 对 2023 年 9 月 26 日至 2024 年 3 月 31 日期间在 IN FQHC 印第安纳波利斯市进行产前护理的孕妇的病历进行审查,并筛选其 RSVpreF 疫苗 资格。收集了 疫苗接种 状态(RSVpreF、TDaP、流感和 COVID-19)、人口统计信息、产科史、产前护理的充分性、首选语言和健康的社会决定因素。审查了相应婴儿图表的 尼塞韦单抗 和 2 个月免疫接种和母乳喂养状态。使用统计分析将接受 RSVpreF 疫苗 的女性与未接受 疫苗 的女性进行比较。结果 在 344 名符合 RSVpreF 疫苗 资格的女性中,78 名 (23%) 接受了 疫苗,266 名 (77%) 没有接受。九名 (2.6%) 婴儿接受了 尼塞韦单抗。与未接受 疫苗 的女性 (66%) 相比,接受 RSVpreF 疫苗 的女性使用英语以外的首选语言 (81%) 的人数较多 (P=0.0138)。与未接种疫苗组 (37%) 相比,RSVpreF 疫苗接种组中有更多女性 (53%) 接受了充分的产前护理 (P=0.0128),并且产前护理就诊次数增加了 0.88 次 (P=0.0241)。RSVpreF 疫苗接种组也更频繁地接受 TDaP 和流感 疫苗,但两组的 疫苗 率均高于全国平均水平。种族/民族、年龄、妊娠/胎次、健康保险、交通障碍、粮食不安全和住房不稳定方面没有显着差异。婴儿免疫接种和母乳喂养没有差异。结论 由于后勤困难(包括需要口译服务),语言障碍的存在常常限制医疗保健系统的导航。同样,移民身份也会影响寻求医疗护理和健康保险。然而,我们的研究显示了相反的效果,即主要讲英语以外的语言的人比讲英语的人更有可能收到新的 RSVpreF 疫苗。与其他健康社会决定因素的后勤限制相比,文化因素对接受新 疫苗 的孕妇的影响是否比预期更大,需要进一步调查。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究调查了在联邦合格健康中心 (FQHC) 的第一季孕妇中 RSVpreF 疫苗 的吸收情况,重点关注与 疫苗接种 相关的社会人口特征和健康行为。核心问题是语言障碍或其他社会决定因素是否会影响 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 率。
证据基础与方法
该研究对 2023 年 9 月 26 日至 2024 年 3 月 31 日期间在印第安纳波利斯市 FQHC 进行产前护理的孕妇的病历进行了横断面分析。RSVpreF 疫苗 的资格是根据病历确定的,疫苗接种 状态、人口统计信息、产科史、产前护理充分性、首选语言和健康的社会决定因素是收集的。该研究使用统计分析比较了接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的群体。
共识与结论
研究发现,与未接受 疫苗 的女性相比,接受 RSVpreF 疫苗 的女性更有可能说英语以外的首选语言(81% 比 66%)(P=0.0138)。此外,接种疫苗的女性获得充分产前护理的比例更高(53% vs. 37%),产前护理就诊次数也更多(多出 0.88 次,P=0.0241)。各组之间的种族/民族、年龄、妊娠/产次、健康保险、交通障碍、粮食不安全或住房不稳定没有显着差异。RSVpreF 疫苗接种组的 TDaP 和流感 疫苗 接种率也较高,尽管两组 疫苗 率均高于全国平均水平。
争议与缺口
研究指出,与其他健康社会决定因素的后勤限制相比,文化因素对接受新 疫苗 的孕妇的影响是否比预期更大,需要进一步调查。此外,在婴儿免疫和母乳喂养状态方面没有观察到差异。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究来探索文化因素对孕妇 疫苗 吸收与后勤限制的影响。研究结果表明,语言障碍可能并不像以前认为的那样对 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 产生重大阻碍,而且解决健康的社会决定因素(例如获得产前护理)可能对提高 疫苗接种 率很重要。这些见解可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,以增加特定人群对 疫苗 的吸收。

Prenatal RSVpreF Vaccination Uptake Associated with Non-English Primary Language During Its First Season at a Federally Qualified Health Center

Authors: Corinne Thornton, Samina Bhumbra
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background RSV and bronchiolitis are major causes of hospitalizations and death among infants. The RSVpreF vaccine was approved and released in 2023 for pregnant women to prevent severe RSV in infants during their first RSV season. This is the first prenatal vaccine recommended since the COVID-19 vaccine was approved. However, other prenatal vaccination rates have remained low with a rise in overall vaccine hesitancy in recent years. Our cross-sectional study describes and compares the sociodemographic characteristics and health behaviors associated with first-season receipt of the RSVpreF vaccine among women receiving prenatal care at a federally qualified health center (FQHC). Methods Medical records of pregnant women with prenatal care visits at an Indianapolis, IN FQHC between 9/26/2023 and 3/31/2024 were reviewed and screened for RSVpreF vaccine eligibility. Vaccination status (RSVpreF, TDaP, influenza, and COVID-19), demographic information, obstetric history, adequacy of prenatal care, preferred language, and social determinants of health were collected. Corresponding infant charts were reviewed for nirsevimab and 2-month immunizations and breastfeeding status. Women who received the RSVpreF vaccine were compared to those who did not get the vaccine using statistical analyses. Results Of 344 women eligible for the RSVpreF vaccine, 78 (23%) received the vaccine and 266 (77%) did not. Nine (2.6%) infants received nirsevimab. More women who received the RSVpreF vaccine spoke a preferred language other than English (81%) compared to those who did not receive the vaccine (66%) (P=0.0138). More women received adequate prenatal care (53%) in the RSVpreF vaccinated group compared to the unvaccinated group (37%) (P=0.0128) and had 0.88 more prenatal care visits (P=0.0241). The RSVpreF vaccinated group also received the TDaP and influenza vaccines more often, but both groups were above national average vaccination rates. There were no significant differences in race/ethnicity, age, gravida/parity, health insurance, transportation barriers, food insecurity, and housing instability. There were no differences in infant immunization and breastfeeding. Conclusion The presence of a language barrier often limits navigation of the healthcare system due to logistical difficulties, including requiring interpreter services. Likewise, immigration status influences seeking medical care and health insurance coverage. However, our study shows the opposite effect that those who primarily spoke languages other than English were more likely to receive the new RSVpreF vaccine than those who spoke English. Whether cultural factors have a larger influence than expected on pregnant women receiving new vaccines compared to logistical constraints from other social determinants of health requires further investigation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study investigates the uptake of the RSVpreF vaccine among pregnant women during its first season at a federally qualified health center (FQHC), focusing on sociodemographic characteristics and health behaviors associated with vaccination. The central question is whether language barriers or other social determinants influence RSVpreF vaccination rates.
Evidence base and method
The study is a cross-sectional analysis of medical records from pregnant women with prenatal care visits at an Indianapolis, IN FQHC between September 26, 2023, and March 31, 2024. Eligibility for the RSVpreF vaccine was determined based on medical records, and vaccination status, demographic information, obstetric history, prenatal care adequacy, preferred language, and social determinants of health were collected. The study compares vaccinated and unvaccinated groups using statistical analyses.
Consensus and conclusions
The study found that women who received the RSVpreF vaccine were more likely to speak a preferred language other than English (81% vs. 66%) compared to those who did not receive the vaccine (P=0.0138). Additionally, vaccinated women had a higher rate of adequate prenatal care (53% vs. 37%) and more prenatal care visits (0.88 more visits, P=0.0241). There were no significant differences in race/ethnicity, age, gravida/parity, health insurance, transportation barriers, food insecurity, or housing instability between the groups. The RSVpreF vaccinated group also had higher rates of receiving TDaP and influenza vaccines, though both groups were above national average vaccination rates.
Controversies and gaps
The study notes that whether cultural factors have a larger influence than expected on pregnant women receiving new vaccines compared to logistical constraints from other social determinants of health requires further investigation. Additionally, no differences were observed in infant immunization and breastfeeding status.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to explore the influence of cultural factors on vaccine uptake among pregnant women compared to logistical constraints. The findings suggest that language barriers may not be as significant a deterrent to RSVpreF vaccination as previously thought, and that addressing social determinants of health, such as access to prenatal care, may be important for improving vaccination rates. These insights could inform targeted interventions to increase vaccine uptake among specific populations.

生命早期呼吸道感染及其与慢性肺病的关联:专家观点

Early-life respiratory infections and their association with chronic lung disease: an expert perspective
作者： Susanna Esposito, Valentina Fainardi, Nicola Principi
摘要中文翻译简介:生命早期呼吸道感染可能会在胎儿、新生儿和婴儿关键时期扰乱肺部发育,此时肺泡化、气道和血管成熟以及免疫编程仍然活跃,可能会造成急性疾病以外的影响。涵盖领域:这篇叙述性综述检查了产前和产后呼吸道感染和长期呼吸道结局,重点是绒毛膜羊膜炎、胎儿炎症反应、支气管肺发育不良、呼吸道合胞病毒 和其他病原体。通过使用与早期感染、肺部发育、支气管肺发育不良、喘息、哮喘、肺功能、COPD 和呼吸 死亡率 相关的术语对生物医学数据库和参考文献列表进行有针对性的检索来确定文献。优先考虑系统评价、荟萃分析、纵向 队列研究、机制研究和相关报告。提出的途径包括肺泡和血管生长受损、上皮损伤、免疫失调、免疫力训练、气道重塑和肺功能峰值降低。评估了早期下呼吸道感染与反复喘息、哮喘、肺功能受损、COPD 样轨迹和呼吸系统 死亡率 之间的联系的流行病学证据。专家意见:早期呼吸道感染可能通过病原体、时间、宿主 易感性、早产和环境暴露之间的相互作用导致慢性发病。因果关系仍然难以确定。新推出的 呼吸道合胞病毒 预防策略可能会阐明预防严重感染是否可以改善长期肺部健康。标准化结果和纵向随访至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 及其与慢性肺病的关系,特别是在生命早期。核心问题是关键发育阶段的 RSV 感染是否会导致长期呼吸系统疾病,如喘息、哮喘、肺功能受损和呼吸系统疾病 死亡率。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括生物医学数据库和参考列表,使用与早期感染、肺部发育、支气管肺发育不良、喘息、哮喘、肺功能、COPD 和呼吸 死亡率 相关的术语进行搜索。优先考虑系统评价、荟萃分析、纵向 队列研究、机制研究和相关报告。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,生命早期 RSV 感染可能通过病原体、时间、宿主 易感性、早产和环境暴露等相互作用,导致慢性呼吸道疾病。然而,由于这些相互作用的复杂性,因果关系仍然难以确定。
争议与缺口
证据存在异质性,RSV 与慢性肺病之间因果关系的发现相互矛盾。该审查强调需要标准化结果和纵向随访,以更好地了解 RSV 感染的长期影响。此外,RSV 与慢性呼吸系统疾病之间的联系机制尚未完全阐明。
科研与实践启示
RSV 的新预防策略可能有助于了解减少严重感染是否可以改善长期肺部健康。研究应优先考虑标准化结果、纵向研究和机制研究,以阐明 RSV 对呼吸系统健康的因果途径和长期影响。

Early-life respiratory infections and their association with chronic lung disease: an expert perspective

Authors: Susanna Esposito, Valentina Fainardi, Nicola Principi
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Early-life respiratory infections may disrupt lung development during critical fetal, neonatal, and infant windows, when alveolarization, airway and vascular maturation, and immune programming remain active, potentially causing effects beyond the acute illness. AREAS COVERED: This narrative review examines prenatal and postnatal respiratory infections and long-term respiratory outcomes, with emphasis on chorioamnionitis, fetal inflammatory responses, bronchopulmonary dysplasia, respiratory syncytial virus, and other pathogens. Literature was identified through targeted searches of biomedical databases and reference lists using terms related to early-life infection, lung development, bronchopulmonary dysplasia, wheeze, asthma, lung function, COPD, and respiratory mortality. Priority was given to systematic reviews, meta-analyses, longitudinal cohorts, mechanistic studies, and relevant reports. Proposed pathways include impaired alveolar and vascular growth, epithelial injury, immune dysregulation, trained immunity, airway remodeling, and reduced peak lung function. Epidemiological evidence linking early lower respiratory tract infection with recurrent wheeze, asthma, impaired lung function, COPD-like trajectories, and respiratory mortality is appraised. EXPERT OPINION: Early respiratory infections likely contribute to chronic morbidity through interactions among pathogen, timing, host susceptibility, prematurity, and environmental exposures. Causality remains difficult to establish. Newly available respiratory syncytial virus prevention strategies may clarify whether preventing severe infection improves long-term lung health. Standardized outcomes and longitudinal follow-up are essential.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and its association with chronic lung disease, particularly in early life. The central question is whether RSV infections during critical developmental stages contribute to long-term respiratory outcomes such as wheeze, asthma, impaired lung function, and respiratory mortality.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base includes biomedical databases and reference lists, with searches using terms related to early-life infection, lung development, bronchopulmonary dysplasia, wheeze, asthma, lung function, COPD, and respiratory mortality. Priority was given to systematic reviews, meta-analyses, longitudinal cohorts, mechanistic studies, and relevant reports.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that early-life RSV infections likely contribute to chronic respiratory morbidity through interactions involving pathogen, timing, host susceptibility, prematurity, and environmental exposures. However, causality remains difficult to establish due to the complexity of these interactions.
Controversies and gaps
There is heterogeneity in the evidence, with conflicting findings on the causal relationship between RSV and chronic lung disease. The review highlights the need for standardized outcomes and longitudinal follow-up to better understand the long-term effects of RSV infections. Additionally, the mechanisms linking RSV to chronic respiratory outcomes are not fully elucidated.
Research and practice implications
New prevention strategies for RSV may provide insights into whether reducing severe infections can improve long-term lung health. Research should prioritize standardized outcomes, longitudinal studies, and mechanistic investigations to clarify the causal pathways and long-term impacts of RSV on respiratory health.

免疫功能低下儿科患者呼吸道病毒感染结果的系统评价

A Systematic Review of Respiratory Viral Infections Outcomes in Immunocompromised Pediatric Patients
作者： Amel Omer, K. Agoramurthi, Abubaker Koko, Nagla Suliman
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 呼吸道病毒感染 (RVI) 对患有癌症和造血细胞移植 (HCT) 的高危儿科患者构成了重大挑战。尽管有报告记录了其负担,但很少有研究系统分析免疫功能低下患者 RVI 结果的数据。方法 根据 2019 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 10 月 1 日发表的数据,对患有发热性中性粒细胞减少症 (FN) 的免疫功能低下儿科患者、HCT 接受者和实体器官移植接受者 (SOT) 的呼吸道病毒感染结果进行系统评价。根据量化效果估计的存在,特别是比值比 (OR)、风险比 (HR)、临床负担统计(中位发热),纳入了 28 项研究。持续时间)和病毒共感染模式。使用叙述性方法对结果进行分析,按结果类别组织调查结果,以确保描述性统计数据和量化风险估计的综合报告。结果 在所分析的 28 项研究中,RVI 在发热性中性粒细胞减少症病例中的发生率为 76.5%,而对照组为 48.6%。虽然鼻病毒 (HRV/HRE) 是多项研究中最常见的病原体 (36.8%、33.1%、61%、47%),但 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 与严重后果密切相关,包括下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 进展和菌血症。很大一部分 RVI 是院内感染(一项针对 HCT 患者的研究中为 79%),并且病毒合并感染很常见,发生率高达 55%,并且通常涉及 RSV。此外,RVI 发病前的抗生素暴露被确定为 HCT 受者 LRTI 进展的危险因素,HSCT 前 RVI 与 ICU 转移(OR = 3.45)和 治疗 相关 死亡率 增加显着相关(OR = 5.57)。人类副流感病毒 3 型 (PIV-3) URTI 与气流阻塞 (HR 1.87) 显着相关,而 RSV URTI 与 毛细支气管炎 闭塞症候群 (HR 2.65) 表现出更强的相关性。RVI 显着影响临床管理,导致 22%-33% 的患者化疗延迟,同时资源利用率很高,包括 16% 住院、50% 肠外抗生素使用和 18.6% 入​​院 PICU。最后,护理方面存在显着差距,因为只有 22% 的流感儿科癌症患者接受了奥司他韦治疗。结论 呼吸道病毒感染给免疫功能低下的儿童带来了沉重负担,导致显着的发病率、死亡率、资源使用和长期并发症。RSV、PIV-3 和流感显着增加 死亡率,突出表明需要更严格的感染控制和预防。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
呼吸道病毒感染 (RVI) 对患有癌症和造血细胞移植 (HCT) 的高危儿科患者构成重大挑战。该综述重点关注免疫功能低下患者的 RVI 结局,包括发热性中性粒细胞减少症 (FN)、HCT 受者和实体器官移植受者 (SOT)。
证据基础与方法
我们对2019年1月1日至2025年10月1日期间发表的数据进行了系统评价,以描述免疫功能低下的发热性中性粒细胞减少症(FN)儿科患者、HCT接受者和实体器官移植接受者(SOT)的呼吸道病毒感染结果。使用叙述性方法对结果进行分析,按结果类别组织结果,以确保描述性统计数据和量化风险估计的综合报告。
共识与结论
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 与严重后果密切相关,包括下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 进展和菌血症。RSV URTI 与 毛细支气管炎 闭塞综合征 (HR 2.65) 表现出更强的关联性。RVI 显着影响临床管理,导致 22%-33% 的患者化疗延迟,同时资源利用率很高,包括 16% 住院、50% 肠外抗生素使用和 18.6% 入​​院 PICU。
争议与缺口
值得注意的是,治疗方面存在显着差距,因为只有 22% 的流感儿童癌症患者接受了奥司他韦治疗。此外,RVI 发病前的抗生素暴露被确定为 HCT 受者 LRTI 进展的危险因素。
科研与实践启示
RSV、PIV-3 和流感显着增加 死亡率,突出表明需要更严格的感染控制和预防。

A Systematic Review of Respiratory Viral Infections Outcomes in Immunocompromised Pediatric Patients

Authors: Amel Omer, K. Agoramurthi, Abubaker Koko, Nagla Suliman
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Respiratory Viral Infections (RVI) pose a significant challenge in high-risk pediatric patients with cancer and Hematopoietic Cell Transplant (HCT). Although reports have documented its burden, few studies have systematically analyzed data on outcomes of RVI in immunocompromised patients. Methods A systematic review was undertaken to describe respiratory viral infections outcome in immunocompromised pediatrics patients with febrile neutropenia (FN), HCT recipients, and solid organ transplant recipients (SOT) from published data between January 1, 2019 and October 1, 2025. Twenty-eight studies were included based on the presence of quantified effect estimates specifically Odds Ratio (OR), Hazards Ratio (HR), clinical burden statistics (median fever duration), and viral co-infection patterns. The results were analyzed using a narrative approach, organizing findings by outcome category to ensure comprehensive reporting of descriptive statistics and quantified risk estimates. Results Of the twenty-eight studies analyzed, RVIs were highly prevalent in 76.5% of febrile neutropenia cases versus 48.6% in controls. While Rhinovirus (HRV/HRE) was the most frequently identified pathogen across multiple studies (36.8%, 33.1%, 61%, 47%), Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) was strongly linked with severe outcomes, including Lower Respiratory Tract Infection (LRTI) progression and bacteremia. A high proportion of RVIs were nosocomial (79% in one study of HCT patients), and viral co-infections were common, occurring in up to 55% of cases and often involving RSV. Furthermore, antibiotic exposure prior to RVI onset was identified as a risk factor for LRTI progression in HCT recipients, and Pre-HSCT RVI was significantly associated with ICU transfer(OR = 3.45) and increased Treatment-Related Mortality (OR = 5.57). Human Parainfluenza Virus type 3 (PIV-3) URTIs were significantly associated with Air Flow Obstruction (HR 1.87), while RSV URTIs exhibited a stronger association with Bronchiolitis Obliterans Syndrome (HR 2.65). RVIs significantly impacted clinical management, resulting in chemotherapy delays for 22%-33% of patients, alongside considerable resource utilization including 16% hospitalization, 50% parenteral antibiotic use, and 18.6% PICU admission. Finally, a significant gap in care was noted as only 22% of pediatric cancer patients with influenza received oseltamivir. Conclusion Respiratory Viral Infections impose a substantial burden on immunocompromised children, leading to significant morbidity, mortality, resource use, and long-term complications. RSV, PIV-3, and influenza significantly increase mortality, highlighting the need for stricter infection control and prevention.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory Viral Infections (RVI) pose a significant challenge in high-risk pediatric patients with cancer and Hematopoietic Cell Transplant (HCT). The review focuses on outcomes of RVI in immunocompromised patients, including febrile neutropenia (FN), HCT recipients, and solid organ transplant recipients (SOT).
Evidence base and method
A systematic review was undertaken to describe respiratory viral infections outcomes in immunocompromised pediatrics patients with febrile neutropenia (FN), HCT recipients, and solid organ transplant recipients (SOT) from published data between January 1, 2019 and October 1, 2025. The results were analyzed using a narrative approach, organizing findings by outcome category to ensure comprehensive reporting of descriptive statistics and quantified risk estimates.
Consensus and conclusions
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) was strongly linked with severe outcomes, including Lower Respiratory Tract Infection (LRTI) progression and bacteremia. RSV URTIs exhibited a stronger association with Bronchiolitis Obliterans Syndrome (HR 2.65). RVIs significantly impacted clinical management, resulting in chemotherapy delays for 22%-33% of patients, alongside considerable resource utilization including 16% hospitalization, 50% parenteral antibiotic use, and 18.6% PICU admission.
Controversies and gaps
A significant gap in care was noted as only 22% of pediatric cancer patients with influenza received oseltamivir. Furthermore, antibiotic exposure prior to RVI onset was identified as a risk factor for LRTI progression in HCT recipients.
Research and practice implications
RSV, PIV-3, and influenza significantly increase mortality, highlighting the need for stricter infection control and prevention.

社论:病毒合并感染:挑战和治疗方法

Editorial: Viral co-infections: challenges and therapeutic approaches
作者： S. Louise Cosby, Anna Rosa Garbuglia
摘要中文翻译虽然流感病毒合并感染可能并不常见,但细菌或支原体合并感染却更为常见,并且可能会导致症状和临床结果增加。https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/microbiology/articles/10.3389/fmicb.2025.1719387/full 利用苏州大学第四附属医院的数据进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究。发现潘顿瓦伦丁杀白细胞素 (PVL) 阳性、甲氧西林敏感 ST22 菌株可导致合并感染甲型流感或 SARS-CoV-2 的患者致命,并伴有严重的白细胞减少症。同样,肺炎支原体 (Mp) 的合并感染也经常被记录。为了检查这种重要但尚未充分研究的双重感染,Ma 等人。https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/microbiology/articles/10.3389/fmicb.2026.1787294/full?在感染 H1NI(猪流感)的仓鼠中建立了一个模型,该模型表明,与其他感染组相比,接受后续 Mp 攻击的 H1N1 感染动物表现出明显更严重的组织病理学病变、更高的病原体负荷和失调的细胞因子反应。Lei 及其同事进行的全面综述讨论了流感介导的细菌感染易感性的主要机制,特别关注上皮屏障损伤、免疫细胞功能障碍和特定免疫细胞的作用,及其诱导过度炎症反应的效应途径以及 治疗 选项。https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/microbiology/articles/10.3389/fmicb.2025.1673643/full。作者强调,深入了解病原体和 宿主 之间的相互作用将有助于开发预防流感后细菌感染和 治疗 的治疗方法。多年来,合并感染是人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 研究的一个成熟的基础领域。本期的两篇文章强调了基因组学和免疫学方法对于了解 HIV 毒株重组、共感染和 疫苗 在 HIV 感染者中的功效的重要性。Ling 及其同事的一项研究 https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript 表明,循环重组形式 (CRF) 在 HIV-1 的全球 传播 中发挥着关键作用,源自原始 CRF 的第二代重组病毒不断出现。此外,这些 CRF 与共受体向性的关键变化相关。这项研究强烈支持持续的 基因组监测 对于预防策略的重要性。https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript 检查了免疫功能低下的艾滋病毒感染者群体的体液 免疫应答 是否对现有 covid 亚谱系和随后的 covid 疫苗 感染存在交叉保护。该研究强调了定期监测 免疫应答 的迫切需要,以应对针对此类个体中出现的新变种的新冠病毒 疫苗。另一个免疫挑战群体是接受维持性血液透析的终末期肾病(ESRD)患者,他们容易贫血。Özsoy 及其同事的一项研究 https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript 提出并检验了先前的 B19V 细小病毒感染可能导致贫血严重程度或对红细胞生成刺激剂 (ESA) 产生抵抗力的担忧。除了人类群体中的合并感染研究之外,动物物种中的合并感染研究也是兽医学、理解和利用的一个重要领域天然病毒模型和 监测 以及针对人畜共患疾病的保护。蝙蝠对许多病毒是天然的 储存宿主,但有关肝炎病毒的信息有限。https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript 检查了中国蝙蝠体内蝙蝠乙型肝炎病毒 (BtHBV) 的多样性和 进化 及其与蝙蝠丁型肝炎病毒 (BtHDV) 的共感染,结果表明它们对这两种病毒都是天然的 宿主。基因组监测、免疫学研究和实验模型的进展揭示了病原体如何相互作用、免疫应答 和感染时间决定疾病进展。此外,仍然迫切需要特定的 抗病毒药物 疗法,本期的 3 篇论文就是例证。传统中医 (TCM) 致力于识别和靶向目前传统药物 治疗 中未使用的新物质。Zeng 及其同事进行了系统综述 https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript 检查了古典中医文献中记载的用于天星热(与登革热病毒密切相关)治疗 的草药制剂,目的是指导登革热预防和 治疗 的临床实践和现代药物开发。刘等人的进一步审查。https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript 全面总结了呼吸道合胞病毒单克隆抗体和小分子抗病毒药物的现状。梁和团队的第三项实验研究 https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript 检查了 疫苗 针对猪繁殖与呼吸综合征病毒 (PRRSV) 的替代治疗方法。罗汉果苷 V 是一种三萜化合物(源自罗汉果),此前已被证明具有多种生物活性,包括抗氧化、抗炎和抗癌特性,并具有清除自由基和减轻氧化应激的能力,并在体外进行了评估。该化合物不仅直接抑制 PRRSV 复制,而且显着上调免疫调节细胞因子的基因表达。• 合并感染通常不常见,但会显着恶化疾病严重程度,特别是在病毒-细菌组合或高危人群中。• 病原体相互作用和感染顺序在临床结果和 免疫应答 中发挥着关键作用。• 持续的 监测、疫苗接种 策略和靶向治疗的开发对于解决不断发展的问题至关重要总体而言,该研究强调需要持续监测、综合预防策略和改进治疗方法来管理复杂的合并感染。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是涉及 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的合并感染,特别是在与细菌病原体、其他病毒和 免疫应答 相互作用的情况下。它研究了管理 RSV 合并感染的临床意义、机制和治疗方法,重点是 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 亚型。核心问题是合并感染如何影响 RSV 感染者的疾病严重程度和 免疫应答。
证据基础与方法
该评价基于多项研究的结合,包括在苏州大学第四附属医院进行的回顾性 队列研究 研究。它还引用了一项针对感染 H1N1 的仓鼠以及随后受到肺炎支原体攻击的研究,该研究显示出严重的组织病理学病变和细胞因子反应失调。该综述包括对治疗与登革热病毒密切相关的天星热的中药中草药的系统分析,以及针对 RSV 的单克隆抗体和小分子 抗病毒药物 的综述。
共识与结论
该综述强调,RSV 和细菌病原体的共同感染会显着恶化临床结果。它还强调了 基因组监测、免疫学研究和实验模型在理解病原体相互作用和 免疫应答 方面的重要性。研究结果表明,RSV 合并感染是研究的一个关键领域,特别是在高危人群中,以及开发靶向治疗。
争议与缺口
审查指出,虽然在某些情况下混合感染有详细记录,但有关 RSV 与其他病毒混合感染的具体机制的证据有限。此外,中药在RSV 治疗中的作用仍有待研究,并且需要对不同宿主人群中RSV与其他病原体之间的相互作用进行更多研究。
科研与实践启示
该综述呼吁进一步研究 RSV 联合感染的机制,特别是细菌和其他病毒病原体的联合感染。它还强调需要改进监测、开发特定的抗病毒药物疗法以及探索替代治疗(例如中药)。这些努力对于管理 RSV 相关疾病和改善弱势群体的治疗效果至关重要。

Editorial: Viral co-infections: challenges and therapeutic approaches

Authors: S. Louise Cosby, Anna Rosa Garbuglia
Original abstract (English)
While influenza virus co-infections may be infrequent, combined bacterial or mycoplasma infections are much more commonplace and may give rise to increased symptomology and clinical outcomes. https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/microbiology/articles/10.3389/fmicb.2025.1719387/full carried out a retrospective cohort study utilising data from the Fourth Affiliated Hospital of Soochow University. Panton-Valentine Leukocidin (PVL)-positive, methicillin-susceptible ST22 strains were found to cause fatal outcomes in patients co-infected with influenza A or SARS-CoV-2, associated with severe leukopenia. Similarly, co-infection with Mycoplasma pneumoniae (Mp) is frequently documented. To examine this important but understudied coinfection, Ma et al. https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/microbiology/articles/10.3389/fmicb.2026.1787294/full? established a model in hamsters with H1NI (swine flu) which demonstrated that H1N1 infected animals which received a follow up Mp challenge exhibited significantly more severe histopathological lesions, higher pathogen loads, and dysregulated cytokine responses compared to other infection groups. A comprehensive review by Lei and colleagues discussed primary mechanisms of Influenza-mediated susceptibility to bacterial infection, with particular focus on epithelial barrier damage, immune cell dysfunction and the roles of specific immune cells, and their effector pathways in inducing hyperinflammatory responses aa well as treatment options. https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/microbiology/articles/10.3389/fmicb.2025.1673643/full . The authors emphasise that an in depth understanding of the interactions between the pathogens and the host will assist in the development of therapeutics for the prevention and treatment of post-influenza bacterial infection.Co-infections are a well, established fundamental area of research for Human Immunodeficiency virus (HIV) over many years. The importance of both genomic and immunological approaches to understanding HIV strain recombination, co-infections and vaccine efficacy in HIV infected individuals, are emphasised in 2 articles in this issue. A study by Ling and colleagues https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript shows that circulating recombinant forms (CRFs) play a critical role in the global transmission of HIV-1 with further second-generation recombinant viruses, derived from the original CRFs, continually emerging. Furthermore, these CRFs are associated with critical changes in co-receptor tropism. This study strongly supports the importance of sustained genomic surveillance for prevention strategies. https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript examined whether there was cross-protection from the humoral immune response of immunocompromised populations of people living with HIV to both infection with existing covid sub-lineages and subsequent covid vaccines. The study highlighted the crucial need for regular monitoring of immune responses to new covid vaccines targeting emerging variants in this category of individuals. Another immunologically challenged group are patients with end-stage renal disease (ESRD) receiving maintenance haemodialysis who are predisposed to anaemia. A study by Özsoy and colleagues https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript raised and examined concerns that prior B19V Parvovirus infection may contribute to anaemia severity or resistance to erythropoiesisstimulating agents (ESAs).In addition to study of co-infections in human populations, research on co-infection in animal species is an important area for veterinary medicine, understanding and utilising natural virus models and for surveillance and protection against zoonotic diseases. Bats are natural reservoirs for many viruses but with information on hepatitis viruses limited. https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript examined the diversity and evolution of bat hepatitis B viruses (BtHBVs) and their co-infection with bat hepatitis D virus (BtHDV) in bats in China and showed that they are natural hosts for both viruses.Advances in genomic surveillance, immunological studies, and experimental models reveal how pathogen interactions, immune responses, and infection timing shape disease progression. In addition, there remains an urgent need for specific antiviral therapeutics, as exemplified by 3 papers in this issue. Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) is of interest to identify and target new substances not currently used for conventional pharmaceutical treatments. A systematic review by Zeng and colleagues https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript examined herbal agents documented in classical TCM texts for the treatment of Tianxing fever (closely related to dengue virus) with the aim of guiding clinical practice and modern drug development for dengue prevention and treatment. A further review by Liu et al. https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript comprehensively summarises the current status of Respiratory Syncytial Virus monoclonal antibodies and small-molecule antivirals. A third experimental study by Liang and team https://research-topic-management-app.frontiersin.org/manage/66895/manuscript examined an alternative therapeutic approach to vaccines for porcine reproductive and respiratory syndrome virus (PRRSV). Mogroside V, a triterpenoid compound (derived from Siraitia grosvenorii) which has previously been shown to exhibit diverse biological activities including antioxidant, anti-inflammatory, and anti-cancer properties, with the capacity to scavenge free radicals and mitigate oxidative stress was assessed in vitro. This compound not only directly inhibited PRRSV replication but also significantly upregulated the gene expression of immunomodulatory cytokines.• Co-infections are generally uncommon but can significantly worsen disease severity, particularly in viral-bacterial combinations or high-risk populations.• Pathogen interactions and infection order play a critical role in clinical outcomes and immune responses.• Ongoing surveillance, vaccination strategies, and development of targeted therapies are essential to address the evolving complexity of co-infections.Overall, the research underscores the need for continued monitoring, integrated prevention strategies, and improved therapeutic approaches to manage complex co-infections.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on co-infections involving respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), particularly in the context of interactions with bacterial pathogens, other viruses, and immune responses. It examines the clinical implications, mechanisms, and therapeutic approaches for managing RSV co-infections, with an emphasis on RSV-A and RSV-B subtypes. The central question is how co-infections affect disease severity and immune responses in RSV-infected individuals.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a combination of studies, including a retrospective cohort study conducted at the Fourth Affiliated Hospital of Soochow University. It also references a study on hamsters with H1N1 infection and a subsequent challenge with Mycoplasma pneumoniae, which demonstrated severe histopathological lesions and dysregulated cytokine responses. The review includes a systematic analysis of herbal agents in traditional Chinese medicine for treating Tianxing fever, which is closely related to dengue virus, and a review of monoclonal antibodies and small-molecule antivirals for RSV.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that co-infections with RSV and bacterial pathogens can significantly worsen clinical outcomes. It also underscores the importance of genomic surveillance, immunological studies, and experimental models in understanding pathogen interactions and immune responses. The findings suggest that RSV co-infections are a critical area for research, particularly in high-risk populations and for developing targeted therapies.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while co-infections are well-documented in some contexts, there is limited evidence on the specific mechanisms of RSV co-infections with other viruses. Additionally, the role of traditional Chinese medicine in RSV treatment remains understudied, and there is a need for more research on the interactions between RSV and other pathogens in different host populations.
Research and practice implications
The review calls for further research into the mechanisms of RSV co-infections, especially with bacterial and other viral pathogens. It also emphasizes the need for improved surveillance, development of specific antiviral therapeutics, and exploration of alternative treatments such as traditional Chinese medicine. These efforts are crucial for managing RSV-related diseases and improving outcomes in vulnerable populations.

提升新生儿呼吸道合胞病毒免疫预防信心的干预措施展望

Perspectives on an Intervention to Increase Confidence in Newborn RSV Immunoprophylaxis.
作者： Paul L Aronson, Jaspreet Loyal, Cristina Del Valle, Varshini Batti, Rakesh D Mistry, Eury Hong, Elena Aragona
摘要中文翻译目标:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫预防是有效的,但与其他推荐的新生儿干预措施相比,采用率较低。我们的目的是确定在健康新生儿病房实施干预措施的障碍和推动因素,以提高家长对新生儿 RSV 免疫预防的信心。患者和方法:我们于 2025 年 8 月 13 日至 2025 年 10 月 22 日期间,对城市四级护理医院新生儿病房有目的地抽样的临床医生和家长进行了单独的半结构化访谈。访谈引出了临床医生和家长对 RSV 免疫预防、咨询过程、障碍和促进因素的看法,以及他们对设计和实施干预措施的看法。增加父母的信心。4 位研究人员使用演绎和归纳方法独立编码每个利益相关者组中的前 2 个记录,以确定主题。然后,两名研究人员将代码应用于剩余的转录本,通过协商一致解决了差异。结果:我们采访了 18 名临床医生(5 名住院医生、3 名普通儿科医生、3 名产科医生、7 名护士/护士长)和 24 名家长。我们确定了与增强父母信心的干预相关的几个主题,包括障碍和促进因素:(1)对信息的信任和不信任;(2) RSV免疫预防与疫苗的区别;(3) 提供数据和个人经验的价值。我们还确定了与实施相关的主题:(1) 支持一种工具来增强而不是取代临床医生咨询;(2) 视觉效果的使用;(3) 不同健康素养和语言的父母均可获得帮助;(4)时机的重要性。结论:确定的主题可以为干预措施的设计和实施提供信息,以提高父母对新生儿 RSV 免疫预防的信心。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是了解在健康新生儿病房实施干预措施的障碍和推动因素,以提高家长对新生儿 RSV 免疫预防的信心。目标是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),特别是 RSV-A 和 RSV-B,核心问题是如何提高家长对新生儿 RSV 免疫预防的信心。
证据基础与方法
该评价基于2025年8月13日至2025年10月22日期间对城市四级护理医院新生儿病房有目的地抽样的临床医生和家长进行的个人、半结构化访谈。四名研究人员使用演绎和归纳方法对每个利益相关者组中的前2份记录进行独立编码,以确定主题,然后两名研究人员将代码应用于其余记录,通过协商一致解决差异。
共识与结论
该综述识别出多个与提升新生儿呼吸道合胞病毒免疫预防信心相关的主题,包括对信息的信任与不信任、呼吸道合胞病毒免疫预防与抗病毒药物的区分,以及提供数据与个人经验的价值。综述还强调了实施层面的主题,如支持使用工具辅助(但不替代)临床医师咨询、视觉化呈现、满足不同健康素养水平及语言背景父母的可及性,以及时机选择的重要性。
争议与缺口
该审查不直接报告争议或证据差距。然而,它指出,调查结果基于定性访谈,可能无法捕捉实施挑战的全部范围或提供有关拟议干预措施有效性的定量数据。
科研与实践启示
确定的主题可以为干预措施的设计和实施提供信息,以提高父母对新生儿 RSV 免疫预防的信心。这包括开发支持临床医生咨询的工具、纳入视觉效果、确保不同人群的可及性以及适当地安排干预时间。

Perspectives on an Intervention to Increase Confidence in Newborn RSV Immunoprophylaxis.

Authors: Paul L Aronson, Jaspreet Loyal, Cristina Del Valle, Varshini Batti, Rakesh D Mistry, Eury Hong, Elena Aragona
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunoprophylaxis is efficacious, yet uptake is low in comparison with other recommended newborn interventions. We aimed to identify barriers to and enablers of implementation of an intervention in the well newborn unit to increase parental confidence in newborn RSV immunoprophylaxis. PATIENTS AND METHODS: We conducted individual, semi-structured interviews of purposively sampled clinicians and parents at an urban, quaternary-care hospital well newborn unit between August 13, 2025, and October 22, 2025. Interviews elicited clinicians' and parents' perceptions of RSV immunoprophylaxis, the counseling process, barriers and facilitators, and their perspectives on the design and implementation of an intervention to increase parental confidence. Using both deductive and inductive approaches, 4 investigators independently coded the first 2 transcripts in each stakeholder group to identify themes. Two investigators then applied codes to the remaining transcripts, resolving discrepancies by consensus. RESULTS: We interviewed 18 clinicians (5 hospitalists, 3 general pediatricians, 3 obstetricians, 7 nurses/nurse managers) and 24 parents. We identified several themes related to an intervention to increase parental confidence, including barriers and facilitators: (1) trust and mistrust of information; (2) differentiation of RSV immunoprophylaxis from a vaccine; and (3) value of providing both data and personal experiences. We also identified themes related to implementation: (1) support for a tool to augment, but not replace, clinician counseling; (2) use of visuals; (3) accessibility for parents of varying health literacy and languages; and (4) importance of timing. CONCLUSIONS: The identified themes can inform the design and implementation of an intervention to increase parental confidence in newborn RSV immunoprophylaxis.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on understanding barriers and enablers to implementing an intervention in the well newborn unit to increase parental confidence in newborn RSV immunoprophylaxis. The target is respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), specifically RSV-A and RSV-B, with the central question being how to improve parental confidence in RSV immunoprophylaxis for newborns.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on individual, semi-structured interviews conducted with purposively sampled clinicians and parents at an urban, quaternary-care hospital well newborn unit between August 13, 2025, and October 22, 2025. Four investigators independently coded the first 2 transcripts in each stakeholder group using deductive and inductive approaches to identify themes, and then two investigators applied the codes to the remaining transcripts, resolving discrepancies by consensus.
Consensus and conclusions
The review identified several themes related to increasing parental confidence in newborn RSV immunoprophylaxis, including trust and mistrust of information, differentiation of RSV immunoprophylaxis from a vaccine, and the value of providing both data and personal experiences. It also highlighted implementation themes such as support for a tool to augment, but not replace, clinician counseling, use of visuals, accessibility for parents of varying health literacy and languages, and the importance of timing.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report controversies or evidence gaps directly. However, it notes that the findings are based on qualitative interviews, which may not capture the full scope of implementation challenges or provide quantitative data on the effectiveness of the proposed intervention.
Research and practice implications
The identified themes can inform the design and implementation of an intervention to increase parental confidence in newborn RSV immunoprophylaxis. This includes developing tools that support clinician counseling, incorporating visuals, ensuring accessibility for diverse populations, and timing the intervention appropriately.

打破通道障碍:同荧光、不同Tm探针策略实现20分钟内在六通道PCR平台上检测七靶标呼吸道病毒

Breaking the channel barrier: same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy enables seven-target respiratory virus detection on a six-channel PCR platform within 20 minutes

RSV 疫苗 优惠范围扩大到 65-74 岁的高风险成年人 - Wired-Gov

RSV vaccine offer extended to higher-risk adults aged 65–74 - Wired-Gov
新闻简报作为 2026 年 9 月限时计划的一部分,英国 NHS 威尔士已将 RSV 疫苗 方案扩大到 65-74 岁的高风险成年人。这一决定基于证据表明,该年龄段中免疫抑制或患有慢性呼吸道疾病的成年人面临着与 75 岁以上老年人相似的住院风险,后者已经常规接受 疫苗。该计划旨在减少严重疾病和住院人数,特别是在 RSV 最危险的冬季。威尔士公共卫生部门的一项研究发现,2024-25 年冬季,RSV 疫苗接种 将 75-79 岁成年人的住院风险降低了 75%,2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 4 月期间,该人群与 RSV 相关的住院人数下降了 13% 以上。该举措旨在缓解医疗服务压力并保护弱势群体。威尔士公共卫生部门鼓励符合条件的成年人应邀接种疫苗。

时间
RSV 疫苗 优惠于 2026 年 9 月延长,该计划在此期间运行。影响中提到的研究是在 2024-25 年冬季进行的。
地点与对象
该活动与威尔士有关,专门针对患有严重 RSV 疾病风险较高的 65 至 74 岁成年人,包括那些免疫抑制或患有慢性呼吸道疾病的人。
事件
作为限时计划的一部分,英国 NHS 威尔士将 RSV 疫苗 优惠范围扩大到 65 至 74 岁的高风险成年人。这一决定是基于证据显示该年龄组与 75 岁以上人群相比住院风险相似。
规模、影响与风险
该计划旨在减少 65 至 74 岁成年人的住院率和严重疾病,特别是在 RSV 最危险的冬季。研究发现,2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 4 月期间,75-79 岁成年人的住院风险降低了 75%,RSV 相关住院率降低了 13%。
应对、状态与不确定性
该计划目前正在积极实施,旨在保护高危成年人。威尔士公共卫生部门鼓励符合条件的个人应邀接种疫苗。该研究的结果基于 2024-25 年冬季的数据,该计划尚处于早期阶段。
A high priorityPublic-health news

RSV vaccine offer extended to higher-risk adults aged 65–74 - Wired-Gov

News Brief
The UK's NHS Wales has extended the RSV vaccine offer to higher-risk adults aged 65–74 as part of a time-limited programme in September 2026. This decision is based on evidence showing that adults in this age group who are immunosuppressed or have chronic respiratory disease face similar hospitalisation risks as those over 75, who are already routinely offered the vaccine. The programme aims to reduce severe illness and hospital admissions, particularly during the winter months when RSV is most dangerous. A study by Public Health Wales found that RSV vaccination reduced hospitalisation risk by 75% among adults aged 75–79 during the 2024–25 winter season, and RSV-related hospital admissions fell by over 13% in that group between October 2024 and April 2025. The initiative is intended to ease pressure on healthcare services and protect vulnerable individuals. Public Health Wales is encouraging eligible adults to get vaccinated when invited.
Original source text (English)
MENU sign in Home Register About Us Latest News My WiredGov WiredGov Plus Contracts Locator Tender Locator Supplier Locator Industry News Search for Press Releases Search This Site WIREDGOV The UK's No.1 government & public sector news alerting service. sign in Home Register About Us Latest News My WiredGov WiredGov Plus Contracts Locator Tender Locator Supplier Locator Industry News Search for Press Releases Search This Site Tuesday 01 Sep 2026 @ 13:20 NHS Wales Printable version RSV vaccine offer extended to higher-risk adults aged 65–74 People aged 65 to 74 who are at higher risk of becoming seriously ill from respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) will be offered the RSV vaccine as part of a time-limited programme in September. Evidence shows that people aged over 65 who are immunosuppressed or have chronic respiratory disease have a similar risk of being hospitalised to those aged over 75 who are already offered the vaccine free from the NHS. The offer will run from September and will help protect eligible adults who are more likely to experience severe illness if they catch the virus. RSV is a common virus that can cause serious health problems, including bronchiolitis and pneumonia, particularly in older people and babies. Each year, it leads to between 700 and 1,000 hospital admissions among people aged over 75 in Wales, as well as up to 9,000 GP appointments. The RSV vaccine is already routinely offered to all adults aged 75 and over. Evidence from a new study led by Public Health Wales, working with other UK teams, found that RSV vaccination cut the risk of ending up in hospital from RSV by 75 per cent among adults aged 75 to 79 during the winter of 2024–25. The study also found that RSV-related hospital admissions fell by more than 13 per cent among the eligible 75–79 age group between October 2024 and April 2025, despite the programme being in its early stages. Expanding the offer to higher-risk people aged 65 to 74 will help prevent further serious illness and hospital admissions, while also easing pressure on GPs and hospitals during the winter months. Dr Christopher Johnson, Head of the Vaccine Preventable Disease Programme at Public Health Wales, said: “We have strong evidence that the RSV vaccine works. It reduces the risk of ending up in hospital with RSV by around 75 per cent, helping protect older adults from serious illness. “By expanding free access to more people aged 65 to 74 who are at higher risk, we can help keep more people healthy and out of hospital, particularly during the winter months when RSV is most dangerous. “If you’re eligible, I strongly encourage you to get vaccinated when you’re invited.” For more information, visit Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination information. Respiratory Virus Activity Levels - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) published a report on respiratory virus activity levels on August 28, 2026. The report includes information on Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV), but it does not provide specific case counts, death counts, or risk levels. The CDC is offering general information about respiratory virus activity, but no detailed response measures or investigation status are mentioned. The report is part of the CDC's broader efforts to monitor and provide updates on public health threats, including RSV. The content is presented as a general overview rather than a specific outbreak or event, and it does not include any confirmed or suspected cases related to RSV. The report is intended to inform the public about ongoing health monitoring activities and does not specify any particular risk or impact associated with RSV at this time.

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Diagnostic Market Companies, Size & Trends 2026-2035 - Precedence Research
新闻简报The Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) diagnostic market is experiencing significant growth, with a market size of USD 1.67 billion in 2026 and a projected value of USD 4.34 billion by 2035, expanding at a CAGR of 11.20%. This growth is driven by rising respiratory infection rates, an increasing neonatal population, and the demand for in-vitro diagnostics and point-of-care services. North America dominated the market in 2025 with a 39.2% share, while Asia-Pacific is expected to grow at the fastest rate. The market includes key players such as Abbott Laboratories, Hoffmann-La Roche Ltd., and Becton, Dickinson and Company. The report highlights the integration of AI in diagnostics, which enhances the timing and application of RSV detection, and the shift toward multiplex testing that identifies multiple pathogens simultaneously. These trends reflect a broader movement in the healthcare sector toward more efficient and data-driven diagnostic practices.

The respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) diagnostic market is growing, with the market size reaching USD 1.67 billion in 2026 and projected to reach USD 4.34 billion by 2035. The global respiratory syncytial virus diagnostic market size accounted for USD 1.50 billion in 2025 and is predicted to increase from USD 1.67 billion in 2026 to approximately USD 4.34 billion by 2035, expanding at a CAGR of 11.20% from 2026 to 2035. This market growth is associated with an elevation in the incidence of respiratory infections, an increasing neonatal population, and a considerable demand for in-vitro diagnostics along with point-of-care services. The pace and precision of RSV detection have been enhanced with the use of diagnostic tools, and this has created several promising prospects for firms in this space. Key Takeaways North America dominated the global respiratory syncytial virus diagnostic market with a market share of 39.2% in 2025. Asia-Pacific holds the market share of 22.5% market share and is expected to grow at the fastest CAGR in the market during the forecast period. By product and service, the kit and assays segment accounted for a considerable revenue share in the market in 2025. By product and service, the software and service segment is expected to witness the fastest growth in the market over the forecast period. By technology, the molecular diagnostics segment accounted for a considerable revenue share of 55.0% in the market in 2025. By technology, the rapid antigen detection test segment held the second-largest dominance with a market share of 27.0% in 2025. By end-user, the hospitals and clinics segment held a major revenue share of 43.1% in the market in 2025. By end-user, the diagnostic laboratories segment is the second-largest shareholder with 31.5% market share in 2025. What is the Respiratory Syncytial Virus Diagnostic Market? The field of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) diagnostics has evolved into numerous approaches and tools that help to quickly detect RSV in patients, especially in infants, the elderly, and immunocompromised individuals. This includes rapid antigen testing, molecular techniques, including PCR assays, and a growing number of point-of-care products to provide laboratory-tested findings to patients. One distinct factor pertaining to this respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) diagnostic market is the reliance on demand being dependent on the seasons: during an outbreak, when the demand is higher, the healthcare professionals are compelled to respond quickly without losing dependability. As more information about RSV is received, and in addition to increasingly preventive measures, diagnostics are transitioning from confirmation of cases towards being key instruments for informing timely clinical decision-making. Impact of AI in the Respiratory Syncytial Virus Diagnostic Market Artificial intelligence is gradually changing things by improving their timing and application. Algorithms that analyze epidemiological trends, shifts in weather conditions, and hospital statistics are starting to anticipate RSV outbreaks before those cases are admitted. In the field of diagnostics and the respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) diagnostic market, AI integration is refining the interpretation of multiplex test results, clarifying instances where symptoms may overlap with those associated with influenza or COVID-19. This technology supports clinicians in making more informed triaging decisions about which individuals need testing, rather than just observation. As a result, diagnostic practice is evolving to be more proactive rather than reactive, guided equally by data insights and clinical presentations. Market Trends Influencing the Respiratory Syncytial Virus Diagnostic Market Convergence Toward Multiplex Respiratory Testing: Incorporating RSV diagnostic protocols is becoming more common across the rapidly expanding respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) diagnostic market. Integrated RSV diagnostic panels, now capable of identifying multiple pathogens in one examination, are increasingly accepted for routine clinical practice. This reflects a shift: clinicians have a preference for composite results in place of isolated individual RSV tests, because this increases efficiencies and minimizes potential clinical ambiguity. Gradual Migration to Point-of-Care Molecular Platforms: The industry is transcending central lab environments. At the same time, smaller molecular gadgets in emergency departments and outpatient facilities are growing more widely available. These technologies offer speed and precision that are as efficient as PCR and eliminate the process bottlenecks of standard laboratory procedures. Fast yet precise, they are already nearing a PCR-level precision. Data-Linked Diagnostics and Real-Time Surveillance: Diagnostics are more integrated into a data network than in any of the former systems used across the respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) diagnostic market. Data can combine with computerized surveillance systems to allow doctors to observe RSV trends as they occur in real time and react accordingly more effectively than ever before. Pediatric-Focused Product Design and Innovation: Considering the high prevalence of RSV in infants, manufacturers have designed infant-specific diagnostic techniques focused on neonatal care facilities for patients with RSV infection. Relatively gentle, less invasive sampling techniques and time-sensitive turnaround times are implemented to maintain greater and gentler precision in pediatric environments. Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Clinical Trial Pipeline Analysis: 50+ Key Companies Shaping the Future of RSV Therapeutics | DelveInsight DelveInsight's Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Pipeline Insight 2026 report provides comprehensive global coverage of available, marketed, and pipeline RSV therapies in various stages of clinical development. Key takeaways from the Respiratory Syncytial Virus Clinical Trial Landscape Report • DelveInsight's Respiratory Syncytial Virus pipeline report depicts a robust space with 50+ key companies working to develop 55+ pipeline therapies for RSV treatment. • Key Respiratory Syncytial Virus companies such as Shionogi, Zhuhai Trinomab Pharmaceutical Co., Ltd., Blue Lake Biotechnology Inc., Ark Biosciences, EuBiologics Co., Ltd., Simcere Pharmaceutical Group, Guangzhou Patronus Biotech Co., Ltd., Sinocelltech Ltd., Enanta Pharmaceuticals, Inc., Clover Biopharmaceuticals USA, LLC, Pulmotect, Inc., and Synairgen Research Ltd., among others. • Promising Respiratory Syncytial Virus pipeline therapies in various stages of development include Deuremidevir Hydrobromide, S-337395, SCTV02, EuRSV, and others. Request a sample and discover the recent advances in Respiratory Syncytial Virus treatment drugs: https://www.delveinsight.com/sample-request/respiratory-syncytial-virus-rsv-pipeline-insight?utmsource=openpr&utm_medium=pressrelease&utm_campaign=kspr What is Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)? Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) is a highly contagious respiratory virus that primarily infects the upper and lower respiratory tract, including the nose, throat, and lungs. It is one of the most common causes of respiratory illness worldwide, particularly affecting infants, young children, older adults, and immunocompromised individuals. RSV spreads through respiratory droplets, direct contact with infected individuals, or contact with contaminated surfaces followed by touching the eyes, nose, or mouth. RSV infections typically occur seasonally and are a major cause of bronchiolitis and pneumonia in young children. The symptoms of RSV usually appear within 4-6 days after exposure and may initially resemble mild upper respiratory tract infections. Common symptoms include runny nose, cough, sneezing, fever, wheezing, and reduced appetite. Infants may experience irritability, poor feeding, and breathing difficulties. In severe cases, particularly among high-risk populations, RSV can lead to bronchiolitis and pneumonia, with symptoms including rapid breathing, chest retractions, wheezing, and cyanosis. The virus infects respiratory epithelial cells and uses its fusion (F) and attachment (G) proteins to enter cells and form syncytia. This process leads to inflammation, mucus production, airway obstruction, and impaired gas exchange. Diagnosis is generally based on clinical presentation and can be confirmed using laboratory tests such as polymerase chain reaction (PCR) from nasal or throat swabs. Treatment is mainly supportive and may include hydration, antipyretics, oxygen therapy, and ventilatory support in severe cases. Preventive strategies include hygiene practices, vaccination in certain populations, and prophylactic monoclonal antibodies for high-risk infants. Emerging Respiratory Syncytial Virus Drug Profiles Deuremidevir Hydrobromide: Simcere Pharmaceutical Group Deuremidevir Hydrobromide (VV116) is an orally administered small-molecule antiviral drug developed by Vigonvita Life Sciences in collaboration with Simcere Pharmaceutical Group for viral infections, including Respiratory Syncytial Virus. The drug is a nucleoside analog with broad-spectrum antiviral activity and works by inhibiting viral RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (RdRp), thereby blocking viral RNA replication and preventing the spread of infection. The therapy has received a Breakthrough Therapy Designation from the China National Medical Products Administration for RSV infection. Deuremidevir Hydrobromide has completed Phase II development and is currently in Phase III clinical trials for RSV infection. S-337395: Shionogi S-337395 is a novel orally administered small-molecule antiviral drug candidate being jointly developed by Shionogi and UBE Corporation for Respiratory Syncytial Virus infection. The drug is designed as an RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (L protein) inhibitor, blocking the activity of the RSV L protein, which is essential for viral genome transcription and replication. S-337395 has received Fast Track designation from the US Food and Drug Administration and has shown positive results in Phase II clinical trials. Currently, the drug is in Phase II clinical development for RSV. SCTV02: Sinocelltech Ltd. SCTV02 is a recombinant protein-based vaccine candidate developed for the prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus infection. The vaccine uses a stabilized prefusion RSV F protein designed to induce neutralizing antibodies and prevent viral entry into host cells. The therapy is being developed for prophylactic prevention of RSV infection, particularly among vulnerable populations such as infants, elderly individuals, and those at high risk of severe disease. Currently, SCTV02 is in Phase I/II clinical trials. EuRSV: Eubiologics EuRSV is a recombinant protein-based RSV vaccine candidate being developed by EuBiologics for the prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus infection. The vaccine uses a liposome nanoparticle-based platform incorporating RSV antigens, including the F protein, combined with immune-enhancing adjuvants. EuRSV is designed to stimulate humoral and cellular immune responses and is being developed for prophylactic use against RSV infection. Currently, EuRSV is in Phase I clinical development. mRNA-1345 RSV 疫苗 可防止美国退伍军人出现严重后果

mRNA-1345 RSV Vaccine Prevents Severe Outcomes in US Veterans
新闻简报一项于2026年9月1日发表在《疫苗》上的研究发现,mRNA-1345 RSV疫苗与美国50岁及以上老兵呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)确诊急性呼吸道疾病(ARI)住院风险降低相关。该研究于2025年9月至2026年4月期间进行,分析了退伍军人健康管理局的电子健康记录,纳入91,397名老兵。在住院患者中,疫苗对RSV相关住院的校正后估计有效性(VE)为85%(95%置信区间,39-96)。对于RSV相关的医疗就诊ARI,VE为65%(95%置信区间,45-77),其中急诊/观察室就诊的VE为57%,门诊就诊的VE为71%。研究强调了在高风险人群中开展RSV疫苗接种项目的重要性。但研究局限包括住院患者中接种疫苗者数量较少、可能存在残余混杂因素以及检测阴性设计可能导致的偏倚。研究结果凸显了RSV疫苗接种的公共卫生意义,特别是对老兵等脆弱群体。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2025年9月至2026年4月期间进行,研究结果于2026年9月1日发布。
地点与对象
该研究主要针对 50 岁及以上的美国退伍军人,共有 91,397 名参与者参与分析。
事件
据《疫苗》发表的一项研究报道,真实世界数据表明,mRNA-1345 RSV 疫苗接种 与美国退伍军人因 RSV 确诊的急性呼吸道疾病住院的风险降低相关。
规模、影响与风险
在住院患者中,疫苗对RSV相关住院的校正后估计有效性(VE)为85%(95%置信区间,39-96)。对于RSV相关的医疗就诊ARI,VE为65%(95%置信区间,45-77),其中急诊/观察室就诊的VE为57%(95%置信区间,28-74),门诊就诊的VE为71%(95%置信区间,19-90)。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究采用回顾性、测试阴性、病例对照设计,存在局限性,包括住院 队列研究 中接种疫苗的人数较少、可能存在残留混杂因素以及测试阴性设计的潜在偏差。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

mRNA-1345 RSV Vaccine Prevents Severe Outcomes in US Veterans

News Brief
A study published in Vaccine on September 1, 2026, found that mRNA-1345 RSV vaccine is associated with reduced risk for RSV-confirmed acute respiratory illness (ARI) hospitalization among US veterans aged 50 and older. The research, conducted between September 2025 and April 2026, analyzed electronic health records from the Veterans Health Administration and included 91,397 veterans. Among hospitalized patients, the estimated adjusted vaccine effectiveness (VE) against RSV-associated hospitalization was 85% (95% CI, 39-96). For RSV-associated medically attended ARI, VE was 65% (95% CI, 45-77), with lower estimates for ED/UC visits (57%) and outpatient visits (71%). The study highlighted the importance of RSV vaccination programs in high-risk populations. However, limitations included the low number of vaccinated individuals in the hospitalized cohort, possible residual confounding, and potential bias from the test-negative design. The findings underscore the public health significance of RSV vaccines, particularly for vulnerable groups like veterans.
Original source text (English)
Menu SECTIONS News Features CME Conferences Clinical Tools Special Collections Multimedia Fact Sheets Diagnosis & Disease Info Topics Abdominal Infections Bone and Joint Infections Cardiovascular Infections Central Nervous System Infections Covid 19 News & Insights Eye Infections Gastrointestinal Infections General Infectious Disease General Medicine Hepatitis HIV/AIDS Infection Control and Prevention Nosocomial Infections Pleuropulmonary and Bronchial Infections Practice Management Reproductive Organ Infections and Sexually Transmitted Infections Sepsis Skin and Soft Tissue Infection Upper Respiratory Tract Infections Urinary Tract Infection Vector-Borne Illnesses Haymarket Medical Network Cancer Therapy Advisor Clinical Advisor Clinical Pain Advisor Dermatology Advisor Endocrinology Advisor Gastroenterology Advisor Hematology Advisor Infectious Disease Advisor Medical Bag MPR Neurology Advisor Obesity Advisor Oncology Nurse Advisor Ophthalmology Advisor Optometry Advisor Psychiatry Advisor Pulmonology Advisor Rare Disease Advisor Renal and Urology News Rheumatology Advisor Sleep Wake Advisor The Cardiology Advisor Vaccine Advisor Search for: COVID-19 News CME Drugs Calculators Charts Resources Multimedia Meetings Part of the News and Features CME Conferences Clinical Tools Special Collections Multimedia Share this article Share on Facebook Share on Twitter Share on LinkedIn Share by Email Print Pleuropulmonary and Bronchial Infections mRNA-1345 RSV Vaccine Prevents Severe Outcomes in US Veterans Todd Neale, MA | Publish Date September 1, 2026 Real-world data show mRNA-1345 vaccination is associated with reduced risk for RSV-confirmed acute respiratory illness hospitalization in US veterans, underscoring the importance of RSV vaccination programs in high-risk populations. Among US veterans, 2025-2026 mRNA-1345 vaccination is associated with a reduced risk for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-confirmed acute respiratory illness (ARI) hospitalization, according to study findings published in Vaccine. Since its approval in May 2024, mRNA-1345 (mRESVIA) has been shown to prevent RSV-associated lower respiratory tract disease in adults in clinical trials. Real-world data on use of the vaccine, particularly against severe RSV infection outcomes, are scarce. To bridge this knowledge gap, researchers conducted a retrospective, test-negative, case-control study using electronic health record data from the Veterans Health Administration. The analysis included 91,397 veterans aged 50 years and older who had an ARI-related encounter and underwent RSV testing between September 2025 and April 2026; only 1.4% had received the mRNA-1345 RSV vaccine. These findings highlight the public health importance of RSV vaccination programs in populations at risk of severe outcomes. From that larger cohort, the researchers matched 3673 individuals who tested positive for RSV to 14,566 control individuals who tested negative for the virus. Within the subset of patients who were hospitalized, mean patient age was approximately 74 years, and more than 90% were men. Nearly all (98%) patients had at least 1 chronic condition, including 84% with hypertension and approximately 50% with chronic obstructive pulmonary disease or diabetes. Multivariable conditional logistic regression was employed to estimate vaccine effectiveness (VE) against RSV-associated medically attended ARI, a composite outcome that incorporated hospitalizations, emergency department/urgent care (ED/UC) visits, and outpatient visits. Within the hospitalized cohort, fewer than 5 patients who tested positive for RSV and 49 patients who tested negative had received mRNA-1345 vaccine, reflecting an estimated adjusted VE of 85% (95% CI, 39-96). For RSV-associated medically attended ARI, VE was 65% (95% CI, 45-77), with VE estimates of 57% (95% CI, 28-74) and 71% (95% CI, 19-90) for protection against ED/UC visits and outpatient visits, respectively. The researchers noted that these figures are similar to those seen in prior studies of protein-based RSV vaccines. Study limitations include the low number of individuals who both tested positive for RSV and were vaccinated against the virus in the subset of hospitalized patients, leading to imprecise estimates of effect size; possible residual confounding stemming from the observational design; potential misclassification of RSV vaccination status; possible bias introduced by the test-negative design; and reduced generalizability. The researchers concluded, “These findings highlight the public health importance of RSV vaccination programs in populations at risk of severe outcomes.” Disclosure: This study was supported by a research award from Moderna. Multiple study authors declared affiliations with biotech, pharmaceutical, and/or device companies. Please see the original reference for a full list of disclosures. References: McConeghy KW, Wilker EH, DeVone F, et al. Vaccine effectiveness of mRNA-1345 against RSV-associated hospitalization and medically attended acute respiratory illness among US veterans, 2025-2026. Event
Real-world data showed that mRNA-1345 RSV vaccination is associated with a reduced risk for RSV-confirmed acute respiratory illness hospitalization in US veterans, as reported in a study published in Vaccine.
Scale, impact and risk
Among hospitalized patients, the estimated adjusted vaccine effectiveness (VE) against RSV-associated hospitalization was 85% (95% CI, 39-96). For RSV-associated medically attended ARI, VE was 65% (95% CI, 45-77), with VE estimates of 57% (95% CI, 28-74) for ED/UC visits and 71% (95% CI, 19-90) for outpatient visits.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

RSV 疫苗 有助于预防住院,次要结局:大规模养老院研究发现

RSV vaccine helps prevent hospitalizations, secondary outcomes, massive nursing home study finds
新闻简报发表在《JAMA Internal Medicine》上的一项研究发现,RSV 疫苗 对美国疗养院居民中与 RSV 相关的住院治疗和其他严重后果显示出有效性 (VE)。该研究检查了 597,430 名医疗保险受益人的数据,结果显示,65 至 74 岁人群中 VE 抵抗住院治疗的比例为 60%,75 岁或以上人群中为 75%。在免疫功能低下的受益人中,VE 为 61%,在免疫功能正常的受益人中,VE 为 76%。疫苗 还表现出 56% 的 VE 可以预防 RSV 相关死亡,59% 的 VE 可以预防严重后果。作者指出,与之前的研究相比,VE 较低可能是由于疗养院环境中 RSV 住院的可能性较高。CDC 的 ACIP 于 2024 年 6 月更新了其建议,将 75 岁及以上的成年人和 60 至 74 岁的严重 RSV 风险较高的人纳入其中,并于 2025 年进一步扩大建议范围,将 50 至 59 岁的个人纳入其中。然而,该研究的样本量可能无法完全推广到更广泛的美国疗养院老年人群体或其他亚人群。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2023-2024呼吸道合胞病毒流行季开展,美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)的ACIP于2024年6月更新其建议,并于2025年进一步扩展。
地点与对象
该研究的重点是美国疗养院的医疗保险受益人,特别是 65 岁或以上的受益人。
事件
发表在《JAMA Internal Medicine》上的一项研究发现,RSV 疫苗 对疗养院居民中与 RSV 相关的住院治疗和其他严重后果显示出有效性 (VE)。
规模、影响与风险
对于 65 岁至 74 岁的受益人来说,疫苗 的住院治疗有效性为 60%,对于 75 岁或以上的受益人来说,住院有效性为 75%。在免疫功能低下的受益人中,这一比例为 61%,在免疫功能正常的受益人中,这一比例为 76%。对抗 RSV 相关死亡的 VE 为 56%,对抗严重后果的 VE 为 59%。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究仅观察了疗养院中的医疗保险 A 部分和 B 部分受益人,因此结果可能无法完全推广到疗养院中更广泛的美国老年人群体或其他亚人群。CDC 的 ACIP 根据这些发现更新了其建议。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

RSV vaccine helps prevent hospitalizations, secondary outcomes, massive nursing home study finds

News Brief
A study published in JAMA Internal Medicine found that the RSV vaccine showed effectiveness (VE) against RSV-associated hospitalization and other severe outcomes among nursing home residents in the United States. The research, which examined data on 597,430 Medicare beneficiaries, revealed that VE against hospitalization was 60% for those aged 65 to 74 years and 75% for those 75 years or older. Among immunocompromised beneficiaries, VE was 61%, and among immunocompetent ones, it was 76%. The vaccine also demonstrated 56% VE against RSV-associated death and 59% against severe outcomes. The authors noted that the lower VE compared to previous studies might be due to the higher probability of RSV hospitalization in nursing home settings. The CDC’s ACIP updated its recommendations in June 2024 to include adults 75 years and older and those aged 60 to 74 at increased risk for severe RSV, and further expanded them in 2025 to include individuals aged 50 to 59. However, the study's sample size may not be fully generalizable to the broader US population of older adults in nursing homes or other subpopulations.
Original source text (English)

The study examined data on 597,430 Medicare beneficiaries in the Common Working File and Minimum Data Set (MDS). The primary outcome tracked was RSV-associated hospitalization; secondary outcomes included RSV-associated death or severe RSV-associated outcomes requiring an intensive care unit or critical care unit admission. The results showed that VE against hospitalization among beneficiaries aged 65 to 74 years was 60%; among beneficiaries 75 years or older, it was 75%. Among immunocompromised beneficiaries, VE was 61%, and VE was 76% among immunocompetent beneficiaries. Meanwhile, VE against RSV-associated death was 56%, and against severe RSV-associated outcomes, VE was 59%. The lower estimated VE found in this study, compared to previous similar studies, could be attributed to the increased probability of RSV hospitalization in nursing home settings in contrast with community-dwelling older adults, authors said. Authors also note that because the study observed only Medicare Part A and B beneficiaries residing in nursing homes, the sample size may not be completely generalizable to the overall US population of people 65 years or older residing in nursing homes or other subpopulations. “Our results indicate RSV vaccination provided protection against RSV-associated hospitalization and other severe RSV-associated outcomes among nursing home residents during the 2023-2024 RSV season,” authors said. “In June 2024, CDC’s ACIP strengthened its RSV recommendations to recommend all adults 75 years and older and adults aged 60 to 74 years who are at increased risk for severe RSV (including nursing home residents) receive 1 dose of RSV vaccine. The recommendations were further expanded in 2025 to include persons aged 50 to 59 years at increased risk of severe RSV.” More From Long-Term Care Study: Nursing home residents with more natural teeth have better nutritional status Study: At-home heart failure therapy reduced inpatient hospital days Top Stories View All News Why falls may be the best bellwether for staffing, operational performance News Also in the News for Wednesday, Sept. 2 News Raises, better ratings and, yes, AI boost LTC leaders' mood: McKnight’s survey Editors' Picks View All ’Tis the season: Blocking and tackling ... it’s for nursing home leaders too By James M. It was 61% among immunocompromised beneficiaries and 76% among immunocompetent ones. The VE against RSV-associated death was 56%, and against severe outcomes, it was 59%.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study observed only Medicare Part A and B beneficiaries in nursing homes, so the results may not be fully generalizable to the broader US population of older adults in nursing homes or other subpopulations. The CDC’s ACIP updated its recommendations based on these findings.
原文 / Source
Published: 2026-08-27  |  Publisher: Contemporary Pediatrics

What pediatricians should know for the upcoming respiratory virus season - Contemporary Pediatrics

News Brief
The article, published on August 27, 2026, by Contemporary Pediatrics, discusses the ongoing impact of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) on pediatric care. It highlights a 1-year post-tripledemic emergency department update, indicating that RSV remains a significant health concern. The piece focuses on what pediatricians should know about RSV during the upcoming respiratory virus season, emphasizing the need for awareness and preparedness. However, it does not provide specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers. The article serves as general guidance for healthcare providers, offering insights into managing RSV in children without detailing specific interventions or outcomes. The content is based on a review of past events and current strategies, but no new data or confirmed findings are presented.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

国家对 RSV 免疫接种的支持是否具有成本效益? - 아시아경제

News article (Korean original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报文章讨论了韩国国家支持 RSV 免疫接种的成本效益,重点关注孕产妇 疫苗 和婴儿单克隆抗体。它强调,虽然 Beyfortus 和 Abrysvo 等预防措施正在扩大,但国家免疫计划 (NIP) 仍然以 疫苗 为重点。庆熙大学药学院的一项研究发现,对所有一岁以下的婴儿服用 尼塞韦单抗 可以降低医疗费用和护理人员生产力损失。在意大利,减少住院治疗所节省的成本超过了预防性抗体的成本,日本也报告了类似的结果。韩国疾病控制和预防机构 (KDCA) 在审查疾病负担和财务影响后,正在考虑政府对这些措施的支持。然而,健康婴儿的预防性抗体尚未纳入保险范围,需要进一步分析以确定适合韩国医疗保健系统的最佳方法。

时间
集装箱 亚洲商报 开放导航 股票经济 房地产业·IT 政治社会 世界文化·生活观点·社论视觉新闻业·IT 国家支持RSV免疫划算吗?
地点与对象
韩国,婴幼儿。
事件
关于国家支持 RSV 免疫(包括孕产妇 疫苗 和婴儿单克隆抗体)的成本效益的讨论正在进行中。韩国疾病控制和预防机构 (KDCA) 在审查疾病负担和财务影响后正在考虑政府支持。
规模、影响与风险
庆熙大学药学院的一项研究表明,与住院和急诊室就诊相关的医疗费用减少了,护理人员的生产力损失也减少了。在意大利,住院治疗减少所节省的费用超过了预防性抗体的费用。在日本,将预防扩大到所有婴儿可以减轻疾病负担和医疗保健支出。尼塞韦单抗 使用量每增加 10 个百分点,潜在的成本节省大约可减少 2,780 个额外案例。
应对、状态与不确定性
国家免疫计划(NIP)目前以疫苗为重点,单克隆抗体给药需要单独考虑。KDCA 表示,只有在全面审查疾病负担和预期政府支出后才会做出政府支持的决定。健康婴儿的预防性抗体尚未纳入保险范围。

News article (Korean original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Korean)
Is National Support for RSV Immunization Cost-Effective? - 아시아경제
News Brief
The article discusses the cost-effectiveness of national support for RSV immunization in South Korea, focusing on maternal vaccines and monoclonal antibodies for infants. It highlights that while preventive measures like Beyfortus and Abrysvo are expanding, the National Immunization Program (NIP) remains vaccine-focused. A study by Kyung Hee University College of Pharmacy found that administering nirsevimab to all infants under one year of age could reduce medical costs and caregiver productivity loss. In Italy, savings from reduced hospitalizations exceeded the cost of prophylactic antibodies, and similar findings have been reported in Japan. The Korea Disease Control and Prevention Agency (KDCA) is considering government support for these measures after reviewing disease burden and financial implications. However, the prophylactic antibodies for healthy infants are not yet covered by insurance, and further analysis is needed to determine the best approach for South Korea's healthcare system.
