container The Asia Business Daily Open Navigation Stock Economy Real estate Industry · IT Politics Society World Culture·Life Opinion·Editorial Visual-News Industry · IT Is National Support for RSV Immunization Cost-Effective? Language Language Close KR CN Open Search bar Search field News Search Remove icon Search icon Dim Industry · IT Bio Health Is National Support for RSV Immunization Cost-Effective? by Lee Sungmin Published 27 Aug.2026 10:04(KST) Updated 27 Aug.2026 11:03(KST) open/close Share Language Text Size Print Pfizer's Maternal Vaccine Joins Sanofi and MSD Preventive Injections Available, But Fully Self-Funded Options for preventing respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection in infants and young children are expanding. In addition to the introduction of two types of long-acting prophylactic antibodies administered directly to infants, a vaccine for pregnant women has also been launched. As the methods of prevention are diversifying, there is increasing discussion about the need to consider not only efficacy but also factors such as cost, target recipients, and the impact on healthcare expenditures. According to industry sources on August 27, two types of long-acting RSV prophylactic antibodies for infants—Sanofi·AstraZeneca's “Beyfortus (nirsevimab)” and “Enfloncia (clessrovimab)” from MSD Korea—are currently competing in the domestic market. Pfizer's RSV vaccine “Abrysvo” has also become an option through its administration to pregnant women to protect infants. While Beyfortus and Enfloncia involve administering fully developed antibodies directly to infants, Abrysvo works by vaccinating pregnant women, enabling them to pass on protective antibodies to the fetus. ChatGPT Generated Image View original image RSV is a common acute respiratory virus among infants and young children, with most children becoming infected at least once before the age of two. In most cases, infection presents with cold-like symptoms, but in relatively younger infants or those in high-risk groups, it can progress to lower respiratory tract infections such as bronchiolitis and pneumonia, sometimes requiring hospitalization. Particularly during the early months of life, infants have narrower airways and underdeveloped immune systems, leaving them more vulnerable to severe disease caused by RSV infection. The World Health Organization (WHO) recommends the introduction of maternal vaccines or long-acting monoclonal antibodies for infant RSV prevention, advising countries to also consider cost-effectiveness, availability, and expected vaccination rates. Once similar levels of preventive efficacy have been established, it becomes increasingly important to determine which method best fits a country’s healthcare environment and financial circumstances. Expanding access to preventive measures ultimately requires an assessment of the benefits relative to financial investment. According to a study conducted last year by Professor Seo Hye-Seon’s team at Kyung Hee University College of Pharmacy, administering nirsevimab to all infants under one year of age as well as high-risk children under two years was compared to the conventional strategy focusing only on high-risk groups. The results showed a reduction in medical costs related to hospitalization and emergency room visits, as well as decreased productivity loss for caregivers. More than 90% of the total health improvement was observed in full-term infants under one year old. Similar analyses have been reported in other countries. In Italy, many regions found that the savings in hospital costs resulting from reduced RSV hospitalizations exceeded the cost of administering the prophylactic antibodies. In Japan, research indicated that expanding the target group for prevention to include all infants could further reduce disease burden and healthcare expenditures. According to estimates, for every 10 percentage point increase in the use of nirsevimab, approximately 2,780 additional lower respiratory tract RSV cases could be prevented. The National Immunization Program (NIP) is currently vaccine-focused, and separate considerations are needed regarding how to support monoclonal antibody administration. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

尼塞韦单抗 可能减少婴儿侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病:研究 - 医学对话

Nirsevimab May Reduce Invasive Pneumococcal Disease in Infants: Study - Medical Dialogues
新闻简报A French retrospective study published in The Lancet Infectious Diseases suggests that nirsevimab, a monoclonal antibody used to prevent respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, may also reduce the risk of invasive pneumococcal disease (IPD) in infants. The study analyzed liveborn infants in metropolitan France born between February 6, 2023, and January 31, 2024, and followed them for up to 9 months. It found that infants immunized with nirsevimab were about 35% less likely to be hospitalized for IPD compared to non-immunized infants. The effectiveness was calculated as a 36% reduction in hospitalization for IPD at six months (OR, 0.64; 95% CI, 0.46-0.82) and a 34% reduction by 9 months (OR, 0.66; 95% CI, 0.51-0.85). The study used propensity score matching with IPTW to control for confounders and baseline clinical characteristics. These findings highlight the potential of nirsevimab as a complementary strategy to pneumococcal vaccination, especially as non-vaccine pneumococcal serotypes emerge. The results suggest a cross-protection effect of RSV monoclonal antibody prophylaxis against IPD, which could aid in healthcare planning during RSV seasons.
查看新闻五要素
时间
The study was conducted on infants born between February 6, 2023, and January 31, 2024, with follow-up through 9 months of age. The publication date of the study is 2026-09-01.
地点与对象
该研究在法国本土进行,重点关注特定时期的活产婴儿。人口包括 527,971 名婴儿,其中 119,435 名婴儿接受 尼塞韦单抗。
事件
法国的一项回顾性研究表明,用于预防 RSV 感染的 尼塞韦单抗 还可以降低婴儿患侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病 (IPD) 的风险。研究发现,接受 尼塞韦单抗 免疫的婴儿在 9 个月大时因 IPD 住院的可能性降低了约 35%。
规模、影响与风险
The study reported 112 IPD cases over six months, with 17 cases in the immunized cohort (14.2 per 100,000) and 95 cases in the non-immunized cohort (23.3 per 100,000). The effectiveness of nirsevimab was calculated as a 36% reduction in hospitalization for IPD (OR, 0.64; 95% CI, 0.46-0.82) and a 34% reduction by 9 months (OR, 0.66; 95% CI, 0.51-0.85).
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究发表在《柳叶刀传染病》上,并使用倾向评分与 IPTW 匹配来控制混杂因素。研究结果表明 尼塞韦单抗 对 IPD 具有潜在的交叉保护作用,但需要进一步的研究来证实这些结果。

Nirsevimab May Reduce Invasive Pneumococcal Disease in Infants: Study Subscribe to Notifications Written By : Dr Riya Dave Medically Reviewed By : Dr. Kamal Kant Kohli Published On 2026-09-01 02:15 GMT | Update On 2026-09-01 08:50 GMT A French retrospective study suggests that nirsevimab (Beyfortus), used to prevent respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, may also reduce the risk of invasive pneumococcal disease. Infants immunized with nirsevimab were about 35% less likely to be hospitalized for invasive pneumococcal disease through 9 months of age, highlighting its potential as a complementary strategy alongside pneumococcal vaccination, especially as non-vaccine pneumococcal serotypes continue to emerge. The study was published in The Lancet Infectious Diseases by Ines F. and colleagues. To investigate if there is any reduction in the frequency of hospitalized cases of IPD with nirsevimab immunization, the study conducted a review of all liveborn infants in metropolitan France born between February 6, 2023, and January 31, 2024. Children who had nirsevimab immunization within their first year of life were considered part of the immunized cohort, while children without immunization who matched their birth month were part of the non-immunized cohort. The outcome used in this study was the admission to the hospital for confirmed IPD 6 months after the patient was included in the study. To control for confounders and even out the baseline clinical characteristics of both immunized and non-immunized groups, researchers used propensity score matching with IPTW. The effectiveness of the vaccine was calculated using the following formula: effectiveness=100% × (1 − odds ratio [OR]). Key findings: Among 608,641 total births live during the study period, 527,971 satisfied inclusion criteria, including 119,435 infants (22.6%) who were given nirsevimab. A total of 112 IPD cases occurred in the six months' follow-up; in immunized cohort, there were 17 cases (14.2 per 100,000) whereas in non-immunized cohort, there were 95 cases (23.3 per 100,000). IPTW corrected analysis showed that nirsevimab was connected with significant odds reduction 36% for hospitalization due to IPD 6 months post-vaccination (OR, 0.64; 95% CI, 0.46-0.82). The effectiveness remained consistently high and there was 34% reduction in odds for IPD by 9 months after vaccination (OR, 0.66; 95% CI, 0.51-0.85). In summary, children less than 12 months who had received nirsevimab vaccination were observed to have reduced susceptibility to IPD for up to 6 months after the procedure. The study has revealed an unexpected yet critical additional benefit of RSV monoclonal antibody prophylaxis other than the protection from the virus infection itself. The increased use of RSV immunization among infants by public health institutions will enable proper healthcare planning during the season with an understanding of this cross-protection effect on infant mortality rates. Reference: Fafi, I., Valtuille, Z., Kaguelidou, F., Levy, C., Cohen, R., Angoulvant, F., Bizot, E., Bonacorsi, S., Birgy, A., Pontual, L. D., Viriot, D., Cohen, J., Bourmaud, A., Assad, Z., Jaboyedoff, M., Lenglart, L., Boutillier, B., Naudin, J., Tubiana, S., ... Ouldali, N. (2026). Effectiveness of nirsevimab immunisation on invasive pneumococcal disease in children nationwide in France: an observational cohort study. The Lancet Infectious Diseases. https://doi.org/10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00253-7 Tags: The Lancet Infectious Diseases Nirsevimab invasive pneumococcal disease IPD respiratory syncytial virus RSV infant immunization French National Health Data System propensity score analysis Article Source : The Lancet Infectious Diseases Disclaimer: This website is primarily for healthcare professionals. Location and population
The study was conducted in metropolitan France, focusing on liveborn infants during the specified period. The population included 527,971 infants, with 119,435 receiving nirsevimab.
Event
A French retrospective study suggests that nirsevimab, used to prevent RSV infection, may also reduce the risk of invasive pneumococcal disease (IPD) in infants. The study found that infants immunized with nirsevimab were about 35% less likely to be hospitalized for IPD through 9 months of age.
Scale, impact and risk
The study reported 112 IPD cases over six months, with 17 cases in the immunized cohort (14.2 per 100,000) and 95 cases in the non-immunized cohort (23.3 per 100,000). The effectiveness of nirsevimab was calculated as a 36% reduction in hospitalization for IPD (OR, 0.64; 95% CI, 0.46-0.82) and a 34% reduction by 9 months (OR, 0.66; 95% CI, 0.51-0.85).
Response, status and uncertainty

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

再次阅读:RSV 疫苗 扩展:对于实践操作意味着什么 - 实践业务

Read Again: RSV Vaccine Expansion: What It Means For Practice Operations - Practice Business
新闻简报The NHS has expanded the RSV vaccination programme in the UK to include adults aged 80 and over, as well as residents in care homes for older adults, starting in April 2026. This initiative aims to protect vulnerable populations from severe respiratory complications caused by RSV, such as pneumonia, bronchitis, and exacerbations of chronic conditions like COPD. The expansion adds approximately 3 million people to the programme, significantly increasing the number of individuals that primary care teams must support. RSV is responsible for around 9,000 annual hospitalisations among those over 75 in the UK. Practice managers are advised to adjust clinic capacity, ensure vaccine stock availability, coordinate with care homes and pharmacies, and update patient recall systems to maximise vaccine uptake and reduce RSV-related complications. The programme builds on an initial rollout launched in September 2024, which targeted adults aged 75 to 79 and those turning 75. Pregnant women from 28 weeks gestation are also offered the vaccine to protect newborns. Effective planning and communication are essential to manage the increased demand and ensure the programme's success.
查看新闻五要素
时间
RSV 疫苗接种 计划的扩展于 2026 年 4 月宣布,文章于 2026 年 8 月 27 日发表。初始计划于 2024 年 9 月启动。
地点与对象
此次扩张正在英国进行,专门针对 80 岁及以上的成年人、老年人护理院的居民以及妊娠 28 周以上的孕妇。
事件
NHS 将 RSV 疫苗接种 计划扩大到包括 80 岁及以上的成年人以及疗养院居民,以保护弱势患者免受严重呼吸道疾病的侵害。这是根据 NHS 官方公告确认的事件。
规模、影响与风险
The expansion adds approximately 3 million people to the programme, significantly increasing the cohort practices must support. RSV can lead to severe complications in older adults, including pneumonia, bronchitis, and exacerbations of chronic conditions like COPD, often resulting in hospitalisations and increased pressure on secondary care services.
应对、状态与不确定性
建议实践经理审查和调整诊所容量,确保充足的 疫苗 库存和冷链管理,与护理院和社区药房协调,更新患者召回系统,并为工作人员提供有关资格和交付途径的明确指导。该计划正在进行中,需要有效的规划和协调。

News Brief
The NHS has expanded the RSV vaccination programme in the UK to include adults aged 80 and over, as well as residents in care homes for older adults, starting in April 2026. This initiative aims to protect vulnerable populations from severe respiratory complications caused by RSV, such as pneumonia, bronchitis, and exacerbations of chronic conditions like COPD. The expansion adds approximately 3 million people to the programme, significantly increasing the number of individuals that primary care teams must support. MENU Home News Digi-Wrap Sign Up About ► About Practice Business Our Team Contact us For Advertisers Sign in Home News Digi-Wrap Sign Up About About Practice Business Our Team Contact us For Advertisers Sign in Management Health & Patient Care Health Tech Practice Operations Recruit and Retain Legal & Compliance Live It Follow us on Twitter Follow us on Facebook Follow us on LinkedIn Email us HomePractice OperationsRead Again: RSV Vaccine Expansion: What It Means For Practice Operations Read Again: RSV Vaccine Expansion: What It Means For Practice Operations August 27, 2026 Practice Operations The expansion of the RSV vaccination programme to older adults and care home residents presents new operational and planning priorities for GP practice managers CREDIT: This is an edited version of an article that originally appeared in England NHS Primary care teams are set to play a key role in delivering an expanded vaccination programme aimed at protecting some of the most vulnerable patients from serious respiratory illness. In April, the NHS widened access to the respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccine, making all adults aged 80 and over, as well as residents in care homes for older adults, eligible. This expansion brings approximately 3 million additional people into the programme, significantly increasing the cohort practices may need to support. Clinical Importance and Patient Risk RSV is a common respiratory virus that can lead to severe complications in older adults, including pneumonia, bronchitis, and exacerbations of chronic conditions such as COPD. These complications often result in hospital admissions and increased pressure on secondary care services. In the UK, RSV is responsible for around 9,000 hospitalisations annually among people aged over 75. A single vaccine dose offers protection lasting several years, making it a valuable preventative intervention. Programme Continuity and Existing Cohorts The expanded rollout builds on the initial programme launched in September 2024, which targeted adults aged 75 to 79 and those turning 75. These groups remain eligible if they have not yet received the vaccine. Additionally, pregnant women from 28 weeks gestation continue to be offered the RSV vaccine to protect newborns from severe respiratory infections. Practices should ensure these cohorts are not overlooked amid the expansion. Access and Delivery Channels Patients can book vaccinations through GP practices, and practice teams should ensure booking systems are updated accordingly. Care home staff will play a role in coordinating vaccinations for residents, while selected community pharmacies are also offering the vaccine in some areas. Practice managers should consider communication strategies to inform eligible patients, including targeted messaging, recall systems and collaboration with local healthcare partners. Key Actions for Practice Managers Review and adjust clinic capacity to meet increased demand Ensure adequate vaccine stock and cold chain management Coordinate with care homes and community pharmacies Update patient recall and invitation systems Support staff with clear guidance on eligibility and delivery pathways Effective planning and coordination will be essential to maximise uptake and reduce RSV-related complications in high-risk populations. View five news elements
Time
The expansion of the RSV vaccination programme was announced in April 2026, with the article published on August 27, 2026. The initial programme was launched in September 2024.
Location and population
The expansion is taking place in the UK, specifically targeting adults aged 80 and over, residents in care homes for older adults, and pregnant women from 28 weeks gestation.
Event
The NHS expanded the RSV vaccination programme to include adults aged 80 and over, as well as care home residents, to protect vulnerable patients from severe respiratory illness. This is a confirmed event based on official NHS announcements.
Scale, impact and risk
The expansion adds approximately 3 million people to the programme, significantly increasing the cohort practices must support. RSV can lead to severe complications in older adults, including pneumonia, bronchitis, and exacerbations of chronic conditions like COPD, often resulting in hospitalisations and increased pressure on secondary care services.
Response, status and uncertainty
Practice managers are advised to review and adjust clinic capacity, ensure adequate vaccine stock and cold chain management, coordinate with care homes and community pharmacies, update patient recall systems, and support staff with clear guidance on eligibility and delivery pathways. The programme is ongoing and requires effective planning and coordination.

将为高危成年人提供 疫苗 预防常见呼吸道感染 - Nation.Cymru

At-risk adults to be offered vaccine against common respiratory infection - Nation.Cymru
新闻简报威尔士公共卫生部门宣布,从 2026 年 9 月开始,根据威尔士 NHS 计划的限时延期,将向 65 岁至 74 岁、免疫系统较弱或患有慢性呼吸道疾病的成年人提供 RSV 疫苗。这一决定是基于有证据表明这些人面临与 75 岁以上老年人相似的 RSV 住院风险。RSV 疫苗 已常规向 75 岁及以上的人提供,以及在怀孕期间,保护婴儿。威尔士公共卫生部门牵头的一项研究发现,疫苗接种 在 2024-25 年冬季将 75 至 79 岁人群因 RSV 入院的风险降低了约 75%,2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 4 月期间该年龄组的入院风险下降了 13% 以上。该计划于 2024 年 9 月在威尔士推出,扩展旨在在 RSV 肆虐的冬季保护更脆弱的成年人。最危险的。
查看新闻五要素
时间
NHS 疫苗接种 计划于 2024 年 9 月在威尔士推出,该研究于 2024-25 年冬季进行。疫苗 将于 2026 年 9 月提供。
地点与对象
威尔士,专门针对 65 至 74 岁免疫抑制或患有慢性呼吸道疾病的成年人。
事件
威尔士公共卫生部门宣布,根据 NHS 计划的限时延期,将为免疫系统较弱或患有慢性呼吸道疾病的 65 至 74 岁成年人提供 RSV 疫苗。这是基于有证据表明这些人面临与 75 岁以上老年人相似的 RSV 住院风险。
规模、影响与风险
据估计,威尔士每年 75 岁以上老年人因 RSV 相关入院的人数为 700 至 1,000 人。疫苗 在 2024-25 年冬季将 75 岁至 79 岁人群的住院风险降低了约 75%,2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 4 月期间该年龄组的住院风险下降了 13% 以上。
应对、状态与不确定性
NHS 疫苗接种 计划已经到位,威尔士公共卫生局表示 疫苗 有效运作。延期是有时间限制的并且持续进行。该研究结果基于 2024-25 年冬季的证据,该计划仍处于早期阶段。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

At-risk adults to be offered vaccine against common respiratory infection - Nation.Cymru

News Brief
Public Health Wales has announced that adults aged 65 to 74 with weakened immune systems or chronic respiratory conditions will be offered the RSV vaccine under a time-limited extension of the NHS programme in Wales, starting in September 2026. This decision is based on evidence showing that these individuals face a similar risk of hospitalisation from RSV as those aged over 75. The RSV vaccine is already routinely offered to people aged 75 and over, as well as during pregnancy, to protect babies. A study led by Public Health Wales found that vaccination reduced the risk of hospital admission from RSV by around 75% among people aged 75 to 79 during the winter of 2024-25, and hospital admissions in that age group fell by more than 13% between October 2024 and April 2025. The programme was introduced in Wales in September 2024, and the expansion aims to protect more vulnerable adults during the winter months when RSV is most dangerous.
Original source text (English)

OFF ON Opted Out CONFIRM Data DeletionData AccessPrivacy Policy Support our Nation today - please donate here Skip to content Wales' News Site of the Year Support Us Advertise Contact Home News Opinion Culture Sport Feature Search for: News At-risk adults to be offered vaccine against common respiratory infection 01 Sep 2026 2 minute read Vaccine being administered. Nation.Cymru staff More people in Wales at increased risk of serious illness from a common respiratory infection are to be offered a vaccine. Adults aged 65 to 74 who are immunosuppressed or have chronic respiratory disease will become eligible for the RSV vaccine under a time-limited extension of the NHS programme during September. The vaccine is already routinely offered to people aged 75 and over, as well as during pregnancy to help protect babies during their first months of life. Public Health Wales said evidence showed people aged over 65 with weakened immune systems or chronic respiratory conditions faced a similar risk of hospitalisation from RSV as those aged over 75. Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) usually causes a mild illness such as a cough or cold in adults, but can lead to pneumonia and other serious health problems in older and more vulnerable people. It can also worsen existing heart and lung conditions. In Wales, RSV is estimated to cause between 700 and 1,000 hospital admissions among people aged over 75 each year, as well as up to 9,000 GP appointments. The NHS vaccination programme was introduced in Wales in September 2024. A study led by Public Health Wales alongside other UK teams subsequently found vaccination reduced the risk of hospital admission from RSV by around 75% among people aged 75 to 79 during the winter of 2024-25. RSV-related hospital admissions among that age group also fell by more than 13% between October 2024 and April 2025, despite the vaccination programme still being in its early stages. Dr Christopher Johnson, head of the Vaccine Preventable Disease Programme at Public Health Wales, said: “We have strong evidence that the RSV vaccine works. It reduces the risk of ending up in hospital with RSV by around 75 per cent, helping protect older adults from serious illness. “By expanding free access to more people aged 65 to 74 who are at higher risk, we can help keep more people healthy and out of hospital, particularly during the winter months when RSV is most dangerous. “If you’re eligible, I strongly encourage you to get vaccinated when you’re invited.” Share this: Click to share on Facebook (Opens in new window) Facebook Click to share on X (Opens in new window) X Click to email a link to a friend (Opens in new window) Email Support our Nation today For the price of a cup of coffee a month you can help us create an independent, not-for-profit, national news service for the people of Wales, by the people of Wales. Time
The NHS vaccination programme was introduced in Wales in September 2024, and the study was conducted during the winter of 2024-25. The vaccine is being offered during September 2026.
Location and population
Wales, specifically targeting adults aged 65 to 74 who are immunosuppressed or have chronic respiratory disease.
Event
Public Health Wales announced that adults aged 65 to 74 with weakened immune systems or chronic respiratory conditions will be offered the RSV vaccine under a time-limited extension of the NHS programme. This is based on evidence showing these individuals face a similar risk of hospitalisation from RSV as those aged over 75.
Scale, impact and risk
RSV-related hospital admissions among people aged over 75 in Wales are estimated to cause between 700 and 1,000 admissions annually. The vaccine reduces the risk of hospital admission by around 75% among people aged 75 to 79 during the winter of 2024-25, and hospital admissions in that age group fell by more than 13% between October 2024 and April 2025.
Response, status and uncertainty
The NHS vaccination programme is in place, and Public Health Wales has stated that the vaccine works effectively. The extension is time-limited and ongoing. The study results are based on evidence from the winter of 2024-25, and the programme is still in its early stages.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

Lancet子刊:尼塞韦单抗大幅降低婴儿RSV毛细支气管炎住院负担-MedSci.cn - medsci.cn

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报This news report discusses a study published in a Lancet subjournal evaluating the clinical value of nirsevimab, a long-acting monoclonal antibody, in reducing the hospitalization burden of RSV-associated bronchiolitis in infants. The study, conducted in Italy, is a multicenter retrospective cohort study assessing the nationwide impact of nirsevimab. RSV infection is a leading cause of hospitalization in infants under one year old, with over 34 million cases globally each year, resulting in 3.3 million hospitalizations and more than 120,000 deaths. The study highlights that nirsevimab can provide long-term protection with a single dose, significantly reducing the use of medical resources such as emergency visits, general ward admissions, and PICU intubations. However, the implementation of the drug in Italy has shown regional disparities in drug distribution and screening strategies, leaving national-level data on its effectiveness and disease dynamics incomplete. The findings suggest that nirsevimab could be a highly effective tool in preventing RSV infections in infants, particularly in high-risk populations such as those with preterm birth or congenital heart disease.

急性病毒性毛细支气管炎是新生儿及1岁以内婴儿首要住院病因,其中呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)感染占全部病例的60%以上,是全球婴幼儿下呼吸道感染的核心致病元凶。全球每年约3400万5岁以下儿童发生RSV相关下呼吸道感染,造成330万例住院及超12万例死亡,低收入地区及合并基础疾病的婴幼儿为高危人群。既往临床仅有的RSV预防药物为帕利珠单抗,该药作用周期短、需每月给药、费用高昂,且仅适用于少数高危早产儿,仅能覆盖25%的重症RSV病例,绝大多数健康足月患儿缺乏有效防护手段。 长效单克隆抗体尼塞韦单抗(nirsevimab)可单次给药,为婴幼儿首个RSV流行季提供长效防护,已在多国获批应用。意大利于2024—2025年首次全国推广尼塞韦单抗预防项目,但该国医疗体系存在区域自治特点,各地区药物投放时间、婴幼儿筛查召回策略存在显著异质性,全国层面的真实应用效果、疾病流行特征及患儿病情变化数据仍处于空白状态。基于此,本研究依托意大利全国儿科研究网络,开展多中心真实世界研究,系统评估尼塞韦单抗全国普及的临床价值。 随着伊利诺伊州密苏里州新冠病例上升,亚当斯县卫生部门提供预防建议 - WGEM

Adams County Health Department offers prevention tips as COVID cases rise in Missouri, Illinois - WGEM
新闻简报随着该地区 COVID-19 病例的增加,密苏里州和伊利诺伊州亚当斯县卫生局 (ACHD) 正在提供预防建议。疾病预防控制中心估计这些州的病例正在增加,但指出严重程度并不像一年前那么高。ACHD 官员鼓励那些能够接受 疫苗接种 的人,以防止今年秋天疾病可能增加。他们强调,虽然疫苗接种不能保证完全保护,但可以降低出现严重症状和住院的风险。预防措施包括经常洗手至少 20 秒和戴口罩以减少细菌飞沫的传播。卫生部门还强调了社区责任在 疫苗接种 决策中的重要性。然而,尚不清楚爱荷华州的病例是否在增加,因为该州不跟踪流行趋势。这些努力旨在减轻病毒的传播并保护公众健康。
查看新闻五要素
时间
美国疾病控制与预防中心估计,密苏里州和伊利诺伊州的 COVID-19 病例正在增加。
地点与对象
密苏里州和伊利诺伊州亚当斯县卫生局 (ACHD)。
事件
ACHD 提供减少患病风险的预防技巧,包括洗手和戴口罩。
规模、影响与风险
疾病预防控制中心表示,与一年前相比,病例没有那么严重。(N11) 卫生官员建议使用 疫苗接种 来预防今年秋季疾病的增加。
应对、状态与不确定性
ACHD 鼓励身体允许的人使用 疫苗接种。(N5,N6) 目前尚不清楚爱荷华州的病例是否正在上升。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Adams County Health Department offers prevention tips as COVID cases rise in Missouri, Illinois - WGEM

News Brief
The Adams County Health Department (ACHD) in Missouri and Illinois is offering prevention tips as COVID-19 cases rise in the region. The CDC estimates that cases are growing in these states, but notes that the severity is not as high as it was one year ago. ACHD officials are encouraging vaccination for those who can receive it to prevent a potential increase in illness this fall. They emphasize that while vaccination does not guarantee complete protection, it can reduce the risk of severe symptoms and hospitalization. Prevention measures include frequent handwashing for at least 20 seconds and wearing masks to reduce the spread of bacterial droplets. The health department also highlights the importance of community responsibility in vaccination decisions. However, it is unclear whether cases are rising in Iowa, as the state does not track epidemic trends. These efforts aim to mitigate the spread of the virus and protect public health.
Original source text (English)

By Austin Johnson Published: Aug. 31, 2026 at 4:50 AM CDT Add Us On GoogleAdd as a preferred source on Google QUINCY (WGEM) - The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention estimates that COVID-19 cases are growing in Missouri and Illinois. The Adams County Health Department (ACHD) is offering tips on preventing sickness and minimizing risk. The ACHD is encouraging vaccination for those whose bodies allow it as it looks to prevent a rise in illness this fall. The Adams County Health Department is encouraging vaccination for those whose bodies allow it as it looks to prevent a rise in illness this fall.(WGEM) “There’s lots of research to show that they work, and it protects you if you get a vaccine,” said Tara Bealor, health protection director. “It doesn’t necessarily mean you’re not gonna get sick, but it will lessen the severity of it. It will decrease your risk of being hospitalized and having severe illness.” Health department officials said people can get both a COVID and flu shot on the same day, with one shot in the left arm and the other in the right arm. “It’s important for all of us to remember that when making a choice about vaccines, yes, it is about you and your family, but it is also about the community,” Bealor said. It is not clear whether cases are rising in Iowa because the state does not estimate an epidemic trend. According to the CDC cases are not as severe compared to one year ago. To lower the risk of sickness, including COVID, flu, RSV or a common cold, health officials recommend proper and frequent handwashing for at least 20 seconds, whether sick or not. According to the National Library of Medicine, wearing a mask reduces the chances of bacterial droplets entering the nose or mouth. All Illinois health departments no longer track COVID cases. According to the latest CDC guidance, people who contract the virus can return to normal activities after symptoms disappear and they are fever-free for 24 hours without medication. People experiencing trouble breathing or chest pain are recommended to call their family doctor. Copyright 2026 WGEM. All rights reserved. 63% 的美国人表示他们可能会接种流感疫苗;CVS 提供更新的流感 疫苗 - 库存泰坦

63% of Americans say they're likely to get a flu shot; CVS offers updated flu vaccines - Stock Titan
新闻简报CVS Health 于 2026 年 8 月 27 日宣布,更新的 2026-2027 流感 疫苗 现已在全国 CVS 药房和 MinuteClinic 地点出售。CVS Health 委托进行的一项调查发现,与往年类似,63% 的美国人可能在本季节接种流感疫苗,其中 68% 计划在 10 月底之前接种。CDC 建议几乎所有 6 个月及以上的人每年接种一次流感 疫苗接种,以降低患流感相关疾病和并发症的风险。CVS 还在参与地点提供甲型流感、乙型流感和 COVID-19 的联合检测,部分州的药剂师能够对符合条件的患者进行检测和治疗。这些服务旨在在呼吸道病毒季节期间提供便捷的预防性护理和相关护理。疫苗 和检测服务的提供是 CVS Health 支持公共卫生和季节性疾病管理的更广泛战略的一部分。
查看新闻五要素
时间
CVS Health 于 2026 年 8 月 27 日宣布推出更新的流感 疫苗。该调查数据是在美国为秋冬呼吸道病毒季节做准备期间收集的。
地点与对象
人口,重点关注计划感染流感 疫苗 的美国人。全国范围内的 CVS Pharmacy 和 MinuteClinic 地点均可提供这些服务。
事件
CVS Health 宣布在其药房和诊所提供更新的 2026-2027 流感 疫苗。CVS Health 委托进行的一项调查发现,63% 的美国人可能在本季节接种流感疫苗,其中 68% 计划在 10 月底之前接种。
规模、影响与风险
该活动涉及在全国范围内推广流感 疫苗 以及一项调查,该调查表明有很高比例的美国人计划接种疫苗。CDC 建议几乎所有 6 个月及以上的人每年接种一次流感 疫苗接种,强调其在减少流感相关疾病和并发症方面的有效性。
应对、状态与不确定性
CVS Health 已实施更新的流感 疫苗 可用性并扩大了测试和 治疗 服务。调查数据基于消费者的反馈,与任何具体的 暴发 或确诊病例无关。疾病预防控制中心的建议是一般公共卫生指南。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

63% of Americans say they're likely to get a flu shot; CVS offers updated flu vaccines - Stock Titan

News Brief
CVS Health announced on August 27, 2026, that updated 2026-2027 flu vaccines are now available at CVS Pharmacy and MinuteClinic locations nationwide. A survey commissioned by CVS Health found that 63% of Americans are likely to get a flu shot this season, similar to prior years, with 68% planning to do so by the end of October. The CDC recommends annual flu vaccination for nearly everyone ages six months and older to reduce the risk of flu-related illness and complications. CVS also offers combination testing for Influenza A, Influenza B, and COVID-19 at participating locations, with pharmacists in select states able to both test and treat eligible patients. These services aim to provide convenient access to preventive care and connected care during the respiratory virus season. The availability of vaccines and testing services is part of CVS Health's broader strategy to support public health and seasonal illness management.
Original source text (English)

Adds StockTitan as a preferred source · opens Google Add on Google Not now 08/27/2026 - 01:00 PM CVS Health survey finds more than 60 percent of consumers say they are likely to get a flu shot this season. Expanded testing and treatment services help make CVS Pharmacy a convenient destination for seasonal care. WOONSOCKET, R.I., Aug. 27, 2026 /PRNewswire/ -- CVS Health® (NYSE: CVS) today announced that updated 2026-2027 flu vaccines are now available at CVS Pharmacy® and MinuteClinic® locations nationwide. According to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC), annual flu vaccination is one of the most effective ways to help reduce the risk of flu-related illness and complications. Patients can schedule vaccination appointments online at CVS.com, MinuteClinic.com or through the CVS Health app. Walk-in appointments are also available at CVS Pharmacy and MinuteClinic locations. As families prepare for the fall and winter respiratory virus season, CVS Health offers a range of services designed to support preventive care and help patients manage seasonal illnesses. In addition to flu vaccination, patients can access testing and treatment options at select CVS Pharmacy and MinuteClinic locations, providing connected care before, during and after illness. With health care professionals serving communities across the country, CVS Health remains committed to helping patients stay protected throughout the season. Most Americans plan to get vaccinated this flu season According to a survey of U.S. consumers commissioned by CVS Health1, 63% of Americans say they are likely to receive a flu vaccine this season. That is similar to the last two years when 63% said they were planning to get a flu shot in 2025 and 62% said they were planning to get a flu shot in 2024. Among those planning to get vaccinated: 68% plan to get a flu vaccine by the end of October. 34% of consumers want a flu shot before the illness is spreading widely; 28% want to get vaccinated before spending time with someone who may be vulnerable to influenza, like a newborn, older adult or someone who is ill; and 24% want to get the flu shot to help avoid missing school, work or other responsibilities. Habit and personal protection are the most significant motivators among people who intend to get the flu shot, with 39% of people saying they get a flu shot every year and 37% saying they get a flu shot to protect their health or their family's health. 43% of consumers say they plan to get a flu shot at a retail pharmacy, while 34% plan to get the flu shot at a doctor's office. "Getting vaccinated is one of the simplest and most effective steps people can take to help protect themselves and those around them during flu season," said Amy Compton-Phillips, MD, Executive Vice President and Chief Medical Officer, CVS Health. "As respiratory viruses continue to circulate each year, it's important that people and families have convenient access to trusted health care professionals who can answer questions, provide recommended vaccinations and help them stay healthy throughout the season." CDC recommends annual flu vaccination for nearly everyone The CDC recommends that everyone ages six months and older, with rare exceptions, receive an annual flu vaccine, with September and October generally being ideal times for vaccination ahead of peak flu activity. The updated 2026-2027 vaccine is designed to help protect against flu strains that experts anticipate will be most common this season. To further support patients during respiratory illness season, participating CVS Pharmacy locations are offering convenient testing options, including a combination test that can detect Influenza A, Influenza B, and COVID-19 with a single swab. In states where pharmacists are authorized to both test and treat eligible patients for flu and COVID-19, patients can receive an assessment and, when appropriate, treatment recommendations during the same visit. Participating states include California, Colorado, Idaho, Illinois, Michigan, Minnesota, Montana, Nebraska, South Dakota, Tennessee, Washington, Wisconsin, and Wyoming. Patients can learn more about testing options by visiting CVS.com. MinuteClinic also offers testing for Influenza A, Influenza B, and COVID-19 in all clinic locations. "Patients want health care that is easy to access and fits into their busy lives," said Sid Tenneti, Senior Vice President and Interim President, Pharmacy and Consumer Wellness, CVS Health. "Whether someone is looking to get vaccinated, needs help understanding symptoms, or is seeking testing and treatment options, CVS Pharmacy is committed to providing connected, convenient care in the communities we serve." Convenient vaccination options for patients Vaccination appointments are available seven days a week at CVS Pharmacy locations, including those inside Target stores, as well as at MinuteClinic. Patients can schedule appointments online at CVS.com, MinuteClinic.com or through the CVS Health app. The online scheduler allows patients to book appointments at CVS Pharmacy and MinuteClinic for up to four people at once and receive multiple CDC-recommended vaccinations during a single visit, including flu, COVID-19, hepatitis B, RSV, MMR, and shingles vaccines. Organizations can also partner with CVS Health to host vaccination clinics at the workplace or within their communities, meeting patients where they are and making it easier to access recommended vaccinations. In addition to vaccinations, MinuteClinic offers evaluation and treatment for select seasonal illnesses, including flu-like symptoms, with testing available for eligible patients. More information and appointments are available at MinuteClinic.com. Year-round access to recommended vaccinations CVS Pharmacy and MinuteClinic offer more than 15 commonly recommended vaccinations throughout the year to help protect against preventable diseases. Patients who receive any CDC-recommended vaccine at CVS Pharmacy will also receive a $5 off $20 to use on in-store purchases.2 Most preventive vaccines are available at no cost through many insurance plans, including Medicare Part B coverage for eligible vaccines. Out-of-pocket costs may vary based on insurance coverage and vaccine type. Patients who receive a CDC-recommended vaccination at CVS Pharmacy may also be eligible for promotional savings and ExtraCare Rewards®, subject to program terms and conditions. Video and photo assets from CVS Pharmacy and MinuteClinic are available here. About CVS Health CVS Health is a leading health solutions company simplifying health care one person, one family and one community at a time. As of June 30, 2026, the Company had approximately 9,000 retail pharmacy locations, more than 1,000 walk-in and primary care medical clinics and a leading pharmacy benefits manager with approximately 87 million plan members. The Company also serves an estimated 37 million people through a broad range of health insurance products and related services. The Company's integrated model uses personalized, technology driven services to connect people to simply better health, increasing access to quality care, delivering better outcomes, and lowering overall costs. 科克父母被敦促考虑用于婴儿的‘颠覆性’呼吸道合胞病毒尼塞韦单抗

Cork parents urged to consider 'gamechanger' RSV vaccine for babies - echo live
新闻简报我们鼓励科克家长为他们的宝宝考虑使用“游戏规则改变者”RSV 疫苗,因为它已被证明可以显着降低冬季患严重 RSV 疾病的风险。HSE South West 正在推动符合条件的婴儿免费接种疫苗,在出院前在妇产医院或通过全科医生诊所注射疫苗。2025/2026 年计划的评估表明,与 RSV 相关的急诊就诊、住院和 ICU 入院率大幅下降。疫苗,称为“尼塞韦单抗”,提供即时保护,被描述为非常安全和高效。该计划针对的是 2026 年 9 月 1 日至 2027 年 2 月 28 日期间出生的婴儿,以及患有严重 RSV 疾病风险较高的婴儿。这篇文章强调了这种免疫接种对于保持婴儿健康和冬季出院的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
文章发表于2026-09-01,该活动与即将到来的冬季有关。
地点与对象
该活动主要针对科克和凯里,针对这些地区符合条件的婴儿的父母。
事件
科克地区的父母被敦促为婴儿考虑使用‘颠覆性’呼吸道合胞病毒尼塞韦单抗(疫苗),该疗法已被证明可减少急诊就诊、住院及ICU入住人次。
规模、影响与风险
2025/2026年度项目评估显示,该疗法接种率高且呼吸道合胞病毒相关健康影响显著降低,但未报告具体病例数或死亡人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
HSE South West 鼓励通过妇产医院和全科医生诊所进行免疫接种,没有迹象表明存在未解决的信息或来源限制。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Cork parents urged to consider 'gamechanger' RSV vaccine for babies - echo live

News Brief
Cork parents are being encouraged to consider the 'gamechanger' RSV vaccine for their babies, as it has been shown to significantly reduce the risk of severe RSV illness during the winter months. The HSE South West is promoting free immunisation for eligible infants, with doses administered in maternity hospitals before discharge or through GP practices. Evaluation from the 2025/2026 programme indicated high uptake and substantial reductions in RSV-related emergency department visits, hospitalisations, and ICU admissions. The vaccine, known as 'nirsevimab', provides immediate protection and is described as very safe and highly effective. The initiative targets babies born between September 1, 2026, and February 28, 2027, as well as those at higher risk of serious RSV illness. The article highlights the importance of this immunisation in keeping babies healthy and out of hospital during the winter season.
Original source text (English)

HSE South West is encouraging parents across Cork to protect their babies this winter with free immunisation against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV).       Amy Campbell HSE South West is encouraging parents across Cork to protect their babies this winter with free immunisation against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). RSV is a common winter virus that can cause serious illness in infants, particularly during their first season. Immunisation is provided as a single dose of ‘nirsevimab’, giving babies immediate protection and helping to prevent severe infection and hospitalisation. Dr Anne Sheahan, regional director of public health at HSE South West, said: “RSV immunisation has been a gamechanger for protecting babies from serious illness during the winter months. “It is very safe, highly effective, and gives babies protection at a time when they are particularly vulnerable to RSV. Here in the South West, we strongly encourage parents of every eligible baby in Cork and Kerry to take up the offer of free RSV immunisation. “It is a simple step that can make a very real difference in keeping babies well and out of hospital this winter.” Babies born between September 1, 2026, and February 28, 2027, will be offered RSV immunisation in maternity hospitals before discharge, where possible. Babies born between March 1 and August 31, 2026, can receive RSV immunisation through participating GP practices. Eligible babies at higher risk of serious RSV illness will be offered immunisation through a specialist pathway. Evaluation from the 2025/2026 programme found high uptake across eligible groups and substantial reductions in RSV-related emergency department presentations, hospitalisations, and intensive care unit admissions. 江西生物公布2026年中期业绩 - TradingView

JIANGXI BIO Announced 2026 Interim Results - TradingView
新闻简报JIANGXI BIO, a biopharmaceutical company, announced its unaudited interim results for the six months ended June 30, 2026. The report highlights the company's financial performance, including revenue of RMB83.3 mn and gross profit of RMB60.3 mn, with a gross profit margin of 72.3%. R&D expenses rose by 18.6% year-on-year, and the company made progress in clinical trials for products targeting viral infections, including Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV). The Group is also advancing its AI-driven development platform for equine polyclonal antibodies, aiming to enhance efficiency and scalability. The report notes the company's focus on both human and veterinary pharmaceutical products, with specific mention of its dual-wheel pipeline strategy. While the financial and R&D progress is confirmed, there is no explicit mention of health-system or operational risks. The company is actively working on platform upgrades and expanding its market presence, indicating ongoing investigation and development efforts.
查看新闻五要素
时间
集团公布截至2026年6月30日止六个月(「期间」)未经审核中期业绩。
地点与对象
该集团、江西生物制品研究所股份有限公司及其子公司在全球制药行业开展业务,专注于人用和兽用药品。
事件
江西生物公布2026年中期业绩,重点介绍了马多克隆抗体技术领域的财务业绩、研发进展和平台开发。该活动以官方公告为准。
规模、影响与风险
The Group achieved revenue of approximately RMB83.3 mn, gross profit of RMB60.3 mn, and adjusted net profit of RMB38.7 mn during the Period. R&D expenses increased by 18.6% year-on-year, and the Group recorded progress in clinical trials and platform development for products related to RSV and other viral infections. The scale of impact is related to financial and R&D progress, with no explicit health-system or operational risk mentioned.
应对、状态与不确定性
集团正通过AEPC平台积极提升研发和生产能力,融合人工智能和数字技术。报告概述了正在进行的开发和未来计划,响应状态正在调查中。

JIANGXI BIO Announced 2026 Interim Results - TradingView

News Brief
JIANGXI BIO, a biopharmaceutical company, announced its unaudited interim results for the six months ended June 30, 2026. The report highlights the company's financial performance, including revenue of RMB83.3 mn and gross profit of RMB60.3 mn, with a gross profit margin of 72.3%. R&D expenses rose by 18.6% year-on-year, and the company made progress in clinical trials for products targeting viral infections, including Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV). The Group is also advancing its AI-driven development platform for equine polyclonal antibodies, aiming to enhance efficiency and scalability. The report notes the company's focus on both human and veterinary pharmaceutical products, with specific mention of its dual-wheel pipeline strategy. While the financial and R&D progress is confirmed, there is no explicit mention of health-system or operational risks. The company is actively working on platform upgrades and expanding its market presence, indicating ongoing investigation and development efforts.
Original source text (English)
Overseas Contract Orders Reach Nearly RMB27.4 mn and R&D Translation Gains Momentum, with the AEPC Platform Reshaping the R&D Paradigm for Equine Polyclonal Antibody Interim Results Highlights Key Financial Indicators Revenue amounted to RMB83.3 mn; gross profit amounted to RMB60.3 mn, representing a gross profit margin of approximately 72.3%; Adjusted net profit (net profit excluding one-off items such as listing expenses and tax rate differential adjustments) amounted to approximately RMB38.7 mn; R&D expenses amounted to RMB11.0 mn, representing a year-on-year increase of 18.6% and accounting for 13.2% of revenue; Key Pipeline Progress Human tetanus antitoxin (Human TAT): As of the end of the Period, total sales volume was approximately 9,577,000 units, comprising approximately 5,911,000 units in the domestic market and approximately 3,666,000 units in overseas markets. The Group successfully won multiple overseas government tender orders, with cumulative contracted orders of approximately 6,859,000 units and a total contract value of approximately RMB27.4 mn; Veterinary Tetanus Antitoxin (Veterinary TAT): As of (the date of the announcement), the product had obtained marketing approval and officially commenced production, becoming the Group's first veterinary product to achieve industrialization; Pregnant Mare Serum Gonadotropin (PMSG): As of the date of the announcement, the commercialization of PMSG APIs and formulations were progressing, and the Group is accelerating the upgrade and construction of a PMSG production line designed in compliance with the Chinese Veterinary Pharmacopoeia and EU GMP standards; Clinical Development (as of the date of the announcement) By pipeline category, the Group had 9 candidate products, including 6 human pharmaceutical products and 3 veterinary pharmaceutical products; Key progress: 2 human pharmaceutical product pipelines, namely Agkistrodon Halys Antivenom and Agkistrodon Acutus Antivenom, initiated Phase II clinical trials; and 2 veterinary pharmaceutical products, namely Bursal Peptide Injection and Pig Spleen Transfer Factor, obtained the new veterinary drug registration approval; Translation of Research Outcomes Phased breakthroughs achieved in patent accumulation, academic publications and accreditations: The Group held 52 granted patents, completed the publication of 2 breakthrough research results in international biomedical journals during the Period; The Group was recognized as the "Advanced Intelligent Factory (先進智能工廠)" and the Specialized, Sophisticated, Distinctive and Innovative "Little Lighthouse Enterprise (小燈塔企業)" in Jiangxi Province, and was awarded the Second Prize of the Jiangxi Provincial Technological Invention Award (江西省技術發明獎二等獎); Platform-level development integrating AI and technology was upgraded across two core segments of the industry chain, namely antigen development and antibody preparation: The Group established an "AI+ fusion protein mRNA antigen" development platform, enhancing the efficiency of antigen design and subsequent product development; The Group developed an equine F(ab')2 process platform based on immobilized dual recombinant endopeptidases and, for the first time, established a synergistic dual-endopeptidase "one-pot" process based on molecular mechanism design. At the laboratory research stage, the Group applied two purification technologies, namely ion exchange chromatography and pathogen-specific affinity chromatography, achieving mean increases in specific activity of relevant products of approximately 30% and 400%, respectively; Next Steps The Group will comprehensively upgrade and build the "AEPC Platform", integrating R&D, production, sales and governance through AI and digital capabilities to form a closed loop, promote resource synergy and commonality reuse across its dual-wheel pipelines, reduce the marginal costs of pipeline development and translation, and continuously unlock the platform-level value of equine polyclonal antibody technology. HONG KONG, Aug. 31, 2026 /PRNewswire/ -- Jiangxi Institute of Biological Products Inc. and its subsidiaries (collectively, "JIANGXI BIO" or the "Group") (stock code: 6915.HK) announced its unaudited interim results for the six months ended June 30, 2026 (the "Period"). During the Period, adhering to its dual-wheel synergistic pipeline strategy of "human pharmaceutical products + veterinary pharmaceutical products" and leveraging its rare integrated full-industry-chain advantages in antiserum, the Group achieved phased progress in core product market share, clinical pipeline advancement, platform technology breakthroughs and international market expansion. Resilient Profitability with Accelerated Momentum for Mid-to-Long-Term Translation Overseas Contracted Orders for Products Rise to Approximately RMB27.4 mn; R&D Investment Increases by Nearly 19% Y-o-Y In the first half of 2026, the global pharmaceutical industry was undergoing structural adjustment, impacted by geopolitical uncertainties, trade frictions and continued strengthening of compliance regulations. The resource allocation across the industry has become more prudent. Facing a complex external environment, the Group leveraged the clinical essential demand for Human TAT, its fully integrated industry chain advantages and its global market presence to maintain steady development in its core business, expanding sales channels and increasing contracted orders through differentiated marketing strategies to enhance its operational resilience. During the Period, the Group recorded revenue of approximately RMB83.3 mn, representing a decrease of 16.5% year-on-year ("Y-o-Y"); gross profit amounted to RMB60.3 mn, representing a gross profit margin of approximately 72.3%; and profit for the Period amounted to approximately RMB14.4 mn. Excluding one-off items such as listing expenses and tax rate differential adjustments, adjusted net profit amounted to RMB38.7 mn, representing a decrease of 21.7% Y-o-Y. The changes in results were primarily attributable to the combined effects of: (i) an increase in R&D expenses; (ii) adjustments to relevant national tax policies; (iii) a decrease in investment income; (iv) an increase in depreciation of fixed assets; and (v) one-off listing expenses. During the Period, the Group continued to increase its R&D investment. R&D expenses amounted to approximately RMB11.0 mn, representing an increase of 18.6% Y-o-Y and accounting for 13.2% of revenue, and were primarily used to advance innovative pipeline development and platform technology upgrades, providing strong support for the translation of mid-to-long-term R&D outcomes and sustainable growth. During the Period, sales revenue from the Group's core product, Human TAT, amounted to approximately RMB77.5 mn, with sales volume of approximately 9,577,000 units, of which approximately 5,911,000 units were sold in the domestic market and approximately 3,666,000 units in overseas markets. The Group continued to promote the synergistic distribution of ampoule and vial packaging formats and expand end markets, actively strengthening its operational resilience. In the domestic market, terminal coverage reached 27,100 medical institutions (including 1,720 tertiary medical institutions). The vial-packaged products were listed on the centralized pharmaceutical procurement platforms in 32 provinces and cities across China and achieved supplementary follow-up bidding under inter-provincial alliance procurement in 26 provinces and cities. In overseas markets, the Group completed market registrations and renewals in four markets, namely India, the DR Congo, Zanzibar and Ghana, and successfully won government tender orders from Ethiopia, Togo and Mongolia. During the Period, the Group's cumulative overseas contracted orders reached 6,859,000 units, with a total contract value of approximately RMB27.4 mn. Steady Progression of the Dual-wheel Pipeline AI+ Technology Platform Upgrade Activates the Pipeline Innovation Engine During the Period, the Group advanced the integration of AI and machine learning technologies into antiserum R&D and production, enabling its technologies to evolve toward greater precision, enhanced stability, higher purity and increased platform scalability. The application boundaries of its products were expanded to four major fields: bio-toxicosis, viral infections, bacterial infections and immunomodulation, while continuously extending into equine plasma-derived product applications. As of the date of the announcement, the Group had commercialized 3 products, while 5 candidate products had entered new stages of clinical research or commercialization. The development progress of specific pipelines and innovative products is as follows: Bio-toxicosis: have initiated Phase II clinical trials with Agkistrodon Halys Antivenom with the first patient enrolled and dosed; completed Phase I clinical trials for Agkistrodon Acutus Antivenom and initiated Phase II clinical trials; and accelerated the antigen design and product development for Polyvalent Snake Antivenom through using AI-assisted antigen design and mRNA-LNP delivery technologies; Viral infections: process research was advanced for Equine Rabies Immunoglobulin F(ab')2 and Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Antibody candidates. Equine immunization validation for the RSV Antigen had been completed and successfully induced high-titer antibody responses. On the veterinary pharmaceutical products side, the new drug registration application for Recombinant Porcine Interferon α is in progress; Bacterial infections: For Equine Anti-Drug-Resistant Bacteria F(ab')2, the Group completed the recognition of key targets in Pseudomonas aeruginosa by polyclonal antibodies and validated their potential for virulence neutralization. On the veterinary pharmaceutical products side, the Group enriched its veterinary anti-infective product portfolio building upon the commercialization of Veterinary TAT; Immunomodulation: Phased breakthroughs were achieved in veterinary pharmaceutical products, with Pig Spleen Transfer Factor and Bursal Peptide Injection obtaining the New Veterinary Drug Registration Certificates. Their commercialization will be advanced successively; Equine plasma-derived product applications: With the commercialization of PMSG as a key entry point, the Group accelerated production line upgrades and global market penetration. Meanwhile, it accelerated the exploration of the development potential of multiple functional components in equine plasma and extended into diversified application scenarios such as veterinary regenerative medicine, research and diagnostic reagents, driving the multi-component development and multi-scenario translation of biological resources and expanding the Group's commercialization potential. Accelerated Translation of R&D Translations Unlocking the Potential of the Equine Polyclonal Antibody Platform, and archiving Promising Laboratory Validation Data for Purification Technologies The continued expansion of the pipeline is underpinned by the Group's equine polyclonal antibody technology platform. Compared with a single-target pathway, equine polyclonal antibodies are characterized by multi-epitope recognition, rapid response and scalable production. Combined with the diverse immunoglobulin repertoires and large-body-size advantages of horses, this technology provides an important foundation of high protective efficacy, high throughput and cost-effectiveness for the Group's pipeline R&D and industrialization. During the Period, the Group deepened its core technological advantages in antiserum and empowered the integrated digital and intelligent upgrade of R&D and manufacturing through AI+, transitioning from the traditional "passive screening" paradigm to "proactive intelligent design" and building a more differentiated technological barrier at the platform level for equine polyclonal antibody. In antigen development, the Group established an "AI+ fusion protein mRNA antigen" development platform, further enhancing the efficiency of antigen design and subsequent product development. In antibody preparation, the Group developed an equine F(ab')2 process platform based on immobilized dual recombinant endopeptidases IdeZ2 and IdeS and, for the first time, established an IdeZ2+IdeS synergistic dual-endopeptidase "one-pot" process based on molecular mechanism design, thereby enhancing R&D and preparation efficiency. During the Period, the Group also achieved phased validation of cutting-edge research results. At the laboratory research stage, the Group applied two purification technologies, namely ion exchange chromatography and pathogen-specific affinity chromatography, achieving mean increases in specific activity of relevant products of approximately 30% and 400%, respectively, compared with existing products (the average specific activity of products as of December 31, 2025 was 82,000 IU/gp, while the Chinese Pharmacopoeia Standard was 45,000 IU/gp). The relevant results provide innovative support for the quality upgrade of existing products and the engineering, standardization and platform-based development of subsequent pipelines. Next Step Comprehensively Upgrading the "AEPC Platform" to Develop the Clinical Value of Dual-wheel Pipelines Across Five Directions Looking ahead, the Group proposes to upgrade and build the "AEPC Platform (馬源多克隆抗體全產業鏈數智平台)" upon its existing integrated full-industry-chain advantages. Anchored by four core segments: Antigen Development, Equine Immunization, Plasma Processing and Commercialization. The platform horizontally integrates key segments of the industry chain and vertically extends across R&D, production, sales and governance, forming an interconnected innovation closed loop. Leveraging this platform, the Group will accelerate resource synergy and commonality reuse across the full industry chain under its dual-wheel pipeline strategy, reduce the marginal costs of pipeline development and translation, consolidate its leadership in Human TAT while cultivating incremental global markets, and deepen the development of its equine polyclonal antibody technology platform. On this basis, the Group will advance the integration of AI, biotechnology and the antiserum industry, broaden the boundaries of antiserum indications, deepen the clinical value of equine plasma-derived products, explore diversified commercial opportunities, and bring more high-quality Chinese antiserum products to patients worldwide. About Jiangxi Institute of Biological Products Inc. Jiangxi Institute of Biological Products Co., Ltd. (the "Group") is a globally leading platform-based biopharmaceutical enterprise integrating the full industrial chain of antiserum products. By 2025 sales volume, the Group was the world's largest provider and exporter of human tetanus antitoxins (Human TAT) and had maintained a dominant position in China's Human TAT market for 19 consecutive years. Its products are exported to over 30 countries and regions worldwide and have cumulatively served more than one billion patients globally. Pursuing its mission of "Make Quality Medicine for People Worldwide", and leveraging the industry's extremely high entry barriers, rare full-industry-chain integration and leading technological advantages, the Group has established a dual-wheel synergistic pipeline of "human pharmaceutical products + veterinary pharmaceutical products". It focuses on four fields: bio-toxicosis, viral infections, bacterial infections and immunomodulation, while continuously extending into equine plasma-derived product applications. In 2026, the Group upgraded and built the "AEPC Platform", integrating R&D, production, sales and governance across the full-industry-chain through AI and digital capabilities, deepening the underlying technological barriers of equine polyclonal antibody, providing safer and more effective solutions to address critical unmet medical and animal health prevention and treatment needs globally, and contributing Chinese strength to global public health. For more information, please visit https://en.jxswzp.cn/. SOURCE Jiangxi Institute of Biological Products Inc

新罕布什尔州 疫苗 学校规则保持不变 - Monadnock Ledger-Transcript

New Hampshire’s rules for school vaccines are unchanged - Monadnock Ledger-Transcript
新闻简报根据 Monadnock Ledger-Transcript,新罕布什尔州学校的 疫苗 规则截至 2026 年 8 月 28 日保持不变。该州要求进入公立学校、天主教学校和有执照的日托机构的学生接种 10 种疾病的疫苗,包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)。文章指出,虽然一些立法者试图削弱这些要求,但所有提议均被否决。它还强调,没有 疫苗接种 证明的儿童如果至少接种了一剂疫苗并预约下一次所需剂量,仍然可以在“有条件注册”下注册。这篇文章强调了维持这些要求的重要性,因为它们是由州制定的,不受联邦政策的影响。文章中没有报告与 RSV 相关的具体病例、死亡或暴露情况,其重点是政策稳定性而不是 暴发 或健康影响。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

New Hampshire’s rules for school vaccines are unchanged - Monadnock Ledger-Transcript

News Brief
New Hampshire’s school vaccine rules remain unchanged as of August 28, 2026, according to the Monadnock Ledger-Transcript. The state requires students entering public schools, Catholic schools, and licensed day care facilities to be vaccinated against 10 diseases, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). The article notes that while some lawmakers attempted to weaken these requirements, all proposals were defeated. It also highlights that children without proof of vaccination can still enroll under 'conditional enrollment' if they have at least one dose and an appointment for the next required dose. The piece emphasizes the importance of maintaining these requirements, as they are set by the state and not affected by federal policies. No specific cases, deaths, or exposures related to RSV are reported in the article, which focuses on policy stability rather than outbreak or health impact.
Original source text (English)

Menu Monadnock Ledger-Transcript LOG IN LOG OUT E-PAPER NEWSLETTERS SUBSCRIBE MANAGE ACCOUNT Open Search Search for: Search Subscribe Menu News Opinion Sports Arts & Life Obituaries Puzzles Topic Highlight Education Business Jobs Calendar Classifieds Submit an Obituary Advertise Posted inNews New Hampshire’s rules for school vaccines are unchanged by David Brooks August 28, 2026August 26, 2026 Share this: Share on Facebook (Opens in new window) Facebook Email a link to a friend (Opens in new window) Email Print (Opens in new window) Print New Hampshire state law requires children be vaccinated against polio, tetanus, hepatitis B, mumps, pertussis, rubella, rubeola, varicella, diphtheria, measles and haemophilus influenzae type B before entering school or childcare. Credit: (Photo by Scott Housley/Centers for Disease Control and Prevention) As New Hampshire children return to school in the coming weeks, they will be just as well-vaccinated as they have always been. Since school vaccine rules are set by states and not the federal government, New Hampshire’s requirements have not been affected by the Trump administration’s attacks on the medical technology. Some New Hampshire lawmakers attempted to weaken school vaccine rules this year, including one bill that would have tossed out all requirements, but all were defeated. Vaccines strengthen the body’s natural immune system by training it to recognize diseases in advance. As has long been the case, students entering public school in New Hampshire, as well as all Catholic schools and licensed day care facilities, must have up-to-date vaccinations for 10 diseases: diphtheria, tetanus, pertussis (whooping cough), measles, mumps, rubella, varicella, polio and hepatitis B. Most require more than one dose, and several are combined into single shots, such as DTaP, which protects against diphtheria, tetanus and pertussis. Doctors say there’s no reason not to get multiple vaccination shots at once rather than taking on the time and expense of multiple visits. Children who don’t have proof from doctors of all vaccines can still start school under “conditional enrollment” when there is documentation that they’ve had at least one dose of each required immunization and an appointment has been made for the next required dose. If you’re unsure of a child’s vaccine status, contact a local medical provider. Even if there’s no paper record of vaccines, many can be confirmed through laboratory tests. Several other vaccines are recommended by pediatricians but are not required for school, including flu and COVID-19. Also recommended are RSV, a respiratory virus that infects the lungs and can be very dangerous for infants, and HPV, or human papillomavirus, for older children. HPV is passed through sexual contact and can cause several types of cancer. Details of state requirements can be seen on the Department of Health and Human Services website. 敦促家庭在冬季之前通过免疫接种来保护婴儿免受 RSV 感染 - dublinlive.ie

Families urged to protect babies against RSV with immunisation ahead of winter season - dublinlive.ie
新闻简报爱尔兰 HSE 启动了 2026-2027 年 RSV 婴儿免疫计划,敦促家长在冬季保护婴儿免受 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的感染。该计划于 2026 年 9 月 1 日开始,通过妇产医院和全科医生诊所提供免费免疫接种。它针对新生儿和年龄较大的婴儿,根据出生日期提供特定资格。去年冬天,超过 37,000 名婴儿接受了免疫接种,从而使 RSV 相关住院率减少了 70%,并避免了 1,000 多次急诊就诊、住院和 ICU 入住。根据国际证据,免疫接种对于预防 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染的有效性超过 80%。HSE 鼓励 2026 年 3 月至 8 月出生的婴儿的父母在 RSV 季节之前通过全科医生诊所寻求免疫接种。该计划旨在提供持续整个呼吸道合胞病毒高峰季节的即时保护,减轻婴儿的疾病负担。
查看新闻五要素
时间
RSV 免疫计划于 2026 年 9 月 1 日开始,旨在为婴儿在第一个秋冬季节提供保护。该计划的影响是在上一个冬季(可能在 2025 年底至 2026 年初左右)观察到的。
地点与对象
该计划正在爱尔兰实施,专门针对新生儿和较大婴儿,重点关注 2026 年 9 月至 2027 年 2 月期间出生的婴儿以及高危婴儿。
事件
HSE 宣布了 2026-2027 年 RSV 婴儿免疫计划,旨在保护婴儿在冬季免受 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染。该计划包括通过妇产医院和全科医生诊所为符合条件的婴儿提供免费免疫接种。
规模、影响与风险
去年冬天,爱尔兰有超过 37,000 名婴儿接种了 RSV 疫苗。该计划使该季节出生的婴儿因 RSV 相关的住院治疗减少了 70%,估计避免了 1,000 多次急诊室就诊、住院和入住 ICU。
应对、状态与不确定性
该计划目前正在积极实施,免疫接种将于 2026 年 9 月 1 日开始。HSE 鼓励家长在 RSV 季节之前通过全科医生诊所接受该计划。该计划的有效性得到了国际证据的支持,显示预防 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染的功效超过 80%。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Families urged to protect babies against RSV with immunisation ahead of winter season - dublinlive.ie

News Brief
The HSE in Ireland has launched the 2026-2027 RSV Infant Immunisation Programme, urging parents to protect their babies against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) during the winter season. The programme, which began on September 1, 2026, offers free immunisation through maternity hospitals and GP practices. It targets newborns and older infants, with specific eligibility based on birth dates. Last winter, over 37,000 infants were immunised, leading to a 70% reduction in RSV-related hospitalisations and the prevention of over 1,000 ED presentations, hospitalisations, and ICU admissions. The immunisation is over 80% effective in preventing RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infections, according to international evidence. The HSE encourages parents of babies born between March and August 2026 to seek immunisation through GP practices before the RSV season. The programme is designed to provide immediate protection that lasts through the peak RSV season, reducing the burden of the illness on infants.
Original source text (English)

The HSE has announced that the 2026-2027 RSV Infant Immunisation Programme starts tomorrow, Tuesday, September 1. The immunisation will be free for babies through maternity hospitals and GP practises. For the 2026-2027 programme: READ MORE: Family of M9 crash sisters issue update as they focus on 'being together' READ MORE: 'Most valuable' Irish coin sells for €780 on eBay – see if you have it at home Babies born between 1 September 2026 and 28 February 2027 will be offered RSV immunisation in maternity hospitals before they go home, where possible. Babies born between 1 March and 31 August 2026 can receive RSV immunisation through participating GP practices. Eligible babies at higher risk of serious RSV illness will be offered immunisation through a specialist pathway. Dr Seán Fennessy, Specialist Registrar in Public Health Medicine, HSE Dublin and Midlands said: “RSV is very common during the autumn and winter months, but for some young babies it can cause serious illness. The RSV immunisation programme protects babies during their first autumn and winter. It protects babies immediately and the protection lasts through autumn and winter – meaning babies are protected when RSV is circulating at highest levels. “We’ve seen the positive impact of RSV immunisation in Ireland over the last two years, helping to reduce the number of babies presenting with RSV and needing hospitalisation. International evidence shows it is over 80% effective in preventing RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infections.” According to the HSE, last Winter more than 37,000 infants were immunised against RSV. Among those were 88% of babies born September to February 2026 received the immunisation in maternity hospitals, and the uptake rate among high-risk infants was 100%. The programme reduced RSV-related hospitalisations by 70% among babies born during the season. Infant immunisation is estimated to have averted over 1,000 ED presentations, hospitalisations and ICU admissions, significantly reducing the burden of RSV illness among infants in Ireland. HSE Dublin and Midlands Spokesperson said: “We would encourage parents of babies who were born between March and August to take up the offer through participating GP practices ideally before the RSV season. B routine priorityPublic-health news

RSV vaccine expanded to high-risk over-65s - Caerphilly Observer

News Brief
On September 1, 2026, health officials in Caerphilly County Borough expanded the free NHS vaccine against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) to adults aged 65 to 74 with underlying health conditions. This policy change aims to protect high-risk individuals from RSV, which can lead to severe respiratory illness. The decision was made by health officials, and no specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers were reported. The expansion is part of broader public health measures, and no details on testing, isolation, or contact tracing were provided. The event is confirmed and reported in the full original article, with no uncertainties noted in the evidence.

For investors, the key question is how the company’s latest quarterly revenue and cash position support its ability to advance antiviral and liver-disease programs without near-term profitability. Market performance and valuation context Recent market-data snapshots for Enanta on August 31, 2026 indicate a share price in the vicinity of $13.30, leaving the stock down from the start-of-year level near $15.80 and signaling a year-to-date decline on the order of mid-teens percentage points. That price range implies a modest market capitalization relative to larger biotech peers, reflecting both the company’s focused pipeline and the lack of marketed products generating substantial recurring revenue in 2026. Against this backdrop, investors often assess Enanta through its cash runway and upcoming data readouts rather than traditional earnings multiples, since negative net income and operating losses remain part of the investment profile. Latest earnings and cash runway The most recent reported quarterly results for Enanta, covering the first half of calendar 2026, point to total revenue in the tens of millions of dollars, largely driven by royalty streams and collaboration income rather than product sales. Within that reporting period, Enanta recorded a net loss that underscores the scale of ongoing R&D spending, yet the balance sheet showed a cash and marketable securities position sufficient to fund operations for several years under current planning assumptions. Compared with the same period a year earlier, revenue in the latest quarter showed single-digit percentage growth, while the net loss widened modestly as the company invested more heavily in clinical development across multiple antiviral and liver-focused programs. Pipeline and recent clinical developments Enanta’s pipeline in 2026 centers on small-molecule therapies targeting viral infections and liver diseases, including candidates for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), and non-alcoholic steatohepatitis (NASH). During the most recent quarter, the company highlighted progress across these programs, such as continued enrollment in mid-stage trials and regulatory interactions that pave the way for larger, potentially registrational studies in future periods. Historically, Enanta’s collaboration-driven revenue model has been supported by milestones and royalties from earlier partnerships, and the current pipeline strategy aims to build on that experience by advancing wholly owned assets that could generate higher-margin revenue over time. Representative product and research focus One representative focus within Enanta’s research portfolio is its work on inhibitors targeting key viral enzymes and pathways, a strategy designed to deliver oral therapies that can be combined with existing standards of care or used as monotherapy in certain patient groups. These small-molecule candidates are engineered to offer favorable pharmacokinetic profiles, with the goal of once-daily dosing and robust antiviral activity that reduces viral load and improves clinical outcomes in infections such as RSV and HBV. 随着呼吸道病毒在学年开始时传播,医生提供了保持孩子健康的建议 - WTOC

Doctors offer tips to keep kids healthy as respiratory viruses spread at start of school year - WTOC
新闻简报新学年开始之际,佐治亚州萨凡纳的当地医生正在提供健康提示,帮助儿童避免呼吸道病毒。Memorial Health 的儿科医生 Aimee Behnke 指出,学生中 RSV、链球菌性喉炎和 COVID-19 病例有所增加。她强调睡眠的重要性,建议每晚睡眠 8 至 12 小时,并强调饮食在支持免疫健康方面的作用。萨凡纳整体健康中心的凯瑟琳·哈格罗夫补充说,天然食品,尤其是富含纤维和营养素的食品,可以帮助儿童的免疫系统更好地发挥作用。医生还建议家长,如果孩子出现病毒症状,请将其留在家中;如果症状没有改善,请联系儿科医生。虽然文章讨论了呼吸道病毒的传播,但没有提供具体的病例数或风险级别。该建议是一般性的,旨在预防,而不是解决已确认的 暴发。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Doctors offer tips to keep kids healthy as respiratory viruses spread at start of school year - WTOC

News Brief
As the school year begins, local doctors in Savannah, Ga, are offering health tips to help children avoid respiratory viruses. A pediatrician at Memorial Health, Aimee Behnke, noted an increase in cases of RSV, strep throat, and COVID-19 among students. She emphasized the importance of sleep, recommending eight to 12 hours per night, and highlighted the role of diet in supporting immune health. Kathryn Hargrove from Holistic Wellness of Savannah added that whole foods, particularly those rich in fiber and nutrients, can help children's immune systems function better. Doctors also advised parents to keep children home if they show symptoms of a virus and to contact their pediatrician if the symptoms do not improve. While the article discusses the spread of respiratory viruses, it does not provide specific case counts or risk levels. The advice is general and aimed at prevention rather than addressing a confirmed outbreak.
Original source text (English)

Close By Stephanie Adkisson Published: Aug. 31, 2026 at 10:15 PM UTC|Updated: 23 hours ago Share Add Us On GoogleAdd as a preferred source on Google SAVANNAH, Ga. (WTOC) - Local doctors said respiratory viruses begin to spread every year as students return to school, and getting the right amount of sleep and eating a balanced diet offers the best protection. Dr. Aimee Behnke, a Memorial Health pediatrician, said she has seen a lot of COVID, strep throat and RSV cases as students wrap up their first month of school. Sleep, diet cited as key defenses Behnke said there are two key things parents can do to help keep their children healthy. “School-aged children need between eight and 12 hours of sleep a night, and almost none of them are getting that amount, but sleep is so important to your overall health, so it’ll definitely help keep your body healthy,” Behnke said. Dr. Kathryn Hargrove, of Holistic Wellness of Savannah, said diet also plays a role in fighting off illness. “Whole foods, in the diet, the better off you are and that helps their immune system work properly and fight off the infection,” Hargrove said. Hargrove said packing a child’s lunch with high protein, good fats and food rich in fiber can also support learning. “It helps your cognitive function, which is your frontal brain, making decisions, being able to focus and pay attention,” Hargrove said. Vitamins versus whole foods Behnke said parents often ask her which vitamins can help keep their children healthy. She recommended getting vitamins from food rather than supplements. “Drinking the juice is like sugar water, so getting the fiber and the water from the fruit would be much healthier, but eat your fruits and veggies, an apple a day might keep the doctor away for a little bit,” Behnke said. Behnke said parents should keep children home if they catch a virus and should contact their local pediatrician if symptoms do not improve after a few days. Copyright 2026 WTOC. All rights reserved.
